### [1] [Multiorbital driving of quantum geometric nonlinear transport in oxide interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28213)
*Irene Gaiardoni,Mattia Trama,Roberta Citro,Annika Johansson*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作以多轨道紧束缚方法研究SrTiO₃基二维电子气中量子几何（quantum geometry）对非线性电导的贡献，比较(001)与(111)界面取向的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何在决定低维电子系统本征非线性输运性质中具有重要作用，但氧化物价界面的量子几何非线性输运响应尚待理论厘清。

Method: 采用多轨道紧束缚方法（multi-orbital tight-binding approach），对比(001)与(111)两种晶向界面的能带结构与量子度规（quantum metric）动量空间分布。

Result: 多带电子结构对非线性输运的量子几何贡献起关键作用；t₂g轨道在避免能带交叉（avoided band crossings）附近的杂化决定带归一化量子度规的动量空间分布与非线性电导。

Conclusion: 晶体取向带来显著差异：(001)界面在Lifshitz转变附近呈现尖锐峰-谷特征，而(111)界面的三方对称性促进强轨道混合；轨道杂化与晶向的相互作用产生独特的量子几何非线性输运响应。

Abstract: Quantum geometry plays a relevant role in determining the intrinsic non-linear transport properties of low-dimensional electron systems. Here, we theoretically investigate the quantum geometric contribution to the non-linear conductivity in $\text{SrTiO}_3$-based two-dimensional electron gases, comparing the prototypical (001) and (111) crystallographic interface orientations. Using a multi-orbital tight-binding approach, we uncover the crucial role of the multiband electronic structure in determining the quantum-geometric contribution to nonlinear transport. In particular, we show how the hybridization of $t_{2g}$ orbitals near avoided band crossings shapes the momentum-space distribution of the band-normalized quantum metric and the resulting nonlinear conductivity. Furthermore, we reveal a strong dependence on crystal orientation: while the (001) interface exhibits sharp peak-and-dip features confined around Lifshitz transitions, the trigonal symmetry of the (111) interface promotes strong orbital mixing. Our results highlight how the interplay between orbital hybridization and crystallographic orientation gives rise to distinct quantum-geometric nonlinear transport responses in oxide interfaces.

</details>


### [2] [Wireless readout and control of cavity magnon-polaritons via photonic modes delocalised across a gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28347)
*Alban Joseph,Ryan D McKenzie,Amy Eden,Morven Last,Shannon Howard,Alzbeta Oravcova,Mawgan A Smith,Robert L Stamps,Rair Macedo*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出利用两个空间分离、无线耦合的环形谐振器（ring resonators），实现对腔磁子极化激元（cavity magnon-polaritons）的远程激发与读取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有腔磁子学（cavity magnonics）研究主要依赖测量腔体响应（cavity response）读取光子-磁子杂化（photon-magnon hybridisation），且readout方式长期未有变化，尤其在二维谐振器（two-dimensional resonators）集成化背景下仍沿用该思路。

Method: 通过两个空间分离的环形谐振器之间的电感耦合（inductive coupling）实现能量转移：一个谐振器激发磁子体系，另一个谐振器测量其杂化模式谱（hybrid-mode spectrum）。

Result: 在毫米至厘米的间距范围内实现了腔磁子极化激元的无线读取（wireless readout），并在远程测得的谱中识别出其特征响应（characteristic response）。

Conclusion: 无线谐振器耦合（wireless resonator coupling）可作为远程探测（remote interrogation）杂化光子-磁子系统的可行途径。

Abstract: In cavity magnonics, photon-magnon hybridisation is typically detected by measuring the cavity response (i.e. measuring cavity resonance shifts through its reflection or transmission spectra). While a large body of work in the field has focused on three-dimensional cavities, a variety of two-dimensional resonators have also been explored as hosts for photonic modes due to their compatibility with on-chip integration. Nevertheless, the readout approach remains largely unchanged. Here, we demonstrate remote excitation and readout of cavity magnon-polaritons using two spatially separated, wirelessly coupled ring resonators. Through inductive coupling, energy is transferred between the resonators, enabling the magnonic system to be excited by one resonator while its hybrid-mode spectrum is detected through measurements of the other. We demonstrate wireless readout of cavity magnon-polaritons at separations ranging from millimetres to centimetres, identifying their characteristic response in the remotely measured spectrum. This establishes wireless resonator coupling as a route to remote interrogation of hybrid photon-magnon systems.

</details>


### [3] [Spherical HgTe nanocrystal: a multi-band effective mass study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28689)
*Rui Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文通过解析求解多带有效质量方程，系统研究了球形HgTe纳米晶中的尺寸量子化能级、本征态及光学选择定则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清球形HgTe纳米晶中多带耦合下的量子化能级结构、能级交叉行为及光学跃迁规则，为相关纳米结构的光电性质理解提供理论基础。

Method: 采用球近似下同时包含Γ6与Γ8能带的六带Kane模型（six-band Kane model），解析求解多带有效质量方程（multi-band effective-mass equations）以获得本征态与能级。

Result: 本征态可按总角动量（total angular momentum）与宇称（parity）分类；随纳米晶半径变化，不仅电子与空穴能级之间发生交叉，空穴能级自身之间亦出现交叉。每个自旋子空间最低的两个电子态为表面态，分别具有偶宇称与奇宇称。

Conclusion: 光学选择定则（optical selection rules）由两部分构成，即宇称选择（parity selection）与角动量选择（angular momentum selection）。

Abstract: The size-quantized energies, eigenstates, and optical selection rules in a spherical HgTe nanocrystal are studied by solving the multi-band effective-mass equations analytically. The six-band Kane model, involving both the $Γ_{6}$ and $Γ_{8}$ bands within the spherical approximation, is employed in the derivation. The eigenstates can be classified by both the total angular momentum and the parity. As the nanocrystal radius varies, crossings occur not only between electron and hole energy levels but also among the hole levels themselves. The lowest two electron states (in each spin subspace) are surface states, one with even parity and the other with odd parity. The optical selection rules consist of two parts, namely, the parity selection and the angular momentum selection.

</details>


### [4] [Quantum Hall Effect in a 3D Cross-Dimensional Quantum Anomalous Hall System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28751)
*Liang-Liang Cheng,Ao-Long Li,Zhi-Qiang Zhang,Hua Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作系统研究由两个Weyl半金属哈密顿量耦合构成的3D量子反常霍尔系统中的跨维度输运与量子霍尔效应，揭示各向异性表面态、手征模式及磁场调控的丰富霍尔响应，并发现磁场下传统基于陈数的体边对应失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清3D拓扑体系中磁场驱动机制，以及量子化霍尔效应与陈数（Chern numbers）在3D系统中的相互关系，此前尚缺乏系统理解。

Method: 方法上通过耦合两个Weyl半金属哈密顿量（Weyl-semimetal Hamiltonians）构造3D量子反常霍尔系统，分析沿三个高对称方向（high-symmetry directions）施加磁场时陈数、手征模式（chiral modes）与霍尔响应的演化，并结合多终端霍尔输运（multi-terminal Hall transport）数值模拟进行验证。

Result: 结果发现系统具有高度各向异性的表面态（surface states）与手征模式，不同方向的手征模式可源自不同机制并呈现各异磁场调制特性；磁场下基于陈数的传统体边对应（bulk-boundary correspondence）发生失效，陈数无法完整刻画手征态演化。

Conclusion: 该失效归因于表面能带在动量空间（momentum space）中的独特分布，研究拓展了对3D拓扑系统磁场驱动机制的理解，并深化了对3D系统中量子化霍尔效应与陈数相互作用的认识。

Abstract: We systematically investigate cross-dimensional transport and the resulting quantum Hall effect in 3D quantum anomalous Hall effect systems, which are constructed by coupling two Weyl-semimetal Hamiltonians. Our study unveils that the system hosts highly anisotropic surface states and chiral modes, leading to rich quantum Hall responses. Crucially, we clarify how magnetic fields applied along three high-symmetry directions dictate the evolution of the Chern numbers, chiral modes, and Hall responses. We discover that chiral modes along different directions can originate from distinct mechanisms, giving rise to various magnetic modulation characteristics. Furthermore, we unveil the breakdown of the conventional Chern-number-based bulk-boundary correspondence under magnetic fields, where the Chern numbers alone fail to fully characterize the evolution of the chiral states. This anomalous behavior is attributed to the unique momentum-space distributions of surface bands. These insights are further validated numerically through multi-terminal Hall transport simulations. Our findings expand the understanding of magnetic-field-driven mechanisms in 3D topological systems and provide deeper insights into the interplay between quantized Hall effects and Chern numbers in 3D systems.

</details>


### [5] [Local Pressure Induced Quantum Dot to Ring Transition and Aharonov Bohm Effect in Reconfigurable MoTe2 Bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28786)
*Ibsal Assi,A. Belayadi,Hocine Bahlouli,Udo Schwingenschlögl,J. P. F. LeBlanc*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用局部压力在MoTe2同质双层中实现无需光刻的可重构量子限域，限域几何可从量子点连续调至量子环，并在量子环区观察到Aharonov-Bohm效应及干涉型电流图样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索以局部压力作为工程手段，在二维材料中实现可重构量子限域（reconfigurable quantum confinement），替代依赖光刻（lithography）的传统图案化方式。

Method: 施加平滑且空间变化的压力分布（spatially varying pressure profile），调控AB堆叠双层中的电子参数空间调制，从而改变有效限域势；并在垂直磁场下计算能谱、持续电流及与源漏电极耦合后的输运性质。

Result: 压力诱导的有效限域势出现偏心极小值，使基态概率密度偏离中心，形成环形限域；量子环区呈现通量周期性能谱与持续电流（Aharonov-Bohm效应），并出现显著的Aharonov-Bohm电导振荡，实空间电流图样在整数与半整数磁通量子处分别表现相长与相消干涉。

Conclusion: 局部加压的MoTe2双层可作为免光刻的可重构量子限域平台，并实现压力可调的Aharonov-Bohm干涉测量（interferometry）。

Abstract: We demonstrate that local pressure provides an effective means of engineering reconfigurable quantum confinement in MoTe2 homobilayers. By applying a smooth, spatially varying pressure profile, we show that the confinement geometry can be continuously tuned from a conventional quantum dot to a quantum ring, with the emergence of annular confinement being a distinctive feature of AB-stacked bilayers. This crossover originates from the pressure-induced spatial modulation in the electronic parameters, which produces an off-center minimum in the effective confinement potential, thereby redistributing the ground-state probability density away from the center and giving rise to an annular confinement geometry. In the quantum-ring regime, a perpendicular magnetic field gives rise to a flux-periodic energy spectrum and persistent currents, providing characteristic signatures of the Aharonov-Bohm effect. By coupling the pressure-induced quantum ring to source and drain leads, we further demonstrate pronounced Aharonov-Bohm conductance oscillations, accompanied by real-space current patterns exhibiting constructive and destructive interference at integer and half-integer magnetic flux quanta, respectively. These findings establish locally pressurized MoTe2 bilayers as a lithography-free platform for reconfigurable quantum confinement and realizing pressure-tunable Aharonov-Bohm interferometry in two dimensional materials.

</details>


### [6] [Floquet-Induced Valley Polarization and Topological Phases in Twisted Bilayer Graphene Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28797)
*A. Belayadi,I. A. Assi,A. Johansson,C. I. Osuala,H. Bahlouli,Udo Schwingenschlögl,J. P. F. LeBlanc,A. Abbout*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该摘要理论研究受圆偏振光照射的扭角双层石墨烯（twisted bilayer graphene, tTBG）异质结构，指出在垂直电场与绝缘衬底协同破坏反演对称性（inversion symmetry）的条件下，Floquet驱动可诱导以陈数（Chern number）C = -2、-1、0为标志的拓扑相变，并实现完美谷极化畴壁通道与自旋-谷过滤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将光场驱动与异质结构工程相结合，实现对石墨烯体系中谷自由度（valley degrees of freedom）及相关拓扑相的可控调控，并探索其在自旋与谷自由度操控方面的可调性。

Method: 采用理论方法，对处于圆偏振光（circularly polarized light）下的tTBG异质结构进行建模分析；通过外加垂直电场并引入绝缘衬底以打破反演对称性，同时引入近邻二维铁磁接触（two-dimensional ferromagnetic contacts）产生的磁性不对称。

Result: 识别出谷简并被解除的区间：一个谷保持无能隙的线性类狄拉克色散，另一谷打开有限带隙并变为电子非活性。Floquet驱动与反演对称性破缺的相互作用诱导了C = -2、C = -1、C = 0的拓扑相变，并出现完美谷极化的畴壁通道（domain-wall channels）；磁性不对称进一步实现自旋-谷过滤（spin-valley filtering）。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明石墨烯基异质结构可作为高度可调体系，用于操控自旋与谷自由度，在谷电子学与拓扑相调控方面具有应用潜力。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate twisted bilayer graphene (tTBG) heterostructures subjected to circularly polarized light. Inversion symmetry is broken through the combined application of an out-of-plane electric field and the incorporation of insulating substrates, enabling precise control of the valley degrees of freedom and associated topological phases. We identify a regime in which the valley degeneracy is lifted: one valley retains a gapless, linear Dirac-like dispersion, whereas the other develops a finite band gap and becomes electronically inactive. The interplay between Floquet driving and inversion-symmetry breaking induces topological phase transitions characterized by Chern numbers C = -2, C = -1, and C = 0, together with the emergence of perfectly valley-polarized domain-wall channels. Furthermore, magnetic asymmetry arising from proximity to adjacent two-dimensional ferromagnetic contacts enables spin-valley filtering. These findings establish the potential of graphene-based heterostructures as highly tunable systems for controlling spin and valley degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [7] [Interface invariants of rhombohedral graphene with layer-dependent potentials and couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29093)
*Yuelong Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究菱面体堆叠（ABC）石墨烯在垂直偏压下畴壁（domain wall）所束缚的谷分辨界面模式（valley-resolved interface modes），确定在任意单调层间势与键依赖层间耦合下，体差不变量（bulk-difference invariant）可取哪些值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 畴壁界面模式的符号数量由体差不变量固定，但实际屏蔽（screening）产生的层间势为任意单调剖面、层间耦合（interlayer coupling）也可能逐键变化，因此需要厘清这些物理条件下不变量（invariant）的可达取值范围。

Method: 在单粒子连续模型（single-particle continuum model）中，将 m 层堆叠的不变量等价为零动量能级（zero-momentum levels）占据角权重之差，从而把分类问题化为能级排序问题；并辅以精确转移矩阵（transfer-matrix）计算界面谱进行验证。

Result: 等耦合情形下，非线性单调势（nonlinear monotone potentials）给出的不变量集合与线性势完全相同，屏蔽只移动相边界（phase boundaries）而不产生新不变量值；键依赖耦合下可达不变量范围不变，但自五层起其内部能隙被填满。反射对称（reflection-symmetric）堆叠构成具共同能隙的大子族，并给出五、六层共同全局能隙下的下界；其中八层门控畴壁的每谷共传播通道数（co-propagating channels）随门强度由 8 切换至 16。

Conclusion: 转移矩阵计算精确复现了所有预测的有符号交叉计数（signed crossing count），并以范数界（norm bound）给出与指定能隙相容的层间耦合最小展宽；结果表明不变量的可达范围对势的单调形状稳健，但对键依赖耦合的能隙结构敏感。

Abstract: Domain walls in the perpendicular bias of rhombohedral (ABC-stacked) graphene bind valley-resolved interface modes whose signed number is fixed by a bulk-difference invariant. We determine which values of this invariant are reachable when the layer potentials are arbitrary monotone profiles, as produced by screening, and when the interlayer couplings vary from bond to bond. Within the single-particle continuum model, the invariant of an $m$-layer stack equals the difference of occupied angular weights of the zero-momentum levels, which reduces the classification to the ordering of those levels. At equal coupling, nonlinear monotone potentials reproduce exactly the set of invariants of linear potentials, also under the requirement of a common energy gap: screening moves phase boundaries but creates no new invariant values. With bond-dependent coupling, we characterize all occupied configurations. The range of attainable invariants is unchanged, but the gaps inside it are filled, beginning at five layers. Reflection-symmetric stacks realize a large subfamily with a guaranteed common gap, and lower bounds on single-bulk gaps complete the set of invariants with a common global gap for five and six layers. Among the examples is an eight-layer domain wall defined purely by gates, whose number of co-propagating channels per valley switches from 8 to 16 as the gate strength increases. Exact transfer-matrix calculations of the interface spectrum reproduce every predicted signed crossing count. A norm bound gives the minimum spread of interlayer coupling compatible with a prescribed gap.

</details>


### [8] [Population of spin orbit-split-electron branches in semimetallic HgTe quantum wells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29487)
*G. M. Minkov,O. E. Rut,A. A. Sherstobitov,A. V. Germanenko,S. A. Dvoretski,N. N. Mikhailov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作通过磁阻、霍尔效应与Shubnikov-de Haas振荡实验研究HgTe基无带隙半导体量子阱，发现半金属谱宽量子阱中电子同时占据两个自旋轨道分裂能支，与理想化模型不符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想化模型预期半金属电荷载流子谱的量子阱中电子仅占据单一自旋轨道分裂分支，实验需检验该预期在真实门控异质结构中的适用性。

Method: 在门控HgTe基无带隙半导体量子阱异质结构上开展磁阻（magnetoresistance）、霍尔效应（Hall effect）和Shubnikov-de Haas振荡（Shubnikov-de Haas oscillations）的输运测量，覆盖不同量子阱宽度。

Result: 在阱宽对应半金属电荷载流子谱（semimetallic charge carrier spectrum）的量子阱中，电子占据两个自旋轨道分裂能支（spin-orbit-split branches），无论费米能级仅位于导带内还是处于导带—价带重叠区（conduction-valence band overlap）。

Conclusion: 实验结果偏离理想化模型，表明HgTe基量子阱中自旋轨道分裂能支的占据需在更真实的能谱图景下理解。

Abstract: We present the results of experimental studies on magnetoresistance, the Hall effect, and Shubnikov-de Haas oscillations performed in gated heterostructures with HgTe-based gapless semiconductor quantum wells. We show that in quantum wells with widths corresponding to a semimetallic charge carrier spectrum, electrons, in contrast to the idealized model, populate both spin-orbit-split branches of the spectrum. This occurs regardless of whether the Fermi level lies exclusively in the conduction band or within the conduction-valence band overlap region.

</details>


### [9] [FlakeForge: automated lithography-ready device layout from optical images of exfoliated van der Waals flakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29509)
*Or Berman,Ilay Mangel*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: FlakeForge 是一套开源流程（open-source pipeline），可从未经处理的标记芯片光学图像（optical images of a marker-patterned chip）自动生成可直接用于光刻（lithography-ready）的多层设计文件，显著缩短二维材料器件制备的前期人工工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从剥离的范德华薄片（van der Waals flake）制备器件，需在显微镜下人工寻找合适薄片并手工设计光刻版图（lithography layout），耗时且依赖专家经验。

Method: 流程包含薄片可用性评分、基于标记点（marker）的图像对齐与缩放、将用户器件设计拟合至所选薄片轮廓，并将每个接触电极（contact）通过绕开先前所有器件的走线（trace）连接至键合焊盘（bond pad），确保互不重叠。

Result: 在霍尔条（Hall bar）与 Little-Parks 环（Little-Parks ring）上完成验证，四块芯片约半小时处理完毕，而专家人工需数天；流程可全自动无人值守运行，亦可暂停等待用户复核。

Conclusion: 适配新材料、标记设计或器件尺寸仅需配置，无需训练数据（training data）；新增器件类型仅需给出其几何定义，具备良好可迁移性。

Abstract: Fabricating a device from an exfoliated van der Waals flake requires finding a suitable flake under a microscope and designing its lithography layout by hand. We present FlakeForge, an open-source pipeline from raw optical images of a marker-patterned chip to a lithography-ready, multilayer design file. It scores flakes for device usability, aligns and scales each image from its markers, fits the user's device design to each selected flake's contour and connects every contact to a bond pad by a trace routed around all earlier devices, so no two overlap. Demonstrated on Hall bars and Little-Parks rings, it processed four chips in about half an hour, work that took an expert days. It runs fully unattended or pauses for user review. Adapting it to a new material, marker design or device dimensions requires only configuration, with no training data; a new device type requires only its geometry definition.

</details>


### [10] [Kinetic inductance of two-dimensional viscous electron fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29645)
*A. A. Grigorev,A. N. Afanasiev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究窄通道二维电子流体（2D electron fluid）的线性交流响应，指出非局域粘性耗散（non-local viscous dissipation）导致动力学电感（kinetic inductance）被非均匀流场增强，并因粘性趋肤效应（viscous skin effect）在碰撞区即主导阻抗，且响应起始频率落在微波频段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代高迁�移率二维电子系统（two-dimensional electron systems）足够纯净，可呈现类似经典流体的流体动力学输运（hydrodynamic regime）区，其中动量守恒的电子-电子碰撞（momentum-conserving electron-electron collisions）主导体散射，使常规金属的德鲁德模型（Drude model）行为系统性失效。原文旨在考察该流体在交流条件下的响应特征。

Method: 研究窄通道（narrow channel geometry）中二维电子流体的线性反应性交流响应（linear reactive AC response），分析非局域粘性耗散所引发的尺寸效应（size effect），并考察非均匀流动分布（non-uniform flow profile）对动力学电感的影响，与扩散输运（diffusive transport）情形作对比。

Result: 动力学电感代表整个电子流体的集体惯性（collective inertia），因非均匀流动分布而被增强；与扩散输运不同，借助粘性趋肤效应，它在碰撞区（collisional regime）即已主导电阻抗（electrical impedance）。在现有二维流体动力学导体中，反应性行为的起始（onset）位于微波（microwave）频段。

Conclusion: 二维流体动力学导体在微波频段呈现由粘性效应主导的反应性行为，使其成为高温高频电子学（high-frequency electronics）应用中具有前景的材料体系。

Abstract: Modern high-mobility two-dimensional electron systems are pure enough to manifest hydrodynamic regime of electron transport similar to dynamics of classical fluids. With the bulk scattering controlled by momentum-conserving electron-electron collisions, these novel hydrodynamic materials demonstrate systematic violation of conventional metallic behavior due to the Drude model in stationary electric transport. In this letter, we study the linear reactive AC response of 2D electron fluid in a narrow channel geometry and show that the essentially non-local viscous dissipation induces non-trivial size effect in it. In particular, kinetic inductance, which represents collective inertia of the entire electron fluid, is enhanced by non-uniform flow profile and, in contrast to diffusive transport, dominates the electrical impedance already in collisional regime by virtue of viscous skin effect. The onset of reactive behavior falls within the microwave frequency domain in the state-of-the-art 2D hydrodynamic conductors which makes them promising material system for applications in high-frequency electronics at elevated temperatures.

</details>


### [11] [Sondheimer oscillations in Weyl semimetal films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29875)
*Lucas Méchet,Louis-Thomas Gendron,Ion Garate*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文将 Sondheimer 振荡的半经典玻尔兹曼描述推广至 I 型外尔半金属薄膜，纳入贝里曲率、轨道磁矩与塞曼耦合，发现其导致振荡出现拍频与啁啾模式，并给出厚膜与薄膜极限下纵向电导率的解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sondheimer 振荡（Sondheimer oscillations）作为垂直磁场下薄膜表面间电子运动引起的尺寸效应，已在传统金属中研究，但其对拓扑材料贝里曲率（Berry curvature）的依赖尚不充分。

Method: 在半经典玻尔兹曼（Boltzmann）框架下扩展 Sondheimer 振荡理论至 I 型外尔半金属（type-I Weyl semimetal）薄膜，计入贝里曲率、轨道磁矩（orbital magnetic moment）以及塞曼耦合（Zeeman coupling）对费米波矢与电子群速度的影响。

Result: 贝里曲率与轨道磁矩使 Sondheimer 振荡产生拍频（beating pattern）与啁啾（chirping）；在厚膜与薄膜区间分别给出纵向电导率（longitudinal conductivity）的解析表达式。

Conclusion: 结果凸显电子能带拓扑（band topology）与边界散射（boundary scattering）在磁输运（magnetotransport）中的相互作用，为拓扑半金属薄膜的尺寸效应研究提供理论依据。

Abstract: In conducting films subject to an out-of-plane magnetic field, electron motion between the surfaces gives rise to conductance oscillations periodic in magnetic field, known as Sondheimer oscillations. While this size effect has been investigated in conventional metals, its dependence on the Berry curvature of topological materials remains less explored. In this work, we extend the semiclassical Boltzmann description of Sondheimer oscillations to type-I Weyl semimetal films. We account for the Berry curvature, the orbital magnetic moment and the effect of the Zeeman coupling on the Fermi wavevector and the electronic group velocity. The Berry curvature and the orbital magnetic moment generate a beating pattern and chirping in the Sondheimer oscillations. In the thick-film and thin-film regimes, we provide analytical expressions for the longitudinal conductivity, which highlight the interplay between electronic band topology and boundary-scattering in magnetotransport.

</details>


### [12] [Geometric Polarization-Virial Theorems for Field-Induced Dipolar Interactions and Force Reversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30039)
*Shuze Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作揭示远距偶极体系在电场下"吸引转排斥"的完整角向力学结构：立方加权自由能 r³F(r) 在物理伸缩下单调不减，导出能量零点与力零点分离的严格角度层级，并由量子谐振子对定量验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外加电场可使相距较远的原子间相互作用由吸引转为排斥，但支配这一反转的完整角向力学结构此前未知，缺乏对能量零点与力零点关系的统一刻画。

Method: 提出并证明一类熵式几何结构——立方加权相互作用自由能（cubically weighted interaction free energy）r³F(r) 在物理伸缩（physical dilation）下非递减；据此建立两个精确的极化位力定理（polarization-virial theorems），分别适用于任意稳定准静态偶极集合与完全延迟偶极对，并以量子谐振子对（quantum harmonic oscillator pair）解析求解角向精细结构。

Result: 自由能至多一个零点，能量抵消先于力学抵消；力零点处能量严格为正，能量零点处力仍为吸引。偶极"魔角"（magic angle）分裂为严格有序的能量零点角与力零点角，后者唯一界定径向排斥扇区并携带正能量势垒。解析结果复现量子微扰（quantum-perturbative）结论，并给出可由实验检验的标度律（scaling laws）与有限球体计算特征。

Conclusion: 该研究给出任意角度下完整的相互作用能量与径向力，把偶极相互作用的角度结构统一为可检验的几何—变分框架，适用范围从微观偶极延伸至宏观可极化物体。

Abstract: Electric fields can turn attraction into repulsion between far-separated atoms, yet the complete angular mechanical structure governing this reversal has remained unknown. Here, we uncover an entropy-like geometric structure: the cubically weighted interaction free energy, r^3 F(r), is nondecreasing under physical dilation. This structure unifies two exact polarization-virial theorems for arbitrary stable quasistatic dipole assemblies and fully retarded dipole pairs under stated conditions. Consequently, the free energy has at most one zero and energetic cancellation precedes mechanical cancellation. A force zero retains strictly positive energy, whereas an energy zero retains an attractive force. The resulting analytical angular fine structure is resolved by a quantum harmonic oscillator pair, yielding the complete arbitrary-angle interaction energy and radial force that recover quantum-perturbative results. The conventional dipolar magic angle splits into strictly ordered energy- and force-zero angles; the latter uniquely bounds the radially repulsive sector and carries a positive energy barrier. Derived scaling laws and finite-sphere calculations provide experimentally testable signatures from microscopic dipoles to macroscopic polarizable bodies.

</details>


### [13] [Domain advantage in two-dimensional quantum-device design with multi-modal agentic harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30043)
*Shenhao Miao,Yizhou Li,Liqiao Xia,Ruihao Liu,Dingke Wang,Pablo Jarillo-Herrero,Eli Zeldov,Jiaqi Cai*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出 qlaybench 基准，用于检验语言模型智能体（language-model agents）能否将显微图像与实验需求转化为适配样本的器件版图；配套的多模态智能体框架 qlaybot 在高算力条件下通过 81.5% 的任务，显著优于常规工具的 24.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械剥离（mechanically exfoliated）晶体制备的二维量子器件形状与位置不规则，需为每个样本定制光刻版图（lithographic layout），而绘制工作目前主要依赖人工，效率低下。

Method: 构建 qlaybench 基准以测试语言模型智能体从显微图像与实验要求生成器件版图的能力；并仿照人类工作流程设计多模态智能体框架 qlaybot，将图像解读与图像对齐、器件特征绘制及版图检查工具相连接，采用源自实际设计经验的检查项评估版图是否适配样本并连通目标组件。

Result: 智能体在给定材料轮廓时通常能完成器件构建，但从图像中识别轮廓存在困难；使用 qlaybot 并增加计算投入时通过 81.5% 的基准任务，而使用常规工具仅为 24.1%。

Conclusion: 该领域优势凸显了将样本的科学解读与几何设计相衔接的重要性，并为减少二维量子器件制备中的人工投入提供了可行路径。

Abstract: High-quality two-dimensional quantum devices are commonly fabricated from mechanically exfoliated crystals, whose irregular shapes and placement require a lithographic layout tailored to each specimen. Drawing these layouts remains largely manual. We introduce qlaybench, a benchmark that tests whether language-model agents can turn microscope images and experimental requirements into device layouts that fit the specimen. Agents with conventional tools often construct devices when material outlines are supplied, but struggle to identify those outlines from images. Modelled on the human workflow, qlaybot, a multimodal agentic harness, connects image interpretation to the tools used to align images, draw device features and inspect the resulting layout. We evaluate each layout with checks drawn from practical design experience, asking whether it fits the specimen and connects its intended components. With greater computational effort, an agent using qlaybot passes 81.5% of the benchmark tasks, compared with 24.1% when it uses conventional tools. This domain advantage highlights the importance of connecting scientific interpretation of a specimen to geometric design and provides a route towards reducing manual effort in two-dimensional quantum-device fabrication.

</details>


### [14] [A Unified Energy-Density Framework for Moiré Interface Energetics and Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30152)
*Zhangke Lei,Weidong Yan,Zhicheng Feng,Wengen Ouyang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出一个基于倒空间（reciprocal space）的普适能量密度框架，将依赖配准（registry-dependent）的对势与原子密度统一处理，可解析描述从单原子/分子吸附到无限双层及有限晶体接触的刚性范德华界面力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔界面（moiré interfaces）的力学响应取决于原子配准、接触尺寸与边界几何的耦合，现有解析描述难以同时保留离散晶格信息并将微观相互作用与有限接触的能量学联系起来。

Method: 在倒空间（reciprocal space）中表达依赖配准（registry-dependent）的对势与原子密度，构建统一能量密度框架；推导圆形与正多边形薄片的显式能量表达式，将面积项与边界依赖项分离，并进行直接求和（direct-summation）基准验证及石墨烯上二维冰（2D ice）的分子晶格计算。

Result: 边界分析重现了圆形薄片势垒包络的面积四分之一次幂标度（quarter-power area scaling），并依据边缘取向区分多边形接触的有界振荡与平方根面积标度；给出以莫尔波长、薄片尺寸和边数表示的多边形—圆形转变（polygon-to-circle crossover）无量纲判据，并将微观起伏系数与能垒、旋转力矩及层间剪切刚度相联系。

Conclusion: 该框架在微观配准、莫尔周期性与有限域几何之间建立了分析联系，可应用于层间剪切与石墨压缩等切向和法向力学响应，为理解与调控范德华接触的能量学与力学提供基础。

Abstract: The mechanical response of moiré interfaces depends on how atomic registry combines with contact size and boundary geometry. An analytical description must retain this discrete lattice information while connecting microscopic interactions to the energetics of finite contacts. Here, we develop a unified energy-density framework for rigid van der Waals interfaces by expressing registry-dependent pair potentials and atomic densities in reciprocal space. The resulting formulation accommodates different lattice bases and atomic species, linking single-atom and molecular adsorption to infinite bilayers and finite crystalline contacts. Explicit expressions for circular and regular polygonal flakes separate the area contribution from boundary-dependent terms and connect microscopic corrugation coefficients to energy barriers, rotational torques, and interlayer shear stiffnesses. The boundary analysis recovers the quarter-power area scaling of the barrier envelope for circular flakes and distinguishes bounded oscillations from square-root area scaling for polygonal contacts according to edge orientation. A dimensionless criterion characterizes the polygon-to-circle crossover in terms of moiré wavelength, flake size, and side number. Direct-summation benchmarks support the finite-domain expressions, while calculations for two-dimensional ice on graphene demonstrate the corresponding boundary-scaling trends in a molecular lattice. Applications to interlayer shear and graphite compression connect the same microscopic interaction description to tangential and normal mechanical responses. The framework establishes an analytical connection between microscopic registry, moiré periodicity, and finite-domain geometry, providing a basis for understanding and tailoring the energetics and mechanics of van der Waals contacts.

</details>


### [15] [Quantum Geometry Effects in Lattice Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30210)
*Haoshu Li,Hao Chen,Qian Niu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在磁性拓扑半金属EuB₆中，通过超越绝热晶格动力学，区分了贝里曲率与量子度规对电子-晶格响应的贡献，发现纯量子度规可导致非手性声子劈裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝里曲率与量子度规在电子输运中已确立，但在晶格动力学中量子度规的作用尚不明确，需确定量子度规如何影响声子。

Method: 超越绝热晶格动力学，识别贝里曲率和量子度规对晶格电子响应的贡献，并对磁性拓扑半金属EuB₆进行第一性原理计算。

Result: 在EuB₆中，具有手性劈裂与非手性劈裂的声子模式共存；手性劈裂源于贝里曲率，而非手性劈裂源于纯量子度规，此时贝里曲率贡献被晶体对称性禁止。

Conclusion: 量子度规可独立于贝里曲率驱动声子非手性劈裂，拓展了量子几何效应在晶格动力学中的认识。

Abstract: Quantum geometric effects such as the Berry curvature and the quantum metric are well established in electronic transport, whereas in lattice dynamics the Berry curvature also plays a key role: adiabatically following the lattice, electrons generate a molecular Berry curvature that splits degenerate optical phonons into chiral partners. To determine how the quantum metric affects phonons, we go beyond adiabatic lattice dynamics, identifying Berry curvature and quantum metric contributions to the electronic response of the lattice. Using first-principles calculations in the magnetic topological semimetal EuB$_6$, we find that phonon modes with chiral splitting and those with nonchiral splitting coexist. Unlike the former, which originate from the Berry curvature, the latter arise from the pure quantum metric, while the Berry curvature contribution is forbidden by crystal symmetry.

</details>


### [16] [Scanning nanowire quantum dot with radio-frequency read-out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30427)
*Aurèle Kamber,Luca Forrer,Valentina Zannier,Fabio Beltram,Lucia Sorba,Martino Poggio,Floris Braakman*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究报告了一种将纳米线量子点扫描单电子晶体管集成于非接触式原子力显微镜悬臂尖端的平台，实现了形貌成像、局域静电势测量及射频反射读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫描单电子晶体管（single-electron transistor, SET）是灵敏的局域电荷与电势探针，但其与扫描探针显微镜的稳健高度控制集成仍有困难，且读出带宽通常仅数十kHz，难以表征更快的电荷动力学。

Method: 将纳米线（nanowire, NW）量子点（quantum dot, QD）集成于非接触式原子力显微镜（non-contact atomic force microscopy, NC-AFM）悬臂尖端构成扫描SET；通过映射纳米尺度偏压栅电极产生的静电势进行局域静电测量，并借助将量子点嵌入LC谐振腔实现射频（radio-frequency, rf）反射测量读出。

Result: 演示了NC-AFM形貌成像与局域静电测量能力，测得的静电势分布与静电仿真结果一致；并成功实现射频反射测量读出。

Conclusion: 该平台为兼具形貌对比的高带宽扫描静电测量（scanning electrometry）建立了可行路径。

Abstract: Scanning single-electron transistors (SETs) are highly sensitive local probes of charge and electric potential, but their integration with robust height control for scanning probe microscopy remains challenging. Furthermore, readout bandwidths are limited to tens of kHz, while characterizing faster charge dynamics is often of interest. Here, we present a scanning SET in the form of a nanowire (NW) quantum dot (QD) integrated at the apex of a non-contact atomic force microscopy (NC-AFM) cantilever. We demonstrate NC-AFM topographic imaging and local electrometry capability by mapping the electrostatic potential generated by a set of nanoscale voltage-biased gate electrodes. Comparison with electrostatic simulations shows agreement with the measured potential profiles. We demonstrate radio-frequency (rf) reflectometry read-out of the sensor, by embedding the QD in an LC-resonator. This platform establishes a route towards high-bandwidth scanning electrometry with simultaneous topographic contrast.

</details>


### [17] [Molecular Pawns on a Surface Chessboard: Computational Investigation of Nanographenes with Atomistic Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30917)
*Antonio Lorenzo Mariano,Jacopo Lupi,Emilio Lorini,Matteo Briganti*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了原子级精确纳米石墨烯（atomically precise nanographenes）的多尺度理论框架，涵盖磁性计算方法层级、衬底—分子界面、表面合成动力学与超分子自组装，并强调机器学习势与自动反应路径发现等工具可在合成前预测磁交换、光学激发与自旋输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子级精确纳米石墨烯兼具可调电子拓扑与局域相干自旋态，被视为分子量子技术（molecular quantum technologies）的候选平台，亟需统一的理论框架支撑其设计。

Method: 采用综述形式，考察从密度泛函理论（density functional theory）、自动反应路径发现（automated reaction-path discovery）到自旋感知机器学习原子间势（spin-aware machine-learned interatomic potentials）的多尺度方法体系，连接静态电子结构与介观动力学。

Result: 这类多尺度工具可将磁交换（magnetic exchange）、光学激发（optical excitations）与自旋输运（spin transport）的预测提前至合成之前，并覆盖衬底—分子界面与表面合成动力学等环节。

Conclusion: 该理论层级为纳米石墨烯分子量子平台的"合成前预测"提供了整体框架，将电子结构、动力学与组装过程纳入统一的多尺度描述。

Abstract: Atomically precise nanographenes combine tunable electronic topology with localized, coherent spin states, making them candidate platforms for molecular quantum technologies. This review examines the multiscale theoretical framework governing these systems: the hierarchy of computational methods for describing magnetic properties, the substrate-molecule interface, on-surface synthetic kinetics, and supramolecular self-assembly. We highlight how density functional theory, automated reaction-path discovery, and spin-aware machine-learned interatomic potentials connect static electronic structure to mesoscale dynamics, and how magnetic exchange, optical excitations, and spin transport can be predicted before synthesis.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基底应变（substrate strain）被认为是调控负载型金属纳米颗粒形变的途径，但其前提是界面耦合（interfacial coupling）能够将外加载荷有效传递至颗粒内部，该可行性尚待检验。

  <summary>Details</summary>

### [20] [Physically Informed Data-Driven Modeling of Low-Pressure Carburizing (LPC): A Self-Calibrating Boundary Condition for Carbon Flux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28190)
*Alex V. Sagalovych,Tymofii Sagalovych,S. F. Dudnik,Vladislav Sagalovych*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LPC模型中不确定性最大的部分并非体扩散（bulk diffusion），而是碳吸收的瞬态表面动力学（transient surface kinetics），因此需要将机器学习用于边界条件闭合，而非采用"工艺区间-结果"型经验关系。

Method: 将问题定位为受偏微分方程（PDE）、物理通量约束、反问题正则化（inverse-problem regularization）与不确定性量化（uncertainty quantification）约束的算子学习（operator learning）问题；用碳浓度剖面、积分增重、表面浓度、有效渗层深度及附加工艺测量标定闭合算子。

Result: 论文给出数学表述、标定泛函（calibration functional）、可辨识性条件（identifiability conditions）、初步调参算法和在线自适应方案，并讨论与物理信息神经网络（PINN）、算子学习、动力系统辨识、边界算子学习及边界控制问题的关系。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为开发LPC工艺可解释数字支持（interpretable digital support）的计算基础。

Abstract: This paper formulates an extended framework for a hybrid physically informed data-driven model of low-pressure carburizing (LPC), in which diffusive carbon transport in austenite is described by a physical equation, whereas the effective carbon flux across the gas-metal interface is treated as an unknown boundary forcing to be identified from experimental and process data. The basis for this formulation is that, in LPC, the most uncertain part of the model is not bulk diffusion but the transient surface kinetics of carbon uptake by the metal. In contrast to direct empirical relationships of the 'process regime-result' type, the proposed scheme uses machine learning as a closure operator for the boundary condition in the diffusion problem. This operator is calibrated using carbon concentration profiles, integral mass gain, surface concentration, effective case depth, and additional process measurements. Theoretically, the model is positioned as an operator-learning problem in a system constrained by a partial differential equation, physical flux constraints, inverse-problem regularization, and uncertainty quantification. The relationships between the proposed approach and physics-informed neural networks, operator learning, dynamic-system identification, boundary-operator learning, and boundary-control problems are considered. The mathematical formulation, calibration functional, identifiability conditions, preliminary tuning algorithm, and online adaptation scheme are formulated. The proposed approach is considered as a computational basis for developing interpretable digital support for LPC processes.

</details>


### [21] [Defect Engineering of the Water-Carbon Interface: Insights from First-Principles Calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28201)
*Peikun Zhang,Hongbo Zhang,Ying Xu,Zhuhua Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）系统揭示无定形碳（amorphous carbon）中拓扑、点、线、边缘及掺杂缺陷如何调控水-碳界面相互作用，并指出缺陷耦合可产生优于单一缺陷的协同效应，为缺陷工程（defect engineering）设计水伏与催化界面提供框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固-液界面相互作用是水伏（hydrovoltaic）与催化技术的基础，碳纳米材料已被广泛研究，但无定形碳中大量缺陷在界面作用中的角色尚不清楚。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），系统考察拓扑（topological）、点（point）、线（line）、边缘（edge）与掺杂（doping）等缺陷类型对水-碳相互作用的调制，并进一步比较单一缺陷与其耦合体系。

Result: 带悬挂键的扶手椅型边缘缺陷（armchair-edge defects with dangling bonds）使水解离（water dissociation）能垒较原始碳降低最多30倍；双空位（di-vacancies）析氢（hydrogen evolution）的吉布斯自由能（Gibbs free energy）接近零，使纯碳基界面具备潜在析氢反应（HER）活性；电子局域化降低的单空位（mono-vacancy）缺陷使向蒸发水分子的电荷转移（charge transfer）提升最多12.4倍，为蒸发诱导发电的关键因素。

Conclusion: 不同缺陷的耦合产生协同效应（synergistic effects），优于单一缺陷，表明其是多功能界面设计的有前景策略；上述结果构建了用于高效水伏与催化器件理性设计的缺陷工程框架。

Abstract: Interactions at solid-liquid interfaces are fundamental to hydrovoltaic and catalytic technologies. While carbon nanomaterials have been extensively studied, the role of abundant defects in amorphous carbon remains poorly understood. Here, using first-principles calculations, we systematically investigate how various defects (topological, point, line, edge, and doping defects) modulate water-carbon interactions. We find that armchair-edge defects with dangling bonds reduce the energy barrier for water dissociation by up to 30 times compared with pristine carbon, while di-vacancies exhibit a near-zero Gibbs free energy for hydrogen evolution, enabling potential hydrogen evolution reaction activity on a purely carbon-based interface. Moreover, mono-vacancy defects, characterized by reduced electronic localization, enhance charge transfer to evaporating water molecules by up to 12.4 times, a key factor in evaporation-induced electricity generation. Crucially, we demonstrate that coupling distinct defects produces synergistic effects that outperform individual defects, revealing a promising strategy for designing multifunctional interfaces. These insights establish a defect-engineering framework for the rational design of high-efficiency hydrovoltaic and catalytic devices.

</details>


### [22] [Metal to Semiconductor Transition in Niobium Sulfoselenide Alloy Films by Compositional Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28238)
*Tinsae Alem,Abir Hasan,Jordan A. Hachtel,Kory Burns,Nikhil Shukla,Stephen McDonnell*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Electrical resistivity of the films and their temperature coefficient of resistance (TCR) were extracted to determine their metallic and semiconducting behavior. Our results show that increasing sulfur content induces a transition from metallic to semiconducting behavior in NbSe2, accompanied by a rise in resistivity. This work highlights the potential for compositional tuning of TMDs for applications in tunable nanoelectronics.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在评估{ScSe2}单层的结构稳定性与电子性质，并考察同一化学组成下不同相（polymorphism）对其电子与光学行为的影响。

Abstract: We present an ab initio investigation of the {ScSe2} monolayer using density functional theory (DFT), as employed in the CP2K code, to assess its structural stability and electronic properties. The electronic behavior of both phases is governed by the local coordination environment of scandium and the associated lifting of degeneracy of its $3d$ orbitals. The valence bands (VBs) are dominated by {Se}$-4p$ states in both phases, while the states near the Fermi level distinguish the metallic 1T phase from the semiconducting 2H phase. ### [24] [A Covalent Bonding Network Using p-Block Elements to Stabilize Lithium-Rich Cathode Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28452)
*Amelia Zhao,Edward Mu,Jessica Luu,John Vinson,Brian Moritz,Eder G. Lomeli,Thomas P. Devereaux*

TL;DR: 该研究揭示 Li4FeSbO6（LFSO）中由 Sb 的 p 区轨道特性促成的共价 Sb-O 键网络是其高电位、低电压滞后（voltage hysteresis）可逆充放电的结构稳定来源，并据此提出以 p 区元素调控氧键合（oxygen bonding）的层状氧化物正极设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 富锂正极（Li-rich battery cathodes）虽具高能量密度，但高价氧化还原（high-valent redox）伴随的结构不稳定性限制其实际应用；LFSO 已展现可逆高电位脱嵌锂与低电压滞后，但其稳定机制尚不明确，难以推广至更丰产元素的正极体系。

Result: 确认 LFSO 中存在由 Sb 的 p 区轨道特征（p-block orbital character）促成的共价 Sb-O 键网络，该键合态贡献晶体稳定性并具备可观测的谱学特征；以其他 p 区元素替代 Sb 可保留这一稳定化键合。

Substituting Sb with other p-block elements preserves this stabilizing bond, pointing to a family of new cathode chemistries beyond LFSO. ### [25] [Persistent Neutron Irradiation Effects and Partial Recovery in Thin-Film CdS/CdTe Solar Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28472)

### [26] [Interstitial Magnetoelectricity in Altermagnetic Electrides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28504)
*Yue Li,Shibo Fang,Jianhua Wang,Qiang Li,Weizhen Meng,Haiyu Meng,Xiong-Xiong Xue,Jianfeng Wang,Yi Du,Jing Lu,Yee Sin Ang*

TL;DR: 本文以交错磁（altermagnet）电子化物（electride）Zr₂N 单层为对象，研究发现电场可打破两个间隙阴离子电子间的磁补偿，产生净磁化并由此实现间隙电子介导的磁电响应（magnetoelectric response），并可用电控自旋电导加以验证。

  <summary>Details</summary>

This review assesses the evidence for tritium speciation and transport in molten FLiBe, with emphasis on the solubility, effective diffusivity, permeability, exchange and boundary-transfer parameters required by continuum models. The available evidence motivates a two-pool continuum description with separate oxidised or fluoride-associated and molecular-hydrogen pools, without assigning a unique microscopic species. Moreover, a systematic study of the films mechanical properties across both micro- and macroscopic scales was also performed to elucidate the underlying mechanisms responsible for the observed phenomena. ### [30] [DeepDOSReasoner: Physics-Grounded Reasoning for Density-of-States Prediction and Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29132)
*Yingheng Wang,Francesco Ricci,Zhilong Wang,Tao Yu,Junwen Bai,Xiangyun Lei,Shufeng Kong,Fengqi You,John M. Gregoire,Carla P. Gomes*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: DeepDOSReasoner 是一个物理约束的深度学习框架，通过原子到能量源注意力（atom-to-energy source attention）与连续性方程传输（continuity-equation transport）从晶体结构直接预测电子与声子态密度（eDOS/phDOS），兼具高精度与守恒性，并完成大规模高通量筛选用例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子与声子态密度决定材料多种关键性质，但密度泛函理论（DFT）计算代价高，Materials Project 中约60%条目缺 eDOS、逾80%缺 phDOS；现有晶体—谱图模型采用池化结构表征与逐点回归，模糊了谱特征并破坏总态数守恒。

Method: 提出 DeepDOSReasoner：以原子到能量源注意力解码谱图，并对归一化 DOS 采用可学习的连续性方程传输进行精修，从构造上保证非负性与总态数守恒（total-state conservation）。

Result: 在 eDOS 与 phDOS 全部评估指标上取得最高精度，无监督恢复元素分辨贡献，最可靠地识别稀有价带隙（VB gaps）；对8,106种未探索材料的前瞻筛选中 Top 50 有25个经新 DFT 计算确认为价带隙材料；消融实验表明两阶段均必要，仅用25%数据训练仍保持相当精度，毫秒级预测支持穷举筛选。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了将机器学习与物理推理结合、面向高通量谱筛选与自主材料发现（autonomous materials discovery）的范式，并发布了为91,405个条目补全 eDOS、为127,768个条目补全 phDOS 的预测结果。

Abstract: Predicting a material's behavior from its atomic structure is a longstanding goal of the physical sciences. The electronic and phonon densities of states (eDOS and phDOS) govern key electronic, optical, vibrational, and thermodynamic properties, yet their calculation using density functional theory (DFT) is costly: about 60% of Materials Project entries lack eDOS and over 80% lack phDOS. Existing crystal-to-spectrum models rely on pooled structural representations and pointwise regression, which blur spectral features and violate total-state conservation. Here we introduce DeepDOSReasoner, a physics-grounded framework that decodes spectra through atom-to-energy source attention and refines the normalized DOS through learned continuity-equation transport, enforcing nonnegativity and total-state conservation by construction. DeepDOSReasoner achieves the highest prediction accuracy across all evaluated metrics for both eDOS and phDOS, recovers element-resolved contributions without supervision, and most reliably identifies rare valence-band (VB) gaps. In a prospective screen of 8,106 uncharted materials validated by new DFT calculations, its predictions remain the most accurate among the methods evaluated, with 25 of its top 50 candidates confirmed as VB-gap materials. Three case studies spanning substituted compounds, photovoltaic chalcogenides, and a high-entropy alloy demonstrate transfer across materials classes. Ablations confirm that both stages are necessary, while the model retains comparable accuracy when trained on only 25% of the data. Predicting spectra in milliseconds enables exhaustive screening; we release predictions filling the missing eDOS for 91,405 and phDOS for 127,768 Materials Project entries. DeepDOSReasoner provides a blueprint for integrating machine learning with physical reasoning toward high-throughput spectral screening and autonomous materials discovery.

</details>


### [31] [CLFF: Automated Framework for Concurrent Learning Force Fields and Material Property Calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29187)
*Cao Thang Nguyen,Sung Youb Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: CLFF 是一个开源 Python 框架（open-source Python framework），用于自动化机器学习原子间势（machine learning interatomic potentials, MLIPs）的并发学习（concurrent learning）工作流，支持异步分布式计算与多类下游自动化任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIPs 虽能在大尺度原子模拟中接近第一性原理（first-principles）精度，但其开发与部署流程复杂，需大量人工操作与领域专业知识，因而需要自动化与可复现的工作流平台。

Method: CLFF 将模型训练、MLIP 驱动采样、基于不确定性的候选筛选（uncertainty-based candidate selection）、量子力学标注（quantum-mechanical labeling）与数据集增广整合为统一工作流，并可将计算任务异步分发至多台异构远程计算资源。

Result: 框架目前支持多种现代图基（graph-based）MLIP 架构，兼容常用计算化学与材料科学工具；除并发学习外，还提供初始数据生成与材料性质计算（如声子色散（phonon dispersion）和势能面扫描（potential energy surface scans））的自动化流程。

Conclusion: CLFF 通过减少人工干预并提升可复现性（reproducibility），为计算材料研究中 MLIPs 的开发与部署提供灵活平台，并可利用碎片化的异构硬件基础设施。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) offer an efficient paradigm for large-scale atomistic simulations with near first-principles accuracy. However, developing and deploying these potentials often involves intricate procedures that require substantial manual effort and domain expertise. In this work, we present CLFF, an open-source Python framework designed to automate concurrent learning workflows for MLIPs. CLFF integrates model training, MLIP-driven sampling, uncertainty-based candidate selection, quantum-mechanical labeling, and dataset augmentation into a unified workflow. The framework currently supports several modern graph-based MLIP architectures and is compatible with widely used computational chemistry and materials science tools. A distinguishing feature of CLFF is its ability to asynchronously distribute computational tasks across multiple heterogeneous remote computing resources, enabling users to aggregate fragmented hardware infrastructure within a single controllable workflow. In addition to concurrent learning, CLFF provides automated workflows for initial data generation and material property calculations, including phonon dispersion and potential energy surface scans. By minimizing manual intervention and enhancing reproducibility, CLFF offers a flexible platform for developing and deploying MLIPs in computational materials research.

</details>


### [32] [AgenticMM: A Material-Centered Multi-Agent Architecture for Scalable First-Principles Materials Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29248)
*Zhenkun Yuan,Geoffroy Hautier*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: AgenticMM 提出以材料为中心的多智能体架构（material-centered multi-agent architecture），为每种材料配置持久智能体（persistent agent）以持有完整工作流与科学上下文，并由持久协调者（coordinator）分配资源、协调通信与跟踪进度，在五类半导体点缺陷多阶段计算中验证可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体框架（multi-agent frameworks）多按计算任务或科学角色组织智能体，在材料规模扩展时会导致材料特定上下文（material-specific context）反复传递，并使持久执行（persistent execution）与资源协调复杂化。

Method: 设计 AgenticMM 架构：每种材料对应一个持有完整工作流与科学上下文的持久智能体，另设持久协调者负责共享资源分配、通信中介与任务进度跟踪；并以可复用的提示式技能（prompt-based skills）作为工作流的模块化定义，采用 DefectSkills 提示实现。

Result: 通过多阶段点缺陷（point-defect）计算活动验证：材料专属智能体对五种化学性质各异的半导体并行完成宿主电子结构（electronic-structure）、相稳定性（phase-stability）与本征缺陷（intrinsic-defect）计算。

Conclusion: 以材料为中心的组织方式可支持持久执行与资源共享协调，提示式模块化技能可复用，AgenticMM 在真实材料建模工作流中具备可行性。

Abstract: Multi-agent frameworks are attracting increasing attention as a means of autonomously driving complex, long-running workflows in first-principles materials modeling. However, such frameworks commonly organize AI agents by computational task or scientific role. When scaled across many materials, this organization can require repeated transfers of material-specific context and complicate persistent execution and resource coordination. Here, we introduce AgenticMM, a material-centered multi-agent architecture in which each material has a persistent agent that owns its complete workflow and retains its scientific context, while a persistent coordinator allocates shared resources, mediates communication, and tracks campaign progress. AgenticMM is designed to incorporate reusable, prompt-based skills as modular definitions of these workflows. Using DefectSkills prompts, we demonstrate the feasibility of AgenticMM through a multistage point-defect campaign in which material-specific agents concurrently perform host electronic-structure, phase-stability, and intrinsic-defect calculations for five chemically diverse semiconductors.

</details>


### [33] [Graph Neural Networks for Accurate Quasiparticle Energy Levels in Large Supercells of Transition Metal Dichalcogenides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29349)
*Rafael R. Del Grande,David A. Strubbe*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出以小型GW计算数据训练的图神经网络（graph neural network, GNN），在显著降低计算成本的前提下，对含缺陷的大超胞体系给出高精度的GW带隙预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GW方法是材料科学中精确计算电子带隙（electronic bandgap）的主要手段，可克服密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）的带隙低估问题；但当体系超过10–100个原子时计算成本过高，难以用于需要大超胞的缺陷、表面与莫尔体系（moiré systems）。

Method: 模型以DFT波函数在局域原子轨道上的投影为特征，图节点表示原子位置、边表示原子间距离以刻画几何结构；作者提出"small AI"工作流，说明如何构建个人化的GW计算数据集，界定训练集需包含的物理要素，并对超参数进行贝叶斯优化（Bayesian optimization）。

Result: 在含硫空位（sulfur vacancy）的单层MoS₂体系上，GNN模型取得较高精度，平均绝对误差（mean absolute error, MAE）约为10–50 meV。

Conclusion: 该简化工作流可推广至其他材料，用户无需依赖大规模数据集，从而加速大体系的高精度电子结构计算。

Abstract: The GW method is the one of the main workhorses of the materials science community to accurately calculate the electronic bandgap of materials, as it overcomes the gap underestimation problem from Density Functional Theory (DFT). Despite its accuracy, its computational cost becomes prohibitive when calculations have more than 10-100 atoms, which makes more difficult its use for defects, surfaces and moiré systems that demand large supercells. To overcome this problem, we developed a Graph Neural Network trained with smaller GW calculations which can make accurate predictions on large supercells at a small fraction of the computational cost.
  Our model uses as features the projection of DFT wavefunctions into localized atomic orbitals, while graph nodes represent the atomic positions and edges represent atom to atom distances, which captures the system geometry. We explore how to do "small AI", providing a workflow of how to create a personal and convenient dataset of GW calculations to train the model that can be applied to different materials so that the final user does not need to rely on massive datasets. We define what must be included in the training dataset to capture the physics of our objective simulation, and perform a Bayesian optimization of hyperparameters. In the case of monolayer MoS$_2$ with a sulfur vacancy our GNN models achieve high accuracy (MAE ranging 10 to 50 meV) with a simplified workflow applicable to other materials, accelerating accurate large electronic structure calculations.

</details>

*V. A. Levashov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出基于粒子共线性（collineations）的多种方法，用于在多组分系统及非FCC/HCP/BCC晶格情形下识别晶核（crystal nuclei），并以单组分系统和Wahnström二元BA₂系统进行演示。

Conclusion: 基于共线性的方法可识别并分离晶核，适用于多组分体系及非FCC/HCP/BCC的复杂晶相，为成核与结晶研究提供了通用工具。

Abstract: We suggest several methods that can be used for identification of crystal nuclei in the studies of the nucleation and crystallization processes in multicomponent systems and in situations when the forming crystalline phase is different from the FCC, HCP or BCC lattices. We suggest several methods that can be used for identification of crystal nuclei in the studies of the nucleation and crystallization processes in multicomponent systems and in situations when the forming crystalline phase is different from the FCC, HCP or BCC lattices. The approaches are based on consideration of collineations of particles. They allow finding nuclei and separating them from each other. We demonstrate the methods by considering two systems. For a single-component system, we perform a comparison between the results derived from the collineations and those obtained using the coarse-grained bond-orientational order parameters. Further, we illustrate the methods by considering the crystallization process in a Wahnström binary $BA_2$ system forming a rather complex crystal structure.

Motivation: 将储能单元直接集成到微电子平台仍具挑战；已报道的固态微型超级电容器多依赖液态或含溶剂凝胶电解质（gel electrolytes），需额外封装并使器件集成复杂化。因此需要可在微电子兼容工艺下制备的全固态、可图案化储能器件。

Abstract: Integrating energy storage directly into microelectronic platforms remains a major challenge. To investigate device behavior and degradation, we employ non-destructive imaging ellipsometry to map individual layer thicknesses and extract the spatial distribution of MXene conductivity across the chip. This approach reveals localized changes in electrode properties following prolonged electrochemical cycling, providing insight into degradation processes that are difficult to capture using conventional spatially averaged measurements. Together, these results establish a route towards fully solid-state, all-2D energy-storage devices and highlight the potential of solution-processable layered materials for integration into microelectronic systems.

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该综述以工程策略而非材料或机制为线索重组ZnO自供电紫外光电探测器（self-powered UV photodetector）领域，提出“零偏压掺杂悖论（zero-bias doping paradox）”作为统一视角，并给出指标—策略—生长路线的决策树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自供电紫外探测可免去待机功耗并抑制暗电流，适配无电池物联网（Internet-of-Things）传感；ZnO具备紫外选择性、储量丰富、无毒、低温溶液可加工等优势，但零偏压工作需要原始ZnO难以提供的内建不对称性。既有文献按材料或机制组织，使设计逻辑不清晰，故需按策略重新梳理。

Method: 将领域按工程策略归纳为五类调控手段（levers）：结与内建电场工程、掺杂与缺陷控制、极化耦合（polarization coupling）、界面/表面与光电化学工程、器件架构与光管理，并把每类手段对应到其所服务的性能指标及其带来的权衡。

Result: 提出“零偏压掺杂悖论”：提高载流子密度会压缩零偏压收集所需的耗尽区（depletion region），故自供电最优掺杂与加偏压最优不同；同时指出探测率（detectivity）、速度与有效面积（active area）等指标易被高估的陷阱，并将综述凝练为连接目标指标、策略与生长路线的决策树。

Here we organize the field by engineering strategy, identifying five families of levers (junction and built-in-field engineering, doping and defect control, polarization coupling, interface/surface and photoelectrochemical engineering, and architecture and light management) and mapping each onto the metrics it serves and the trade-offs it imposes. Abstract: Transmission electrons - with few-picometer wavelengths - can theoretically resolve all atoms in solids. Here, our experiment-guided simulations reveal that unlike heavy-atom imaging - governed by wave-based imaging process - ballistic propagation of particle-like electrons in Cs-corrected TEM enables super-resolution imaging (SRI) of light atoms, achieving a resolution down to 14 pm and a robust hydrogen contrast of ~24%. Our in-situ SRI study not only uncovers polymetric hydrogen aggregation in TiHx and H-LaAlO3, but also inter-site migration, rotational displacement of hydrogen species and collective hydrogen tunneling-induced polarization surge in the oxide. ### [38] [MagPot: A magnetoelastic potential generator for molecular dynamics simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29574)

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: MagPot是一个Python程序，可为LAMMPS的SPIN包自旋-晶格分子动力学模拟自动生成Bethe-Slater参数集，将紧凑的材料性质转换为磁交换与Néel相互作用的径向函数，并以模块化方式与非磁性势叠加。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁弹性（magnetoelastic）现象源于原子位移与磁自由度的耦合，其原子尺度模拟需要磁相互作用显式依赖于原子间距的原子间模型，而现有参数化过程缺乏简便可复现的途径。

Abstract: Magnetoelastic phenomena originate from the coupling between atomic displacements and magnetic degrees of freedom, and their atomistic simulation requires interatomic models in which magnetic interactions depend explicitly on interatomic distance. From a compact set of material properties, the program determines the radial functions describing Heisenberg exchange and the dipole and quadrupole contributions of the two-ion Néel interaction. A central feature of MagPot is its modular architecture, which allows the magnetic contribution it generates to be combined additively with a broad range of compatible non-magnetic interatomic potentials. The parameterisation workflow is assessed through equation-of-state, Curie-temperature, volume-magnetostriction, pressure-dependent magnetocrystalline-anisotropy and anisotropic-magnetoelastic tests. By automating the construction of the magnetic contribution to the interatomic model, MagPot offers a straightforward and reproducible route from material properties to LAMMPS-ready magnetic interactions, facilitating large-scale atomistic simulations of magnetoelasticity and magnetostriction, as well as physical phenomena associated with magnon-phonon coupling.

</details>

TL;DR: 该文指出，层状极性金属Sr₃Co₂O₇中电荷歧化（charge disproportionation）可重构局域钴晶体场、恢复面内磁化下的轨道角动量，从而产生巨磁晶各向异性（magnetocrystalline anisotropy），为无稀土永磁提供设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体场淬灭（crystal-field quenching）通常抑制过渡金属氧化物中的轨道角动量，限制其磁各向异性，因此需要寻找突破该限制的新机制。

Result: 计算得到面内[110]磁化相对[001]每原胞能量偏好234.0 meV；电荷歧化及伴随的极性位移重构局域晶体场，形成轨道层级不同的不等价位点，在位移钴位点实现轨道去淬灭，自旋–轨道耦合（spin–orbit coupling）将其转化为大磁各向异能；中心对称参考中轨道矩近乎淬灭、各向异性显著降低，且常规二阶贡献在所采用近似下较小。

Conclusion: 电荷歧化驱动的轨道重构可作为获得大各向异性的设计原则，并使层状钙钛矿（layered perovskites）与磁性极性金属（magnetic polar metals）成为无稀土永磁（rare-earth-free permanent magnets）的潜在平台。

Abstract: Crystal-field quenching usually suppresses orbital angular momentum and limits magnetic anisotropy in transition-metal oxides. Here we identify charge disproportionation as a route to giant magnetocrystalline anisotropy in the layered polar metal Sr$_3$Co$_2$O$_7$. First-principles calculations reveal an energy preference of 234.0 meV per cell for in-plane [110] magnetization over [001]. Charge disproportionation and the associated polar displacement reconstruct the local cobalt crystal fields, creating inequivalent sites with distinct orbital hierarchies. This reconstruction enables a strongly directional orbital response, particularly at the displaced cobalt site, where orbital angular momentum is recovered for in-plane magnetization. Spin--orbit coupling converts this response into a large magnetic anisotropic energy. In the centrosymmetric reference, the orbital moments remain nearly quenched and the anisotropy is strongly reduced. A local Green-function and orbital-moment analysis connects the large energy scale to orbital unquenching, while the conventional second-order contribution is small within the adopted approximation. These results identify charge-disproportionation-driven orbital reconstruction as a design principle for large anisotropy and motivate layered perovskites and magnetic polar metals as platforms in the search for rare-earth-free permanent magnets.

</details>


### [40] [Inferring Experimental Objectives in Microscopy Experiments from Decision Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29814)
*Kamyar Barakati,Boris N. Slautin,Aditya Raghavan,Babita Babita,Surya Prakash Reddy,Jordan Marshall,Munireach Nannory,Stephen Puplampu,Alla Slautina,Faith M. Hines,Newsha Javanmardi,Sergei V. Kalinin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究探讨能否从显微镜操作者的决策历史中学习奖励函数（reward function），并将其转化为可驱动自动化实验的空间奖励场，同时提出基准评测方法并考察偏好随时间变化的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代显微镜采集与分析数据的速度远超人类操作者的决策速度，实现自动化实验需要将操作者的科学逻辑编码为奖励函数；本文因此考察这类奖励函数能否直接从操作者的历史决策中学习得到。

Method: 提出多种框架，将对备选测量位置的历史选择作为操作者偏好的比较性证据；比较了基于物理意义图像描述子的可解释线性模型、同一描述子的非线性函数、无监督图像表征加偏好建模、以及受偏好训练的深度核（deep kernel）启发模型等算法，并给出基于留出决策与已知目标生成决策历史的基准评测方案；此外用隐马尔可夫模型（hidden Markov model）处理优先级突变，用连续状态模型刻画偏好的平滑漂移。

Result: 分析得到覆盖样品的奖励函数空间场，可直接用于运行仪器；在真实显微镜候选集上生成的可控序列表明，隐藏的策略切换与偏好的渐变漂移均可被恢复。

Conclusion: 直接从记录决策中学习得到的奖励函数可为未来自动化显微镜实验提供候选目标（candidate objectives）。

Abstract: Modern microscopes can acquire, analyze and act on data far faster than human operators can decide what to measure next. Exploiting this speed in automated experiments requires the instrument to follow the scientific logic of the operator, and one way to encode that logic is through reward functions that become part of the operation process. Here we explore whether such reward functions can be learned from the decision histories of microscope operators. We propose multiple frameworks in which recorded choices among alternative measurement locations serve as comparative evidence about the operator's preferences, and we compare several algorithms for reconstructing the reward including interpretable linear models built on physically meaningful image descriptors, nonlinear functions of the same descriptors, an unsupervised image representation followed by preference modeling, and a preference trained deep kernel inspired model. The result of this analysis is a spatial field of reward functions over the sample that can be used to run the instrument. We also propose a way to benchmark these approaches, based on withheld decisions and on decision histories generated with known objectives. We then examine the possibility that a decision history contains changing priorities throughout the experiment. A hidden Markov model assigns distinct rewards to recurring selection strategies, whereas a continuous-state model tracks smoothly varying preference weights. Controlled sequences generated over real microscopy candidate sets show that both concealed switches and gradual drift can be recovered. These reward functions, learned directly from recorded decisions, provide candidate objectives for future automated microscopy experiments.

</details>

Abstract: Sodium exhibits a remarkable concentration of complex crystalline phases near its melting-curve minimum, yet the atomic arrangements of several experimentally observed phases remain unresolved. ### [42] [Pyroelectric Harvesting from Steady Heat Sources via Active Piezoelectric Thermal Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29928)
*Jun Usami,Yuki Okamoto,Shimpei Saito,Saeko Tachikawa,Soumei Baba,Takeshi Fujii,Sucheta Gorwadkar,Natsumi Makimoto,Takeshi Kobayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究基于PZT/Si双压电晶片悬臂梁（bimorph cantilever），利用主动压电热开关（active piezoelectric thermal switching）实现从稳定热源中收集热释电能量（pyroelectric energy harvesting），实验达到110 Hz频率与5 K温差下的能量转换。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热释电能量收集依赖温度随时间波动，难以利用低品位稳定热源（low-grade steady heat sources）；本文旨在通过主动热开关方式突破这一限制并提升工作频率。

Method: 同一PZT层同时充当机械致动器（mechanical actuator）与热释电能量转换元件，双输出电极（dual output electrodes）实现热释电与压电信号的分离；并结合独立测得的热释电系数（pyroelectric coefficient）预测响应与瞬态热仿真（transient thermal simulations）进行分析。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种面向低品位稳定热源、可高频工作的紧凑MEMS（microelectromechanical systems）热释电能量收集方案。

Abstract: This study demonstrates pyroelectric energy harvesting from a steady heat source using active piezoelectric thermal switching in a PZT/Si bimorph cantilever. The same PZT layer serves as both the mechanical actuator and the pyroelectric energy-conversion element. Dual output electrodes enable separation of the pyroelectric and piezoelectric signals. Pyroelectric energy harvesting is experimentally demonstrated at frequencies up to 110 Hz and temperature differences as small as 5 K. Comparison with the response predicted from the independently measured pyroelectric coefficient, together with transient thermal simulations, identifies dynamic interfacial heat transfer as a key limitation to high-frequency operation. In a 2.79-million-atom ferroelectric twin, the cycloid rotation planes tilt by different amounts from their shared initial plane and remain distinct over 10~ps at 100~K. At 10~ps the propagation axes lie within about $7^\circ$ of their initial directions. With inertial spin dynamics, GPUMD advances a 5.93-million-atom BiFeO$_3$ cell at 21.8 million atom-steps per second on four RTX 4090 GPUs.

Here we demonstrate mechanically induced polar-domain reconfiguration in the charge-density-wave (CDW) semiconductor EuTe4 at room temperature, whose resistivity is on the order of 10 m$Ω$cm. Polarized-light microscopy and second-harmonic-generation measurements reveal CDW-induced in-plane polarization domains with an approximately 90 $^\circ$ domain configuration. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面声波（surface acoustic waves, SAWs）长期用于探测近表面材料性质与微结构，但其驱动永久性结构改性的潜力尚未被充分探索；本文旨在揭示非线性SAW诱导缺陷生成与演化的微观机制。

Conclusion: 重复SAW通过逐步降低位错密度实现非热退火（nonthermal annealing）；位错与自由表面相互作用产生原子级台阶、平台及配位不足构型，为永久性表面改性提供微观路径，并可能解释Pt表面声学增强催化活性。研究确立非线性SAW作为非热缺陷生成、重构与退火驱动因素的地位，为近表面微结构的声学加工提供依据。

Abstract: Surface acoustic waves (SAWs) are widely used to probe near-surface material properties and microstructure, but their potential to drive permanent structural modification remains largely unexplored. Large-scale molecular dynamics simulations reveal the mechanisms of defect generation and evolution induced by nonlinear Rayleigh waves in Pt. Strong crystallographic dependence of the nonlinear evolution is observed. SAWs on Pt(111) undergo rapid nonlinear sharpening and shock formation, with localized elastic energy and increasing near-surface stresses, whereas much weaker sharpening on Pt(001) allows dissipation to compete effectively with shock formation. For sufficiently strong SAWs on Pt(111), Shockley partial dislocations are emitted from the surface when the resolved shear stress approaches the theoretical shear strength. The highly localized and rapidly changing stress field at the shock front activates multiple slip systems and promotes crossslip and reactions between Shockley partials, producing complex dislocation structures with sessile stair-rod segments. Subsequent SAW interactions activate dislocation sources on slip systems where the stress is insufficient for homogeneous nucleation, leading, in one case, to sequential emission of Shockley partials and formation of a subsurface twinned domain. Repeated SAW passages progressively reduce dislocation density, demonstrating SAW-driven nonthermal annealing. Dislocation interactions with the free surface generate atomic-scale steps, terraces, and other under-coordinated configurations, providing a microscopic pathway for permanent surface modification that may contribute to the acoustically enhanced catalytic activity observed on Pt surfaces. These results establish nonlinear SAWs as drivers of nonthermal defect generation, restructuring, and annealing, opening opportunities for acoustic processing of near-surface microstructure.

</details>


### [47] [Apparent thermal conductivity governed by interface-injected phonon spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30276)
*D. G. Jeong,Terumasa Tadano,J. S. Lee*

TL;DR: 该研究通过逐个声子模式求解半无限Si的表面温度响应，揭示热反射技术（thermoreflectance）测得的表观热导率κ_app（apparent thermal conductivity）究竟被增强还是被抑制，取决于注入声子按平均自由程（mean free path）加权的谱特征；短平均自由程加权注入升高表面温度，长平均自由程加权注入则降低表面温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当声子平均自由程可比于热穿透深度时，傅里叶分析给出的κ_app偏离体热导率κ_bulk；已有研究指出换能器界面注入的非平衡声子谱可导致κ_app>κ_bulk，但支配κ_app增强或降低的谱特性尚不清楚。

Conclusion: 热反射技术测得的κ_app不仅反映衬底热性质，也反映跨换能器界面注入的声子谱，界面透射谱是影响测量结果的关键因素。

Abstract: Thermoreflectance techniques such as time- and frequency-domain thermoreflectance (TDTR and FDTR) are widely used to measure the thermal conductivity of bulk materials, thin films, and interfaces. When phonon mean free paths $Λ_ω$ become comparable to the thermal penetration depth, a Fourier analysis yields an apparent thermal conductivity $κ_{\rm app}$ that deviates from the bulk value $κ_{\rm bulk}$. Recent studies have highlighted the role of the phonon spectrum injected through the transducer interface, which is generally out of equilibrium owing to frequency-dependent transmission, and have shown that accounting for this nonequilibrium distribution can yield $κ_{\rm app}>κ_{\rm bulk}$, unlike conventional suppression-function analyses that predict only $κ_{\rm app}\leqκ_{\rm bulk}$. However, the spectral characteristics governing whether $κ_{\rm app}$ is enhanced or reduced remain unclear. Here, we resolve the surface-temperature response of semi-infinite Si mode by mode and show that short-$Λ_ω$-weighted injection raises the surface temperature, whereas long-$Λ_ω$-weighted injection lowers it. This behavior is governed by how deep the injected phonons travel before thermalizing with the lattice, which is set by their mean free paths, and by how effectively the substrate conducts the injected energy away, which is limited by its long-mean-free-path phonons. For an Al (70 nm)/Si structure, Fourier fits of synthetic TDTR phase signals give $κ_{\rm app}$ of Si from about 93 to 360 W/(m K) for different interfacial transmission spectra, and $κ_{\rm app}$ correlates with the heat-flux-weighted $Λ_ω$ of the transmitted phonons. Thus, $κ_{\rm app}$ from thermoreflectance reflects not only the substrate thermal properties but also the phonon spectrum injected across the transducer interface.

</details>

Abstract: Boron (B) microalloying can effectively refine microstructures of as-cast TiAl alloys and regulate their mechanical properties. This strengthening is accompanied by reduced shear strain accumulation. By linking solute redistribution to defect interactions and deformation, these findings establish an atomistic framework for understanding and designing high-performance alloys based on interstitial solutes.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声子带隙可抑制声子散射，是设计特定热功能材料的重要机制，但逐一探索需借助第一性原理（first-principles）方法与实验，成本过高，亟需可扩展的高通量手段。

Band gaps computed as ensemble averages over MD snapshots blue-shift under tensile strain, consistent with experiment, whereas static calculations on averaged structures predict a red shift. Our study positions MD with constrained lattice vectors as a method for predicting finite-temperature strain phase diagrams and guiding the epitaxial design of perovskite absorbers.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电（ferroelectric, FE）序的非接触式光调控（noncontacting light control）在凝聚态物理中备受关注，但不同光子能量区间内光场究竟削弱还是增强铁电序、其机制尚需厘清。

If the photon energy is below-gap without carrier excitation, only off-resonant scattering occurs and the FE order tends to be diminished. Slightly increasing photon energy at the near-gap regime would lead to a centrosymmetric non-polar (NP) to FE phase transition, which is not thermally accessible in conventional pressure-temperature phase diagram. We apply phenomenological Landau theory and show that they belong to the continuous and first-order phase transitions, respectively. Our mechanism provides a potential route to reversible light-stabilized polar phases with reduced lattice heating and marginal lattice damages.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Resolving the chemical footprint of molecules in analytes is of fundamental interest in biosensing and biomedical applications.   <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 结果证实铁磁LCO层与多铁BFO层之间存在电子耦合，该LCO/BFO体系有望用于电压控制磁各向异性（VCMA）系统的构建。

In the present scenario, an interionic charge transfer from Co ion to Fe ion via oxygen is observed at the interfacial region of LaCoO3 (LCO)/BiFeO3 (BFO) grown on SrTiO3 substrate, leading to induce multivalency in Co, Fe ion and also induces exchange biasing in the system.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The interplay of compensated magnetism, spin--orbit coupling (SOC), and valley degrees of freedom provides a promising route toward spin--valley control without conventional ferromagnetism. ### [56] [Janus Asymmetry Unlocks Rashba Spintronics and Chiral Phononics in Two-Dimensional Carbo-Chalcogenides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30767)

*Juan S. Rodríguez-Hernández,Gabriel F. Freitas,Nicolas M. Kawahala,Rafael S. Freitas,Felix G. G. Hernandez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在压制多晶样品（pressed pellet）中观测到亚太赫兹磁振子（magnon）对两种圆偏振吸收的强烈不对称，表明磁振子手性（chirality）测量无需单晶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁体（antiferromagnet）中磁振子进动的手性（handedness）通常只能在单晶上测量，这限制了对同时具有两种结构手性、取向随机的材料体系的表征。

Method: 以手性螺旋磁体 CsCuCl₃ 的压制多晶靶为对象，在 5 至 7 T 场区间测量亚太赫兹磁振子的圆偏振吸收，并用自旋波理论（spin-wave theory）对结果进行解释。

Result: 在该场区间内，磁振子对一种圆偏振的吸收强度超过另一种 20 倍以上；这一结果排除了磁振子绕各晶粒自身轴进动的解释，自旋波理论将其归因于塞曼频率（Zeeman frequency）——此处为交换增强能隙（exchange-enhanced gap）的 2 至 4 倍——使进动轴在每个晶粒中都被锁定到外场方向。

Conclusion: 反铁磁体中的磁振子手性无需单晶即可探测，为多晶样品中手性自旋动力学的表征提供了可行途径。

Abstract: In antiferromagnets, the handedness of magnon precession is usually measured on single crystals. In a pressed pellet of the chiral helimagnet CsCuCl$_3$, whose grains carry both structural handednesses and all orientations, we found that, from 5 to 7 T, the sub-terahertz magnon absorbs one circular polarization more than 20 times as strongly as the other, which rules out precession about each grain's own axis. Spin-wave theory attributes this to the Zeeman frequency, here two to four times the exchange-enhanced gap, which locks the precession axis to the field in every grain. Magnon chirality in antiferromagnets is thus accessible without a single crystal.

</details>

### [59] [A Transformer-based Method for Neutrino Event Generator Reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28199)
*Andrew Cudd,Roger G. Huang,Benjamin Nachman,Vinicius Mikuni,Callum Wilkinson*

Main category: physics.data-an

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于 Transformer（transformer）的架构，利用几乎完整的粒子栈（particle stack）信息在不同中微子事件产生器（neutrino event generator）之间进行事件重加权（reweighting），从而在显著扩展的相空间（phase space）上获得有效事件，并引入基于能量距离（energy distance）的多维无分箱评价指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前测试不同中微子事件产生器对基于模拟的分析的影响，受限于将模拟事件通过高成本探测器模拟（detector simulation）传播的计算开销。重加权可绕过该限制，但现有中微子物理中的方法通常一次仅能处理少数几个观测量。

Method: 采用基于 Transformer（transformer）的架构，基于几乎整个粒子栈（particle stack）在中微子事件产生器之间进行重加权；以多个 GENIE 配置与 NuWro 产生的事件为示例进行验证，并引入基于能量距离（energy distance）的指标，以多维、无分箱（unbinned）方式评估结果。

Result: 结果显示该方法在现实应用中具有良好性能，并为比较不同重加权技术（reweighting techniques）的表现提供了评价手段。

Conclusion: 该工作将重加权的适用范围扩展至更大相空间（phase space），为中微子事件产生器的比较与基于模拟的分析提供了一种可行且可评估的方法。

Abstract: The ability to test the effects of different neutrino event generators on simulation-based analyses is currently bottlenecked by the computational cost of propagating simulated events through expensive detector simulations. One way to circumvent this limitation is to reweight events produced with one generator to replicate the predictions of another. However, existing methods for doing this in neutrino physics tend to be limited to only a few observables at a time. We present a transformer-based architecture that can reweight between neutrino event generators based on nearly the entire particle stack, providing reweighted events that are valid over a greatly expanded phase space. We present results with events generated from multiple GENIE configurations and NuWro as examples, and we introduce an energy distance-based metric for evaluating the results in a multidimensional, unbinned manner. Our results show promising performance for realistic applications and provide a method for comparing the performance of different reweighting techniques.

</details>

### [60] [The Thue-Morse route to chaos in the open quantum baker map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28242)
*Leonardo Ermann*

Main category: nlin.CD

TL;DR: 该工作数值研究由宽度为 $w$、中心位于 $q_c$ 的窗口打开量子面包师映射（quantum baker map）后的共振（resonances）。当 $q_c=1/2$ 时，幸存集沿 Thue-Morse 路线从零拓扑熵变为正，共振谱逐步跟随该路线，包括熵为零的阶段，直至 $N=2^{25}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在把经典开映射的符号动力学（symbolic dynamics）路线（Thue-Morse 路线、阈值结构）与量子开系统的共振谱联系起来，厘清损失窗口如何控制量子的存活与共振分布。

Method: 对 $q_c=1/2$ 的开口经典倍映射（doubling map）与对应量子面包师映射作数值计算，规模 $N$ 达 $2^{25}$；用周期轨道（周期 $2^\nu$）的超精细多重态（multiplet）图像、Ehrenfest 时间分辨判据，以及 Walsh 谱计数和傅里叶计数来刻画谱结构与标度。

Result: 阈值 $w_c=4a^*-1$（$a^*$ 为 Thue-Morse 常数）以下，存活周期为 $2^\nu$ 且阈值双重指数累积；每个周期给出模量接近 $2^{-1/2}$ 的共振多重态，相位由作用量决定；分辨率约 $\log_2 N$，故最多约 $\log_2\log_2 N$ 步（最大规模时约 4 步）。近 $w_c$ 处 16-周期经一条仅在 $w_c$ 才经典打开的回路微扰 8-多重态，剪除该回路少数格点即可消除微扰。$w_c$ 之上一个格点或更多时，主导共振处于拓扑压力（topological pressure）预测值的 0.93–1.00 倍，且无自由参数；Walsh 特征值计数随分形 Weyl 指数（fractal Weyl exponent）增长，傅里叶计数指数略小约百分之几。

Conclusion: 量子共振谱以“逐步”方式继承经典开映射的 Thue-Morse 阈值路线，并在阈值之上与拓扑压力、分形 Weyl 指数等半经典（semiclassical）预言定量吻合，表明阈值附近的轨道多重态微扰图像具有解释力。

Abstract: We study the resonances of the quantum baker map opened by a window of width $w$ centred at $q_c$. For $q_c=1/2$ the survivor set of the classical doubling map with a hole goes from zero to positive topological entropy along the Thue-Morse route, and we find numerically, for $N$ up to $2^{25}$, that the resonance spectrum follows this route step by step, also where the entropy is zero. Below the threshold $w_c=4a^*-1$, with $a^*$ the Thue-Morse constant, the surviving cycles have periods $2^ν$ and their thresholds accumulate doubly exponentially. Each cycle gives, as isolated orbits do, a multiplet of resonances close to modulus $2^{-1/2}$, with phases set by its action. Since a cycle is resolved only if its period is shorter than about $\log_2 N$, twice the Ehrenfest time, about $\log_2\log_2 N$ steps are resolved, four at our largest sizes. Near $w_c$ the 16-cycle perturbs the 8-multiplet through a return path that classically opens only at $w_c$. Cutting a few sites of this path removes the perturbation. At $w_c$ the leading resonance jumps within one or two lattice sites, since a window one site wider than the threshold already has an entropy of order $1/\log_2 N$. One site or more above $w_c$ it lies between 0.93 and 1.00 times the value set by the topological pressure, with no free parameter. There the Walsh eigenvalue count grows with the fractal Weyl exponent, and the Fourier count with an exponent a few hundredths smaller at the sizes we reach.

</details>
