### [61] [Computational Complexity and Energy Landscapes of Coulomb Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30657)
*Paco Navarro,Michael Emmerich*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 该文用非均匀（nonuniform）计算复杂性界厘清囚禁离子库仑晶体与Thomson问题基态搜索中输入编码的作用，并证明：在离子固定于候选格点时，选取占据格点以最小化库仑能的问题在两维和三维为NP难（precision版为NP完全），困难性仅由格点位置承载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟退火（simulated annealing）被广泛用于搜索囚禁离子库仑晶体及球面电荷（Thomson问题）的基态，但输入编码方式对问题复杂性的影响此前不明确，且缺乏对实际搜索难度的严格刻画。

Method: 引入基本非均匀界（nonuniform bounds / advice bounds）分析不同输入编码下的复杂性类；通过将既有的平方反比归约（inverse-square reduction）推广到库仑相互作用，推导其角度律（含四次场项）并锐化两项估计；构造四极子（quadrupole）阵列使公共单极项在合法构型能量差中抵消，并借助势垒树（barrier tree）分析能量景观。

Result: 固定阱中相同离子情形下，一元离子数与二元能量阈值构成P/poly中的判定问题，NP难性将导致多项式层级（polynomial hierarchy）坍塌，球面情形同理；一元离子数加对数精度参数时，近似构型搜索有FP/poly中的解函数，但不提供一致搜索算法。至多100个离子的模拟中，不同极小值数目与到达已知最优极小的时间均近似指数增长。格点占据选取问题在两、三维为NP难，有限精度承诺版为NP完全。势垒树显示近简并极小间存在大势垒，模拟退火在立方体实例中陷于基态之上。实验室参数下编码在半满方格（half-filled square lattice）上数值上成立，多普勒冷却锶离子需相距几至数十微米、势阱刚度大的电极阱阵列。

Conclusion: 问题的计算困难性由格点位置编码单独承载，而非离子间裸库仑排斥本身；所给advice界不蕴含一致搜索算法，且势垒结构可解释模拟退火在实际参数下的失效，为实验实现提供阱阵列条件。

Abstract: Simulated annealing is widely used to search for ground states of trapped-ion Coulomb crystals and of charges on a sphere (the Thomson problem). Elementary nonuniform bounds clarify the role of the input encoding. For identical ions in a fixed trap, unary ion count and a binary energy threshold define a decision problem in P/poly; NP-hardness would imply a collapse of the polynomial hierarchy. The same holds on the unit sphere. With unary ion count and logarithmic precision parameter, approximate configuration search on the sphere and in a fixed positive-definite harmonic trap admits a solution function in FP/poly. These advice bounds provide no uniform search algorithm. In simulations of up to 100 ions, the number of distinct minima found and the time to reach the best-known minimum both grow approximately exponentially. When ions are pinned to prescribed candidate sites, choosing which sites to occupy to minimize the Coulomb energy is NP-hard in two and three dimensions; a finite-precision promise version is NP-complete. All ions are identical, the interaction is bare Coulomb repulsion, and the hardness is carried by the site positions alone. We extend a previous inverse-square reduction to the Coulomb interaction by deriving its angular law, including the quartic field term, and sharpening two estimates. The construction is an array of quadrupoles whose common monopole term cancels from energy differences between legal configurations. Its energy landscapes exhibit large barriers between nearly degenerate minima. Barrier trees resolve this structure, and simulated annealing becomes trapped above the ground state in the cube example. At laboratory parameters, with individually tunable well depths, the encoding survives numerically on a half-filled square lattice. For Doppler-cooled strontium ions, this requires stiff wells a few to tens of micrometers apart, as in electrode trap arrays.

</details>

### [62] [Intrinsic volume fraction can decouple from hydrodynamic size in p(NIPAm-co-AA) microgels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28284)
*Peter Edimeh,Howard Chen,Jacinta C. Conrad*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究检验了“粒径与聚合物体积变化成正比”这一堆积分数（packing fraction）外推假设，发现p(NIPAm-co-AA)微凝胶在体积相变温度以上时，流体力学半径变化与黏度变化解耦，提示多响应共聚物微凝胶的堆积分数外推需谨慎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量微凝胶的堆积分数（packing fraction）是理解其流变学（rheology）与输运性质的关键，而现有做法常基于流体力学尺寸（hydrodynamic size）与本征体积分数外推，隐含假设颗粒尺寸与聚合物体积变化成正比，该假设尚缺乏验证。

Method: 采用毛细管黏度法（capillary viscometry）与静态/动态光散射（static and dynamic light scattering），在丙烯酸（AA）pKa上下（pH 8与pH 4）测试两种交联剂浓度（1 wt%与7 wt% 亚甲基双丙烯酰胺）的p(NIPAm-co-AA)微凝胶。

Result: pH 8时AA完全电离、无明显体积相变且两种流体力学体积测量一致；pH 4时微凝胶弱带电并在LCST附近发生体积相变，低交联样品尺寸变化更大；相变温度以上，由流体力学半径得到的体积变化与由黏度得到的体积变化解耦，且R_g/R_h超过均质球理论值并随交联度增加而偏离加剧。

Conclusion: 高温下的异常解耦可能源于聚合物内部非均匀分布；对多响应共聚物微凝胶（multi-responsive copolymer microgels）外推堆积分数须谨慎，并提示可通过共聚物组成与交联密度经内部微结构调控流变学等宏观性质。

Abstract: Quantifying the packing fraction of microgels is key to understanding their rheology and transport properties. Often, packing fractions are extrapolated from the hydrodynamic size and intrinsic volume fraction under the assumption that particle size and polymer volume change are proportional. Here, we test this assumption for p(N-isopropylacrylamide-co-acrylic acid) [p(NIPAm-co-AA)] microgels of two crosslinker concentrations (1 wt\% and 7 wt\% methylenebisacrylamide) using capillary viscometry and light scattering (static and dynamic) above and below the pKa of acrylic acid (AA). At pH 8, where AA is fully ionized, the microgels do not undergo a pronounced volume phase transition because electrostatic repulsion hinders thermal collapse, and both measures of hydrodynamic volume are equal. At pH 4, however, p(NIPAm-co-AA) microgels are weakly charged and exhibit a volume phase transition near the lower critical solution temperature of pNIPAm; the magnitude of the size change is greater for the less-crosslinked microgel. Above the volume phase transition temperature, the volume change from hydrodynamic radius decouples from that from viscosity. The ratio of the radius of gyration and hydrodynamic radius R_g/R_h of p(NIPAm-co-AA) microgels at high temperature exceeds the value theoretically predicted for homogeneous spheres and the deviation increases with the extent of crosslinking, suggesting that a non-uniform internal distribution of polymer gives rise to the anomalous decoupling at high temperatures. Our results indicate that care must be taken in extrapolating packing fractions for multi-responsive copolymer microgels, and suggest that copolymer composition and crosslink density may be used to tune bulk properties such as rheology through the internal microstructure.

</details>


### [63] [Exploring new speciation patterns: architecture of genetic similarity networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28358)
*David M. Schneider,Claudio Pastorino*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文利用个体为本模型（individual based model）构建遗传相似性网络（genetic similarity networks），发现同域与邻域物种形成模式（sympatric and parapatric speciation modes）可通过特定网络指标加以区分和度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在寻找能够识别和区分不同物种形成模式（speciation modes）的可测量特征，借助网络视角刻画物种形成过程的遗传结构信号。

Method: 以二进制串（binary strings）表示基因组，通过汉明距离（Hamming distances）定义个体间遗传相似性并据以分组构建网络，开展大规模模拟（extensive simulations），考察同域与邻域物种形成模式下的网络指标。

Result: 同域与邻域物种形成模式可被清晰区分并可度量；结构属性（structural properties）如聚类系数（clustering coefficient）、平均度（mean degree）、测地距离（geodesic distance）与同配性（assortativity）在稳态值（stationary values）和瞬态阶段（transient phases）均表现出显著差异。

Conclusion: 上述网络结构差异提供了一个区分物种形成模式的新框架（novel framework），表明遗传相似性网络指标可作为识别物种形成模式的有效工具。

Abstract: We use an individual based model to identify signatures of speciation modes in genetic similarity networks. These networks group individuals based on their genetic similarity, defined through Hamming distances between binary strings representing their genomes. The results of our extensive simulations demonstrate that sympatric and parapatric speciation modes are clearly distinguishable and measurable through specific network metrics. In particular, structural properties -- including clustering coefficient, mean degree, geodesic distance, and assortativity -- show significant differences in both stationary values and transient phases, giving rise to a novel framework for distinguishing between speciation modes.

</details>


### [64] [Geometry Reorganizes Nonequilibrium States that Gate Topological Switching in an Active Polymer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29649)
*Shiqi Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究以受限部分活性聚合物（partially active polymer）为对象，证明相同外部条件下驱动系统可占据对同一稀有跃迁敏感性不同的长寿命态，且几何（孔半径）会重构力学坐标与拓扑命运之间的映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于检验一个此前未被充分实证的命题：即使外部条件完全相同，驱动系统也可能存在多个长寿命、对同一稀有跃迁（rare transition）敏感性不同的状态。研究希望超越“平均速率位移”的解释框架，探讨几何是否在其中选择非平衡态并重组其与未来拓扑结局的对应关系。

Method: 采用受限的部分活性聚合物模型，其中彼此独立的无结复制体（unknotted replicas）处于不同力学态并具有不同拓扑未来。方法包括匹配对照（matched-control）与事件分辨（event-resolved）分析，识别出易感储库（susceptible reservoir）与短暂的穿线前驱（threading precursor）；并以独立数据预先设定的分类器，从71个保留的无结相空间态（phase-space states）出发，通过Langevin噪声（Langevin noise）分支进行前瞻性检验，共2272支。

Result: 前瞻性检验显示分类器表现突出：在同一孔半径下能强烈区分有限时间结化（finite-time knotting），但在相邻孔半径下其敏感性排序发生反转。事件分辨分析揭示了易感储库与短暂穿线前驱的存在，说明状态差异具有可预测的微观基础。

Conclusion: 几何的作用不止是平移平均结化速率，而是在长寿命非平衡态之间进行选择，并重组既有力学坐标（mechanical coordinate）与未来拓扑命运（topological fate）之间的映射。该结果为驱动系统中状态依赖的稀有事件敏感性提供了直接证据。

Abstract: Even under identical external conditions, a driven system can occupy distinct long-lived states with different susceptibilities to the same rare transition. We demonstrate this in a confined partially active polymer, where independent unknotted replicas occupy distinct mechanical states with different topological futures. Matched-control and event-resolved analyses reveal a susceptible reservoir and a short threading precursor. We test this state dependence prospectively by launching 2272 Langevin-noise branches from 71 preserved unknotted phase-space states using a classifier prespecified from independent data. Most strikingly, the same classifier strongly separates finite-time knotting at one pore radius, yet reverses its susceptibility ordering at a neighboring radius. Thus geometry does not merely shift an average knotting rate; it selects among long-lived nonequilibrium states and reorganizes the mapping between a pre-existing mechanical coordinate and future topological fate.

</details>


### [65] [Elastic crumple pair potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29654)
*D. Gimeno,E. Hamm,J. A. Hanna*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过准一维（quasi-1D）实验装置研究薄膜褶皱（crumple）之间的成对相互作用，发现其具有非单调配对势：短程吸引、长程排斥，并存在中间禁区，且用唯象能量模型再现了分岔行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究多关注孤立的月牙形顶点（crescent vertex）与脊（ridge）形状，而对几乎普遍存在于变形过程中的多顶点动力学、生成/湮灭及相互作用缺乏研究；超出单褶皱的下一步即是由背景曲率介导的成对相互作用。

Method: 采用准一维（quasi-1D）实验装置，实现受控的边界运动与力测量，并结合褶皱位置的追踪，针对两种常见的成对相互作用类型开展实验；同时构建唯象能量（phenomenological energy）模型。

Result: 实验给出了非单调配对势（non-monotonic pair potentials）的证据，表现为短程吸引、长程排斥和中间禁区（forbidden region）；所构建的唯象能量可再现观测到的分岔（bifurcations）及各观测量之间的相互关系。

Conclusion: 研究结果为理解和建立薄弹性板壳结构（thin elastic plate and shell structures）中动态变形与斑图形成、演化的约化描述（reduced descriptions）提供了新见解与输入。

Abstract: Thin structures across scales and materials, from graphene sheets to clothing to aircraft skins, respond to forcing and confinement by crumpling, with deformation localized into zones of characteristic shape. Much attention has been paid to isolated crescent vertex and ridge shapes, but there has been almost no investigation into the dynamics, creation/destruction, and interactions between multiple vertices (``crumples'') that are inherent in nearly all deformation processes. The next step beyond single crumples are pair interactions, mediated by background curvature. Of these, we focus on two common types, using a quasi-1D setup allowing controlled boundary motion and force measurement coupled with tracking of crumple positions. These results provide a new insight and input towards understanding and building reduced descriptions of dynamic deformation and pattern formation and evolution in thin elastic plate and shell structures.

</details>

The inner viscous body thus acts as an internal governor of the dynamical state.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该工作提供了七实参数协变映射类中可分解性的完整判据，并将其转化为具体相位依赖族的精确阈值结果，进而通过 Choi matrix 与 PPT edge states 建立了不可分解正映射与纠缠探测之间的明确联系。

Abstract: Decomposable positive maps cannot detect entangled states with positive partial transpose (PPT). We obtain a necessary and sufficient criterion for decomposability in the entire seven-real-parameter class of Hermiticity-preserving cyclic-sign covariant maps on $M_3$. The criterion reduces to a one-variable inequality and, for positive maps in this class, characterizes indecomposability exactly. We apply it to a phase-dependent family obtained by adding a completely positive map to the Choi map. Every map in this family is positive, and we find the exact phase-dependent threshold for indecomposability and atomicity. At three phase values, the maps remain indecomposable for every finite deformation strength. Each indecomposable map gives an indecomposable entanglement witness through its Choi matrix. We construct PPT edge states of birank $(5,5)$ detected by these witnesses throughout the indecomposable region.

</details>

By systematically studying the spectral properties of quantum channels, we present general structure theorems that characterize asymptotic behaviour, illustrated through several paradigmatic examples. We further discuss how the asymptotic dynamics can be exploited for dissipative state engineering, the identification of decoherence-free subspaces, the design of quantum error-correction schemes, and the evaluation of the asymptotic capacities of quantum channels.

We construct examples of {\it locked states} which are stationary states of the corresponding averaged dynamics in the interaction picture. The states are nonnegative Hermitian density operators with finite Hilbert-Schmidt norm. The calculations rely on the recurrence equation for diagonal entries.

### [70] [Undecidable quantum storage: no algorithm can tell from the noise rate whether a fixed circuit remembers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29134)
*Seth Douglas,Nidhal Mghirbi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在单量子比特噪声下，存在一维平移不变、时间周期的局部电路，其是否能在任意小的噪声率下无线延长量子存储时间，是不可判定的（对应 Π^0_1-完备集），因此仅凭噪声率无法判断电路是否具备纠错能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错（quantum error correction）在噪声率低于阈值时可使更大的量子存储器（quantum memory）存储量子比特更久且无上限。论文追问：能否仅凭噪声率判断给定含噪电路是否具有此性质，即噪声阈值（noise threshold）是否存在可算法判定的刻画。

Method: 构造固定局部电路：平移不变（translation-invariant）、时间周期，每条线每层后施加去极化噪声（depolarizing noise），并含全部经典控制。电路以通用图灵机（universal Turing machine）的输入 n 为索引，划分出可计算的不相交噪声率区间；局部噪声统计选择被模拟的输入 n，停机（halting）触发擦除。编码与解码映射无噪声且为全局操作，仅依赖 n、系统尺寸（解码还依赖时间），不依赖噪声率。

Result: 若图灵机对 n 永不停机，则量子比特在第 n 个区间内以至少 15/16 的纠缠保真度（entanglement fidelity）被存储，存储时间随尺寸无界增长：环上线性、六维指数增长。若停机，则任何编码的保真度在尺寸与噪声率无关的时间内降至 1/2+η 以下（任意固定 η>0）。具有无界存储的区间标签构成 Π^0_1-完备（Π^0_1-complete）集，两类区间均在零处累积，说明这些电路不存在噪声阈值。

Conclusion: 结果表明仅凭噪声率不足以判定电路能否实现无限期量子存储，纠错能力的判定具有不可判定性。无速率选择时，环构造是一个平移不变、时间周期的存储器，在每个足够小的噪声率下存储时间随环长线性增长，回应了 Dünnweber、Styliaris 与 Trivedi 提出的一维问题中的存储部分。该结论为存在性结果：寄存器与相互作用范围极大，噪声率极小。

Abstract: Below a threshold noise rate, error correction lets larger quantum memories store a qubit for longer, without limit. Can the noise rate alone tell us whether a given noisy circuit does this? We construct fixed local circuits for which no algorithm can, already in one dimension under single-qubit noise. Each circuit is translation-invariant and time-periodic, with depolarizing noise on every wire after every layer, including all classical control. A computable list of disjoint rate intervals is indexed by the inputs $n$ of a universal Turing machine; local noise statistics select the input that the circuit simulates, and halting triggers erasure. If the machine runs forever on $n$, a qubit is stored throughout the $n$-th interval at entanglement fidelity at least $15/16$ for a time that grows without bound with size: linearly on rings, exponentially in six dimensions. The encoding and decoding maps are noiseless and global; they depend on $n$, the system size and, for decoding, the time, but not on the rate. If it halts, the fidelity of every encoding falls below $1/2+η$, for any fixed $η>0$, after a time independent of size and rate. The labels of the intervals with unbounded storage form a $Π^0_1$-complete set, and intervals of both kinds accumulate at zero, so these circuits have no noise threshold. Without rate selection, the ring construction is a translation-invariant, time-periodic memory: from an initialized classical hierarchy it stores a qubit for a time linear in the ring length at every sufficiently small rate, addressing the memory part of a one-dimensional question raised by Dünnweber, Styliaris and Trivedi. These are existence results: registers and interaction ranges are very large and the rates astronomically small.

</details>


### [71] [Reconstructing Pure-State OTOCs from Forward-Time Linear Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30068)
*Yu-Hang Jin,Xudong He,Yu Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明任意纯态（pure state）的连通乱序关联函数（OTOC）可仅由对易子线性响应（commutator linear responses）精确重构，且无需时间反演、辅助比特、双拷贝或弱测量，仅需一次幺正脉冲串、正向多体演化与单点局部读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OTOC测量方案通常依赖时间反演（time reversal）、辅助系统（ancilla）、双拷贝（double copy）或弱测量（weak measurement）等难以在实验上实现的要素，论文旨在提供一种更简便可行的替代协议。

Method: 采用幺正脉冲串（unitary string pulse）、正向多体演化与单次局部读出（local readout）重构OTOC；通过对脉冲生成元完备基（complete basis）取平均实现精确重构，并将脉冲限制在固定算符权重（operator weight）使测量稀疏化，把截断转化为可解析控制的谱滤波（spectral filter）。

Result: 对高斯态（Gaussian states），有限权重响应经已知组合重标度（combinatorial rescaling）后精确正比于完整OTOC；对量子混沌系统，典型残差随扇区尺寸（sector size）呈幂律抑制（power law）。萨查德-叶-基塔耶夫模型（Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model）与不可积伊辛链（nonintegrable Ising chain）的精确对角化（exact-diagonalization）基准验证了上述预测。

Conclusion: 该协议在实验上更为可行，并提供确定性偏差与统计采样方差（statistical sampling variance）的干净分离；理论预测已获两类典型模型的数值基准支持。

Abstract: We show that the connected out-of-time-ordered correlator (OTOC) of any pure state can be reconstructed exactly from commutator linear responses alone. The protocol requires no time reversal, no ancilla, no double copy, and no weak measurement: a unitary string pulse, forward many-body evolution, and a single local readout suffice. An average over a complete basis of pulse generators yields the OTOC exactly. We further show that the measurement can be made sparse by restricting the pulses to a fixed operator weight, which turns the truncation into an analytically controlled spectral filter: the deterministic bias can be computed before the experiment and cleanly separated from the statistical sampling variance. For Gaussian states, the finite-weight response is exactly proportional to the full OTOC after a known combinatorial rescaling. For quantum chaotic systems, the typical residual is suppressed as a power law in the sector size. Exact-diagonalization benchmarks in the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model and a nonintegrable Ising chain confirm these predictions.

</details>


### [72] [Improved two-way capacity upper bounds for amplitude-damping channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29650)
*Stefano Pirandola*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为量子比特振幅阻尼信道（amplitude-damping channel）推导出一个改进的双向辅助容量（two-way-assisted capacity）上界 log₂[1+(1−γ)γ^{γ/(1−γ)}]，对所有 0<γ<1 严格优于 PLOB 相对熵界（relative-entropy bound）、解析最大相对熵界（max-relative-entropy bound）及优化平衡压缩纠缠界（squashed-entanglement bound），并将 sector teleportation simulation 推广至量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有双向辅助容量上界（PLOB 相对熵界、最大相对熵界、压缩纠缠界）在振幅阻尼信道上不够紧致，尤其在高阻尼（high damping）区域，需要更紧的定量极限来刻画量子通信、纠缠分发（entanglement distribution）、私密通信与密钥协商（secret-key agreement）的可达性能。

Method: 将 sector teleportation simulation 推广到量子比特：利用相位旋转（phase rotations）与置换（permutations）在固定激发子空间（fixed-excitation subspaces）内实现精确模拟，同时保持二能级输入结构；据此确定振幅阻尼资源的精确可加相对熵纠缠（additive relative entropy of entanglement）以及固定输入布居下的精确正则化纠缠增益（regularized entanglement gain）；进一步推导广义振幅阻尼（generalized amplitude damping，含热激发）的显式界，并将结果与 unital 信道和纠缠破坏信道（entanglement-breaking channels）组合成闭式表达。

Result: 所得上界对每个 0<γ<1 严格改进三个已有基准界；高阻尼下已知 triple-rail 协议可达到其主导系数（leading coefficient）的 99.54% 以上。组合表达式在零温情形下回到已知结果，并在整个纠缠破坏区域内取零，在广泛参数范围内优于既有基准；广义资源（generalized resources）的精确纠缠量仍未确定，两类结果均可在平均激发约束（average-excitation constraint）下进一步细化。

Conclusion: 结果为通过弛豫量子比特（relaxing qubits）进行的自适应通信提供了定量极限，并表明 sector simulation 即使超出资源纠缠可精确已知的信道范围仍具实用性；广义振幅阻尼资源的精确纠缠量仍为开放问题。

Abstract: We derive an improved upper bound on the two-way-assisted capacities of the qubit amplitude-damping channel with decay probability $γ$, given by $\log_2[1+(1-γ)γ^{γ/(1-γ)}]$. It applies to quantum communication, entanglement distribution, private communication, and secret-key agreement, and, for every $0<γ<1$, strictly improves the PLOB relative-entropy bound, the analytic max-relative-entropy bound, and the optimized balanced squashed-entanglement bound. At high damping, a known triple-rail protocol attains more than $99.54\%$ of its leading coefficient. Our method extends sector teleportation simulation to qubits: phase rotations and permutations give exact simulations within fixed-excitation subspaces while preserving the two-level input structure. We determine the exact, additive relative entropy of entanglement of the amplitude-damping resources and the exact regularized entanglement gain at fixed input population. We then derive explicit bounds for generalized amplitude damping, which includes thermal excitation. Combining the amplitude-damping gain with unital and entanglement-breaking channels gives a closed expression that recovers the zero-temperature result and vanishes throughout the entanglement-breaking region. It improves established benchmarks over broad parameter ranges, while the exact entanglement of the generalized resources remains open. Both results admit refinements under an average-excitation constraint. The bounds provide quantitative limits for adaptive communication through relaxing qubits and show how a sector simulation can remain useful beyond channels whose resource entanglement is known exactly.

</details>


### [73] [Local redundancies are necessary for long-lived memories in finite spatial dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29689)
*Chao Yin,Yaodong Li,Benedikt Placke,Vedika Khemani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明了指数长寿命热记忆（thermal memory）的不可能性定理（no-go theorem）：对无局部冗余（local redundancy）的有限维局部稳定子哈密顿量（local stabilizer Hamiltonian），任何恒定非零温度下热浴动力学快速混合，混合时间为 O(N)。这一结论排除了类似 Haah 立方码（Haah's cubic code）机制在欧几里得体系中支撑被动量子记忆的可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非欧几里得图上的 LDPC 码可借玻璃态动力学（glassy dynamics）在无热力学相变时维持长寿命记忆，但有限维欧几里得体系中能否实现被动（量子）记忆长期存疑，Haah 立方码即为典型悬案。

Method: 以哈密顿量各项间冗余（redundancy）结构为切入点，证明无局部冗余条件下热吉布斯态（Gibbs state）具有强关联衰减性质；并在对特定区域诱导冗余的更强条件下进一步论证快速混合。

Result: 恒定非零温度下局部热浴动力学以 O(N) 混合时间快速混合（N 为量子比特数）；在更强条件下混合时间可降至 O(log N)，该结果适用于 Haah 立方码及其推广与三维层码（three-dimensional layer codes）等实例。

Conclusion: 冗余结构（redundancy structure）是理解热记忆的关键性质；对无局部冗余的有限维局部稳定子体系，指数长寿命热记忆机制不成立。

Abstract: We prove a no-go theorem for exponentially long-lived thermal memories, based on the structure of redundancies among Hamiltonian terms. For local stabilizer Hamiltonians in finite spatial dimensions, the absence of local redundancies---relations in which the product of a small number of Hamiltonian terms equals the identity---is known to preclude bulk thermodynamic phase transitions. Yet glassy dynamics can still sustain long-lived memories even without a thermodynamic transition, as demonstrated by LDPC codes on non-Euclidean graphs. A longstanding question, exemplified by Haah's cubic code, is whether such a mechanism can also support passive (quantum) memory in finite-dimensional Euclidean systems. We show that this route fails for local stabilizer Hamiltonians without local redundancies: at any constant nonzero temperature, local heat-bath dynamics is fast mixing, with $O(N)$ mixing time, where $N$ is the number of qubits. We establish this by proving a strong decay of correlations property of the thermal Gibbs state. We further prove rapid mixing with $O(\log N)$ mixing time under a stronger condition on induced redundancies in suitably chosen regions. This stronger result applies to several well-studied examples, including Haah's cubic code and its generalizations, as well as three-dimensional layer codes. Our work identifies redundancy structure as a key property for understanding thermal memories.

</details>


### [74] [Quantifying superposition via coherence by stochastic orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30656)
*Gökhan Torun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文以量子相干性（quantum coherence）为工具，提出通过正交化（orthogonalization）测量映射来操作性地量化非正交参考态（nonorthogonal reference states）的叠加（superposition），定义“叠加度（degree of superposition）”，并刻画非正交性带来的态转换约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接以基变换处理非正交参考态的叠加会失败：任何正交归一基都会使某些无叠加态显得相干，因此需要一种不依赖基变换的操作性量化框架。

Method: 引入正交化测量，将每个参考态映射为各结果下某个基向量的倍数，并证明这类测量恰为把叠加转化为相干性的自由操作（free operations）；以可提取的最大平均相干性定义叠加度，将任一相干度量（coherence measure）转化为具有相同操作意义的叠加度量。

Conclusion: 结果为叠加提供了操作性量化，并揭示非正交性所导致的确定性转换约束，表明相干度量族可系统导出叠加度量及其不可转换性结构。

Abstract: Here we present an approach to quantifying superposition of nonorthogonal reference states through quantum coherence. No change of basis achieves this: every orthonormal basis makes some superposition-free states appear coherent. We show that orthogonalization succeeds instead as a measurement mapping every reference state onto a multiple of a basis vector in each outcome; such measurements are exactly the free operations that convert superposition into coherence. The largest average coherence they extract from a state, which we call its degree of superposition, turns every coherence measure into a superposition measure with the same operational meaning. An interference term becomes coherence at most with the probability of unambiguously discriminating its two reference states, a bound attained when there are no others and, for positive coefficients and nonnegative overlaps, exactly when the overlaps are probabilities of a random grouping. Maximal degree means conversion into a maximally coherent state with certainty, possible for up to three reference states but not in general. Each coherence measure gives its own degree; when two degrees order two superpositions oppositely, neither converts into the other with certainty, and we find such pairs already for two reference states, unlike in qubit coherence. For equal positive overlaps, in-phase superpositions are rigid: deterministic conversions that keep all components can only permute their coefficients. As the reference states merge, the degree stays bounded where widely used measures can diverge, and at every amplitude a rotation-symmetric cat state attains its maximum. Our results quantify superposition operationally and reveal conversion constraints created by nonorthogonality.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子技术有望革新物理世界的观测方式，但如何以最小开销获得实质性优势仍是一项核心追求；本文旨在探索在探测量子多体动力学时实现低开销、大优势的方案。

Method: 提出一种仅增加一个额外量子比特的算法，利用该量子比特对动力学轨迹进行相干干涉（coherent interference），从而同时估计指数级多个多时关联函数（multi-time correlators）。

Result: 相较任何无辅助量子比特（ancilla-free）方案，所需演化次数呈指数级减少；该优势即使在不限制相干时间（coherence time）且允许任意自适应控制的对照下依然成立，甚至对单个关联函数也存在。此外，延长相干时间可指数级增强观测能力，表明协议在空间与时间上均具最优性；数值上以数量级更少的实验运行预测了多体系统的高阶探针信号。

Conclusion: 单量子比特辅助框架在量子学习（quantum learning）、传感（sensing）与模拟（simulation）领域具有广泛适用性，并确立了其在空间与时间资源上的最优性。

Abstract: While quantum technologies promise to revolutionise how we observe the physical world, achieving substantial advantages with minimal overhead remains a central pursuit. Here, we show that augmenting a quantum machine with just one extra qubit yields an exponential advantage in probing quantum many-body dynamics. We propose an algorithm that uses this qubit to coherently interfere dynamical trajectories, simultaneously estimating exponentially many multi-time correlators with exponentially fewer evolutions than any ancilla-free scheme. Remarkably, this advantage holds even when the ancilla-free machine has unrestricted coherence time and arbitrary adaptive control, and persists even for a single correlator. We further prove that extending coherence time exponentially enhances observational power, establishing our protocol's optimality in both space and time. Finally, we numerically demonstrate our approach by predicting higher-order probe signals in a many-body system with orders-of-magnitude fewer experimental runs than previous methods. These results highlight the broad utility of our framework across quantum learning, sensing, and simulation.

</details>


### [76] [A Quantum Model from the Riemann-Siegel Formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31116)
*Germán Sierra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个双分量量子模型，利用破坏性干涉（destructive interference）在连续谱中选取正则化 zeta 函数（regularized zeta function）的临界线零点（critical-line zeros），并以数值方式将其与黎曼零点（Riemann zeros）对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是建立一个量子/谱框架，使黎曼 zeta 函数的临界线零点可由物理可观测谱与量子干涉而非纯解析延拓方式刻画。

Method: 以精确的 Riemann–Siegel 积分表示（Riemann–Siegel integral representation）为起点，通过减去原点极点贡献定义 ζ_reg(s)；剩余两支围道积分提供干涉振幅。再用 Dirichlet 热核滤波（Dirichlet heat filter）抑制高空间频率，并由素幂伸缩（prime-power dilations）生成算术修饰（arithmetic dressing），把伸缩生成元（dilation generator）映射为物理 Hilbert 空间上具正度量的自伴、宇称–时间（PT）对称哈密顿量。

Result: 该哈密顿量谱为实且连续；计算得到的 ζ_reg(s) 下半临界线零点（lower critical-line zeros）接近相应黎曼零点，其间距（spacing）与对关联（pair-correlation）统计在所采样范围内与高斯幺正系综（Gaussian unitary ensemble, GUE）一致。

Conclusion: 作者明确将“下方临界带（critical strip）内每个解析零点是否都属于物理谱族（physical spectral family）”列为未解决问题，即该构造的普适性仍有待论证。

Abstract: We construct a two-component quantum model in which destructive interference selects the critical-line zeros of a regularized zeta function within a continuous spectrum. Starting from the exact Riemann--Siegel integral representation, we define $ζ_{\rm reg}(s)$ by subtracting the contribution of the pole at the origin. The two remaining contour integrals supply the interfering amplitudes.
  A Dirichlet heat filter suppresses high spatial frequencies, and an arithmetic dressing has a profile generated by prime-power dilations. These transformations map the dilation generator to a self-adjoint, parity--time ($PT$) symmetric Hamiltonian on a physical Hilbert space with a positive metric. Its spectrum is real and continuous. The computed lower critical-line zeros of $ζ_{\rm reg}(s)$ lie close to the corresponding Riemann zeros and exhibit spacing and pair-correlation statistics consistent with the Gaussian unitary ensemble over the sampled range. Whether every analytic zero in the lower half of the critical strip belongs to the physical spectral family remains open.

</details>


### [77] [Advancing the Theory of Pretty Good Quantum Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31207)
*Bikun Li,Guo Zheng,Liang Jiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将 pretty good measurements 与 Petz 映射统一到一个带可调幂次的半定规划（semidefinite programming）框架中，并给出幂次介于 1 至 3 时更强的性能界（在二次幂处达到峰值）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找最优量子测量（quantum measurement）或恢复映射（recovery map）通常计算代价高；已有的 pretty good measurements 与 Petz 映射虽为显式近似，但仅由 Barnum-Knill 界保证达到最优性能的平方。作者希望统一并推广这些操作以覆盖更广的量子任务。

Method: 构建一个带可调幂次（tunable power）的半定规划（semidefinite programming）框架，将 pretty good measurements 与 Petz 映射统一为其中的特例，并将其扩展至更广泛的量子任务。

Result: 证明了当幂次大于 1 且不超过 3 时的更强界，且该界在二次幂（quadratic power）处达到峰值。对结构化的信道（structured channels），如 Pauli 噪声下的 stabilizer codes，大幂次会使恢复映射趋向于针对最大混合输入（maximally mixed inputs）的最大似然解码器（maximum likelihood decoder）。

Conclusion: 可调幂次的半定规划框架统一并强化了经典近似操作，在幂次较高时与结构化噪声下的最大似然解码建立联系，为量子测量与恢复映射的设计提供了统一视角。

Abstract: Finding optimal quantum measurements or recovery maps is often demanding. Pretty good measurements and Petz maps offer explicit approximations achieving at least the square of optimal performance by the Barnum-Knill bound. We unify these operations in a semidefinite programming framework with a tunable power, extending them to broader quantum tasks. We prove a stronger bound for powers above one up to three that peaks at quadratic power. For structured channels such as stabilizer codes under Pauli noise, large powers steer recovery to a maximum likelihood decoder for maximally mixed inputs.

</details>

### [78] [Machine-learning-assisted photon pairing for neutral-pion reconstruction at future Higgs factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28180)
*Yi Wang,Chao Yang,Tianyi Yang,Enrui Tian,Yuexin Wang,Song Pan,Jun Guo,Manqi Ruan*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该文在高能对撞机的四个基准物理过程（味物理、τ物理、希格斯物理）中比较中性π⁰候选者的选择方法：以优化的双光子不变质量窗（diphoton mass-window, MW）为基线，对比BDT、MLP、ParticleNet与Particle Transformer四种机器学习方法，综合效率与纯度评估性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性π⁰（neutral pion）重建对高能对撞机上的精确测量至关重要，尤其在味物理（flavor physics）、τ物理与希格斯（Higgs）物理中；需要更优的候选者选择以提升重建质量。

Method: 在电子—正电子对撞机的四个基准物理过程中，将优化的双光子质量窗（mass-window, MW）基线与四种机器学习方法——提升决策树（boosted decision tree, BDT）、多层感知机（multilayer perceptron, MLP）、ParticleNet（PN）和Particle Transformer（ParT）——进行对比，并以选择效率（efficiency）与纯度（purity）的乘积作为评价指标。

Result: 在参考电磁量能器（ECAL）能量分辨率下，四种机器学习方法均优于MW；PN与ParT分别取得53%–95%与60%–128%的相对提升，主要来自对错误匹配光子对的更好排除，且该优势在所研究的不同ECAL能量分辨率下得以保持。

Conclusion: 改进后的候选者选择为后续ρ与强子τ（hadronic τ）重建研究提供了起点；训练样本量扫描显示，多数PN与ParT学习曲线呈现两段快速提升期，其间为较缓慢的增长。

Abstract: Neutral-pion ($π^0$) reconstruction is important for precision measurements at high-energy colliders, particularly in flavor, $τ$, and Higgs physics. We study $π^0$ candidate selection in four benchmark physics processes at electron--positron colliders, comparing an optimized diphoton mass-window (MW) baseline with four machine-learning methods: a boosted decision tree (BDT), a multilayer perceptron (MLP), ParticleNet (PN), and Particle Transformer (ParT). Performance is evaluated using the product of selection efficiency and purity. At the reference ECAL energy resolution, all four machine-learning methods outperform MW. PN and ParT achieve relative improvements of 53%--95% and 60%--128%, respectively, primarily through better rejection of incorrectly matched photon pairs. Their performance advantage is maintained across the investigated ECAL energy resolutions. Training-sample-size scans reveal two periods of rapid performance improvement separated by slower growth in most PN and ParT learning curves. The improved candidate selection provides a starting point for further studies of $ρ$ and hadronic $τ$ reconstruction.

</details>

*Prashansa Mukim,Shanshan Gao,Hucheng Chen,Grzegorz W. Deptuch,Jack Fried,Niccolo' Gallice,Dominik Gorni,Lingyun Ke,Giovanni Pinaroli,Sergio Rescia,Chao Zhang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为惰性液体探测器（noble liquid detectors）中产生的电荷或光信号提供低噪声读出，需开发适用于低温环境的前端专用集成电路（ASIC）；CHARMS250V1作为下一代芯片原型，为后续16通道集成奠定设计基础。

Method: 基于65 nm CMOS工艺设计单通道低温前端ASIC原型，包含前置放大与脉冲成形级（pre-amplification and pulse shaping stages），并提供可编程的电荷增益、峰化时间与基线设置。

Result: 芯片实现4.3 mV/fC至25 mV/fC的可编程电荷增益，峰化时间（peaking time）可在250 ns至2 μs间编程，基线可编程为200 mV或900 mV，分别用于单极性与双极性信号处理；摘要给出了设计与测量结果。

Conclusion: CHARMS250V1作为单通道原型验证了面向惰性液体探测器低噪声读出的低温前端ASIC设计，其后续设计将被集成到16通道ASIC中。

Abstract: This work presents the design and measurement results of CHARMS250V1, a next-generation cryogenic front-end application specific integrated circuit (ASIC) developed using a 65 nm CMOS process. It is designed for low-noise readout of charge or light signals generated in noble liquid detectors. CHARMS250V1 is a single channel prototype whose design in the future will be integrated within a 16-channel ASIC. It comprises pre-amplification and pulse shaping stages that provide programmable charge gain between 4.3 mV/fC and 25 mV/fC, peaking time programmable between 250 ns and 2 us, and programmable baseline of either 200 mV or 900 mV for processing of unipolar and bipolar signals, respectively.

</details>


### [80] [Real-time event-level zero-shot learning at 150 ns: towards a foundation model for particle physics experiment triggering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28355)
*Leo Rozanov,Lauri Laatu,Benedikt Maier,Arianna Cox,Aadeesh Sharma,Zhiqiang Que,Alex Tapper*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该文提出一种用于大型强子对撞机（LHC）硬件级触发的事例嵌入（event embedding）表示，经离线—在线蒸馏与量化后部署于现场可编程门阵列（FPGA），可在单一低延迟表示上支持多触发任务，并对未参与训练的物理过程具备良好迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬件级一级触发（Level-1 trigger）针对每种物理特征分别设计算法，仅依赖低延迟、低粒度的事例视图，缺乏可复用的统一表示。作者希望以“学习得到的事例嵌入”作为共享基础，实现多任务乃至面向基础模型（foundation model）的触发范式。

Method: 利用同一模拟碰撞的离线（offline）与一级触发（Level-1）配对视图（paired views），先以监督度量损失（supervised metric loss）训练离线 transformer，再逐事例蒸馏（distil）为量化的一级触发 transformer；其位宽（bit widths）在固定资源预算下学习获得。

Result: 在 25 个标准模型（Standard Model）过程上训练后，嵌入将 28 个留出过程置于其物理近亲附近；对从未进入编码器训练的背景下，最近邻选择可剔除 99%；对已见与未见标准模型事例的中位保留率分别为 90% 与 96%，对 52 个超出标准模型（beyond-the-Standard-Model）基准样本为 88%，在同等事例上优于所评估基线。第二道针对已见过程的门控可将高质量双轻子（dilepton）信号聚为无标签簇，在具备位移径迹信息时亦适用于长寿命（long-lived）信号。

Conclusion: 该编码器仅含 7512 个参数，FPGA 延迟为 147 ns，以一次学习得到的事例表示派生多种选择，为 LHC 硬件触发的基础模型奠定可行性基础。

Abstract: The hardware-based Level-1 triggers of the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) experiments select collisions with algorithms designed for one signature each, acting on a coarse, low-latency view of every event. We present a learned event embedding for several trigger tasks and evaluate its transfer to processes absent from training. Using paired offline and Level-1 views of the same simulated collisions, we train an offline transformer with a supervised metric loss and distil it, event by event, into a quantised Level-1 transformer whose bit widths are learned under a fixed resource budget. Trained on 25 Standard Model processes, the embedding places 28 held-out processes next to their physical relatives. A nearest-neighbour selection against background collisions, which never enter encoder training, rejects 99% of them. It retains a median of 90% of seen and 96% of unseen Standard Model events and 88% of a set of 52 beyond-the-Standard-Model benchmark samples, the best-performing score across the three groups among the baselines evaluated on the same events. A second gate against the seen processes isolates high-mass dilepton signals into label-free clusters, and long-lived signals too when displaced-track information is available. With 7512 parameters and an FPGA latency of 147 ns, the encoder uses one event representation, learned once, to derive several selections, opening the door to a foundation model for the LHC's hardware-based triggers.

</details>


### [81] [Ion Backflow Suppression and Absorption Dynamics in Cascaded GEM Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28806)
*Md Kaosor Ali Mondal,Shakir Eqbal,Sachin Rana,Poojan Angiras,A. Sarkar*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该文基于ANSYS与Garfield++模拟，研究单至五层GEM探测器的离子回吸（ion backflow, IBF）抑制特性，提出层分辨吸收形式化框架，并证明单锥形孔结构相较双锥形孔可将离子吸收比例显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离子回吸（IBF）会导致正离子漂回灵敏体积、畸变漂移场并恶化空间与能量分辨率，是GEM探测器长期未解的问题；亟需区分箔级与探测器级吸收指标，并寻找比简单增加层数更有效的抑制途径。

Method: 采用ANSYS与Garfield++对Ar/CO₂（70:30）气体中单至五层GEM进行模拟，建立层分辨（layer-resolved）形式化方法，区分条件性箔级吸收与按各级倍增正确加权的探测器级吸收指标；对比双锥形（biconical）与单锥形（single-conical）孔结构，并改变三层GEM第二转移间隙（transfer gap）长度（1.2–3.5 mm）作补充测试。

Result: 双锥形几何下，层叠箔仅使总吸收比例由单层的47.83%温和升至五层的59.06%；改用单锥形孔后，同一层数范围内吸收比例升至75%–87%，IBF/Gain比降低二至三倍，优于增加四层双锥形箔的收益。间隙长度测试中增益呈非单调依赖（约2.0–2.5 mm处最大），IBF/Gain比随间隙单调下降，单锥形几何始终保留约两倍优势。

Conclusion: 级联GEM结构中的离子抑制不仅取决于箔片数量，更主要受各箔局部离子收集特性支配；单锥形孔是提升高计数率探测器性能的有前景途径。

Abstract: Ion backflow (IBF) is a persistent challenge in Gas Electron Multiplier (GEM) detectors: positive ions drifting back into the sensitive volume distort the drift field and degrade both spatial and energy resolution. We investigate the ion-absorption characteristics of single- to quintuple-GEM detectors using ANSYS and Garfield$^{++}$ for an Ar/CO$_2$ (70:30) gas mixture. A layer-resolved formalism is developed that distinguishes conditional foil-level absorption from a detector-level absorption metric that correctly weights ion production at every amplification stage. For the conventional biconical geometry, cascading foils improves the overall ion-absorption fraction only moderately, from 47.83\% for a single GEM to 59.06\% for a quintuple GEM. Replacing the biconical holes with a single-conical profile raises the absorption fraction to 75\%-87\% across the same range of multiplicities and reduces the IBF/Gain ratio by a factor of two to three an improvement that exceeds what is gained by adding four additional biconical foils. As a secondary test under more realistic conditions, the second transfer-gap length of a triple-GEM detector is varied from 1.2 to 3.5~mm. The gain shows a non-monotonic dependence with a maximum near 2.0-2.5~mm, while the IBF/Gain ratio decreases monotonically with increasing gap length; the single-conical geometry retains its factor-of-two advantage throughout. These findings establish that ion suppression in cascaded GEM structures is governed not only by the number of foils but primarily by the local ion-collection properties of each foil, and that single-conical holes represent a promising route to improved performance in high-rate detector applications.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用费米实验室自研的软件基础设施与生产工具（software infrastructure and production tools），高效利用计算资源，并改进由合作组开发的重建工作流（reconstruction workflow）。

Result: 在上述措施下，实验的数据吞吐能力（data throughput capacity）得到提升，分析周转时间（analysis turnaround time）在实验周期内缩短了一个数量级（an order of magnitude）。

Conclusion: 通过克服大规模数据的计算与运行挑战，Muon g-2实验建立了可行的计算策略体系，实现了127 ppb精度的测量，并为超大数据量物理实验的数据处理与分析提供了可借鉴的经验。

Abstract: This paper details the computing strategies of the Muon g-2 Experiment at Fermilab, which measured the muon's anomalous magnetic moment with a precision of 127 ppb by processing over 10 petabytes of detector and simulation data. This paper discusses the computational and operational challenges of data production, data handling, and data analysis of such large datasets. These challenges were overcome by using Fermilab-developed software infrastructure and production tools, making efficient use of resources, and improving the collaboration-developed reconstruction workflow. By overcoming these challenges, the experiment improved the data throughput capacity and reduced the analysis turnaround time by an order of magnitude over the course of its lifetime.

</details>


### [83] [FGPA-based advanced muon trigger algorithms for long-lived particle detection at the High-Luminosity LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30394)
*Daniel Estrada,Javier Prado,Pelayo Leguina,Andrea Cardini,Elena Aller,Santiago Folgueras*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: CMS 一级触发（Level-1 trigger）在相位二（Phase-2）升级中提出两项互补方案：桶部（barrel）基于漂移管（Drift Tube）击中模式的簇射识别与匹配算法，以及面向桶-端盖重叠区（barrel–endcap overlap）位移缪子（displaced muon）图神经网络（graph neural network）重建的硬件导向量化优化流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMS 一级触发需在严格延迟与硬件资源限制下实时重建缪子；传统算法对发生辐射能量损失的高能缪子效率下降，且对位移径迹表现欠佳，从而限制了对长寿命粒子（long-lived particles, LLP）等新物理特征的灵敏度。相位二升级引入的先进 FPGA 为更复杂的重建算法提供了可能。

Method: 桶部方案在第一处理阶段用专用算法从漂移管击中模式中探测簇射，并在后续过滤阶段将簇射原语（shower primitives）与传统触发原语关联；同时对桶-端盖重叠区未来基于图神经网络（GNN）的位移缪子重建，采用 GraphSAGE 代理模型，研究整数量化（integer quantization）、二次幂缩放（power-of-two scaling）与数据驱动位宽优化（bit-width optimization）相结合的工作流。

Result: 模拟研究得到约 80% 的簇射分类准确率，辐射缪子的估计匹配效率高于 80%；固件原型显示簇射探测延迟约 20 ns，初步匹配逻辑延迟低于一个束团交叉（bunch crossing）。GNN 量化工作流在基准数据集上达到 (75.0 ± 1.1)% 准确率，确定性延迟 19 个时钟周期（360 MHz 下 52.8 ns），数字信号处理器（DSP）利用率仅 20%。

Conclusion: 两项结果分别为桶部簇射触发与重叠区 GNN 位移缪子重建的硬件实现研究奠定基础，后续将基于 CMS 缪子实测数据开展实现层面的验证。

Abstract: The CMS Level-1 trigger must reconstruct muons in real time under stringent latency and hardware resource constraints. Conventional algorithms lose efficiency for high-energy muons undergoing radiative energy losses and can be suboptimal for displaced trajectories, limiting sensitivity to exotic signatures such as long-lived particles (LLPs). The Phase-2 upgrade introduces advanced FPGAs that enable more sophisticated reconstruction algorithms. This work presents two complementary developments addressing these challenges. In the barrel region, a dedicated algorithm detects showers from Drift Tube hit patterns at the first processing stage. At the subsequent filtering stage, shower primitives are correlated with conventional trigger primitives, currently targeting radiatively showering high-momentum muons, with LLP signatures as a broader objective. Simulation studies yield approximately $80\%$ shower-classification accuracy and an estimated matching efficiency above $80\%$ for radiating muons. Firmware prototypes demonstrate a shower-detection latency of approximately $20\,\mathrm{ns}$ and sub-bunch-crossing latency for preliminary matching logic. In parallel, a hardware-oriented optimization methodology is investigated for future graph neural network based displaced-muon reconstruction in the barrel--endcap overlap region. Using a GraphSAGE proxy on a benchmark dataset, a workflow combining integer quantization, power-of-two scaling, and data-driven bit-width optimization achieves $(75.0 \pm 1.1)\%$ accuracy and a deterministic latency of $19$ cycles ($52.8\,\mathrm{ns}$ at $360\,\mathrm{MHz}$), with only $20\%$ digital signal processor utilization. These results establish a basis for implementation studies using CMS muon data.

</details>

### [84] [An Implicit Discrete Adjoint Unified Gas-Kinetic Scheme for Sensitivity Analysis and Shape Optimization in Multiscale Gas Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28923)
*Yuze Zhu,Hangkong Wu,Kun Xu*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文针对稳态隐式统一气体动理学格式（UGKS）提出无矩阵离散伴随（matrix-free discrete adjoint）方法，通过精确线性化与转置实现高效梯度灵敏度评估，并在跨流域流动中验证其精度与加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂几何下多尺度流动（multiscale flows）的性能优化仍缺乏准确且高效的梯度灵敏度（gradient-based sensitivity）评估手段，全局雅可比装配与端到端反向模式自动微分（AD）均带来显著内存与计算开销。

Method: 对收敛固定点算子做精确线性化与转置，构建稳态隐式UGKS的无矩阵离散伴随；采用混合微分策略，即对复杂非线性动理学核（kinetic kernels）使用局部自动微分，对宏观—微观耦合的隐式更新与质量守恒投影采用解析推导的切线与转置算子。

Result: 连续、过渡与自由分子流域（continuum, transitional, free-molecular regimes）的验证测试确认了与切线线性求解器的机器精度逐步对偶性（stepwise duality）及与中心有限差分（central finite differences）的高度一致；六参数形状灵敏度分析中单次伴随求解分别较切线线性与中心差分加速7.54倍与3.88倍。

Conclusion: 周期Knudsen泵与Mach-2 NACA0012翼型的约束形状优化均在十次设计更新内收敛，归一化质量流提升最高29.68%，气动阻力降低7.46%，表明该方法适用于内流与外流多尺度场景。

Abstract: Accurate and efficient gradient-based sensitivity evaluation remains challenging for performance optimization in multiscale flows with complex geometries. To address this challenge, this work develops a matrix-free discrete adjoint method for the steady implicit unified gas-kinetic scheme (UGKS) through the exact linearization and transposition of its converged fixed-point operator. The method employs a hybrid differentiation strategy that combines local automatic differentiation (AD) of complex nonlinear kinetic kernels with analytically derived tangent and transpose operators for the coupled macroscopic--microscopic implicit updates and mass-conservation projection. This formulation ensures exact discrete consistency across both flow and geometric dependencies while avoiding global Jacobian assembly and the memory overhead of end-to-end reverse-mode AD. Verification tests across continuum, transitional, and free-molecular regimes confirm machine-precision stepwise duality with the corresponding tangent-linear solver and close agreement with central finite differences. In a six-parameter shape-sensitivity analysis, a single adjoint solve achieves speedups of 7.54X and 3.88X over tangent-linear and central finite-difference evaluations, respectively. Finally, constrained shape optimizations of a periodic Knudsen pump and a Mach-2 NACA0012 airfoil converge within ten design updates, achieving up to 29.68% improvement in normalized mass flow and 7.46% reduction in aerodynamic drag across internal and external multiscale flows.

</details>


### [85] [A Steady Theory for Highly Unsteady Thin Airfoil Motions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30074)
*Scott A. Bollt*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 论文提出一种从"绝对涡量（absolute vorticity）场定常"的参考系出发构造的广义定常薄翼型理论，通过相空间叶状分层（foliation）描述翼型运动，并重新解释若干非定常失速与经典理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统定常薄翼型理论（steady thin airfoil theory）建立在定常流动之上，难以涵盖非定常（unsteady）现象；论文旨在以绝对涡量场的定常性为更一般的出发点，统一处理非定常失速与准定常（quasi-steady）理论之间的关系。

Method: 将一般思想特化，在由翼型总旋转速率、速度与攻角（angle of attack）构成的相空间中构造叶状分层结构，使停留在同一叶层上即可保持流体绝对涡量恒为零；并证明现有准定常流动与Theodorsen非定常流动理论的部分内容可由该分层的平面近似构建。

Result: 在该类流动中，一个著名的非定常失速条件被证明等价于传统定常失速条件；多项经典结果可在绝对涡量场意义下重新解释为定常，并对McGowan等（AIAA J. 49, 2011）的实验结果提供了洞察；纯非定常效应被归结为叶层的横穿（transversal），仅占一个维度。

Conclusion: 该理论使准定常推广变得自然，并为非定常扩展提供了构造思路；非定常效应可简化为单维的叶层穿越，为后续实验与理论研究提供了新的切入点。

Abstract: A generalized steady thin airfoil theory is built not from steady flow, but from flows in which there exists a frame of reference where the absolute vorticity field is steady. We specialize this general idea to construct a foliation of a domain of phase space for thin airfoil motions in which staying on a leaf of the foliation maintains zero absolute vorticity in the fluid indefinitely. The phase space is described by the airfoil's total rotation rate, speed, and angle of attack. It is found using this definition that one of the preeminent unsteady stall conditions is equivalent to the traditional steady stall condition for this class of flows, various other classical results can be re-interpreted as steady in the absolute vorticity field, and insight is also provided into the surprising experimental results of McGowan et al. (AIAA J. 49, 2011). We show parts of existing quasi-steady flow and Theodorsen unsteady flow theory can be built from a planar approximation of our foliation, making the quasi-steady extension of our theory trivial, and suggesting how an unsteady extension can be created as well. Furthermore, pure unsteady effects in the new theory amount to a transversal of the leaves, occupying just one dimension, inviting interesting prospects for future experimental and theoretical work.

</details>

### [86] [ACT Excited: Reconciling Starobinsky and Quadratic Chaotic Inflationary Models with ACT Results via Excited States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28144)
*Amjad Ashoorioon,Abdolreza Yousefi-Sostani*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 放宽标量扰动初态的 Bunch–Davies 假设（通过 Bogoliubov 系数引入激发参数与相对相位），可在不改动暴胀势的前提下使 Starobinsky 模型重新与 ACT DR6 等数据相容，并为张标量比与自洽关系提供可检验预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACT DR6 联合 Planck、CMB 透镜（lensing）与 DESI BAO 数据倾向于更高的标量谱指数（scalar spectral index）n_s 及略正的跑动（running）α_s，使 Starobinsky 模型承压，而二次混沌模型（quadratic chaotic model）已因其过大的张标量比（tensor-to-scalar ratio）r 被排除；因此需要一种不修改暴胀势（inflaton potential）即可恢复相容性的机制。

Method: 将标量扰动的初态由 Bunch–Davies 态推广为激发态，用激发参数 χ_S 与相对相位 2φ_S 参数化 Bogoliubov 系数，推导其标度依赖（scale dependence）对 n_s、α_s 的移动，并在 (φ_S, χ_S) 平面上映射满足 1σ ACT DR6 区间、r<0.038 及新物理能标 Λ 高于哈勃尺度 H 的区域。

Result: 对 Starobinsky 模型，标度依赖的 χ_S 或 φ_S 在该平面上几乎处处可行，仅 φ_S=0,π 附近且 χ_S≳1.22 的小区域被排除，标度依赖相位在 φ_S=0,π/2,π 及 χ_S=0 处因 dφ_S/dlnk 与二阶导数发散而失效。QC 模型还需激发态压低 r，要求 χ_S≳0.63 且 Λ≲6H；两模型均要求 |dχ_S/dlnk|≳0.0021，QC 模型解析给出 0.0021≲dχ_S/dlnk≲0.0058 与 0.0008≲d²χ_S/d(lnk)²≲0.0062。

Conclusion: 若张量模（tensor modes）仍处于 BD 态，该情景预言自洽关系（consistency relation）r=-8n_T 被破坏，可在张量功率谱及其倾斜（tilt）被测量后加以检验。

Abstract: Data Release 6 of the Atacama Cosmology Telescope, combined with Planck, CMB lensing and DESI BAO data, favours a higher scalar spectral index $n_{s}$ than Planck and a mildly positive running $α_{s}$, putting the Starobinsky model in tension with the data, while the quadratic chaotic model is already excluded by its large tensor-to-scalar ratio $r$. We show that relaxing the Bunch--Davies assumption for the initial state of the scalar perturbations can restore agreement without modifying the inflaton potential. Parametrising the Bogoliubov coefficients by an excitation parameter $χ_{S}$ and a relative phase $2\varphi_{S}$, we derive the shifts of $n_{s}$ and $α_{s}$ induced by their scale dependence and map the regions of the $(\varphi_{S},χ_{S})$ plane compatible with the $1σ$ ACT DR6 ranges, the bound $r<0.038$, and the requirement that the scale of new physics $Λ$ exceed the Hubble scale $H$. For the Starobinsky model, a scale-dependent $χ_{S}$ or $\varphi_{S}$ works almost everywhere in this plane: only small neighbourhoods of $\varphi_{S}=0,π$ with $χ_{S}\gtrsim1.22$ are excluded, and a scale-dependent phase fails at $\varphi_{S}=0,π/2,π$ and at $χ_{S}=0$, where the required ${\rm d}\varphi_{S}/{\rm d}\ln k$ and ${\rm d}^2\varphi_{S}/{\rm d}(\ln k)^2$ diverge. For the QC model, the excited state must also suppress $r$, which requires $χ_{S}\gtrsim0.63$ and $Λ\lesssim6H$. A scale-dependent $χ_{S}$ must satisfy $|{\rm d}χ_{S}/{\rm d}\ln k|\gtrsim0.0021$ in both models, and in the QC model we show analytically that $0.0021\lesssim{\rm d}χ_{S}/{\rm d}\ln k\lesssim0.0058$ and $0.0008\lesssim{\rm d}^2χ_{S}/{\rm d}(\ln k)^2\lesssim0.0062$. If the tensor modes remain in the BD state, the scenario also predicts a violation of the consistency relation $r=-8n_{T}$, testable once the tensor power spectrum and its tilt are measured.

</details>


### [87] [Scale-dependent Dark Matter in a Multi-component Cosmological Framework: Distinct Components Governing Galactic and Cosmological Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29007)
*V. K. Oikonomou*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文将暗物质（dark matter）的“标度依赖状态方程（scale-dependent equation of state）”从星系尺度推广至宇宙学尺度，提出普适形式 ⟨P(χ)⟩=⟨ρ(χ)⟩⟨σ_DM²⟩，并据此讨论其宇宙学含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系尺度上标度依赖状态方程已能成功描述暗物质主导星系，本文旨在将其推广至宇宙学尺度，并解释为何各星系的 K₀ 值不同。

Method: 借助理论论证，把星系尺度的近等温状态方程 P(r)=K(r)ρ(r) 推广为以研究尺度 χ 为变量的普适形式 ⟨P(χ)⟩=⟨ρ(χ)⟩⟨σ_DM²⟩，并引入熵函数（entropy function）K(χ)=⟨σ_DM²⟩ 及可差异聚类（cluster differently）的多个暗物质组分概念。

Result: 分析表明该标度依赖状态方程暗物质不能是宇宙中暗物质的唯一组分；同时为 K₀（星系核心之外中、大半径处 K(r) 的取值）在不同星系间变化提供了解释。

Conclusion: 标度依赖状态方程暗物质可控制星系尺度动力学，而宇宙学尺度需由另一暗物质组分主导；不同尺度动力学由聚类方式不同的暗物质组分分别控制。

Abstract: In this work we generalize the concept of scale-dependent equation of state dark matter from galactic to cosmological scales. At galactic scales, the scale-dependent equation of state which successfully describes dark matter dominated galaxies has a nearly isothermal equation of state of the form $P(r)=K(r)ρ(r)$, where the entropy function $K(r)$ is radius dependent and slowly varying with the galactic radius. Using appropriate theoretical arguments, the successful galactic scale dark matter model is generalized to have a universal equation of state of the form $\langle P(χ)\rangle =\langle ρ(χ)\rangle \langle σ_{DM}^2\rangle$, where $χ$ is the scale of the system under study and it can describe cosmological or galactic scales and $σ_{DM}$ is the characteristic velocity dispersion of dark matter at the relevant scales. This results to the entropy function behaving universally as $K(χ)=\langle σ_{DM}^2\rangle$. We investigate the cosmological implications of scale-dependent equation of state dark matter, and we conclude that this scale-dependent equation of state dark matter cannot be the sole component of dark matter in the Universe. Also the second aim of this work is to provide a sufficient explanation for the question why $K_0$ changes for each galaxy, where $K_0$ is the value of $K(r)$ at intermediate and large radii from the core of the galaxy. Furthermore, we introduce the concept of having distinct dark matter components which cluster differently, and thus controlling differently the dynamics of the Universe at various scales. Scale-dependent equation of state dark matter can control the galactic scales, while another component can dominate at cosmological scales and thus control the Universe at cosmological scales.

</details>


### [88] [Constraints on ultralight dark matter couplings from orbital perturbations of binary pulsars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30487)
*Federico Huxhagen,Diana López Nacir*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 利用NANOGrav 15年数据集50颗双脉冲星的宽带计时数据，通过超轻暗物质引起的轨道共振效应，对三种暗物质—普通物质耦合给出95%可信度上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻暗物质（ultralight dark matter）若直接与普通物质耦合，会扰动双脉冲星轨道，且当场频与轨道运动谐波匹配时扰动长期增长；该效应可用于在宽广质量区间约束多种耦合。

Method: 对每颗脉冲星、质量区间10^-23至10^-18 eV，由受扰轨道根数构造计时特征（timing signature），将耦合与场的局域振幅、相位、方向及脉冲星红噪声（red noise）联合采样，对计时模型（timing model）解析边缘化，再合并单脉冲星似然得到各质量点的95%可信上限。

Result: 线性普适耦合下，偏心系统的轨道共振使上限接近卡西尼（Cassini）界限；二次耦合下把中子星视为点粒子时，在2×10^-22至2×10^-21 eV区间给出当前最强限制；矢量超轻暗物质情形在3×10^-22至10^-21 eV与PPTA、Eöt-Wash界限相当，并在孤立轨道共振处达到或超越Eöt-Wash界限，但MICROSCOPE在整个质量范围内仍更强。

Conclusion: 双脉冲星宽带计时数据可作为探测超轻暗物质耦合的有效探针，其轨道共振显著增强灵敏度，在多参数区间提供具竞争力的上限。

Abstract: Ultralight dark matter coupled directly to ordinary matter perturbs the orbits of binary pulsars, and the perturbation grows secularly when its frequency matches a harmonic of the orbital motion. We use this effect to constrain three couplings with the wideband timing data for the $50$ binary pulsars of the NANOGrav 15 yr data set: a linear and a quadratic universal coupling of a scalar field to matter, and a vector field coupled to baryon minus lepton number ($B-L$). For every system and for masses between $10^{-23}$ and $10^{-18}\,$eV, we build the timing signature from the perturbed orbital elements. We then sample the coupling together with the local amplitude, phase and direction of the field and the red noise of the pulsar, marginalizing analytically over the timing model, and combine the single-pulsar likelihoods into $95\%$ credible upper limits at each mass. For a universal linear coupling, the orbital resonances of the eccentric systems bring the limit close to the Cassini bound. For the quadratic coupling, treating the neutron star as a point particle, the limits are the strongest available between $2\times10^{-22}$ and $2\times10^{-21}\,$eV. In the case of vector ultralight dark matter, the limit is comparable to the PPTA and Eöt-Wash bounds between $3\times10^{-22}$ and $10^{-21}\,$eV and reaches or beats the Eöt-Wash bound at isolated orbital resonances, while MICROSCOPE remains stronger over the entire mass range.

</details>

### [89] [Constraining Dehnen-inspired dark matter halo with S2 star's orbit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29245)
*Qi Qi,Qiang Wu,Xiao-Mei Kuang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用S2恒星绕人马座A*的轨道，对三类基于Dehnen暗物质分布的时空背景给出尺度半径与密度的贝叶斯上限，并揭示其进动效应的物理来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验由Dehnen型暗物质分布（内斜率γ=0、1、5/2）所刻画的黑洞背景，借助S2恒星近星点进动这一高精度观测量约束暗物质晕参数。

Method: 采用精确转折点积分（turning-point integral）计算有限偏心率下的近星点进动（pericenter advance），并通过外部展开（exterior expansion）提取晕的主要贡献；随后进行十五参数贝叶斯分析（Bayesian analysis），联合天体测量与光谱观测及实测进动参数。

Result: 得到1σ边缘化上限：三个背景下r_s/M分别小于0.236、0.168、0.030，ρ_sM²分别小于0.536、0.578、0.004。前两种度规中晕单极项（monopole）与中心质量简并；固定动力学质量时径向修正导致逆行进动（retrograde precession）；γ=5/2度规另含角亏（angular deficit）贡献，增强进动响应，故参数上限更严。

Conclusion: S2轨道进动可有效约束暗物质晕尺度；γ=5/2背景因额外角亏效应而受到最强限制。

Abstract: We use the orbit of S2 star around Sagittarius A* to constrain three black hole backgrounds motivated by Dehnen profiles with inner slopes $γ=0$, $1$, and $5/2$, which are characterized by the scale radius $r_s$ and density $ρ_s$ of the dark matter halo. An exact turning-point integral gives the pericenter advance at finite eccentricity, and an exterior expansion identifies the leading halo contributions. A fifteen-parameter Bayesian analysis combines astrometric and spectroscopic observations with the measured precession parameter, from which we obtain the marginalized $1σ$ upper limits $r_s/M<0.236$, $0.168$, and $0.030$, and $ρ_sM^2<0.536$, $0.578$, and $0.004$, respectively. The leading halo monopole in the first two metrics is degenerate with the central mass. At fixed dynamical mass, their leading radial profile correction produces retrograde precession. The $γ=5/2$ metric introduces an additional angular deficit contribution, which enhances the precession response and accounts for its smaller parameter limits.

</details>

### [90] [KAM theory for contact Hamiltonian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28587)
*Zhicheng Tong,Yong Li,Xuefeng Zhao*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 该文在有限维与无穷维下首次建立保频率 KAM 定理，针对接触哈密顿族（contact Hamiltonian families）中的满维勒让德环面（full-dimensional Legendrian tori），在外部参数调节后使环面流解析共轭于给定平移，且估计对阻尼参数趋于零一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 KAM 理论缺乏对含动量与接触变量一般解析耦合（general analytic coupling）的接触哈密顿系统的保频率结果，尤其缺少阻尼参数（damping parameter）跨越零时的一致性估计与弱 KAM 解的唯一性刻画。

Method: 通过调节外部参数，构造频率保持的 KAM 迭代，在有限维与无穷维中证明环面上的流在原始时间下解析共轭于指定平移（translation），并使估计对阻尼参数趋于零保持一致。

Result: 耦合可在阻尼参数为零时于一阶产生非零平均阻尼（nonzero mean damping）；有限维中非零平均阻尼导致环面附近的时间保持解析接触线性化（contact linearization）；两种情形下不变图给出平稳 Hamilton–Jacobi 方程（stationary Hamilton–Jacobi equations）的全局解析解，其特征流共轭于指定平移。

Conclusion: 在全局 Tonelli 假设（global Tonelli assumptions）与接触变量严格递增条件下，该解析解同时是唯一的前向与后向弱 KAM 解（weak KAM solution），其勒让德图（Legendre graph）为携带准周期动力学（quasi-periodic dynamics）的不变环面。

Abstract: In both finite and infinite dimensions, we establish the first frequency-preserving KAM theorems for full-dimensional Legendrian tori in contact Hamiltonian families with general analytic coupling between the momenta and the contact variable. After adjustment of external parameters, the flow on each torus is analytically conjugate in the original time to the prescribed translation, with estimates uniform as the damping parameter crosses zero. This coupling can generate nonzero mean damping at first order even when the damping parameter vanishes. In finite dimensions, nonzero mean damping yields a time-preserving analytic contact linearization near the torus. In both settings the invariant graphs give global analytic solutions of stationary Hamilton--Jacobi equations whose characteristic flows are conjugate to the prescribed translation. A novel consequence in finite dimensions is that, under global Tonelli assumptions and strict increase in the contact variable, this analytic solution is both the unique forward and the unique backward weak KAM solution, and its associated Legendrian graph is an invariant torus carrying quasi-periodic dynamics.

</details>


### [91] [On the Finite-Time Singularities of Point-Vortex Systems II: Upper Right Quadrant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30946)
*Cheng Yang*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究右上象限内点涡系统（point-vortex system）的有限时间奇点问题：证明两涡情形下解的全局存在唯一性，并对三涡情形排除有限时间边界逼近；同时证明当涡量两两乘积之和为零时必存在可任意指定位置与时刻的有限时间内部坍缩，且解渐近自相似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点涡系统的有限时间奇点（finite-time singularity）是否存在、以何种形式出现，是流体力学与动力系统交叉的核心问题；带边界（两面墙与角点）的象限区域使碰撞、边界逼近与逃逸等机制更为复杂，需要对全局存在性与坍缩现象给出严格刻画。

Method: 结合哈密顿估计（Hamiltonian estimates）、带符号坐标矩的单调性（monotonicity of signed coordinate moments）以及针对两面墙与角点的几何估计（geometric estimates）。对坍缩解，则在一个增添复尺度（complex scale）的相似系统（similarity system）中构造实维数为二的局部强稳定流形（local strong stable manifold）。

Result: 两涡（任意非零涡量、互异初始位置）情形下，通过排除内部碰撞、边界逼近与有限时间逃逸至无穷，得到全局存在唯一性；三涡（任意非零涡量）情形下，在下极限（limit inferior）意义下排除有限时间边界逼近，且无需假设轨迹有界或收敛。此外，对涡量三元组两两乘积之和为零的每一情形，均存在有限时间内部坍缩，坍缩点可在内部任意指定，坍缩时间可为任意充分小的正数，所得解渐近自相似，表现为平方根收缩（square-root contraction）与对数旋转（logarithmic rotation）。

Conclusion: 象限内点涡系统的有限时间奇点结构被明确区分：两涡与三涡情形下不存在边界型有限时间奇点，而内部坍缩在特定涡量条件下必然发生且具有高度可设计性；相似系统强稳定流形的构造为自相似坍缩解提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We study finite-time singularities of point-vortex systems in the upper right quadrant. For two vortices with arbitrary nonzero circulations and distinct initial positions, we prove global existence and uniqueness by excluding interior collisions, boundary approach, and finite-time escape to infinity. For three vortices with arbitrary nonzero circulations, we rule out finite-time boundary approach in the limit inferior sense, without assuming boundedness or convergence of the trajectories. The argument combines Hamiltonian estimates, monotonicity of signed coordinate moments, and geometric estimates adapted to both walls and the corner.
  We also establish the existence of finite-time interior collapse for every nonzero circulation triple whose pairwise products sum to zero. The collapse point can be prescribed arbitrarily in the interior, with any sufficiently small positive collapse time. The resulting solutions are asymptotically self-similar, exhibiting square-root contraction and logarithmic rotation. They are constructed using a local strong stable manifold of real dimension two in a similarity system augmented by a complex scale.

</details>

### [92] [Is the learner or the summary the limit? Auditing machine-learning energy reconstruction for air-shower arrays on KASCADE and GRAPES-3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29363)
*Mugdha Raje Ashiya,Samarjeet Malik*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出一套五步审计（five-step audit）方法，将训练好的机器学习能量估计器（energy estimator）与一个与学习器无关（learner-independent）的误差下界估计——即拟合摘要所允许的最低平方损失贝叶斯风险（squared-loss Bayes risk, floor）——进行对比，据此判断性能瓶颈来自模型容量不足还是输入摘要信息已耗尽。应用于KASCADE与GRAPES-3数据表明，梯度提升模型已接近该下界（差距约2.2%），进一步增大模型容量预计收益不超过约2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙射线阵列的机器学习能量估计器通常只用少量拟合出的簇射参数（fitted shower parameters）训练，而非台站信号（station signals）。当分辨率不再提升时，常规诊断手段无法区分是更好的模型能带来改进，还是这些汇总参数本身已无更多信息可用。

Method: 采用五步审计：将训练模型与一个与学习器无关的最低误差估计（平方损失贝叶斯风险下界）比较；检验哪些输入增加信息；标记仅由拟合计算得到的输入。将其应用于公开KASCADE模拟（三种强子相互作用模型：QGSJET-II-04、EPOS-LHC、SIBYLL-2.3d）、13,848,509个实测KASCADE事件以及模拟GRAPES-3事件。

Result: 在10^16 eV以下的已知能量区间内，基于九个重建特征的梯度提升（gradient boosting）在所有估计器设置下与所估下界相差2.2%以内；用真实粒子数替代重建量可在10^15 eV以下消除70–72%的误差方差、在10^16–10^17 eV消除24–26%，而模拟台站信号仅弥补其中约十分之一。在GRAPES-3上，由拟合渲染图像输入的网络并不优于拟合本身。实测数据中，固定电子大小时的平均缪子大小落在各模型质子-铁区间内，无标签探针（label-free probe）显示缪子通道使用方式与模拟一致，但二者均未能确定能量标度（energy scale）。

Conclusion: 该下界本身是估计值（在半合成基准中精度约1%，区间存在欠覆盖，且并非真实贝叶斯风险的严格上界）。结论表明拟合参数摘要的信息已基本被榨取，台站信号蕴含未被利用的信息；带能量标签的结果均为模拟所得。

Abstract: Machine-learning energy estimators for cosmic-ray arrays are usually trained on a few fitted shower parameters, not station signals. When their resolution stops improving, standard diagnostics cannot say whether a better model would help or the summary has run out of information. We use a five-step audit that compares trained models with a learner-independent estimate of the lowest error the summary allows (its squared-loss Bayes risk, or floor), tests which inputs add information and flags inputs merely computed from the fit. We apply it to public KASCADE simulations with three hadronic-interaction models (QGSJET-II-04, EPOS-LHC and SIBYLL-2.3d), to 13,848,509 measured KASCADE events and to simulated GRAPES-3 events. Within known energy decades below 10^16 eV, gradient boosting on nine reconstructed features is within 2.2% of the estimated floor under every estimator setting, so more capacity is not expected to gain more than about 2%. The floor is an estimate, accurate to about 1% in a semi-synthetic benchmark with intervals that under-cover, and not a bound on the true Bayes risk. Replacing reconstructed by true particle numbers removes 70 to 72% of the error variance below 10^15 eV and 24 to 26% between 10^16 and 10^17 eV on all three models, yet simulated station signals close about a tenth of that gap. On GRAPES-3, networks given images rendered from the fit do no better than the fit. In measured data, the mean muon size at fixed electron size lies within every model's proton-to-iron bracket and a label-free probe finds the muon channel used as in simulation; neither fixes the energy scale. Energy-labelled results are simulated.

</details>


### [93] [A Positron Spectral Dip near 20 GV in Selected Orbital Projections of AMS-02 Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29849)
*Yi Yang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该工作将Ams-02十年间日度电子与正电子谱与轨道记录按3268个共同日期对齐，发现16.6-22.8 GV刚度区间正电子归一化通量在若干投影中出现下凹，且在银心与蟹状星云处正电子下凹比电子深约3-3.6个百分点；各向同性强度可精确复现所有投影，而天顶笔束模型下的正电子湮灭仅能产生<0.10个百分点的下凹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是检验AMS-02日度电子与正电子谱中是否存在与天区方向（天顶指向）相关的通量下凹结构，并判断其能否用各向同性强度或常规正电子湮灭机制解释。

Method: 将2011-2021年日度电子与正电子谱与轨道天顶记录在3268个共同日期上匹配，按各天区窗口的驻留时间加权，并将每个刚度（rigidity）bin除以全日期均值做归一化；随后在银心、蟹状星云及以1177个Fermi-LAT星表源为中心的扫描中检验下凹，并以固定的刚度做平移谱的置换检验（permutation-like trial）估计偶然概率，同时与各向同性强度及天顶笔束（pencil-beam）湮灭模型对比。

Result: 在16.6-22.8 GV，归一化正电子通量在若干投影中低于相邻bin；银心与蟹状星云处正电子下凹分别比电子下凹深3.04±1.19与3.57±1.22个百分点（统计误差）；星表扫描在PKS~0301$-$243与PKS~1424+240也发现下凹。固定刚度下，13.8%的谱-轨道相对平移试验可在星表某处产生至少与观测最大下凹同样深的下凹；各向同性强度结合实测日度谱可精确复现全部投影，而天顶笔束模型下正电子在银心与蟹状星云的共同冷电子柱上直接湮灭仅产生不足0.10个百分点的下凹深度。

Conclusion: 摘要提示观测到的方向性正电子下凹在统计上并非极稀有（约13.8%的平移试验可复现），且各向同性假设即可复现全部投影，常规天顶笔束湮灭机制亦不足以解释其深度，因而该结构的物理来源仍待进一步厘清。

Abstract: We analyze more than a decade of public Alpha Magnetic Spectrometer (AMS-02) data (2011-2021), matching daily electron and positron spectra to the orbital record on 3268 common dates. For each sky window we weight the spectra by the time the orbital zenith spends there and divide each rigidity bin by its all-date mean. At 16.6-22.8 GV, the normalized positron flux dips below its neighboring bins in several projections. At the Galactic center and the Crab, the positron dip exceeds the electron dip by $3.04\pm1.19$ and $3.57\pm1.22$ percentage points, respectively (statistical errors). A scan centered on 1177 Fermi-LAT catalog sources also finds dips at PKS~0301$-$243 and PKS~1424+240. At fixed rigidity, 13.8\% of trials that shift the spectra relative to the orbit produce a dip somewhere in the catalog at least as deep as the observed maximum. An isotropic intensity with the measured daily spectra reproduces every projection exactly. In a zenith pencil-beam model, direct positron annihilation on a common cold-electron column at the Galactic center and the Crab produces less than 0.10 percentage points of dip depth in either projection.

</details>

Pérez-Pérez,C. D. Rho,D. Rosa-González,M. Roth,A. Sandoval,J. Serna-Franco,M. Shin,Y. Son,R. W. Springer,O. Tibolla,K. Tollefson,I. Torres,F. Ureña-Mena,E. Varela,X. Wang,Z. Wang,I. J. Watson,H. Wu,S. Yu,S. Yun-Cárcamo,X. Zhang,H. Zhou,C. de León*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过联合似然分析七个红移 \(z=0.14-0.60\) 的 TeV BL Lac 天体的 HAWC 与 H.E.S.S./MAGIC/VERITAS 观测，搜寻光子-轴子类粒子（ALP）混合导致的高能伽马射线通量增强信号；未发现显著信号，并给出光子-ALP 耦合的 95% \(\mathrm{CL}_s\) 排除限低至 \(5.5\times10^{-12}~\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}\)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子类粒子（axion-like particles, ALPs）可在天体物理磁场中与光子混合，改变遥远耀变体（blazar）高能伽马射线的传播，并相对河外背景光（extragalactic background light, EBL）吸收的预期产生能量依赖的通量增强。该特征为探测 ALP 提供了间接手段，而此前工作多基于单一源或单一仪器，亟需多源多仪器的联合检验。

Method: 研究采用对七个 TeV BL Lac 天体的联合似然分析（joint likelihood analysis），结合约 3000 天的 HAWC 观测与已发表的 H.E.S.S.、MAGIC、VERITAS 能谱数据，并利用 \(\mathrm{CL}_s\) 方法在未发现显著信号时推导排除限。

Result: 未发现统计显著的 ALP 信号。在 95% \(\mathrm{CL}_s\) 水平上排除光子-ALP 耦合至 \(5.5\times10^{-12}~\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}\)（\(m_a\lesssim 4\) neV）以及 \(4.6\times10^{-12}~\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}\)（\(m_a\approx 6-9\) neV 附近）；排除参数空间面积的系统不确定度为 \(-36.5\%\) 至 \(+32.6\%\)，主要来自 EBL 模型选择。

Conclusion: 由于存活概率（survival probability）随耦合振荡，排除区域并非单连通，故对每个质量给出最低被排除耦合而非上限。联合限几乎完全由红移最高的已探测源 PG 1553+113 决定。

Abstract: Axion-like particles (ALPs) can mix with photons in astrophysical magnetic fields, modifying the propagation of very-high-energy gamma rays from distant blazars and producing an energy-dependent enhancement of the observed flux relative to the standard expectation from absorption on the extragalactic background light (EBL). We search for this signature through a joint likelihood analysis of seven TeV BL Lac objects at redshifts $z = 0.14-0.60$, combining approximately 3,000 days of HAWC observations with published High Energy Spectroscopic System (H.E.S.S.), Major Atmospheric Gamma Imaging Cherenkov Telescopes (MAGIC), and Very Energetic Radiation Imaging Telescope Array System(VERITAS) spectra. We find no statistically significant ALP signal and derive exclusion limits using the $\mathrm{CL}_s$ method. We exclude photon-ALP couplings at the 95% $\mathrm{CL}_s$ level down to $5.5 \times 10^{-12}~\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$ for $m_a \lesssim 4$ neV and $4.6 \times 10^{-12}~\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$ near $m_a \approx 6-9$ neV. The systematic uncertainty on the excluded parameter-space area is $-36.5\%$ to $+32.6\%$, dominated by the choice of EBL model. Because the survival probability oscillates with the coupling, the excluded region is not simply connected, and we quote at each mass the lowest excluded coupling rather than an upper bound. The combined limit is set almost entirely by the highest-redshift detected source, PG 1553+113.

</details>


### [95] [Dual Horizon-MAD: A New Mode of Binary Black Hole Magnetically Arrested Accretion with Saturated Horizon Magnetic Fluxes and Intermittent Minidisks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28237)
*Vikram Manikantan,Vasileios Paschalidis*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文给出迄今最长的超巨质量双黑洞（SMBBH）全广义相对论（GR）磁流体力学（MHD）模拟，模拟时长约56个轨道周期，并首次识别出一种新的“双视界磁囚禁盘”（DH-MAD）吸积模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠预言旋近中超巨质量双黑洞的多信使（multimessenger）信号，需要能把吸积流解析到黑洞视界的全GR MHD模拟；但此类模拟难以运行足够长时间以弛豫双黑洞附近的磁流体动力学状态，成为建模不确定性的来源。

Method: 采用等质量、准圆轨道、无自旋的双黑洞，初始间距d/M=30，在完整广义相对论下对环双星盘（circumbinary disk, CBD）吸积进行MHD模拟，演化约56个轨道（t≈52,000 GM/c³），使CBD腔内磁场动力学充分弛豫。

Result: 约23个轨道后、在持续的磁囚禁小盘（minidisks）出现之后，吸积进入新的DH-MAD模式：视界无量纲磁通饱和于φ_BH∼20并驱动磁通爆发，腔内垂向磁通饱和但腔-CBD边界无磁通爆发。总质量吸积率变率呈f∼1.8 f_orb周期性，单黑洞吸积率调制约为轨道周期（f∼0.9 f_orb），φ_BH在f∼0.4 f_orb处呈稳健周期性；DH-MAD阶段小盘变得间歇，即便在d/M=24时亦然，可能源于光学薄腔内的高等离子体磁化强度。

Conclusion: DH-MAD代表一种新的磁囚禁吸积状态；视界附近磁重联产生的准周期非热辐射，可能成为旋近SMBBH的“确凿”电磁对应体信号。

Abstract: Reliable first-principles predictions of multimessenger signals from inspiralling supermassive binary black holes (SMBBHs) require magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) simulations in full general relativity (GR) that resolve the accretion flow down to the black hole horizons. However, performing such simulations long enough to relax the magnetohydrodynamics near the BBHs is computationally challenging and a source of uncertainty in modeling efforts. In this work, we present the longest MHD simulation of circumbinary disk (CBD) accretion onto a SMBBH in full GR. The equal-mass, quasicircular, nonspining BBH starts at an initial separation of $d/M=30$ and evolves over $\sim56$ orbits ($t \simeq 52,000 \, GM/c^3$), relaxing the magnetic field dynamics within the CBD cavity. After $\sim 23$ orbits and persistent magnetically arrested minidisks, the accretion enters a new magnetically arrested mode which we call dual horizon-MAD (DH-MAD). In DH-MAD, the dimensionless horizon magnetic fluxes remain saturated at $φ_{BH}\sim 20$ driving magnetic flux eruptions from the vicinity of the BH horizons, and the vertical magnetic flux in the cavity saturates, but no flux eruptions occur at the cavity-CBD boundary. During this DH-MAD phase, there is also a shift in the total mass accretion rate variability, which exhibits a periodicity of $f\sim 1.8 f_{\rm orb}$, while the accretion rate modulation on the individual black holes occurs almost at the orbital period, $f\sim 0.9 f_{\rm orb}$. $φ_{BH}$ also exhibits a robust periodicity at $f\sim 0.4 f_{\rm orb}$, suggesting that quasiperiodic nonthermal emission from magnetic reconnection near the horizons could be a smoking gun electromagnetic counterpart to inspiraling SMBBHs. Finally, minidisks around the BHs become intermittent in the DH-MAD phase, even at a separation of $d/M = 24$, likely due to the large plasma magnetization in the optically thin cavity.

</details>


### [96] [Sound of Keplerian binaries in a homogeneous Newtonian fluid background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29183)
*Yan Cao,Mu-Chun Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文在线性微扰理论下，将嵌入均匀牛顿流体背景的开普勒双星视为局域周期质量源，通过诺特流（Noether currents）计算声波辐射带走的能流、线动量流与角动量流，并给出任意质量比圆轨道与偏心轨道的显式流量公式，进而讨论紫外行为、源有限尺寸正则化、偏心率演化及量子流体与脉冲星观测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究通常从局部尾迹力（local wake force）入手处理双星在介质中的耗散，本文希望改用整体通量视角，以统一处理声波辐射的能量、动量与角动量输运，并厘清点源在非色散流体中的紫外（ultraviolet, UV）发散问题及其物理正则化。

Method: 在线性微扰理论（linear perturbation theory）框架下，把双星作为局域周期质量源（localized periodic mass source），由流体微扰的诺特流计算时间平均通量；对经典无粘正压牛顿流体（inviscid barotropic Newtonian fluid）保留密度微扰自引力并采用金斯判据（Jeans prescription），推导显式通量公式；用均匀球模型刻画有限源尺寸，并分析薛定谔-泊松量子流体（Schrödinger-Poisson quantum fluid）情形。

Result: 得到圆轨道与偏心轨道、任意质量比的能流、线动量流与角动量流公式；发现非色散流体中谐波求和仅在至少一个成分变为超音速（supersonic）时对截断敏感，有限源尺寸可消除该发散并等效为有效谐波截断；偏心率长期演化在流体自引力足够弱的亚音速（subsonic）区表现为圆化（circularization），在此区域外可变号，在严格超音速紫外极限下偏心率增长。量子压力使高频贡献软化，使点源通量在非相对论区紫外有限，数值结果表明所探参数空间内声辐射导致偏心率增长。

Conclusion: 该通量表述统一刻画了双星声波辐射耗散及其紫外行为，并可由两颗脉冲星（binary pulsars）的轨道衰减测量反推其周围均匀量子流体密度的量级上限。

Abstract: We formulate the dissipation due to sound-wave emission from a Keplerian binary embedded in a homogeneous Newtonian fluid background at rest in the binary center-of-mass frame within linear perturbation theory. Rather than starting from the local wake force, we treat the binary as a localized periodic mass source and compute the sound-wave fluxes carried away by the medium. The time-averaged energy, linear-momentum, and angular-momentum fluxes are obtained from Noether currents of the fluid perturbations. For a classical inviscid barotropic Newtonian fluid, retaining the self-gravity of density perturbations while adopting the Jeans prescription, we derive explicit flux formulas for circular and eccentric binaries with arbitrary mass ratio. This formulation also clarifies the ultraviolet behavior of point sources: in a nondispersive fluid, the harmonic sum becomes cutoff-sensitive precisely when at least one component becomes supersonic. We examine explicitly how finite source size removes this divergence using a uniform-sphere model and relate the resulting regularization to a corresponding effective harmonic cutoff. The associated secular eccentricity evolution exhibits circularization in the subsonic regime when the fluid self-gravity is sufficiently weak but can change sign outside this regime, with eccentricity growth in the strictly supersonic UV limit. For a Schrödinger-Poisson quantum fluid, quantum pressure instead softens the high-frequency contribution and renders the point-source fluxes UV finite within the nonrelativistic regime; our numerical results further indicate eccentricity growth due to sound radiation over the parameter space explored. Finally, we translate orbital-decay measurements of two binary pulsars into order-of-magnitude bounds on the homogeneous quantum-fluid density surrounding each system.

</details>
