### [97] [A simple counterexample to the Hohenberg-Kohn theorem for excited states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28296)
*Dominic Shillingford*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文在一维单位环面上构造了非相互作用费米子激发态的Hohenberg–Kohn定理反例：存在Lamé型势使第一激发态密度为常数，且势到密度的映射不唯一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hohenberg–Kohn定理（Hohenberg–Kohn theorem）的激发态推广是否成立存疑，需明确势（potential）与粒子密度（particle density）映射在一维环面激发态情形下是否单射。

Method: 采用半显式构造（semi-explicit construction）：对偶数n=2l，取重整化Lamé势 v_*(x)=2mℓ²sn²(ℓx;m)；再通过势的平移（translation）生成一参数族，并在谱非共振假设（spectral nonresonance assumption）下推广至奇数n≥3。

Result: 所得体系具有简单的第一激发能（simple first excited energy）且在环面上为常数的第一激发态密度；平移产生的一参数势族均给出同一常数密度，故势到密度映射非单射；n=2时与平面弹性线（elastica）分类问题相关联。

Conclusion: 在一维单位环面的非相互作用费米子体系中，激发态Hohenberg–Kohn定理不成立，存在势到密度映射的非唯一性（nonuniqueness）；并由此引出弹性线分类及相关开放问题。

Abstract: For an even number of non-interacting fermions on the one-dimensional unit torus, a semi-explicit counterexample to the Hohenberg--Kohn theorem for excited states is exhibited. Indeed, for each $n = 2l$, it is shown that there exist constants $m \in (0, 1)$ and $\ell > 0$ such that an $n$-body system with the rescaled Lamé potential $v_*(x) = 2m \ell^2 \operatorname{sn}^2(\ell x; m)$ has a simple first excited energy and a first excited-state particle density which is constant on the torus. By translating this potential, we exhibit a non-trivial one-parameter family of potentials, all of which have a constant first excited-state density. As such, the potential-to-density map is not one-to-one in this setting. Under a spectral nonresonance assumption, we obtain the same nonuniqueness at a simple excited energy for odd $n \geq 3$. We also explore connections to the classification problem of elastica in the plane when $n = 2$, and outline some related questions of interest.

</details>


### [98] [Effective Hamiltonians and asymptotic band coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28328)
*Guillaume Bal,Paul Cazeaux,Daniel Massatt,Solomon Quinn*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用多尺度渐近展开（multiple-scale asymptotic expansion），为一类具有慢空间变化的紧束缚薛定谔方程（tight-binding Schrödinger equations）推导有效的宏观模型，并允许谱局域点（K;E）处于简并情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧束缚模型在大尺度、长时间下的有效宏观行为缺乏统一处理，尤其当未微扰色散关系上的谱局域点为简并时，多能带在频率K处取同一能量E，需要刻画简并子空间与其他能带间的耦合。

Method: 采用多尺度渐近展开（multiple-scale asymptotic expansion），针对谱局域于未微扰紧束缚色散关系上一点（K;E）的波包（wave packet），在相关简并子空间（degenerate subspace）上构造有效哈密顿量（effective Hamiltonian），从而量化简并能带与其他能带之间的耦合。

Result: 所得有效模型给出紧束缚动力学的长时间近似（long-time approximation），在渐近参数下可达到任意给定阶精度；该框架允许点（K;E）为简并，即多个（但不必然全部）能带在频率K处达到能量E。文中并以若干应用与数值模拟（numerical simulations）说明理论。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一类可处理简并谱点的紧束缚方程有效宏观建模框架，兼顾任意阶精度与能带耦合刻画，并通过应用实例与数值模拟验证其有效性。

Abstract: By a multiple-scale asymptotic expansion, we derive effective macroscopic models for a large class of tight-binding Schrödinger equations with slow spatial variations. For wave packets that are spectrally localized to some point $(K;E) \in \mathbb{R}^d \times \mathbb{R}$ in the unperturbed tight-binding dispersion relation, our effective models produce long-time approximations of the tight-binding dynamics, accurate to any prescribed order in the asymptotic parameter. Our framework allows the point $(K;E)$ to be degenerate, meaning that multiple (but not necessarily all) energy bands of the tight-binding Hamiltonian can attain the value $E$ at the frequency $K$. We construct an effective Hamiltonian on the relevant degenerate subspace, which quantifies the coupling between the degenerate and other bands. Several applications and numerical simulations illustrate the theory.

</details>


### [99] [On a class of affine Temperley-Lieb modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28389)
*Yacine Ikhlef*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明仿射Temperley-Lieb模（affine Temperley-Lieb modules）在“图解”张量积下构成拟张量（quasitensor，即幺正编织）范畴；当参数q非单位根时，由可约标准模的单商生成的无穷子范畴为半单ribbon范畴，并给出其单模与一对U_q(sl_2)最高权模等价的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于为仿射Temperley-Lieb模赋予范畴化结构，借助新引入的图解张量积考察其张量范畴性质，并探索与量子群U_q(sl_2)表示之间的对应关系。

Method: 方法上，全文以最近提出的“图解”张量积（diagrammatic tensor product）装备仿射Temperley-Lieb模类，证明其满足拟张量（monoidal braided）范畴公理；在q非单位根条件下，取可约标准模的单商（simple quotients）所生成的无穷子范畴，论证其半单ribbon范畴性质，并结合融合规则（fusion rules）与结合映射（associativity maps）分析单模结构。

Result: 主要结果为：仿射Temperley-Lieb模类构成拟张量范畴；q非单位根时相应无穷子范畴是半单ribbon范畴；并给出证据表明该子范畴中每个单模在融合规则与结合映射意义下等价于一对U_q(sl_2)最高权模（highest-weight modules）。

Conclusion: 结论是仿射Temperley-Lieb模具备良好的编织张量范畴结构，其半单子范畴展现出ribbon范畴特征，并暗示与U_q(sl_2)表示理论存在深层对应；关于单模等价性的论断目前仅为证据性支持，尚待严格证明。

Abstract: We prove that the class of all affine Temperley-Lieb modules, equipped with the recently introduced "diagrammatic" tensor product, forms a quasitensor (i.e. monoidal braided) category. When the Temperley-Lieb parameter $q$ is not a root of unity, we consider the infinite subcategory generated by the simple quotients of reducible standard modules, and we argue that it is a semi-simple ribbon category. We present some evidence that, under the fusion rules and the associativity maps, each simple module in this subcategory is equivalent to a pair of $U_q(sl_2)$ heighest-weight modules.

</details>


### [100] [Integrable Orbital Exchange between Ellipsoidally Deformed $p$, $d$, and $f$ Electron States: Two-Angle Sklyanin Representations and Fused Elliptic $R$-Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28444)
*Vladimir Mikhalev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文由椭球形变形的 $p$、$d$、$f$ 多重态出发构造可积轨道交换模型（integrable orbital exchange models），借助 Sklyanin 代数的双角实现给出 Jacobi 差分算子、完整形变谱与正定内积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有可积交换模型多基于自旋自由度，需将交换相互作用推广到具有形变的轨道角动量多重态，以描述角向投影与关联的变化。

Method: 采用 Sklyanin 代数（Sklyanin algebra）的双角实现，构造显式 Jacobi 差分算子 $D,L_i$ 及特征函数，并将 Sklyanin 积分内积迁移为厄米内积；通过椭圆融合（elliptic fusion）在角向生成元上得到显式 $R$-矩阵，由 Yang–Baxter 关系导出交换荷与交换哈密顿量。

Result: 得到齐次链、交替链及同中心角向自由度间混合对相互作用的交换哈密顿量；交换过程改变角向投影与关联，但保持每个组分的两个局域 Casimir（角动量平方与形变加旋转动能不变量）。

Conclusion: $p$ 扇区的 $p$–$p$ 模型可退化为 Fateev 的椭圆自旋一相互作用，并在有理各向同性极限下还原为 Takhtajan–Babujian 哈密顿量，验证了该框架的一致性。

Abstract: We construct integrable orbital exchange models from ellipsoidally deformed $p$, $d$, and $f$ multiplets. A two-angle realization of the Sklyanin algebra gives explicit Jacobi difference operators $D,L_i$, complete distortion spectra and eigenfunctions, and a positive inner product transported from Sklyanin's integral. In the $p$ sector the coordinate functions carry an exact spin-one action, and applying $D$ componentwise maps the confocal ellipsoid to a sphere. Elliptic fusion gives explicit $R$-matrices in the angular generators on both factors. Their Yang--Baxter relations yield commuting charges and exchange Hamiltonians for homogeneous and alternating chains, as well as mixed-pair interactions that can act between angular degrees of freedom on the same center. Exchange changes angular projections and correlations while preserving the two local Casimirs of each constituent, which fix its angular-momentum-squared and distortion-plus-rotation kinetic-energy invariants. The $p$--$p$ model recovers Fateev's elliptic spin-one interaction and reduces to the Takhtajan--Babujian Hamiltonian in the rational isotropic limit.

</details>


### [101] [An Elasticity Complex for Kirchhoff--Love Shells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28480)
*Hang Yin,Albert Chern*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于线性化Gauss–Codazzi方程作为壳应变的相容性条件，构造了Kirchhoff–Love壳理论的弹性复形（elasticity complex），并揭示其与刚体运动层、Airy势表示、自然边界条件及Hodge–Morrey–Friedrichs分解的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 壳理论中应变相容性条件（compatibility conditions）与应力平衡表示缺乏统一的结构性框架；作者希望以复形（complex）语言系统刻画Kirchhoff–Love壳的运动学与动力学结构。

Method: 以线性化Gauss–Codazzi方程作为壳应变的相容性条件，构造壳弹性复形（shell complex），并借助层上同调（sheaf cohomology）分析其正合性，进一步推导平衡应力场的Airy势（Airy potential）表示与自然边界条件。

Result: 所得壳复形构成无穷小刚体运动层（sheaf of infinitesimal rigid motions）的精细消解（fine resolution），其上同调群由相应层上同调决定；同时得到平衡应力场的Airy势表示，并识别出运动学与动力学变量的自然边界条件。

Conclusion: 对带边界的壳，建立了应变场与应力场的Hodge–Morrey–Friedrichs分解（Hodge–Morrey–Friedrichs decomposition），为壳理论提供了统一的复形与分解框架。

Abstract: We develop an elasticity complex for Kirchhoff--Love shell theory, based on the linearized Gauss--Codazzi equations as compatibility conditions for shell strains. The resulting shell complex forms a fine resolution of the sheaf of infinitesimal rigid motions, so its cohomology groups are determined by the corresponding sheaf cohomology. Remarkably, the complex also reveals an Airy potential representation of equilibrium stress fields. The framework further identifies natural boundary conditions for both kinematic and dynamic variables. Finally, we establish a Hodge--Morrey--Friedrichs decomposition for strain and stress fields on shells with boundary.

</details>


### [102] [Feshbach resonances of the second order in a Friedrichs model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28743)
*Dmitry S. Grudko,Alexander K. Motovilov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该摘要研究矩阵 Friedrichs 模型（matrix Friedrichs model）的扩展版本，指出当耦合常数（coupling constant）增大时，嵌入连续谱（continuous spectrum）的一对特征值先变为一阶共振（first-order resonances），随后合并为单个二阶共振（second-order resonance），并给出该合并事件的条件及传递函数（transfer function）算子根的特殊结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画可产生二阶 Feshbach 共振（Feshbach resonances of the second order）的模型机制，并确定一阶共振合并为二阶共振的条件。

Method: 采用共振的算子解释（operator interpretation of resonances），在矩阵 Friedrichs 模型框架下将耦合常数作为控制参数，分析嵌入连续谱的特征值演化及传递函数的算子根结构。

Result: 随着耦合常数增大，未微扰哈密顿量（unperturbed Hamiltonian）嵌入连续谱的一对特征值先转变为两个一阶共振，之后二者合并为一个二阶共振；摘要给出了该事件发生的固定条件，并发现传递函数的算子根具有非常特殊（very particular）的结构。

Conclusion: 矩阵 Friedrichs 模型的扩展形式能够实现二阶 Feshbach 共振，其形成源于一对一阶共振在特定条件下的合并；共振的算子解释可揭示传递函数算子根的特殊结构。

Abstract: We consider an extended version of the matrix Friedrichs model that may produce Feshbach resonances of the second order. In this model, under enlarging of the coupling constant, a pair of eigenvalues of the unperturbed Hamiltonian embedded into the continuous spectrum first transforms in resonances of the first order. At some moment later on, these two resonances merge into a single resonance but of the second order, and we fix conditions for this event. Being based on the operator interpretation of resonances, we found that, in the case under consideration, the operator roots of the transfer function have a very particular structure.

</details>


### [103] [Pauli-consistent ensemble Monte Carlo method for bipolar charge transport in graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28773)
*Tigran Zalinyan,Giovanni Nastasi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种满足泡利不相容原理（Pauli exclusion principle）的系综蒙特卡洛（ensemble Monte Carlo）方法，用于石墨烯（graphene）中双极电荷输运（bipolar charge transport）模拟，并通过与确定性间断伽辽金（discontinuous Galerkin）解对比验证其正确性与实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯双极输运涉及电子-空穴产生与复合等带间过程，需在保持泡利约束与电荷守恒的前提下进行微观模拟；直接模拟高占据价带代价高昂，故需发展兼顾物理一致性与计算可行性的数值方法。

Method: 采用价空位（valence-vacancy）表示描述耦合的电子与空穴布居，避免显式模拟高占据价带；显式处理声子辅助带间散射（phonon-assisted interband scattering），包括电子-空穴产生与复合；在自由飞行（free flight）与散射过程中均保持离散占据界限并守恒净电荷。通过敏感性分析、网格细化研究及与同一动理学模型的独立确定性间断伽辽金解对比进行评估。

Result: 两种方法在时间演化、载流子分布及稳态输运可观测量（transport observables）上在所考察的输运区间内表现出很好的一致性；敏感性与细化研究进一步刻画了该方法的数值特性。

Conclusion: 结果验证了该泡利一致系综蒙特卡洛方法用于石墨烯双极输运微观模拟的计算实用性。

Abstract: We develop a Pauli-consistent ensemble Monte Carlo method for bipolar charge transport in graphene, with an explicit treatment of phonon-assisted interband scattering, including electron-hole generation and recombination. The method employs a valence-vacancy representation to describe coupled electron and hole populations without explicitly simulating the highly populated valence band. It preserves the discrete occupation bounds during both free flight and scattering while conserving net charge. The numerical properties of the method are assessed through sensitivity and refinement studies and comparison with an independent deterministic discontinuous Galerkin solution of the same kinetic model. The two approaches show very good agreement for the time evolution, carrier distributions, and steady-state transport observables across the investigated transport regimes. The results also demonstrate the computational practicality of the method for microscopic simulations of bipolar graphene transport.

</details>


### [104] [Trigonal Elliptic Coordinates on the Sphere and Some Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28992)
*Vladimir Mikhalev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个适配八个三角方向（trigonal directions）的单位球面 theta 函数参数化（theta-function parametrization），并给出其在 Jahn–Teller 模型几何中的应用，包括二阶与三阶实球谐函数（real spherical harmonics）的紧凑表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是获得与三角对称方向对齐的球面坐标系，以便在 O'Brien 约化 Jahn–Teller 模型（reduced Jahn–Teller model）等具有三重对称性的几何问题中简化计算，并联系三分量 Volterra 链（three-component Volterra chain）等可积结构。

Method: 基于 Bobek 讨论的三次 theta 恒等式（cubic theta identity）构造参数化，并给出 Jacobi 椭圆函数（Jacobi elliptic functions）等价表示；所导出的角参数与 theta nome 构成非正交坐标系，其笛卡尔分量平方与三分量 Volterra 链相关联；通过保持 nome 的角的代数变换得到带显式度规系数与对角 Laplace–Beltrami 算子的正交坐标。

Result: 两种参数化均被应用于 O'Brien 约化 Jahn–Teller 模型的几何，给出二阶与三阶实球谐函数的紧凑形式；其中正交角对应 \(E_g\) 四极二重态（quadrupolar doublet）的极角，而某个八极谐波（octupolar harmonic）在每个卦限内仅依赖于 nome。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了 theta 函数参数化、可积链结构与 Jahn–Teller 球谐函数几何之间的显式联系，所提供的正交坐标与紧凑谐波表达式可用于相关对称性问题的解析处理。

Abstract: We construct a theta-function parametrization of the unit sphere adapted to the eight trigonal directions. The construction follows from a cubic theta identity discussed by Bobek and admits an equivalent representation in Jacobi elliptic functions. The angular parameter and theta nome give a nonorthogonal coordinate system whose squared Cartesian components are related to the three-component Volterra chain. An algebraic change of angle, retaining the nome, yields orthogonal coordinates with explicit metric coefficients and a diagonal Laplace--Beltrami operator. We apply both parametrizations to the geometry of O'Brien's reduced Jahn--Teller model and give compact expressions for real spherical harmonics of degrees two and three. The orthogonal angle is the polar angle of an $E_g$ quadrupolar doublet, while one octupolar harmonic depends only on the nome within each octant.

</details>


### [105] [Spin-geodesic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29005)
*Renato Feres,Zijie Hu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究黎曼流形上的一类几何动力系统——自旋-测地系统（spin-geodesic systems），证明其等价于抽象几何中的无滑滚动系统，并给出任意对称自旋耦合场下流保持刘维尔体积的定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是刻画测地运动与内部经典自旋耦合所产生的几何动力系统，并厘清其与标架流（frame flows）、磁测地流（magnetic geodesic flows）及非完整滚动系统（nonholonomic rolling systems）之间的联系。

Method: 通过在黎曼流形上引入对称自旋耦合场（spin-coupling field）B，构造自旋与质点运动耦合的动力学系统，并将其与由B决定的度量联络（metric connection）及抽象环境几何中的无滑滚动（no-slip rolling）相对应。

Result: 证明每个自旋-测地系统均可等价解释为抽象环境几何中的无滑滚动系统；当B对应的度量联络平坦时，在通常的可积性假设下退化为欧氏超曲面上的普通滚动。此外证明刘维尔定理（Liouville theorem）：对任意对称B，流保持力学相空间上的标准刘维尔体积（Liouville volume）。

Conclusion: 自旋-测地系统可视为测地运动的B依赖形变，其保持刘维尔体积的性质在流非辛（non-symplectic）的情形下（B不恒为零）依然成立，揭示了该类系统的几何与测度结构特征。

Abstract: We introduce and study a class of geometric dynamical systems on Riemannian manifolds called spin-geodesic systems, in which the motion of a point is coupled to an internal classical spin through a symmetric endomorphism field $B$, the spin-coupling field. These systems may be viewed as $B$-dependent deformations of geodesic motion and have connections with frame flows, magnetic geodesic flows, and nonholonomic rolling systems. We show that every spin-geodesic system admits an equivalent interpretation as a no-slip rolling system in an abstract ambient geometry; when the associated metric connection, determined by $B$, is flat, this construction reduces, under the usual integrability hypotheses, to ordinary rolling on a Euclidean hypersurface. Our second main result is a Liouville theorem: for an arbitrary symmetric spin-coupling field B, the spin-geodesic flow preserves the canonical Liouville volume on its mechanical phase space. This holds even though the flow is not symplectic unless $B$ is identically zero.

</details>


### [106] [The marginal sine-Gordon field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29024)
*Dyuman Bhattacharya*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文在 β_*^2=8π 处，用具有最近邻动能、正余弦活度与零 Dirichlet 数据的非紧致实格点场构造二维 sine-Gordon 真空，并证明其质量标度、非高斯性与若干 Ward/迹/接触恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是在渐近自由（asymptotically free）耦合点给出二维 sine-Gordon 模型的严格构造，使质量、真空唯一性与共形/平移等对称性及关联函数性质在同一框架下得到控制。

Method: 采用非紧致实格点场（noncompact real lattice field），取最近邻动能、正余弦活度与零 Dirichlet 数据，通过流离开弱耦合（weak coupling）的长度尺度 a_exit 刻画标度，并利用固定方块内对光滑背景的规定响应确定足够细截断下的裸参数。

Result: 质量 M 为真空之上能谱下边缘，且在紧致正端点区间上满足 0<c_-≤M a_exit≤c_+<∞；连续极限中真空唯一，物理伸缩（dilation）实现任意正质量，字面导数生成反射 Hilbert 空间；导数涂抹的方差与指数矩排除高斯律（Gaussian law），某个有限的偶数阶连通关联在分离支集上非零。

Conclusion: 该构造给出指定有限非负高次谐波、场方程、拓扑流（topological current）与几何应力张量及其 Ward、迹与接触恒等式，以及带显式有限尺度假设的归一化源转移定理；从而为该模型提供超出高斯性的普适性结论。

Abstract: At $β_*^2=8π$ we construct the two-dimensional sine--Gordon vacuum from a noncompact real lattice field with nearest-neighbor kinetic energy, positive cosine activity and zero Dirichlet data. Its mass $M$ is the lower edge of the energy spectrum above the vacuum. In units of the length $a_{\rm exit}$ at which the flow leaves weak coupling it obeys $0<c_-\le M a_{\rm exit}\le c_+<\infty$ on compact positive endpoint intervals, with uniform constants. A prescribed response to a smooth background in a fixed square determines the nonlinear asymptotically free bare parameters at each sufficiently fine cutoff. In the local relativistic continuum theory the vacuum is unique, physical dilation realizes every positive mass, and literal derivatives generate the reflected Hilbert space even for finite composite and contact families. Variance and exponential moments of a derivative smear rule out a Gaussian law. A connected correlation of some finite even order above two is nonzero on separated supports. The construction gives universality for the specified finite nonnegative higher harmonics, the field equation, topological current and geometric stress tensor with their Ward, trace and contact identities, and a normalized source-transfer theorem with explicit finite-scale hypotheses.

</details>


### [107] [Path Integrals on Functional Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29045)
*Alexey A. Kryukov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 提出“泛函格点（functional lattice）”方案：时空保持连续、场在离散完备局域函数族上展开，以有限维截断逼近路径积分，并在自由与相互作用情形证明极限收敛且与格点选择无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子场论（quantum field theory）中的路径积分（path integral）通常依赖有限维近似，但无限维极限的存在性及对正则化方案（regulator）的独立性需单独论证。

Method: 时空保持连续，将场按离散完备局域函数族展开并截断到有限维泛函子空间，从而得到普通有限维积分而保持连续微分算子不变；用 Galerkin 论证证明协方差算子与归一化生成泛函的收敛，并讨论 Minkowski 构造（固定 Feynman 正则化 $iε$ 后取 $ε\downarrow0$ 的分布极限）及相互作用理论逐阶收敛条件。

Result: 对有质量自由欧氏标量场，有限维协方差与生成泛函收敛，且极限与相容泛函格点无关（二次型域内稠密子空间序列给出相同连续极限）；相互作用情形在固定微扰阶下，若有限分辨率图被积函数逐点收敛且存在与分辨率无关的可积控制函数则收敛。数值上一维 $φ^4$ 理论趋近已知一、二阶质量间隙系数，四维计算再现预期质量发散与波函数对数重整化。

Conclusion: 泛函格点为路径积分提供了一种连续空间方法，在所考察情形下具有数学上可控的极限。

Abstract: Path integrals in quantum field theory are commonly approached through finite-dimensional approximations, but existence and regulator independence of the infinite-dimensional limit require separate analysis. We introduce a scheme in which spacetime remains continuous and the field is expanded in a discrete complete family of localized functions, called a functional lattice. Truncation to finite-dimensional functional subspaces yields ordinary finite-dimensional integrals while leaving the continuum differential operator unchanged. For the massive free Euclidean scalar field, we prove convergence of the finite-dimensional covariance operators and normalized generating functionals by a Galerkin argument. The limit is independent of the compatible functional lattice: any sequence of finite-dimensional subspaces dense in the quadratic-form domain converges to the same continuum covariance and generating functional. A direct Minkowski construction is obtained at fixed Feynman regulator $iε$, followed by the distributional limit $ε\downarrow0$. For interacting theories, each truncation gives an ordinary non-Gaussian integral. At fixed perturbative order, convergence follows when the finite-resolution graph integrands converge pointwise and admit an integrable majorant independent of the functional resolution. Numerical results for one-dimensional $φ^4$ theory approach the known first- and second-order mass-gap coefficients, while four-dimensional calculations exhibit the expected mass divergence and logarithmic wave-function renormalization. Thus functional lattices provide a continuum-space approach to path integrals with mathematically controlled limits in the cases considered here.

</details>


### [108] [A Novel Algebraic Approach to Medium-Range Solvable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29351)
*Yuan Miao,Kohei Fukai*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 将琼斯（Jones）的Baxterisation方法推广至中程相互作用量子自旋链，利用建立在相邻两格点生成元上的扩展Yang-Baxter方程构造可积模型，并以3-着色色代数（chromatic algebra）给出具体解，得到四个可解模型，其中两个具有非可逆对称性（non-invertible symmetries）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原Jones的Baxterisation可直接由代数生成元产生Yang-Baxter方程的解，但限于特定构造；本文旨在将其推广到具有中程相互作用（medium-range interactions）的量子自旋链，以拓展可积模型的构造范围。

Method: 核心是提出针对由相邻两格点生成元构成的R矩阵的扩展Yang-Baxter方程（extended Yang-Baxter equation），配合相应的Lax算子，使转移矩阵（transfer matrices）在边界项意义下对合；并以3-着色色代数的生成元给出非差分形式（non-difference form）的解，进而导出两族中程格点哈密顿量，再借助色代数与融合范畴（fusion category）Rep(S₃)的关系构造四个表示。

Result: 构造出四个中程可解模型：重现了折叠XXZ模型（folded XXZ model）与硬棒变形XXZ模型（hard-rod deformed XXZ model），并给出三态反铁磁体（three-state antiferromagnet）与Rydberg阻塞梯子（Rydberg-blockade ladder）的新中程模型；四个模型中有两个具备非可逆对称性。

Conclusion: 该推广表明，代数的每个表示均可对应一个中程可解模型；色代数与Rep(S₃)的联系为系统构造此类模型提供了途径，并揭示了非可逆对称性在中程可积模型中的出现。

Abstract: We extend Jones' Baxterisation, which produces solutions to the Yang-Baxter equation directly from the generators of an algebra, to quantum spin chains with medium-range interactions. The key ingredient is an extended Yang-Baxter equation for R matrices that are built from the generators attached to two neighbouring sites. Together with the accompanying Lax operator, it gives rise to transfer matrices that are in involution up to boundary terms, exactly as in Jones' construction. We find a solution to the extended Yang-Baxter equation, of non-difference form, in terms of the generators of the chromatic algebra of 3-colouring, and obtain from it two families of medium-range lattice Hamiltonians. Thus, each representation of the algebra provides a medium-range solvable model. Using the relation between the chromatic algebra of 3-colouring and the fusion category $\mathrm{Rep}(S_3)$, we construct four such representations. They reproduce the folded XXZ model and the hard-rod deformed XXZ model, and give new medium-range models of a three-state antiferromagnet and of a Rydberg-blockade ladder. Two of the four models possess non-invertible symmetries.

</details>


### [109] [A thermodynamically consistent variational formulation and structure-preserving finite element methods for non-isothermal liquid--vapor flows with moving contact lines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29390)
*Junkai Wang,Ting Xiao,Qiaolin He*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文基于动态范德华（dynamic van der Waals）理论与表面热力学，通过广义哈密顿原理（generalized Hamilton's principle）推导非等温液-汽流动的变分形式，并构造两个保结构（structure-preserving）全离散有限元格式，严格证明其质量守恒、密度有界、温度正性、精确总能量平衡及加权的弱熵不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对带移动接触线（moving contact lines）的非等温液-汽两相流动，需要同时刻画梯度贡献与表面热力学的热力学相容数值方法，而现有格式难以保证离散层面的能量与熵结构。

Method: 从非平衡热力学的广义哈密顿原理出发推导控制方程与流体动力学边界条件，分别以内能（internal energy）与亥姆霍兹自由能（Helmholtz free energy）建立两套等价变分形式，熵密度与温度分别作为主要热变量；借助相容空间算子与离散热力学链式法则构造两个热力学相容的全离散有限元格式。

Result: 对两个全离散格式严格证明了质量守恒、数密度（number density）的界保持、温度正性、含外部功与热交换的精确总能量平衡，以及具有非负体相与边界熵产的温度加权弱熵不等式（weak entropy inequality）；二维与三维数值实验验证了精度与结构保持性，并应用于非等温液滴润湿、池沸腾（pool boiling）与液滴喷射。

Conclusion: 所提变分框架与保结构有限元格式在离散层面保持能量与熵结构，适用于带移动接触线的非等温液-汽流动模拟，并为相关相变与界面热力学问题提供可靠数值工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a variational formulation and structure-preserving finite element methods for non-isothermal liquid--vapor flows with moving contact lines. Based on the dynamic van der Waals theory with gradient contributions and surface thermodynamics, the governing equations and hydrodynamic boundary conditions are derived from a generalized Hamilton's principle for nonequilibrium thermodynamics. Two equivalent variational formulations based on internal energy and Helmholtz free energy are developed, using entropy density and temperature, respectively, as the primary thermal variable. Using the resulting weak forms, compatible spatial operators and discrete thermodynamic chain rules, we construct two thermodynamically consistent fully discrete finite element schemes. We rigorously prove the following properties for both fully discrete schemes: mass conservation, bound preservation for the number density, positivity of the temperature, an exact total energy balance incorporating external work and heat exchange, and a temperature-weighted weak entropy inequality with nonnegative bulk and boundary entropy production. Numerical experiments in two and three dimensions assess accuracy and structure preservation, with applications to non-isothermal droplet wetting, pool boiling and droplet ejection.

</details>


### [110] [Reconstruction and geometry of $4\times4$ quantum Sudoku](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29420)
*Fabio Deelan Cunden,Paolo Facchi,Roberto La Scala,Marilena Ligabò,Karol Życzkowski*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究 \(4\times 4\) 量子数独（quantum Sudoku）的重构与几何，给出两个相对区块可补全的充要条件、唯一性及显式重构公式，并用五个闭正方形参数化酉等价类，其商空间为含恰好两个经典类的闭圆盘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典数独推广到量子框架，把网格行、列、块视为秩一投影（rank-one projections）且分解恒等算子，从而探讨其重构性质与几何结构。

Method: 用矩阵表示对网格建模，借助量子逻辑（quantum logic）语言推导重构公式；以十六个重叠量刻画酉等价类（unitary equivalence class），并以五个闭正方形进行参数化，进而对经典格对称性取商。

Result: 得到补全的充要条件、唯一性与显式重构公式；酉等价类由十六个重叠确定，非零重叠数等于基数；商空间为闭圆盘且恰含两个经典类；在第一个块基下测量得到分数数独（fractional Sudoku），保持完整酉类且为至多四个经典网格的凸组合。

Conclusion: 测量映射将五方形模型嵌入一个七维多面体（seven-dimensional polytope），其顶点为十二个经典数独，从而建立量子与经典数独之间的几何联系。

Abstract: We study the reconstruction and geometry of $4 \times 4$ quantum Sudoku, viewed as arrays of rank-one projections whose rows, columns and blocks decompose the identity. We give necessary and sufficient conditions for two prescribed opposite blocks to admit a completion, prove its uniqueness, and obtain explicit reconstruction formulas, also expressed in the language of quantum logic. The sixteen overlaps between these blocks determine the unitary equivalence class of the grid, while the number of nonzero overlaps equals its cardinality. Using a matrix representation, we parametrise the equivalence classes by five closed squares, with the sides of one square identified with the diagonals of the other four. The quotient by classical cell symmetries is a closed disc containing exactly two classical classes. Finally, measurement in the basis of the first block gives a fractional Sudoku that retains the full unitary class and is a convex combination of at most four classical grids. This measurement map embeds the five-square model into a seven-dimensional polytope whose vertices are the twelve classical Sudoku.

</details>


### [111] [Analytic continuation of the T- and S-matrices to unphysical sheets in a multichannel extension of the Friedrichs-Faddeev model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29441)
*Mikhail V. Kremenetsky,Alexander K. Motovilov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将 Friedrichs-Faddeev 模型推广到矩阵多通道情形，给出核条件以保证 T-矩阵与 S-矩阵可解析延拓至非物理能量叶，并用物理叶上的矩阵自身表示其解析延拓，从而将共振刻画为该截断散射矩阵的零特征值点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 Friedrichs-Faddeev 模型的矩阵多通道推广，需要明确势矩阵核的条件如何保障散射矩阵的可解析延拓性，并给出非物理叶上共振的判据。

Method: 对势矩阵的核施加一组条件，据此证明 T-矩阵（T-matrix）及 S-矩阵（S-matrix）可解析延拓到非物理能量叶（unphysical energy sheets）；并推导其在非物理叶上由物理叶（physical sheet）内同名矩阵显式表示的表达式。

Result: 获得非物理叶上解析延拓 T-矩阵与 S-矩阵的显式表示；由此可知，某一非物理叶上的共振（resonances）恰为物理叶内相应散射矩阵截断（truncation）取零特征值（eigenvalue 0）的点。

Conclusion: 研究表明，矩阵多通道 Friedrichs-Faddeev 模型中非物理叶上的共振可完全由物理叶内散射矩阵的谱性质（零特征值）刻画，为共振分析提供了显式判据。

Abstract: We consider a matrix multichannel extension of the Friedrichs-Faddeev model. First, we formulate conditions on the kernels of the potential matrix that guarantee for the T-matrix (and then for the S-matrix) the existence of analytical continuation to unphysical energy sheets. We also obtain explicit representations for the analytically continued T- and S-matrices on unphysical sheets in terms of these matrices themselves, but only taken in the physical sheet. From the explicit representations obtained, it follows that the resonances on a certain unphysical sheet are nothing but the points at which the corresponding truncation of the scattering matrix in the physical sheet has an eigenvalue of 0.

</details>


### [112] [$μ$- and $q$-extension of special functions in quantum field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29543)
*J. Blümlein,A. M. Gavrilik,U. Y. Lunha,O. Mykhailiv,C. Schneider*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本工作系统构建了微扰量子场论中单尺度特数量（special numbers and functions）的 μ-扩展与 q-扩展，指出两类扩展性质差异显著：μ-扩展基本保持函数类而 q-扩展产生新的高超越函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微扰量子场论（perturbative quantum field theory）中解析计算归结为特殊字母表上的迭代积分（iterated integrals）及对应的嵌套和（nested sums）；在引入修正（反）对易关系（modified (anti)commutation relations）的 μ-与 q-扩展量子场论中，预期会出现相应的扩展特殊函数，因此需要系统构造这些函数。

Method: 以特殊字母表上的迭代积分为统一框架，涵盖多重对数（polylogarithms）、Nielsen 积分、线性分母项迭代积分、分圆字母（cyclotomic letters）、二次型诱导字母及平方根值字母（square-root valued letters）；并借助逆 Mellin 变换（inverse Mellin transform）联系调和和（harmonic sums）、广义调和和、二次型嵌套和、分圆调和和及含中心二项式的嵌套和；在此基础上构造其 μ-扩展与 q-扩展。

Result: μ-扩展特殊函数与对应的 q-扩展函数差异显著：μ-扩展呈现以多项式 μ-参数刻画的类保持性（class-preservation），仅根值字母（root-valued letters）情形例外；q-扩展则产生新的高超越函数（higher transcendental functions）。

Conclusion: 所有相关特殊函数均可统一地作 μ-与 q-扩展，且两类扩展在结构性质上本质不同；这些扩展函数预期出现在 μ-与 q-扩展量子场论的圈图计算（loop calculations）中。

Abstract: Analytic calculations up to single-scale quantities in perturbative quantum field theory result into special numbers and functions, which are generalizations as iterative integrals over special alphabets. In this way one obtains polylogarithms, Nielsen integrals, the iterated integrals over linear denominator terms, cyclotomic letters, letters induced by quadratic forms, and square-root valued letters. The inverse Mellin transform relates special nested sums to these quantities, which are harmonic sums, generalized harmonic sums, nested sums implied by quadratic forms, cyclotomic harmonic sums, and nested sums containing central binomials. We construct the $μ$- and $q$-extensions of all these special functions. They are expected to occur in loop calculations within $μ$- and $q$-extended quantum field theories via modified (anti)commutation relations. The $μ$-extended special functions are quite different form the corresponding $q$-extended functions. While in the former case one observes class-preservation characterized by the polynomial $μ$-parameter, except for the case of root-valued letters. In the latter case new higher transcendental functions are obtained.

</details>


### [113] [Interface currents of magnetized differential systems: quantization and bulk-interface correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29719)
*Guillaume Bal*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究由任意阶自伴微分系统（self-adjoint differential systems）描述的两绝缘体界面上的输运，证明界面电流可表为 Fredholm 指数，并在椭圆情形及冻结条件下满足体-界面对应（bulk-interface correspondence）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有体-边界对应多针对椭圆型象征（elliptic symbol），而旋转浅水、冷等离子体、Maxwell 方程与赝自旋一材料等体系的象征具常秩核，存在大动量处有界的平带，需要将椭圆性推广为 Douglis-Nirenberg 结构与有效平坦象征上的能隙条件。

Method: 对耦合磁场（Peierls 替换或在因子 L 中耦合）的任意阶系统 H，在椭圆情形对全部壁面、非椭圆情形在冻结条件下，将界面电流 σ_I=Tr i[H,P]φ'(H) 与两侧体量 J(a_±,B_±;α) 及壁面积分项 C 相联系；非零体场下 J 由推广 Landau 能级的延拓修正计数计算，零场下 J 为无场象征的 Chern 积分。

Result: 对远离平带集且位于两侧体能隙内的能量 α，界面电流良定义且 2πσ_I 为 Fredholm 指数，并满足 2πσ_I=J(a_+,B_+;α)−J(a_-,B_-;α)+C；该电流在大扰动下稳定，均匀材料内部外场边界携带量子化电流且在场诱导相变处改变。

Conclusion: 结果将体-界面对应推广至非椭圆象征体系；在平带能量附近广义 Landau 能级可能累积，界面电流可能发散，表明该对应在该极限下失效。

Abstract: This paper concerns transport along an interface between two insulators modeled by self-adjoint differential systems $H$ of arbitrary order, coupled to a magnetic field either by Peierls substitution or, for operators of the form $L^*L+W$, in the factor $L$. Each bulk carries a constant field, which may vanish. The principal symbol of $H$ is either elliptic or has a kernel of constant rank, in which case finitely many bands remain bounded at large momentum, as in rotating shallow water, cold plasmas, Maxwell's equations and pseudospin-one materials. Ellipticity is then replaced by a Douglis-Nirenberg structure and by a gap condition on an effective flat symbol. We show that, for every energy $α$ away from the flat-band set and in a spectral gap of both bulks, the interface current $σ_I=\mathrm{Tr}\,i[H,P]\varphi'(H)$ is well defined, with $2πσ_I$ a Fredholm index, and that it is given, for all walls in the elliptic case and under a freezing condition otherwise, by a bulk-interface correspondence of the form $2πσ_I=J(a_+,B_+;α)-J(a_-,B_-;α)+\mathsf{C}$. For a nonzero bulk field, $J=2πσ_H$ is the Kubo-Chern Hall conductivity, which we compute by an extension-corrected count of generalized Landau levels. For a vanishing field, $J$ is the Chern integral of the field-free symbol, which is also, under a weak-field condition, the weak-field limit of the Hall conductivity. The term $\mathsf{C}$ is a Chern integral of the occupied projector at infinite momentum across the wall. The current is stable under large perturbations, and boundaries of the applied field inside a homogeneous material carry quantized currents that change at field-induced transitions. Near flat-band energies, where generalized Landau levels may accumulate, the current may diverge.

</details>


### [114] [Characterizing Ultrafast Processes via Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29778)
*Bernard Derrida,Michael Entov,Leonid Polterovich,Lenya Ryzhik*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究平均场系统（mean-field system）在“瞬时参数跃迁—弛豫—反向跃迁”循环中的功分布，核心结论是：当两平衡宏观态在热力学极限下重合（matching regime）时，总功呈非高斯极限分布（non-Gaussian limiting distribution）且人均功趋于零；否则重标度后的功涨落为高斯型，人均功收敛到严格正常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关注的动机是：在某些系统与参数取值下，循环前后两个平衡宏观态在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）中相互重合，这一现象需要借助循环所需做功（work required）来刻画。进一步的动机来自热力学的接触几何模型（contact-geometric model of thermodynamics），其中“重合”对应两个平衡勒让德子流形（Legendre submanifolds）之间的 Reeb 弦（Reeb chord）。

Method: 研究方法为：考察初始处于热力学平衡的平均场系统（mean-field system）的一个循环过程——瞬时参数跃迁、弛豫至新平衡、反向跃迁；并在适当假设下，聚焦标量多粒子平均场系统（scalar many-particle mean-field systems），分析该循环总功的极限分布与人均功的渐近行为。

Result: 主要结果（main result）：在重合情形（matching regime）下，总功具有非高斯极限分布（non-Gaussian limiting distribution），且人均功（average work per particle）趋于零；在非重合情形下，总功经适当重标度后的涨落（fluctuations）服从高斯极限分布（Gaussian limiting distribution），人均功收敛到严格为正的常数。

Conclusion: 结论表明，循环功可作为区分平衡宏观态是否重合的判据：重合与非重合两种情形在极限分布类型（非高斯/高斯）与人均功的渐近值（零/正常数）上呈现质的不同。该结果亦与接触几何（contact geometry）中勒让德子流形间的 Reeb 弦存在性相对应。

Abstract: Consider the following cycle for a mean-field system initially in thermodynamic equilibrium: an instantaneous parameter jump, relaxation to the new equilibrium, and a reverse jump. For certain systems and parameter values, the two equilibrium macrostates coincide in the thermodynamic limit. We characterize this matching phenomenon through the work required to perform the cycle. Our main result concerns scalar many-particle mean-field systems under suitable assumptions. In the matching regime, the total work has a non-Gaussian limiting distribution, and the average work per particle tends to zero. Away from matching, the properly rescaled fluctuations of the total work have a Gaussian limiting distribution, and the average work per particle converges to a strictly positive constant. Further motivation arises from a contact-geometric model of thermodynamics, in which matching corresponds to a Reeb chord between two equilibrium Legendrian submanifolds.

</details>


### [115] [Modulation equations and coherence dynamics in a qubit chain coupled to a circulating fluxon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29827)
*O. M. Kiselev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文推导了循环通量子（fluxon）与端点与场耦合的交换耦合量子比特链（exchange-coupled qubit chain）联合量子振幅的调制方程，并用后验误差界与数值算例验证了低阶近似的高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画场源剖面（source profile）与场记忆（field memory）如何影响链内相干性，需要一套能分离慢变振幅演化的方程体系。

Method: 构建酉通过算子（unitary passage operator）统一描述平移波包、真空修饰（vacuum dressing）与离散振荡模式；采用共振正规形（resonant normal form）把慢振幅演化与非共振修正分离，并计算有序发射与返回振幅产生的二阶修正及下一基带（ground-band）系数。

Result: 在速度选择的共振（velocity-selected resonance）下，81次通过的直接传播给出约6×10⁻⁸的模贡献；立方基带近似与展开的完整计算在测试观测量上吻合到1.5×10⁻⁹。二阶修正改变内部量子比特的布居数与相干性，下一基带系数对累积误差给出对角与非对角补偿项。

Conclusion: 所得调制方程可描述源剖面与场记忆对链内相干性的影响，并能对状态误差给出后验界定，低阶基带近似在测试条件下精度很高。

Abstract: We derive modulation equations for the joint quantum amplitudes of a circulating fluxon and an exchange-coupled qubit chain whose endpoint interacts with the field. A unitary passage operator combines the translational packet, vacuum dressing, and discrete oscillatory modes. A resonant normal form separates the slow amplitude evolution from nonresonant corrections. Ordered emission and return amplitudes generate a second-order correction that changes the population and coherence of an internal qubit. The next ground-band coefficient gives compensating diagonal and near-resonant off-diagonal contributions to the accumulated error. We bound the state error a posteriori. At a velocity-selected resonance, direct propagation over 81 passages gives a mode contribution of order $6\times10^{-8}$; the cubic ground-band approximation agrees with an expanded full calculation to $1.5\times10^{-9}$ in the tested observables. The equations describe how the source profile and field memory affect coherence inside the chain.

</details>


### [116] [Frobenius $2$-algebras and $R$-matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29898)
*Hibiki Shibuya,Yuji Terashima*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文引入 Frobenius 2-代数（Frobenius 2-algebra）这一 Frobenius 代数的范畴化推广，证明每个 Frobenius 2-代数在其底层对象自同态的幺半范畴（monoidal category）中给出满足 Yang-Baxter 方程（Yang-Baxter equation）的 R-矩阵，并借助三个例子说明该构造，从而将 Kashaev 的通用 R-矩阵构造范畴化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在为 R-矩阵与 Yang-Baxter 方程提供更高范畴层级的统一来源，特别是给出 Kashaev 通用 R-矩阵构造的范畴化推广，因此需要引入 Frobenius 代数的 2-范畴版本作为出发点。

Method: 引入 Frobenius 2-代数（Frobenius 2-algebras）作为 Frobenius 代数的范畴推广，并证明其可在底层对象自同态的幺半范畴中构造出满足 Yang-Baxter 方程的 R-矩阵；随后以三个例子（编织幺半范畴中的对象对、可逆对极的 Hopf 代数、有限维 Hopf 代数 Heisenberg 双重的表示）加以说明。

Result: 每个 Frobenius 2-代数均确定一个满足 Yang-Baxter 方程的 R-矩阵；第三个例子中重新得到 Kashaev 的通用 R-矩阵（universal R-matrix）构造，表明新构造是其范畴化推广。

Conclusion: 该构造为 Kashaev 的通用 R-矩阵构造提供了范畴层面的推广，并通过多个典型例子验证了其适用性与一致性。

Abstract: In this paper, introducing Frobenius $2$-algebras, which are categorical generalizations of Frobenius algebras, we show that every Frobenius $2$-algebra determines an $R$-matrix satisfying the Yang-Baxter equation in the monoidal category of endomorphisms of its underlying object. Moreover, we illustrate this construction through three examples: a pair of objects in a braided monoidal category, a Hopf algebra with invertible antipode, and representations of the Heisenberg double of a finite-dimensional Hopf algebra. In particular, in the third example, we recover Kashaev's construction of the universal $R$-matrix. Thus, our construction provides a categorical generalization of Kashaev's construction.

</details>


### [117] [A decomposition of Hamiltonian matrices with symplectic kernels and its application to Williamson's diagonal form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29967)
*Shaowu Huang,Yingxin Huang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文建立了具有辛核（symplectic kernel）的哈密顿矩阵（Hamiltonian matrices）的一种分解，推广了 Ikramov（2018）关于正定矩阵辛特征值的主要结果，并据此给出具有辛核的半正定矩阵 Williamson 对角形式（Williamson's diagonal form）的另一种证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ikramov（2018）给出了正定矩阵辛特征值相关的主要结果，但该结果所适用的矩阵类别存在进一步扩展的空间；同时 Williamson 对角形式已有证明，寻找新的、更简洁的证明路径具有独立价值。

Method: 核心手段是构造具有辛核的哈密顿矩阵的分解（decomposition），这一分解被视为对 Ikramov 结果的推广；随后将该分解作为工具，应用于半正定矩阵（positive-semidefinite matrices）情形。

Result: 得到了哈密顿矩阵在辛核条件下的分解定理；作为应用，给出了具有辛核的半正定矩阵 Williamson 对角形式的一个替代性证明（another proof）。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于两点：一是通过分解定理推广了已有文献中关于正定矩阵辛特征值的结论；二是表明该分解可作为推导 Williamson 对角形式的有效工具，从而为这一经典结果提供新的证明途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a decomposition of Hamiltonian matrices with symplectic kernels, which extends the main result in [K. D. Ikramov, On the Symplectic Eigenvalues of Positive Definite Matrices. Moscow Univ. Comput. Math. Cybern., 42(2018)1-4]. As an application, we present another proof of Williamson's diagonal form for positive-semidefinite matrices with symplectic kernels.

</details>


### [118] [Derivation of BCS functionals and gap equations from quantum many-body fermionic system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30367)
*Xuwen Chen,Jiahao Wu,Zhifei Zhang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对有限体积内一般色散关系与一般吸引相互作用的三维单组分自旋-1/2费米系统，证明了离散BCS能隙方程正极大解的存在性与非零非负解的唯一性，并由此导出SU(2)对称性的涌现及无穷体积极限下连续BCS泛函的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BCS能隙方程解的存在性与唯一性（well-posedness）是超导/超流理论的基础，但以往结果多依赖具体色散关系或特定相互作用。本文旨在在更一般的色散关系与吸引相互作用下建立严格的适定性结果，并厘清有限系统与无穷系统极限之间的关系。

Method: 在有限盒中考虑离散BCS能隙方程，在由相互作用与系统尺寸共同决定的临界温度T_c以下，构造正极大解；利用正极大解作为比较工具证明非零非负解的唯一性；继而假设准自由性（quasi-freeness）证明有限系统SU(2)对称性；最后取系统尺寸趋于无穷的极限。

Result: 证明了T_c以下离散能隙方程正极大解存在，且任何非零非负解必等于该正极大解；在准自由性假设下证明有限系统SU(2)对称性涌现；并在无穷体积极限下得到连续BCS泛函与能隙方程及其非零非负解的唯一性，同时证明临界温度的收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作表明上述费米系统在[0,T_c)区间存在正能量（因而质量）间隙；能隙方程的任何非零非负解均来自其所描述的体多体系统；并给出态密度（density of states）中Cooper发散从有限系统到无穷系统的过渡。

Abstract: We consider 3D single-species spin-$1/2$ Fermi systems with general dispersion relation and general attractive interaction initially inside a finite box. Up to some critical temperature $T_c$ determined by the interaction and system size, we establish, for the expected discrete BCS gap equation, existence of positive maximal solution. We then prove the uniqueness of nonzero (not identically zero) nonnegative solution. That is, any nonzero nonnegative solution, if exist or obtained via other methods, must equal to the positive maximal solution. Building on such a well-posedness result and assuming quasi-freeness, we prove the emergence of the $SU(2)$ symmetry of the finite system. Finally, letting the system size tend to infinity, we prove the emergence of the continuous BCS functional and gap equation for which we also have the uniqueness of nonzero nonnegative solution, and the convergence of the critical temperature. In particular, our work also implies there is a positive energy (hence mass) gap for aforementioned Fermi systems for $[0,T_c)$, any nonzero nonnegative solution for the gap equations are from the many-body system it suppose to describe, and the Cooper divergence of the density of states from a finite to an infinite system.

</details>


### [119] [Quantum walks and conformal mappings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30403)
*Fedor Kuyanov,Michael Drmota,Mikhail Skopenkov,Alexey Ustinov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一维电子运动的最基本离散模型（Feynman checkers、量子行走或具特殊复权重的六顶点模型），给出波函数在整个 xt 平面上的一致渐近公式，证明除 Airy 函数主导的振荡—指数衰减过渡外，还存在由 Gamma 函数支配的第二类过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认识主要集中在 Airy 函数描述的振荡区与指数衰减区之间的过渡，而对电子速度接近光速时的行为缺乏统一的渐近刻画。作者旨在为整个 xt 平面提供单一的、一致的渐近描述，并揭示此前未被注意的第二类过渡现象。

Method: 以 Feynman checkers（等同量子行走、特殊复权重六顶点模型）为研究对象，基于其积分表示（integral representations），借助相应的共形映射（conformal mappings），证明两个产生涉及 Airy 函数与 Gamma 函数的一致渐近公式（uniform asymptotic formulae）的一般性定理。

Result: 得到覆盖整个 xt 平面的一致渐近公式：既包含已知的、由 Airy 函数描述振荡区与指数衰减区之间过渡的结果，也识别出当电子速度趋近光速时由 Gamma 函数支配的第二类过渡。所证两个定理具有一般性，可适用于其他若干情形。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Feynman checkers 波函数的渐近分析从单一 Airy 过渡扩展为 Airy 与 Gamma 函数共同构成的统一图景，并给出可迁移至其他模型的一般性渐近定理方法。

Abstract: We study the most elementary discrete model of an electron moving along a line, known as Feynman checkers, quantum walk, or the six-vertex model with special complex weights, and provide a uniform asymptotic formula for the wave function in the whole xt-plane. In addition to the well-known appearance of the Airy function that describes the transition between the oscillating and exponentially decaying regimes, we observe a second transition governed by the gamma function when the electron speed approaches the speed of light. We prove two general theorems producing uniform asymptotic formulae involving the Airy and gamma functions, using conformal mappings associated with the underlying integral representations. They are applicable in several other situations as well.

</details>


### [120] [Uniform Ionization Bounds in Molecular Thomas--Fermi--Dirac--von Weizsäcker Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30418)
*Shuang Wu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究分子 Thomas–Fermi–Dirac–von Weizsäcker（TFDW）理论，证明固定核数 $K$ 时任意质量 $N$ 的极小元满足 $N \\leq Z + C_K$，从而回答了 Frank、Nam 与 Van Den Bosch 在 CPAM 2018 提出的分子大电荷电离问题，并给出电离能的统一上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是解决 Frank、Nam 和 Van Den Bosch（CPAM, 2018）提出的分子大电荷电离问题（molecular large-charge ionization question），即分子在质量增大时的电离上限是否受核电荷控制。

Method: 在分子 Thomas–Fermi–Dirac–von Weizsäcker 理论框架下，对固定核数 $K$ 的极小元（minimizer）进行解析估计，并将 TFDW 极小元的屏蔽势（screened potentials）与电子半径同相应的中性分子 Thomas–Fermi 极小元作比较。

Result: 证明对每个固定核数 $K$，质量 $N$ 的极小元满足 $N \\leq Z + C_K$，其中 $Z$ 为总核电荷、$C_K>0$ 与核电荷及位置无关；在 $N \\geq Z$ 时得到屏蔽势与电子半径的比较结果，并建立电离能（ionization energy）的统一界。

Conclusion: 结果表明分子 TFDW 理论中的电离程度受总核电荷加一个仅依赖核数的常数控制，为正的大电荷电离问题提供了肯定回答，并给出电离能的定量一致界。

Abstract: We study the molecular Thomas--Fermi--Dirac--von Weizsäcker theory. For each fixed number $K$ of nuclei, we prove that every minimizer of mass $N$ satisfies $N\leq Z+C_K$, where $Z$ is the total nuclear charge, and $C_K>0$ is independent of the nuclear charges and positions. This answers the molecular large-charge ionization question posed in [R. L. Frank, P. T. Nam and H. Van Den Bosch, CPAM, 2018]. For $N\geq Z$, we also compare the screened potentials and electronic radii of Thomas--Fermi--Dirac--von Weizsäcker minimizers with those of the corresponding neutral molecular Thomas--Fermi minimizers and establish a uniform bound on the ionization energy.

</details>


### [121] [Non-Perturbative Analysis of Resonances by a Contraction Mapping on Complex Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30425)
*Toshimitsu Takaesu,Tamotsu Teruya*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究抽象 Friedrichs–Lee 哈密顿量（Friedrichs–Lee Hamiltonian）在内嵌本征值（embedded eigenvalue）扰动下的谱性质，通过围道变形（contour deformation）与复域上的压缩映射（contraction mapping）证明共振极点（resonance pole）的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对象为内嵌本征值的扰动问题（perturbation problem of embedded eigenvalues），此类本征值嵌于连续谱中，其扰动后的谱行为难以用常规微扰方法刻画，故需在抽象框架下加以分析。

Method: 采用围道变形（contour deformation）技术，并在复域（complex domains）上构造压缩映射（contraction mapping），据以论证共振极点的存在性。

Result: 摘要指出已证明共振极点（resonance poles）的存在性；摘要未给出具体极点位置、唯一性或稳定性等定量结论。

Conclusion: 该工作为抽象 Friedrichs–Lee 哈密顿量内嵌本征值扰动下的谱性质提供了一个基于复分析方法的共振存在性结果；摘要未涉及进一步推广或应用。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider a perturbation problem of embedded eigenvalues for an abstract Friedrichs--Lee Hamiltonian and analyze its spectral properties. By deforming the contour and constructing a contraction mapping on complex domains, we prove the existence of resonance poles.

</details>


### [122] [For whom the Bell tolls: joint measurements and state updates in quantum field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30468)
*Christopher J. Fewster,Christiane K. M. Klein*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文在Fewster-Verch量子场论测量框架下，针对形如 $A(f_i)^*A(f_i)$ 的可观测对，推导联合矩生成泛函与唯一联合概率分布，并据此构造Proca-标量耦合模型中的Bell不等式检验，证明其可违背CHSH不等式并逼近Tsirelson界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 态更新规则（state update rules）及其在Bell检验中的行为是理解测量方案与量子信息的关键；需检验Fewster-Verch框架的态更新是否能容纳强量子关联，并厘清态更新与可得信息之间的关系。

Method: 在玻色Fock空间上具有良好表示的假设下，利用测量结果概率分布与态更新的联系，对由形式厄米、Green双曲型微分算子诱导的场论中相互对易的正规算子 $A(f_i)^*A(f_i)$ 导出联合矩生成泛函（joint moment generating functional）与唯一联合概率分布；并以近期提出的耦合Proca-标量模型（coupled Proca-scalar model）构造极化测量的Bell检验模型。

Result: 所构造的Bell检验模型可违背CHSH不等式，且在真空效应被完全排除的极限下、对最大纠缠态逼近Tsirelson界（Tsirelson's bound）。

Conclusion: Fewster-Verch框架导出的态更新规则与强量子关联相容，并支持将态更新理解为相对于可得信息（available information）而言的观点。

Abstract: State update rules and their behaviour in Bell tests play an important role in the study of measurement schemes and quantum information. We consider the state updates of the Fewster-Verch measurement framework for quantum field theory using their connection to the probability distribution of measurement outcomes. We focus on a set of observables of the form $A(f_i)^*A(f_i)$, where $A(f)$ is the (complex) smeared field operator for any theory induced by a formally hermitian, Green hyperbolic differential operator, and where the $(A(f_i))_{i=1, \dots, m}$ are assumed to be normal and mutually commuting. Under the assumption that these operators have a sufficiently nice representation on a bosonic Fock space, we derive the joint moment generating functional and the unique joint probability distribution for the observables $(A(f_i)^*A(f_i))_{i=1,\dots,m}$. As an application of these results, we construct a model of a Bell inequality test within quantum field theory using a recently developed coupled Proca-scalar model for polarization measurements. We show that our Bell test model can violate the CHSH inequality, approaching Tsirelson's bound for maximally entangled states in the limit where vacuum effects are perfectly rejected. These results show that the state update rules derived in the Fewster-Verch framework are compatible with strongly quantum correlations. They also support a viewpoint in which state updates should be understood in relation to available information.

</details>


### [123] [Density of states for random Schrödinger operators with linearly correlated heavy-tailed disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30529)
*Margherita Disertori,Luca Fresta,Liza Schonlau*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文利用积分分部超对称表示（integration-by-parts supersymmetric representation），研究线性相关重尾无序（linearly correlated heavy-tailed disorder）下随机薛定谔算子（random Schrödinger operators）的态密度（density of states），证明强无序区（strong-disorder regime）下态密度的光滑性与Wegner估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画重尾且线性相关无序下随机薛定谔算子的态密度性质，并特别关注混合符号相关核（mixed-sign correlation kernels），将其作为分析平均格林函数平方（averaged squared Green's functions）中出现的双曲结构（hyperbolic structures）的玩具模型（toy model）。

Method: 采用积分分部超对称表示（integration-by-parts supersymmetric representation）作为核心技术工具，在强无序区（strong-disorder regime）证明态密度（density of states）的光滑性并建立Wegner估计。该方法适用于广泛的相關核（correlation kernels）与无序分布（disorder distributions），包括α稳定律（α-stable laws）。

Result: 在强无序区（strong-disorder regime）下，证明了态密度（density of states）的光滑性（smoothness）与Wegner估计；结果覆盖广泛的相關核（correlation kernels）与无序分布（disorder distributions），其中包括α稳定律（α-stable laws）。

Conclusion: 该工作表明积分分部超对称表示（integration-by-parts supersymmetric representation）可有效处理线性相关重尾无序（linearly correlated heavy-tailed disorder）情形，并通过对混合符号相关核（mixed-sign correlation kernels）的考察，为平均格林函数平方（averaged squared Green's functions）分析中的双曲结构（hyperbolic structures）提供可借鉴的模型框架。

Abstract: We study the density of states of random Schrödinger operators with linearly correlated heavy-tailed disorder. Using an integration-by-parts supersymmetric representation, we prove smoothness of the density of states and Wegner estimates in the strong-disorder regime. We are particularly interested in mixed-sign correlation kernels, which serve as a toy model for the hyperbolic structures arising in the analysis of averaged squared Green's functions. Our approach applies to a broad class of correlation kernels and disorder distributions, including $α$-stable laws.

</details>


### [124] [Reproducing kernel and matrix representation of the deformed quantum optical density operator of $\mathcal{R}(p,q)-$super-coherent states on $\mathcal{B}_0\oplus\mathcal{B}_1$ fermionic quantum algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30576)
*Kawèyim Lankpetre,Isiaka Aremua,Joseph Désiré Bukweli Kyemba,Bienvenu Gnim Adewi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究基于Hounkonnou–Bukweli-Kyemba的$\mathcal{R}(p,q)$-形变量子代数的超相干态（super-coherent states），在四扇区分解下建立密度算符的对角表示、再生核与Berezin量子化，并证明其为智能态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为推广$\mathcal{R}(p,q)$-形变代数框架至含两个Grassmann变量的超对称情形，需刻画其超Fock空间上的相干态结构及相应密度算符表示。

Method: 构造含Grassmann变量$(\zeta_1,\zeta_2)$的$\mathcal{R}(p,q)$-超相干态；利用$\mathcal{R}(p,q)$-导数、角积分与Berezin积分，在玻色、$\phi_1$-费米、$\phi_2$-费米与双费米四扇区分别求密度算符对角表示，并引入扇区参数函数统一表述。

Result: 得到四个扇区贡献之和的密度算符显式表达（可视为四扇区混合）；定义扇区再生核，计算密度算符在超相干态基下的矩阵元，并证明核的厄米性、正定性与矩阵乘法性质及其对角值的级数展开；给出联系密度算符、密度函数与再生核的积分表述，以及玻色扇区中$z,\bar z,|z|^2,z^2,\bar z^2$的Berezin量子化算符与均值。

Conclusion: 超位置与超动量算符的色散关系表明该类超相干态为智能态（intelligent states），验证了该形变超代数框架下相干态性质的良好可控性。

Abstract: We study the $\mathcal{R}(p,q)-$super-coherent states with two Grassmann variables $(ζ_1,ζ_2)$ built on the superalgebra $\mathcal{B}_0\oplus\mathcal{B}_1$ associated with the $\mathcal{R}(p,q)-$deformed quantum algebras of Hounkonnou and Bukweli-Kyemba, the two dilation parameters $φ_1(p,q)$ and $φ_2(p,q)$ being meromorphic functions of two variables evaluated at $(p,q)$. The graded super-Fock space splits into four sectors: bosonic, $φ_1-$fermionic, $φ_2-$fermionic and bi-fermionic. In each of them we establish the diagonal representation of the density operator by means of the $\mathcal{R}(p,q)-$derivative, angular integration and Berezin integration, and we give a unified compact form in terms of sectorial parametric functions. The total density operator is obtained in explicit form as the sum of the four sectorial contributions, and can be read as a mixture of the four sectors. We define the sectorial reproducing kernels, obtained from the resolution of the identity, we compute the matrix elements of the density operator in the super-coherent state basis, and we prove the hermiticity, positivity and matrix multiplication properties of the kernels, together with the series expansion of their diagonal values. An integral formulation relates the density operator, its density function and the reproducing kernel, the latter being the function associated with the identity operator. Berezin quantization in the bosonic sector yields the quantized operators associated with $z$, $\bar z$, $|z|^2$, $z^2$ and $\bar z^2$ and their mean values; the dispersions of the super-position and super-momentum operators show that the super-coherent states are intelligent states.

</details>


### [125] [Gaudin's determinantal norm formula and Hall--Littlewood polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30987)
*Jan Felipe van Diejen*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对与圆上 q-玻色子（q-bosons）Lieb–Liniger 模型离散化所导出的离散测度正交的 Hall–Littlewood 多项式（Hall–Littlewood polynomials），证明了此前被猜想成立的二次范数（quadratic norms）Gaudin 型行列式公式（Gaudin-type determinantal formula）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hall–Littlewood 多项式的二次范数此前仅以猜想形式存在 Gaudin 型行列式表达，缺乏证明；该文旨在填补这一缺口。

Method: 以圆上 q-玻色子 Lieb–Liniger 模型离散化所产生的离散正交测度（discrete orthogonal measure）为出发点，对二次范数建立并证明 Gaudin 型行列式公式。

Result: 证实了先前猜想的 Gaudin 型行列式公式，给出 Hall–Littlewood 多项式二次范数的行列式表达。

Conclusion: 该结果将 Hall–Littlewood 多项式的正交性理论与 Lieb–Liniger/q-玻色子离散模型相联系，并解决了相关猜想。

Abstract: The Hall--Littlewood polynomials are orthogonal with respect to a discrete measure stemming from a discretization of the Lieb--Liniger model that describes $q$-bosons on the circle. This note establishes a previously conjectured Gaudin-type determinantal formula for their quadratic norms.

</details>


### [126] [Symmetries, conservation laws, series solutions and singularity formation for the spherically symmetric Euler-Poisson equations in mass coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31005)
*Adnan Shamaoon*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: No AI summary available.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: No AI summary available.

Method: No AI summary available.

Result: No AI summary available.

Conclusion: No AI summary available.

Abstract: Written in Lagrangian mass coordinates, the spherically symmetric Euler-Poisson equations for a polytropic gas become a single second-order equation for the radius $r(t,m)$ of the shell enclosing mass $m$, with adiabatic index $γ>1$ and entropy profile $S(m)$. We determine all point symmetries of this equation. Apart from time translation there is at most one scaling, except at the critical index $γ=4/3$, where a scaling exists for every entropy profile; these scalings are the entropy-fixed stabilisers of the two-parameter scaling group of the non-isentropic system, and we give the exact symmetry defect when $γ\neq4/3$. Noether's theorem singles out the profiles $S=Km^{14-10γ}$, for which a scaling is variational and gives a conservation law beyond energy; otherwise the scalings yield virial identities. We construct exact collapse solutions and reduce the self-similar problem to one ordinary differential equation. Using the Briot-Bouquet theorem we prove that the regular centre of self-similar and homologous collapse carries a convergent analytic expansion with free central amplitude, and we derive a Puiseux expansion at the stellar surface whose exponent is the polytropic index $1/(γ-1)$. In mass coordinates a density singularity is a zero of $r_m$ or of $r$; for the first kind we prove a Hopf-type fold law and an exact shell-crossing criterion for free-falling dust. Finally, linear perturbations of the critical Einstein-de Sitter collapse separate exactly into spherical Bessel profiles, with exponents solving $3κ^2+κ-2+μ=0$. All identities were checked by computer algebra, and the numerical statements rest on convergence studies of a second-order Lagrangian scheme, including a collapse with a free surface.

</details>

### [127] [Stability of a Paraboloidal Steady Solution for the 2D Surface Growth Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29076)
*Won-Suk Lee*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究二维表面生长模型（2D surface growth model）的稳定性问题，证明抛物面稳态解（paraboloidal steady solution）的小扰动在 L∞(R²) 中衰减到零，扰动及其梯度的衰减率分别为 O(t^{-1/2}) 与 O(t^{-1}log t)，并在初始数据与梯度于 L¹ 和 Wiener 代数（Wiener algebra）中足够小的条件下给出稳定性充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注二维表面生长模型的稳定性问题，即抛物面稳态解在小扰动下是否保持稳定并随时间衰减，这是刻画该模型长期动力学行为的基础问题。

Method: 证明基于对扰动方程（perturbation equation）的线性化（linearization），并采用自举论证（bootstrap argument）将线性结论推广至非线性方程；同时论证扰动方程温和解（mild solution）的存在唯一性。

Result: 证明了扰动及其梯度均在 L∞(R²) 中衰减到零，衰减率分别为 O(t^{-1/2}) 和 O(t^{-1}log t)；稳定性的充分条件要求初始扰动及其梯度在 L¹(R²) 与 Wiener 代数 A 中足够小，而无需对二阶导数（second derivative）作小性假设。

Conclusion: 稳定性仅需初始数据及其梯度的小性条件，二阶导数的小性并非必要，这与大二阶导数导致高表面能、并预期在短时间内被松弛的物理直觉一致；此外，在上述条件下扰动方程的温和解存在且唯一。

Abstract: In this paper we consider the stability problem of 2D surface growth model. To be specific, we prove that the small perturbation of the paraboloidal steady solution decays to zero in $L^\infty(\mathbb{R}^2)$. The decay occurs both for the perturbation and its gradient, whose decay rates are $O(t^{-1/2})$ and $O(t^{-1}\log t)$, respectively. The proof is based on the linearization of the perturbation equation and the bootstrap argument to close the statement in nonlinear equation.
  Sufficient conditions for stability require the initial data and its gradient of the perturbation to be small in $L^1(\mathbb{R}^2)$ and Wiener algebra $\mathcal{A}$. It is notable that the smallness in second derivative is not necessary, which is physically reasonable because large second derivative would lead to high surface energy and it is expected to be relaxed in short time. We also prove existence and uniqueness of mild solution for perturbation equation under those conditions.

</details>


### [128] [Local and Global Bifurcation for Axisymmetric Stationary Relativistic Vlasov--Maxwell Equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29153)
*Katherine Zhiyuan Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究有界轴对称区域上双物种相对论 Vlasov–Maxwell 平衡态的分岔（bifurcation），应（对应）给出基于速度矩与椭圆谱界的可计算判据及分支方向公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对相对论 Vlasov–Maxwell 系统中的稳态平衡态，需要判定其何时发生简单分岔（simple bifurcation）并刻画分支结构，此前缺乏可计算的判别工具与分支方向信息。

Method: 在带界轴对称区域（bounded axisymmetric domains）上，利用速度矩（velocity-moment）界与椭圆谱界（elliptic spectral bounds）构造简单交叉的可计算检验，给出交叉数目与间隔估计；再由局部定理给出分支方向公式，并构造显式双物种族进行验证。

Result: 所得判据可计算地检验简单交叉及其数目与分离度；显式双物种族展现出仅电场（electric-only）、仅磁场（magnetic-only）以及联合场范数逃逸（joint field-norm escape）三类行为。

Conclusion: 该框架将谱估计与局部分岔理论结合，为相对论 Vlasov–Maxwell 平衡态的分岔分析提供可计算判据，并揭示场型依赖的多种逃逸/分岔模式。

Abstract: We study bifurcation of stationary two-species relativistic Vlasov--Maxwell equilibria on bounded axisymmetric domains. Velocity-moment and elliptic spectral bounds give computable tests for simple crossings, their number and separation, while the local theorem gives a branch-direction formula. Explicit two-species families exhibit electric-only, magnetic-only, and joint field-norm escape.

</details>


### [129] [Split Hamiltonian structure for Vlasov--Maxwell equations and cylindrical pinch equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29164)
*Justin Holmer,Katherine Zhiyuan Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究柱形箍缩（cylindrical pinch）平衡态的线性化 Vlasov–Maxwell 方程谱稳定性，借助动量守恒将 Lin–Strauss 能量原理（energy principle）推广至 Bennett z-箍缩、Gaussian θ-箍缩、刚性旋转 θ-箍缩与螺旋箍缩（screw pinch）等几何，并在对称性类中证明谱位于虚轴或给出不稳定性的方向性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于把已知的 Lin–Strauss 能量原理与谱稳定性判据推广到具有轴向或螺旋对称性的柱形箍缩平衡（如经典 Bennett 箍缩），厘清粒子数约束、磁通约束与电约束对谱的作用。

Method: 沿对称方向的粒子动量守恒被用于把 Lin–Strauss 能量原理引入相应几何；矢势对称分量的选取落在图空间（graph space）上，并结合粒子数约束分离径向方程、分析螺旋谐波。

Result: Bennett z-箍缩的裸势形式有一个负方向，但被粒子数约束消除，全部 z-无关谱位于虚轴；轴向漂移可变时每个 z-无关增长模都是径向的。Gaussian θ-箍缩在显式轴斜率范围内、以及中心峰值刚性旋转 θ-箍缩均证明轴对称谱稳定；螺旋箍缩中螺旋扭曲耦合磁通与粒子、电约束，径向方程仍可分离，所有足够高阶的螺旋谐波谱为虚，两个 Gaussian 螺旋箍缩平衡在全螺旋对称类中验证谱稳定。

Conclusion: 结论表明约束（粒子数、磁通与电约束）在决定谱稳定性中起关键作用，且高螺旋谐波普遍稳定；谱稳定性可在显式参数范围内对多类柱形箍缩平衡建立。

Abstract: We study the linearized Vlasov--Maxwell equations for cylindrical pinch equilibria, including the classical Bennett pinch, Gaussian equilibria from our earlier work, and a rigidly rotating $θ$-pinch. Conservation of particle momentum along a symmetry brings the energy principle of Lin and Strauss into these geometries. We work on a graph space for the symmetry component of the vector potential. For the Bennett $z$-pinch the bare potential form has one negative direction, but the particle-number constraint removes it and the entire $z$-independent spectrum lies on the imaginary axis. For a $z$-pinch with variable axial drift, every $z$-independent growing mode is radial. We prove axisymmetric spectral stability for a Gaussian $θ$-pinch in an explicit range of axis slopes and for a center-peaked, rigidly rotating $θ$-pinch. In a screw-pinch the helical twist couples the magnetic flux to the particle and electric constraints. The radial equations still separate, and all sufficiently high helical harmonics have imaginary spectrum. For two Gaussian screw-pinch equilibria we verify spectral stability in the full helical symmetry class.

</details>

### [130] [$p$-Harmonic gauge and the regularity of spacetime isometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29643)
*Mathias Braun*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了任意 $C^{k,\alpha}$ 类洛伦兹度规局部可用 $C^{k+1,\alpha}$ 的调和时间函数作为坐标，并由此推出等距映射的正则性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典调和坐标依赖波动算子，而波动算子在时间函数上非椭圆，难以直接用于洛伦兹度规的正则性研究，因此需要寻找在时间函数上具椭圆性的替代算子。

Method: 引入一族非线性 $p$-波算子（$p\in(-\infty,1)$），该算子在时间函数上具有椭圆性；构造以关于此算子的调和时间函数为坐标函数的局部坐标系。

Result: 证明每个 $C^{k,\alpha}$（$k\in\mathbf{N}_0$，$\alpha\in(0,1)$）洛伦兹度规均存在 $C^{k+1,\alpha}$ 类局部坐标，且所有坐标函数均为关于该非线性 $p$-波算子的调和时间函数。

Conclusion: 作为应用，证明 $C^{k,\alpha}$ 类洛伦兹度规之间的等距映射（isometry）具有 $C^{k+1,\alpha}$ 正则性，即正则性自动提升一阶。

Abstract: We show every Lorentzian metric of class $C^{k,α}$, where $k\in\mathbf{N}_0$ and $α\in(0,1)$, admits local coordinates of class $C^{k+1,α}$ all of whose coordinate functions are temporal functions that are harmonic with respect to a nonlinear $p$-wave operator, where $p\in(-\infty,1)$. Unlike the classical wave operator, this operator is elliptic on temporal functions. As an application, we show that an isometry between Lorentzian metrics of class $C^{k,α}$ is of class $C^{k+1,α}$.

</details>


### [131] [A note on Kaluza-Klein theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30285)
*Fr{é}d{é}ric H{é}lein*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种卡鲁扎-克莱因理论（Kaluza-Klein theory）的变体：其作用泛函的临界点无需逐点约束（point-wise constraint），在合理假设下即可导出主纤维丛（principal fiber bundle）的存在性及爱因斯坦-杨-米尔斯系统（Einstein-Yang-Mills system）的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者此前工作（ATMP 2020、LMP 2022、Ann. Fac. Sci. Toulouse 2025）中的构型需施加逐点约束，才能由作用量临界点得到主纤维丛与爱因斯坦-杨-米尔斯方程；本文旨在消除这一约束要求，简化理论框架。

Method: 延续基于作用泛函（action functional）的卡鲁扎-克莱因式构造，考察其在逐点约束缺席情形下的临界点，并借助合理假设推导其几何与场论含义。

Result: 在无需施加逐点约束的条件下，临界点仍可在合理假设下对应主纤维丛的存在性与爱因斯坦-杨-米尔斯系统的解；摘要未给出具体定理形式或证明细节。

Conclusion: 该变体表明逐点约束并非必要条件，从而使卡鲁扎-克莱因框架下的统一场构造更为宽松、适用范围更广；进一步的技术细节与证明需参阅全文。

Abstract: We present a variant of the Kaluza-Klein theory that was introduced in previous papers (ATMP, 2020; LMP 2022; Ann. Fac. Sci. Toulouse, 2025), based on an action functional whose critical points, in the presence of point-wise constraints, lead-under reasonable assumptions-to the existence of a principal fiber bundle and solutions to the Einstein-Yang-Mills system. The difference is that it is not necessary to impose a point-wise constraint.

</details>


### [132] [Superpotentials for F-manifolds and bi-flat F-structures on the orbit spaces of reflection groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29736)
*Alessandro Arsie,Paolo Lorenzoni*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: No AI summary available.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: No AI summary available.

Method: No AI summary available.

Result: No AI summary available.

Conclusion: No AI summary available.

Abstract: We extend Dubrovin's construction of a superpotential from Frobenius manifolds to biflat F-manifolds. The key step in this extension is choosing a flat coordinate of one of the connections studied in [11] and invert this coordinate with respect to the spectral parameter and the construction does not require an invariant metric. Suppose that the manifold has dimension n and that the superpotential admits a holomorphic continuation to n simple critical points with distinct critical values. These values give canonical coordinates, and the residue formulas recover both the product and the flat connection from the superpotential alone. Conversely, under suitable nondegeneracy conditions, a solution of the superpotential equations for a given product defines a biflat F-manifold. For $B_n$ and $G(m,1,n)$ we obtain the whole family of biflat structures from a polynomial superpotential by varying a twisted primary differential. For $G(m,m,n)$ we obtain the natural biflat structure from a Laurent superpotential. Keeping the Laurent polynomial fixed and varying the primary differential gives a new family of flat F-manifolds depending on one parameter. We use the superpotential and the poles of the dual product along the reflecting hyperplanes to prove the conjecture we formulated in [8] for irreducible Coxeter groups and in [10] for well generated complex reflection groups. The conjecture concerns biflat F-structures whose dual connection has the form studied by Dunkl and Kohno. When the reflecting hyperplanes form a single orbit, we identify the biflat structure associated with the flat structure of [24,25,27]. We obtain the remaining families from the polynomial construction for $G(m,1,n)$ and an explicit computation for $F_4$. In every case the first flat structure extends polynomially to the whole quotient, and the biflat structure is defined on the complement of the reflection discriminant.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

### [133] [Rank-Two Attractors and Modularity for a Mirror Bicubic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30234)
*Yibing Chen,Emanuel Scheidegger*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文研究双三次卡拉比-丘三维流形（bicubic Calabi-Yau threefold）镜像族在 $\lambda^6=-8$ 处的 Hodge 结构分裂，证明其在半单化后对应两个模形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示镜像族特殊纤维上四维权三 Hodge 结构的分解性质，并将相应的 $\ell$-adic 伽罗瓦表示（$\ell$-adic Galois representation）与具体模形式（modular form）建立联系。

Method: 采用 Hesse 束纤维积实现（Hesse pencil fiber-product realization）、等变点计数（equivariant point counting）以及 Serre-Faltings-Livne 方法。

Result: $\lambda^6=-8$ 处纤维的四维权三 Hodge 结构分裂为两个有理秩二部分，Hodge 类型分别为 $(1,0,0,1)$ 与 $(0,1,1,0)$，即为秩二吸引子点（rank-two attractor points）；其半单化 $\ell$-adic 伽罗瓦表示分别对应权四新形式（newform）$f_{\mathsf{54.4.a.c}}$ 及权二新形式 $f_{\mathsf{54.2.a.a}}$ 的 Tate 扭转（Tate twist）。

Conclusion: 该结果将双三次三维流形镜像族的秩二吸引子点与 L-函数与模形式数据库（L-functions and modular forms database）中标注的模形式对应起来，为研究其算术性质提供了明确刻画。

Abstract: We study the mirror family $\mathcal X_λ$ of the bicubic Calabi-Yau threefold, obtained from a family of complete intersections of two cubics in $\mathbb P^5$. We show that the four-dimensional weight-three Hodge structure of the fibers over $λ^6 = -8$ splits into two rational rank-two pieces of Hodge types $(1,0,0,1)$ and $(0,1,1,0)$; in particular, these points are rank-two attractor points. Using a Hesse pencil fiber-product realization, equivariant point counting, and the Serre-Faltings-Livne method, we identify their semisimplified $\ell$-adic Galois representations with those attached to the weight-four newform $f_{\mathsf{54.4.a.c}}$ and the Tate twist of the weight-two newform $f_{\mathsf{54.2.a.a}}$, where these labels refer to the L-functions and modular forms database.

</details>


### [134] [Virasoro operators for relative Gromov--Witten theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30566)
*Shuai Guo,Longting Wu,Lei You*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文在光滑射影簇（smooth projective variety）的光滑除子（smooth divisor）背景下构造相对 Virasoro 算子（relative Virasoro operators），并借助边界修正将相对约束与绝对约束联系起来，最终证明 Enriques 曲面（Enriques surfaces）在任意整曲线类上的 Virasoro 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Virasoro 猜想（Virasoro conjecture）对绝对 Gromov–Witten 理论已有表述，而相对情形（relative theory）与退化公式（degeneration formula）下的行为尚缺相应框架；本文旨在建立相对 Virasoro 约束并借此向退化情形与具体曲面推广。

Method: 对 $(X,D)$ 的相对后代势（relative descendant potential）构造相对 Virasoro 算子，其形式为 $(X,D)$ 的对数 Virasoro 算子（logarithmic Virasoro operator）加上仅由 $D$ 及其在 $X$ 中法丛（normal bundle）决定的边界修正项。

Result: 假设 $D$ 满足 Virasoro 猜想，则证明相对约束与 $X$ 的绝对约束等价；在同一假设下，得到与满足完备性条件（completeness condition）的两分量射影半稳定退化（projective semistable degeneration）退化公式的相容性。

Conclusion: 由两分量与粘合除子（gluing divisor）的 Virasoro 猜想可推出一般纤维（general fiber）的猜想；作为应用，证明 Enriques 曲面在每个整曲线类（integral curve class）上的 Virasoro 猜想。

Abstract: Let $D$ be a smooth divisor in a smooth projective variety $X$. We construct relative Virasoro operators acting on the relative descendant potential of $(X,D)$. Each operator is the logarithmic Virasoro operator of $(X,D)$ plus a boundary correction determined only by $D$ and its normal bundle in $X$. Assuming the Virasoro conjecture for $D$, we prove that the relative constraints are equivalent to the absolute constraints for $X$. Under the same assumption, we establish compatibility with the degeneration formula for two-component projective semistable degenerations satisfying a completeness condition. Consequently, the Virasoro conjecture for the two components and the gluing divisor implies the conjecture for the general fiber. As an application, we prove the Virasoro conjecture for Enriques surfaces in every integral curve class.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Geometric foundations of mesoscopic nucleation theory: commutativity of kinetic and probabilistic reduction for the Dean-Kawasaki equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28163)
*Miguel A. Durán-Olivencia*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文研究Dean-Kawasaki型守恒随机偏微分方程的降维问题，提出两条路径（分别从轨迹层面的Dean方程与概率分布层面的Kawasaki方程出发），并证明二者一般不等价，其差异由噪声诱导的漂移修正（anomaly）决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将随机场方程约化为少数自由度是物理、应用数学与工程中的核心问题；已有降维几乎都在轨迹层面（Dean方程）进行，尚未在概率分布层面（Kawasaki方程）开展，因此需要考察两条路径是否一致。

Method: 第一条路径采用Lutsko引入的密度参数化，将其代入场方程并对合适权重积分以得到序参量的随机方程；第二条（新）路径对Kawasaki方程对应的泛函前向Kolmogorov方程沿流形周围的Fermi管状坐标（Fermi tubular coordinates）对快速横向自由度做平均。

Result: 证明两条约化生成元重合当且仅当各自的噪声诱导漂移修正（称为anomalies）相等，而该条件一般不成立；在球域上以相关长度δ正则化噪声时，理想气体的失配按δ^{-1}发散，含局部超额自由能的流体在横向动力学闭合下按δ^{-3}发散（该阶数由闭合决定）。

Conclusion: 尽管Dean方程与Kawasaki方程形式等价，其降维结果并不等价；在相同闭合、正则性假设与归一化ε^2=N^{-1}下，两种约化理论经时间t后的差异上界为O(t/(nδ^3))，n为平均数密度。

Abstract: The reduction of a stochastic field equation to a few degrees of freedom is a central problem in many areas of physics, applied mathematics and engineering. In this work we present two routes for the dimensional reduction of conservative stochastic partial differential equations of Dean-Kawasaki type. To our knowledge, such reductions have always been carried out at the level of the trajectories, i.e. on Dean's equation, and never at the level of the probability distribution, i.e. on Kawasaki's equation. The first route starts from the parametrisation of the density introduced by Lutsko [J. F. Lutsko, J. Chem. Phys. 136, 034509 (2012)], who imposed it on the field equation and obtained stochastic equations for the order parameters by integrating against a suitable weight. The second route, which reduces Kawasaki's equation, is new. This route averages the functional forward Kolmogorov equation over the fast transverse degrees of freedom, using Fermi tubular coordinates around the manifold. Since the equations of Dean and Kawasaki are formally equivalent, one would expect both reductions to be equivalent as well. We show that this is not the case. In particular, we prove that the two reduced generators coincide if and only if the noise-induced drift corrections arising along each route, which we call anomalies, are equal, and that this condition fails in general. For noise regularised over a correlation length $δ$ on a spherical domain, the mismatch diverges as $δ^{-1}$ for the ideal gas and, under a closure of the transverse dynamics, as $δ^{-3}$ for a fluid with a local excess free energy, the latter order being set by the closure. Finally, we show that, under the same closure, regularity hypotheses and the normalisation $\varepsilon^2 = N^{-1}$, the two reduced theories differ after a time $t$ by at most $O(t/nδ^3)$, where $n$ is the mean number density.

</details>


### [136] [Free energy of the bipartite random assignment problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30801)
*Jacob Shkrob*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文在尺度 n^{1/d} 下计算了任意伪维度 d>0、任意逆温度 λ>0 的二部随机指派问题（bipartite random assignment problem）的极限自由能，证明其由 Poisson 加权无限树上的递归分布方程（recursive distributional equation）唯一解给出的严格复制对称公式（replica-symmetric formula）决定，证实了 Aldous 与 Talagrand 的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在解决二部随机指派问题中极限自由能的精确刻画问题，并验证 Aldous 与 Talagrand 此前提出的相关猜想，同时推广至任意伪维度与逆温度的一般情形。

Method: 将问题置于 n^{1/d} 尺度下，利用 Poisson 加权无限树（Poisson-weighted infinite tree）上的递归分布方程（recursive distributional equation）构造复制对称公式（replica-symmetric formula），并证明该方程解的唯一性以确定极限自由能。

Result: 证明极限自由能由该递归分布方程唯一解对应的严格复制对称公式给出，从而证实 Aldous 与 Talagrand 的猜想；由此推出固定逆温度 λ>0 时 Gibbs 指派（Gibbs assignment）代价的指数集中性（exponential concentration）。

Conclusion: 研究给出二部随机指派问题自由能的一般性严格解，并进一步确定近最优指派数量（number of near-optimal assignments）的指数增长率。

Abstract: We compute the limiting free energy of the bipartite random assignment problem for every pseudodimension $d>0$ and every inverse temperature $λ> 0$ at the scale $n^{1/d}$. We show that the limit is given by a rigorous replica-symmetric formula determined by the unique solution of a recursive distributional equation on a Poisson-weighted infinite tree, confirming conjectures of Aldous and Talagrand. As a consequence, for a fixed inverse temperature $λ> 0$, we deduce exponential concentration of the cost of a Gibbs assignment and determine the exponential growth rate of the number of near-optimal assignments.

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [Gaussian Belief Propagation and Periodic Jacobi Matrices on Trees: Spectral Curve, Antibound Poles, and Fixed Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29381)
*Yusuke Watanabe*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该文在研究通用覆盖树（universal covering tree）上周期 Jacobi 算子（periodic Jacobi operator）的对角格林函数（diagonal Green functions），证明其满足基多重图（base multigraph）上的标量高斯信念传播（Gaussian belief propagation）协方差方程，并解决了 Christiansen–Simon–Zinchenko 猜想及 Avni–Breuer–Simon 提出的若干问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在将树覆盖上的格林函数与高斯信念传播方程联系起来，证明 Christiansen、Simon 和 Zinchenko 的猜想，并回答 Avni、Breuer 和 Simon 关于格林函数代数度数与谱间隙参数密度的问题。

Method: 将周期 Jacobi 算子的对角格林函数刻画为有限无环基多重图上的标量高斯信念传播协方差方程的解；借助环秩（cycle rank）、反束缚极点（antibound poles）、Perron 值、游走可和性阈值（walk-summability threshold）与协方差曲线（covariance curve）的不可约性进行分析，并在块模型（block models）中推广，另用 Lean 4 与 Mathlib 对部分标量理论作形式化验证。

Result: 在环秩至少为 2 时，对任意实势与非零实权重，格林函数在 Perron 值处延续为简单反束缚极点，证明上述猜想（正权重时含自环）；无环图上较低极点为游走可和性阈值，其与谱底之间的能量至少有两个正定局部信念不动点。一般实系数下，协方差曲线不可约，其层数等于无边子集数，且对角格林函数、无限半树 m-函数（m-functions）与态密度 Stieltjes 变换（density-of-states Stieltjes transform）均具该度数，回答了 Avni–Breuer–Simon 的问题；带号 Jacobi 矩阵的所有特征值均为反束缚极点，并给出亏格公式（genus formula）。在 K_4 上独立变动系数时覆盖谱保持为区间，反驳了 Avni–Breuer–Simon 关于全间隙打开参数密度的猜想；块模型中度数可超过 L^p。

Conclusion: 对角格林函数与高斯信念传播协方差方程之间的对应为研究周期 Jacobi 算子的谱性质提供了统一框架，解决了多个已有猜想并给出反例，同时通过 Lean 4 形式化验证增强了部分标量理论结果的可信度。

Abstract: Diagonal Green functions of a periodic Jacobi operator on a universal covering tree satisfy the scalar Gaussian belief propagation covariance equations on the finite connected loopless base multigraph. At cycle rank at least two, the Green functions continue to simple antibound poles at both Perron values for arbitrary real potentials and nonzero real weights. This proves the conjecture of Christiansen, Simon and Zinchenko. For positive weights the pole result includes self-loops. On loopless graphs the lower pole is the walk-summability threshold. Each energy strictly between it and the spectral bottom has at least two fixed points with positive definite local beliefs. On a connected simple graph, some coefficients yield a compact component of positive solutions exactly when the graph contains two vertex-disjoint connected subgraphs of cycle rank at least two. The covariance curve is generically irreducible, with one sheet per edge subset with no vertex of degree one. For cycle rank at least two and generic real coefficients, the diagonal Green functions, infinite half-tree $m$-functions and the density-of-states Stieltjes transform all have this degree, answering a question of Avni, Breuer and Simon. Then all eigenvalues of the signed Jacobi matrices are antibound poles. Signed principal submatrices classify finite points with nonzero residue of $(\sum_i V_i) dz$, where $V_i$ are the vertex variances. We give a genus formula. Near zero potentials and unit weights on $K_4$, independently varying the coefficients leaves the cover spectrum an interval. This refutes Avni, Breuer and Simon's conjecture on the density of parameters with all gaps open. In block models, generic irreducibility and a uniqueness criterion extend, and at uniform dimension $p$ the degree can exceed $L^p$ for scalar degree $L$. Part of the scalar theory is formally verified in Lean 4 with Mathlib.

</details>


### [138] [A strict Neumann-Dirichlet eigenvalue inequality on planar domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31134)
*Jonathan Rohleder*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该文证明了在单连通有界平面 Lipschitz 区域（simply connected, bounded planar Lipschitz domains）上，Neumann Laplacian 的第 (k+2) 个特征值严格小于 Dirichlet Laplacian 的第 k 个特征值，从而强化了作者此前已建立的非严格不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者早前工作已证明该特征值不等式成立，但仅为非严格形式；严格性此前只在附加假设下已知，因此需要给出一般情形的严格性证明。

Method: 属于研究简报（note）性质，摘要未具体说明证明技术路线，仅指出通过论证将原有不等式精炼为严格不等式。

Result: 在所设定的区域类上，Neumann 第 (k+2) 个特征值不超过 Dirichlet 第 k 个特征值的结论可加强为恒为严格不等式。

Conclusion: 该特征值比较不等式在单连通有界平面 Lipschitz 区域上普遍取严格形式，去除了以往所需的额外假设。

Abstract: On simply connected, bounded planar Lipschitz domains it is known from our earlier work that the ($k+2$)-nd eigenvalue of the Neumann Laplacian cannot exceed the $k$-th Dirichlet eigenvalue. In this note we sharpen this statement by showing that this inequality is always strict. This was previously only known under additional assumptions.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)
