### [139] [From raw magnetoresistance to reliable cyclotron masses: A practical guide to processing quantum oscillation data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28044)
*Josh Leeman,Sudipta Chatterjee,Max Hirschberger,Leslie M. Schoop*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文指出，量子振荡中基于Lifshitz-Kosevich（LK）公式提取的回旋质量（cyclotron mass）强烈依赖信号处理选择（场窗口、背景扣除、谱加窗），并可通过数值计算与RTe3的Shubnikov-de Haas实验证明这些选择可造成数十百分比的质量偏差与虚假峰移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回旋质量被用于评估关联强度（correlation strength）与拓扑态（topological states）指认，但其提取依赖场窗口、背景扣除方法与傅里叶变换前的谱窗等处理选择，这些选择常凭目视判断，可能改变谱特征并系统性偏置质量，需要定量厘清其影响。

Method: 结合数值计算与稀土三碲化物RTe3（R = Pr, Nd, Gd, Tb）在电荷有序态（charge ordered state）下的Shubnikov-de Haas实验，系统考察三种处理效应：以单一有效场代表有限场区间、多项式背景扣除、谱加窗（spectral tapering）。

Result: （1）用单一有效场代表有限场区间会低估回旋质量；（2）多项式背景扣除会衰减并移动低频峰；（3）谱加窗可减小相邻峰间泄漏（leakage）引起的质量偏差，但以频率分辨率下降为代价。实验示例显示质量随场窗口移动数十个百分点、仅由背景拟合产生表观峰移，且一个原被报告为偏离LK行为的现象可分解为两个相邻频率。

Conclusion: 量子振荡质量提取对信号处理流程高度敏感，需审慎选择并报告处理参数；表观的质量变化或LK行为偏离可能源于分析伪影而非本征物理。

Abstract: Quantum oscillations are an essential probe of the Fermi surface in conductors, and cyclotron masses extracted using the Lifshitz-Kosevich (LK) formula are used to evaluate correlation strength and the assignment of topological states. These masses depend on choices in signal processing, including the field window, method of background subtraction, and spectral window applied before the Fourier transform. These choices are often guided by visual inspection, yet they can alter spectral features and bias the extracted masses. We show that (1) representing a finite field interval by a single effective field can underestimate the cyclotron mass; (2) polynomial background subtraction can attenuate and shift low-frequency peaks; and (3) spectral tapering can reduce mass bias from leakage between neighboring peaks, at the cost of frequency resolution. We demonstrate these effects using numerical calculations and Shubnikov-de Haas experiments on the rare-earth tritellurides RTe3 (R = Pr, Nd, Gd, Tb) with a dense low-frequency spectrum in the charge ordered state. The examples reveal masses shifting by tens of percent with the field window, apparent peak shifts produced only by the background fit, and a reported departure from LK behavior that resolves into two neighboring frequencies.

</details>


### [140] [Symmetry classes of hyperdeterminant wavefunctions: Application to phase transitions between a bosonic fractional Chern insulator and superfluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28276)
*Guan-Lin Lin,Xiaodong Hu,Di Xiao,Ying Ran*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用对称超行列式（hyperdeterminant, Hdet）波函数结合投影对称群（projective symmetry group）分析、变分蒙特卡洛（variational Monte Carlo）与有效理论，研究蜂窝晶格硬核玻色子模型中超流相与分数量子陈绝缘体相的相变，并指出该相变由带四个狄拉克节点（Dirac nodes）的涨落 SU(2)_g 规范场描述，对应 N_f=2 的 QCD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对超流相与分数量子陈绝缘体相之间相变性质的常见认识（通常被视为连续相变）提出质疑，并寻求对相图进行定量研究、改善微观描述的方法。

Method: 在蜂窝晶格硬核玻色子模型中采用对称超行列式（Hdet）波函数，结合投影对称群（PSG）分析与变分蒙特卡洛（variational Monte Carlo）构造并优化不同对称类的 Hdet 态，并借助相应有效理论（effective theories）将 PSG 表示与具体量子相联系起来。

Result: 不同对称类（即投影对称群的表示）对应特定的量子相；相比传统费米子部分子构造（fermionic parton construction），对称 Hdet 波函数显著改善了微观描述，使量子相图的定量研究成为可能。该相变被描述为涨落的 SU(2)_g 规范场与四个狄拉克节点耦合，这些节点构成两个 SU(2)_g 二重态（doublets），即 N_f=2 的 QCD。

Conclusion: 与普遍看法不同，该超流—分数量子陈绝缘体相变并非简单地属于通常的连续相变范畴，而应由 N_f=2 QCD 形式的涨落 SU(2)_g 规范场理论描述。

Abstract: We study a phase transition between a superfluid phase and a fractional Chern insulator phase in a hard-core boson model on the honeycomb lattice using symmetric hyperdeterminant (Hdet) wavefunctions. Combining projective symmetry group analysis with variational Monte Carlo, we construct and optimize Hdet states in various symmetry classes. Based on the associated effective theories, we show that different symmetry classes, i.e., representations of the projective symmetry group, describe specific quantum phases. Compared with traditional fermionic parton construction, the symmetric Hdet wavefunctions are shown to improve microscopics significantly, allowing quantitative investigation of quantum phase diagrams. We argue that, in contrast with the common belief, this putative continuous phase transition is described by a fluctuating $SU(2)_g$ gauge field coupling with four Dirac nodes forming two $SU(2)_g$ doublets (i.e., $N_f=2$ QCD).

</details>


### [141] [Intertwined Magneto-Structural Criticality Driving Heterogeneous Collective-Pseudospin Dynamics in Sr2IrO4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28395)
*Arnab Singh,Junyoung Kwon,AM Milinda Abeykoon*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在Sr2IrO4单晶中，自发结构畸变与磁序参量呈二次标度关系，并继承反铁磁序的二维XY类临界行为；XPCS进一步揭示多尺度、压缩指数型弛豫及临界慢化等非常规磁动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）自由度所导致关联量子现象是新兴量子技术与自旋功能的基础范式，精确探测其中磁性与结构构型相互交织的协同行为是该领域的核心需求。

Method: 对Sr2IrO4单晶采用互补的共振X射线衍射（resonant X-ray diffraction）与高能全散射（high-energy total scattering）测量，并结合X射线光子相关谱（X-ray photon correlation spectroscopy, XPCS）研究磁动力学。

Result: 自发结构畸变随磁序参量二次方标度，晶格畸变继承反铁磁序的二维XY类临界行为；XPCS显示随温度与波矢变化的多尺度磁动力学，表现为压缩指数弛豫（compressed-exponential relaxation）及磁转变附近的临界慢化（critical slowing down）特征。

Conclusion: 结果提示自旋-轨道耦合与局域结构畸变可能是驱动此类非常规动力学的机制。

Abstract: Correlated quantum phenomena arising from spin-orbit-coupled degrees of freedom provide foundational paradigms in emerging quantum technologies and spin-based functionalities. Elucidating the cooperative behaviors of these systems requires a precise probing of their intertwined magnetic and structural configurations. Using complementary resonant X-ray diffraction and high-energy total scattering measurements on single crystals of Sr2IrO4, we observe that spontaneous structural distortions scale quadratically with the magnetic order parameter. Importantly, the lattice distortion inherits the characteristic 2D XY -like critical behavior of the underlying antiferromagnetic order. X-ray photon correlation spectroscopy (XPCS) reveals multiscale, temperature- and wave-vector-dependent magnetic dynamics characterized by compressed-exponential relaxation and signatures of critical slowing down near the magnetic transition. These findings suggest the role of spin-orbit coupling and local structural distortions as the possible mechanism orchestrating such unconventional dynamics.

</details>


### [142] [Competing magnetic correlations in the square-lattice Hubbard model with spin-dependent nearest-neighbor and next-nearest-neighbor hopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28593)
*Rana Imran Mushtaq,Ji Liu,Yinlong Li,Wing Chi Yu,Xiaosen Yang,Ho-Kin Tang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用约束路径量子蒙特卡洛（constrained-path quantum Monte Carlo）研究同时包含自旋相关最近邻跃迁（NN hopping）各向异性 $t_A$ 与次近邻跃迁（NNN hopping）$t'$ 的二维正方晶格哈伯德模型（Hubbard model），给出联合调控下的四类磁关联相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁体（altermagnet）的自旋劈裂可分别源于 $t_A$ 或 $t'$，二者在 $90^\circ$ 旋转下均变号且无镜面时共存，但此前研究仅将二者分开处理，缺乏对二者共同作用的系统认识。

Method: 采用 $U=4t$ 的约束路径量子蒙特卡洛方法，对含 $t_A$ 与 $t'$ 的二维正方晶格哈伯德模型扫描电子填充 $n$、$t_A$ 与 $t'$，并结合无相互作用磁化率（susceptibility）与三种晶格尺寸的有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）分析。

Result: 识别出奈尔（Néel）、条纹（stripe）、对角（diagonal）与螺旋自旋密度波（spiral spin-density-wave）四类磁关联；对角关联需有限 $t_A$ 且被 $t'$ 抑制，条纹关联仅在 $t'$ 足以克服 $t_A$ 所设定的对角倾向时出现，而奈尔关联在大 $t_A$ 与大 $t'$ 下仍可存续。

Conclusion: 磁相图依赖 $t_A$ 与 $t'$ 的共同作用；无相互作用磁化率仅在小 $t'$ 时与主导波矢一致，$t'\geq0.3$ 的奈尔关联源于相互作用；有限尺寸标度显示仅在近半填充（half filling）处与长程有序（long-range order）一致，所测条纹、螺旋与对角点的每格点结构因子（structure factor）外推为零；该联合行为在 $U=6t$ 下依然保持。

Abstract: Altermagnets have spin-split bands without net magnetization. The splitting can come from a spin-dependent nearest-neighbor (NN) hopping anisotropy $t_A$ or from a spin-dependent next-nearest-neighbor (NNN) hopping $t'$. Both change sign under a $90^\circ$ rotation and are present together when the lattice has no mirror planes, yet they have so far been studied only separately. Here we study the two-dimensional square-lattice Hubbard model with both $t_A$ and $t'$, using constrained-path quantum Monte Carlo at $U=4t$ and scanning the electron filling $n$, $t_A$ and $t'$. We find four types of magnetic correlations: Néel, stripe, diagonal, and spiral spin-density-wave. The phase diagram depends on both hoppings together. Diagonal correlations require finite $t_A$ and are suppressed by $t'$. Stripe correlations appear only once $t'$ is large enough to overcome the diagonal tendency set by $t_A$. Néel correlations survive at large $t_A$ and large $t'$, where the NNN model alone gives stripe or spiral correlations. The noninteracting susceptibility matches the dominant wavevector at small $t'$, but not the Néel correlations at $t'\geq0.3$, which come from the interaction. Finite-size scaling with three lattice sizes is consistent with long-range order only near half filling; at the stripe, spiral and diagonal points we tested, the structure factor per site extrapolates to zero. The joint behavior persists up to $U=6t$.

</details>


### [143] [Hartree--Fock analysis of the bilayer Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28597)
*E. Langmann,J. Lenells*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文在Hartree–Fock近似下研究双层平行方格子上半满Hubbard模型的磁有序，导出弱耦合极限下Néel温度与反铁磁能隙比的渐近公式，并给出偏离半满时的数值相图及结构猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层方格Hubbard模型（Hubbard model）是描述层状强关联电子体系（如铜氧化物超导体）的最小模型，其弱耦合区磁有序参量（Néel温度、反铁磁能隙）的解析行为及相图结构尚需系统刻画。

Method: 在半满（half-filling）条件下，对层内跃迁t、层间跃迁t⊥、在位排斥U>0的双层方格Hubbard模型采用Hartree–Fock近似（Hartree–Fock approximation），进行弱耦合极限解析推导，并结合数值计算获得相图。

Result: 得到弱耦合极限下Néel温度（Néel temperature）与反铁磁能隙比（antiferromagnetic gap ratio）的渐近公式；确定反铁磁能隙比在顺磁—反铁磁相界（critical curve）附近的行为；并给出偏离半满时的数值相图。

Conclusion: 弱耦合区磁有序参量可由解析渐近式描述，能隙比在相界附近呈特定行为；对非半满相图结构提出解析猜想，为后续严格验证提供依据。

Abstract: We study the half-filled Hubbard model on two parallel square lattices with on-site repulsion $U>0$ and with nearest-neighbor hopping specified by a constant $t>0$ within each plane and by another constant $t_\perp \geq 0$ between the planes. Working in the Hartree--Fock approximation, we obtain asymptotic formulas for the Néel temperature and the antiferromagnetic gap ratio in the weak coupling limit. We also determine the behavior of the antiferromagnetic gap ratio near the critical curve separating the paramagnetic and antiferromagnetic regions in the phase diagram. Furthermore, we present numerical phase diagrams away from half-filling, and we make an analytical conjecture regarding their structure.

</details>


### [144] [Observation of nonadiabatic quantum tunneling in a driven correlated insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28980)
*Mingyao Guo,Radu Andrei,Jaena Park,Je-Geun Park,Eugene Demler,David Hsieh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文通过时间分辨光谱（time-resolved spectroscopy）研究关联范德华绝缘体（correlated van der Waals insulator）NiPS$_3$在Keldysh交叉区（Keldysh crossover）的载流子激发机制，发现隧穿主导吸收通道中叠加了由有质动力（ponderomotive）效应蓝移的多光子型共振，构成绝热性破缺（adiabaticity breakdown）的光谱证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Floquet工程（Floquet engineering）、非线性输运与高次谐波产生等新兴固态平台多工作在Keldysh交叉区，但该区域光载流子产生的微观路径尚不清楚，微扰多光子吸收（multiphoton absorption）与绝热隧穿（adiabatic tunneling）两种描述均不适用。

Method: 对关联范德华绝缘体NiPS$_3$开展时间分辨光学光谱测量，系统扫描驱动电场强度与频率；并采用基于含时高斯变分方法（time-dependent Gaussian variational approach）的Hubbard模型模拟进行对照。

Result: 在交叉区观测到整体呈隧穿主导的吸收通道中出现多光子型共振，且该共振因有质动力效应发生蓝移，成为绝热性破缺的清晰光谱特征；Hubbard模型模拟定性再现了上述特征。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了Keldysh交叉区电荷激发的微观机制，并指出有质动力蓝移共振等要素是调控关联材料（correlated materials）强场非线性动力学的关键因素。

Abstract: Many emerging solid-state platforms for Floquet engineering, nonlinear transport, and high harmonic generation operate near the Keldysh crossover, where optical fields are sufficiently strong and fast to reshape electronic states on sub-cycle timescales. Despite its importance, the microscopic pathways for photocarrier generation in this regime remain poorly understood, as neither perturbative multiphoton absorption nor adiabatic tunneling provides an adequate description. Here, we perform time-resolved optical spectroscopy on the correlated van der Waals insulator NiPS$_3$ while systematically tuning the drive electric field and frequency over a wide range. In the crossover regime, we observe multiphoton-like resonances that are blue-shifted by ponderomotive effects within an otherwise tunneling-dominated absorption channel - a clear spectroscopic signature of adiabaticity breakdown. Hubbard-model simulations based on a time-dependent Gaussian variational approach qualitatively reproduce these features. Together, our results elucidate the microscopic mechanisms of charge excitation in the Keldysh crossover regime and identify a key ingredient for controlling strong-field nonlinear dynamics in correlated materials.

</details>


### [145] [Finite-size reversal of a multiparticle correction to local spin spectra in a flux-disordered Kitaev honeycomb model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29070)
*David C. Ronquillo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文检验在Kitaev蜂窝模型中，固定通量无序（flux disorder）下“单粒子近似（one-particle approximation）”在匹配总自旋强度（total spin intensity）后，是否会误判局域自旋谱凹陷（depression）的深度。结果显示：在较大体系上该近似的符号会反向，从24格点外推会得到错误的修正方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋翻转（spin flip）会改变Kitaev蜂窝模型的通量背景与物质费米子（matter fermions）所处哈密顿量；即便调整单粒子近似的总强度使其与实际总自旋强度一致，其在描述局域自旋谱凹陷深度上的可靠性仍不清楚，论文即针对此问题开展检验。

Method: 在冻结的、通量无序的系综中，保留自旋翻转后真实的一粒子跃迁能量（one-particle transition energies）与相对谱权重（spectral weights），再将这些权重重新标度以匹配完整强度；用两侧频率处平均值减去中心强度来度量凹陷深度，并对每个状态、两种能量分辨率（energy resolutions）比较“配对修正”的中位数。

Result: 对自旋产生态（spin-created states）中末态准粒子数（quasiparticle number）为奇数且一粒子权重非零者，恢复多粒子权重（multiparticle weight）在24格点上使凹陷变浅，而在两个1,536格点形状及3,456、6,144格点环面上使凹陷变深；单粒子近似在24格点上高估平均凹陷深度约16-18%，在6,144格点样本中低估约33-56%。

Conclusion: 在上述四种较大环面及相同无序规定下，由24格点外推将得到错误的修正符号，说明即使总自旋强度正确，单粒子近似仍可能误估局域自旋谱凹陷深度。

Abstract: A spin flip in the Kitaev honeycomb model changes the flux background and therefore the Hamiltonian seen by the matter fermions. Can a one-particle approximation with the correct total spin intensity nevertheless misestimate the depth of a depression in the local spin spectrum? We test this question in a frozen, flux-disordered ensemble by retaining the actual one-particle transition energies and relative spectral weights after the spin flip, then rescaling those weights to match the full intensity. We measure the depression by subtracting the central intensity from the average at two flanking frequencies. For spin-created states with odd final quasiparticle number and nonzero one-particle weight, restoring multiparticle weight shallows the depression on 24 sites and deepens it on both 1,536-site shapes and the 3,456- and 6,144-site tori, as judged by the median paired correction at each of two energy resolutions. For these states, the one-particle approximation overestimates the mean dip depth by about 16-18% on 24 sites and underestimates it by 33-56% in the 6,144-site sample. Extrapolating from 24 sites would therefore give the wrong sign for this correction on those four larger tori under the same disorder prescription.

</details>


### [146] [A Fiber-Optic Platform for Cryogenic Laser-Pump Neutron-Probe Magneto-Optical Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29114)
*Lazar L. Kish,Andrei T. Savici,Tyler K. White,Vlad N. Sedov,Andrey A. Podlesnyak,Matthias D. Frontzek,Barry L. Winn,Igor A. Zaliznyak*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文报道一种光纤激光泵浦-中子探测（fiber-optic laser-pump neutron-probe）方法，可在低温下对光激发后的磁化动力学进行动量与频率分辨的测量，并在Yb基量子磁体（Yb-based quantum magnets）中演示了非平衡热化动力学（nonequilibrium thermalization dynamics）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅可推断其旨在建立一种将共振光激发与非弹性中子散射（inelastic neutron scattering）结合的手段，以获取时间依赖的两点磁化关联函数（two-point magnetization correlations）S(Q,ω)。

Method: 采用光纤传输的激光泵浦（laser-pump）与中子探测（neutron-probe）相结合的方案，在低温条件下先进行共振光激发（resonant photoexcitation），再用非弹性中子散射测量时间依赖的S(Q,ω)。

Result: 在Yb基量子磁体中实现了动量与频率分辨的磁光测量，并测得光激发后的非平衡热化动力学。

Conclusion: 该光纤激光泵浦-中子探测方法可作为一种动量与频率分辨的磁光测量手段，适用于研究量子磁体的非平衡磁化关联与热化过程。

Abstract: We report a fiber-optic laser-pump neutron-probe method for momentum- and frequency-resolved magneto-optical measurements. The technique uses inelastic neutron scattering to measure time-dependent two-point magnetization correlations, S(Q,$ω$), after resonant photoexcitation at cryogenic temperatures. We demonstrate it by measuring nonequilibrium thermalization dynamics in Yb-based quantum magnets.

</details>


### [147] [Quantum-Metric-Driven Hollow Wigner Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29317)
*Zekun Zhuang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文通过一个量子度规（quantum metric）与贝里通量（Berry flux）可独立调控的最小三带连续模型，结合Hartree-Fock方法，揭示量子度规可在贝里曲率为零时驱动维格纳晶体（Wigner crystal）由常规s波向"空心"电荷分布重构，并显著改变交换相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究揭示贝里曲率（Berry curvature）对电子结晶的深刻影响，但量子度规（quantum metric）的独立作用仍不明确。作者旨在构建可将量子度规与贝里通量分离调控的模型，以厘清二者的独立贡献。

Method: 采用最小三带连续模型（three-band continuum model），其中量子度规与贝里通量可分别调节；运用Hartree-Fock方法研究电子晶体的形成与电荷分布。

Result: 常规s波维格纳晶体可发展出晶格位点密度耗尽的空心电荷分布，且对称性与拓扑均不发生改变；该重构在贝里曲率消失时仍可出现，源于强量子度规与形状因子（form factor）的非平凡角结构。空心态与常规态的连续渡越伴随反常直接交换相互作用，在中等量子度规下近乎消失，而在强度规区增强超过一个数量级。

Conclusion: 量子度规不仅通过波函数离域化，还能重塑电子晶体的电荷分布与交换相互作用，构成独立于贝里曲率的调控自由度。

Abstract: Recent studies have revealed the profound influence of Berry curvature on electron crystallization, yet the independent role of the quantum metric remains unclear. Here, we introduce a minimal three-band continuum model with separately tunable quantum metric and Berry flux. Employing the Hartree-Fock approach, we find that a conventional s-wave Wigner crystal can develop a hollow charge distribution with depleted density at lattice sites, without a change in symmetry or topology. We show that this density reconstruction occurs even at vanishing Berry curvature and arises from strong quantum metric and the nontrivial angular structure of the form factor. In our model, the continuous crossover between hollow and conventional WCs is accompanied by anomalous direct exchange interactions, nearly vanishing at intermediate quantum metric but enhanced by over an order of magnitude in the strong-metric regime. These results reveal how quantum metric can reshape both the charge distribution and exchange interactions of electron crystals beyond simple wave-function delocalization.

</details>


### [148] [Ground-State Manifold Curvature and Phase Transitions in the Kitaev Honeycomb Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29495)
*Omid Yahyayi Monem,Setare Bashari,Hosein Mohammadzadeh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用量子度规（quantum metric）的Ricci标量研究Kitaev蜂窝模型（Kitaev honeycomb model）的相变：在三个有能隙相中曲率为负并在临界线上发散；在无能隙相中红外正则化后曲率为正且随系统尺寸对数衰减；磁场使无能隙相打开能隙并充当红外截断，从而完整重现相图并以Ricci标量的发散作为相变的几何标志。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kitaev蜂窝模型的基态可实现量子自旋液体（quantum spin liquid），包含有能隙Abelian相以及在外磁场下转变为非Abelian相的无能隙相；本文旨在通过基态流形（ground-state manifold）的几何，即量子度规的Ricci标量（Ricci scalar），刻画其相变。

Method: 计算量子度规的Ricci标量来描述各相的几何性质：对三个有能隙相给出曲率的闭式表达式，并指出三相之间可由精确坐标变换相互联系；对无能隙相采用红外正则化（infrared regularization）的度规，分析曲率随系统尺寸的变化及在相边界处的行为。

Result: 三个有能隙相的曲率为负，并在临界线上发散；无能隙相中红外正则化度规的曲率为正，随系统尺寸的对数成反比衰减，其幅度与尺寸无关但在相边界处发散，且发散指数不同于有能隙一侧。曲率的符号改变与发散共同重现完整相图。磁场使无能隙相打开能隙并充当红外截断，使非Abelian相在远离临界线处曲率为有限正值，并在临界线附近形成宽度随磁场二次（quadratically）标度的crossover区域。

Conclusion: 由于几何量的发散常被视为相变的标志，Ricci标量在临界线上的发散为这些相变提供了几何特征签名（geometric signature）。

Abstract: The Kitaev honeycomb model is an exactly solvable spin model whose ground state realizes a quantum spin liquid with gapped Abelian phases and a gapless phase that becomes a non-Abelian phase in a magnetic field. Here we study its phase transitions through the geometry of the ground-state manifold, using the Ricci scalar of the quantum metric. In the three gapped phases we obtain closed-form expressions for the curvature, which is negative and diverges at the critical lines; the three phases are related to one another by an exact change of coordinates. In the gapless phase, the curvature of the infrared-regularized metric is positive and decays inversely with the logarithm of the system size, with a size-independent amplitude that diverges at the phase boundaries, with an exponent different from that of the gapped side. The change of sign and the divergences together reproduce the complete phase diagram. A magnetic field gaps out the gapless phase and plays the role of the infrared cutoff, so that away from the critical lines the curvature of the non-Abelian phase becomes finite and positive, while around the critical lines it opens a crossover region whose width scales quadratically with the field. Since divergences of geometric quantities are often regarded as signatures of phase transitions, the divergence of the Ricci scalar at the critical lines provides a geometric signature of these transitions.

</details>


### [149] [Exact many-body scars of generic spin models from singlet covers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29754)
*Camilla Polvara,William Witczak-Krempa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文指出，正方格子横场 Ising 模型（transverse-field Ising model）在任意场强下均存在精确零能本征态，其形式为交替格胞对角线上单态（singlet）的乘积；纵向场（longitudinal field）可将这些态从对称保护简并中分离出来。其机制是：与单态两端等耦合的格点会湮灭该单态，因而结论可推广至任意自旋 s、各向异性交换、大范围键无序（bond disorder）及某些三自旋项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于揭示横场 Ising 模型中精确零能态（zero-energy eigenstates）的构造原理及其普适性，并据此判定哪些晶格可承载此类态，同时提供一种冷却（cooling）到这些态的手段。

Method: 方法为解析构造：以交替格胞对角线上的单态乘积作为零能本征态，借助“与单态两端等耦合的格点湮灭单态”这一机制进行推广分析，并考察纵向场对对称保护简并（symmetry-protected degeneracy）的解除作用，以及对不同晶格、自旋与相互作用形式的适用性判定。

Result: 结果表明该机制在任意自旋 s、各向异性交换（anisotropic exchange）、一大类键无序和某些三自旋项下均成立；在 11 种阿基米德晶格（Archimedean lattices）中有两种可承载此类态（s=1/2 时为四种），此外体心立方（bcc）晶格及自旋位于边上的准晶（quasicrystals）亦可行；单态泵浦（singlet pumping）可将任意态冷却至这些态。

Conclusion: 结论是：单态湮灭机制决定了哪些晶格存在此类精确零能态，并给出了一个可实现的状态制备途径（单态泵浦冷却）；该结果对相互作用形式与自旋大小具有强鲁棒性。

Abstract: The square-lattice transverse-field Ising model has exact zero-energy eigenstates at any field: products of singlets on alternate plaquette diagonals. A longitudinal field isolates them from a symmetry-protected degeneracy. The mechanism is that a site coupled equally to both ends of a singlet annihilates it. The result thus holds for any spin $s$, anisotropic exchange, a large class of bond disorder and certain three-spin terms. This mechanism decides which lattices host such states: two of the 11 Archimedean lattices (four at $s=1/2$), bcc and quasicrystals with spins on the edges. Singlet pumping cools any state into them.

</details>


### [150] [Projecting Fermions into Exact Scar Towers: Local Protection across Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30143)
*Wenjie Wei,Kun Jiang,Hui-Ke Jin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过在 $n$ 味平带 Kitaev 链（flat-band Kitaev chains）上施加 Gutzwiller 投影（Gutzwiller projection），可在 $SO(n)$（$n\ge3$）自旋链中构造出精确疤塔（exact scar towers），其波函数与绝对能量在对称保护拓扑相至铁磁相的一阶相变中保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探究量子疤痕（quantum scars）是否必须继承基态（ground state）的对称性、序参量与临界性，即能否构造出与基态性质解耦的精确疤痕塔。

Method: 采用 $n$ 味平带 Kitaev 链的 Gutzwiller 投影构造 scar tower；投影后的根态（root）在每条键上仅含单态（singlet）与反对称（antisymmetric）分量，此局域约束使交错味翻转（staggered flavor flip）可生成等间距塔。根态及其第一后代（first descendant）穷尽塔访问的两格点构型，留下未访问的正交键子空间（orthogonal bond subspace），并在其中引入形变。

Result: 该子空间内的形变可破坏 $SO(n)$ 对称性并重塑低能物理，而不改变精确 scar tower；极化选择性形变（polarization-selective deformation）驱动从对称保护拓扑相（symmetry-protected topological phase）到铁磁相（ferromagnetic phase）的精确一阶相变，塔在相变过程中乃至无能隙点（gapless point）均保持波函数与绝对能量不变。

Conclusion: 精确疤痕（exact scars）无需继承基态的对称性、序参量或临界性，表明疤痕态可与基态相图在本质上解耦。

Abstract: We construct exact scar towers in $SO(n)$ ($n\ge3$) spin chains by Gutzwiller projection of $n$-flavor flat-band Kitaev chains. The projected roots have only singlet and antisymmetric components on each bond. These local constraints allow a staggered flavor flip to generate an equidistant tower. The root and its first descendant exhaust the two-site configurations that are visited by the tower, leaving an orthogonal bond subspace unvisited. Deformations within this subspace can break $SO(n)$ symmetry and reshape the low-energy physics without altering the exact scar tower. A polarization-selective deformation drives an exact first-order transition from the symmetry-protected topological phase to a ferromagnetic phase. The tower retains its wave functions and absolute energies across the phase transition, including at the gapless point. Exact scars therefore need not inherit the symmetries, order parameters, or criticality of the ground state.

</details>


### [151] [Critical Topology: A New Perspective for Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30368)
*Xue-Jia Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文综述“临界拓扑（critical topology）”这一超越朗道范式（Landau paradigm）的量子临界性新形式，梳理其理论开放问题与实验发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统普适类（universality class）分类方法难以涵盖临界拓扑现象，需引入拓扑性质作为额外判据；尽管理论进展显著，关于通用判据、统一框架及高维强关联数值方法等问题仍待解决。

Method: 属观点/展望性综述：提出理论问题（通用判据、超越一维量子多体系统的统一框架、高维强关联体系的高效数值方法）与实验路线（Rydberg原子阵列、超导量子处理器、量子材料）。

Result: 指出现有实验研究主要集中于单粒子（single-particle）临界拓扑，多体（many-body）临界拓扑的实现在量子平台（如Rydberg原子阵列、超导量子处理器）尚属待探索方向。

Conclusion: 人工声学超材料（acoustic metamaterials）的相关实验观测标志着临界拓扑研究的开端而非终点，预计该方向将有进一步进展。

Abstract: Critical topology has emerged as a form of quantum criticality beyond the Landau paradigm, challenging conventional approaches to classifying universality classes by introducing topological properties as an additional criterion. Despite substantial progress over the past five years, several fundamental questions remain open. Theoretically, can we establish a general criterion for the emergence of critical topology? Can a unified theoretical framework be extended beyond one-dimensional quantum many-body systems? What efficient numerical methods can be developed to investigate critical topological states in higher-dimensional strongly correlated systems? Experimentally, research has so far focused primarily on single-particle critical topology. An important next step is to realize and characterize many-body critical topology in quantum systems using platforms such as Rydberg atom arrays, superconducting quantum processors, and quantum materials.
  Major advances in statistical physics often arise from re-examining fundamental concepts. The theory of critical phenomena developed by Landau, Ginzburg, and Wilson, together with the topological phase transitions pioneered by Kosterlitz and Thouless, has now inspired new discoveries in artificial acoustic metamaterials. These experimental observations mark a beginning rather than an endpoint for research on critical topology, and further advances in this direction are anticipated.

</details>


### [152] [Strain-induced non-trivial band topology and large magnetoresistance in epitaxial SrTaO$_3$ thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30681)
*Aditya Mehta,Soumyakanta Panda,Pratik Sahu,Amit Chauhan,Dibyajyoti Sahoo,Shwetha G Bhat,Rohit Soni,Goutam Tripathy,Niharika Mohapatra,B. R. K. Nanda,D. Samal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究表明外延压应变可将5$d^1$顺磁金属SrTaO$_3$薄膜驱动为非平庸拓扑半金属，其输运呈现多带特征并伴随有限贝里曲率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂过渡金属氧化物外延薄膜被视为通过应变调控晶体对称性与能带拓扑来实现关联拓扑相（correlated topological phases）的理想平台，本文以此为背景考察压应变下SrTaO$_3$薄膜的电子性质。

Method: 结合电子输运测量（磁阻、霍尔效应、Kohler标度检验、半经典双带分析）与密度泛函理论（density functional theory）计算及紧束缚模型（tight binding model）研究。

Result: 薄膜表现出大的不饱和准二次磁阻（magnetoresistance, MR）与双带输运特征的非线性霍尔效应，并出现场诱导的再入金属行为，Kohler标度律失效；双带分析给出载流子密度约$10^{21}~\mathrm{cm^{-3}}$、迁移率约$10^{4}~\mathrm{cm^2 V^{-1} s^{-1}}$。理论计算表明稳定结构具$a^0a^0c^+$八面体旋转，其非对称群对称性（non-symmorphic symmetry）保证费米能级附近存在产生有限贝里曲率（Berry curvature）的拓扑节点线（topological nodal lines），同时费米面附近还存在平庸电子与空穴口袋。

Conclusion: 外延应变使SrTaO$_3$进入块体材料中无法实现的非平庸拓扑半金属（topological semimetal）相，凸显应变工程调控氧化物拓扑态的可行性。

Abstract: Epitaxial thin films of complex transition metal oxides constitute an ideal platform for realizing correlated topological phases through strain engineering of crystal symmetry and band topology. Here, we investigate the electronic transport properties and the band structure of the 5$d^1$ paramagnetic metal SrTaO$_3$ thin film under epitaxial compressive strain. The film exhibits a large non-saturating quasi-quadratic magnetoresistance (MR) and a nonlinear Hall effect characteristic of two-band transport, together with the field induced re-entrant metallic behavior. The violation of Kohler's scaling further corroborates the scattering from multiple bands. Semiclassical two-band analysis yields carrier densities and mobilities of the order $\sim 10^{21}~\mathrm{cm^{-3}}$ and $\sim 10^{4}~\mathrm{cm^2 V^{-1} s^{-1}}$, respectively. The observed large mobility and unsaturated MR, indicates the presence of a non-trivial band structure. Consistent with this, results from density functional theory calculations and tight binding model studies reveal that the stable structure has an octahedral rotation $a^0a^0c^+$, where the non-symmorphic symmetry ensures the presence of topological nodal lines yielding finite Berry curvature in the vicinity of the Fermi level. Besides the nodal line, trivial electron and hole pockets exist near the Fermi level, contributing to electron transport. These findings establish that epitaxial strain drives SrTaO$_3$ into a nontrivial topological semimetal, a phase inaccessible in the bulk counterpart.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [From joint attention to shared symbols: A free-energy principle account](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30788)
*Tadahiro Taniguchi,Koji Hashimoto,Ryuichiro Ishikawa*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该文从自由能原理（free-energy principle）出发，论证共同注意（joint attention）如何通过边缘化共同对象在私人感知状态间引入统计依赖，并借由混合先验（mixture prior）的分量索引实现共享指称（shared reference）。集体预测编码（collective predictive coding）模型与Metropolis-Hastings命名博弈（naming game）实验表明，词语交换可协调私人类别并改善集体模型拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共同注意先于词汇习得，但感官经验不同的两个个体如何借由共同关注同一对象而习得共享词汇，这一问题尚缺乏机制性解释。论文旨在为共同注意与共享符号（shared symbols）之间建立可计算的联系。

Method: 在自由能原理框架下，将共同对象边缘化以导出私人感知状态间的统计依赖；在范畴化世界中，联合先验（joint prior）继承混合结构，其分量索引（component index）作为共享指称的候选。集体预测编码模型通过该共享索引表示依赖关系，并采用既有的Metropolis-Hastings命名博弈使智能体（agents）基于私人证据推断与交换词语。

Result: 玩具实验（toy experiments）与多视图图像实验（multiview image experiments）显示，词语交换能够协调各智能体的私人类别（private categories），并提升集体模型（collective models）的拟合程度。

Conclusion: 该解释通过私人推断（private inference）的耦合，将共同注意与共享符号联系起来，为词汇习得的群体协调机制提供了统一的规范性（normative）说明。

Abstract: Joint attention precedes vocabulary acquisition. How can attending to the same object help two people with different sensory experiences come to use a shared word? In a free-energy principle account, marginalizing the common object induces a statistical dependence between private perceptual states. In a categorical world, the joint prior inherits a mixture structure whose component index provides a candidate for shared reference. The collective predictive coding model approximates this prior by representing the dependence through that shared index. An established Metropolis-Hastings naming game lets agents infer and exchange words using private evidence. Toy and multiview image experiments illustrate how word exchange can coordinate private categories and improve the fit of collective models. The account connects joint attention to shared symbols through the coupling of private inference.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Probing CP violation in weak radiative and dielectron decays of $Λ$ and $Ξ^-$ hyperons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27944)
*Xiao-Gang He,Jusak Tandean,German Valencia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出稀衰变（rare decay）Λ→ne⁺e⁻与Ξ⁻→Σ⁻e⁺e⁻可作为探测CP破坏（CP violation）的灵敏探针，其长程主导成分提供可观的吸收相位（absorptive phase），与超出标准模型（beyond the standard model）的新物理效应干涉后可产生显著CP破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找对CP破坏、尤其是超越标准模型相互作用所诱导的CP破坏敏感的物理过程，是当前粒子物理的重要目标；该文据此考察超子稀衰变是否具备这种探针能力。

Method: 以模型无关（model-independent）方式处理新物理贡献，利用这些过程中占主导的长程成分所提供的吸收相位，与潜在新物理效应发生干涉，从而计算CP奇（CP-odd）的衰变率不对称性。

Result: 在当前K介子与超子数据允许的范围内，Λ与Ξ⁻情形下的CP奇衰变率不对称性可分别达到10%与20%水平；相关的弱辐射模式（weak radiative mode）Λ→nγ与Ξ⁻→Σ⁻γ给出可比拟的结果。

Conclusion: 这些结果可在未来的高统计量超子装置（high-statistics hyperon facility）上得到检验，表明超子稀衰变与弱辐射衰变是有前景的CP破坏探针。

Abstract: The rare decays $Λ\to ne^+e^-$ and $Ξ^-\toΣ^-e^+e^-$ can serve as sensitive probes of $CP$ violation, especially that induced by interactions from beyond the standard model. We demonstrate that this is so because the dominant long-distance components of these processes provide sizable absorptive phases that can give rise to significant $CP$ violation via interference with potential new-physics effects. Treating the new contributions model-independently, we find that this leads to $CP$-odd rate asymmetries that are currently allowed by kaon and hyperon data to reach the 10% and 20% levels in the $Λ$ and $Ξ^-$ cases, respectively. Interestingly, we obtain comparable outcomes for the related weak radiative modes $Λ\to nγ$ and $Ξ^-\toΣ^-γ$. These results are testable at future high-statistics hyperon facilities.

</details>


### [155] [Revealing Dark Matter in the Dark-Electron Rate of Xenon Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28211)
*Lillian Santos-Olmsted,Rebecca K. Leane,Carlos Blanco,John F. Beacom*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文主张氙（xenon）探测器不应依赖严格切选（cuts）来压低单电子（single-electron）本底，而应利用几乎全部的暗电子（dark-electron）原始命中率（raw electron hit rate）开展年调制（annual-modulation）搜索，使灵敏度标度由 S ≲ B 改善为 S ≲ √B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对与电子散射的亚-GeV 暗物质（sub-GeV dark matter）的实验灵敏度提升有强烈需求，且相关理论模型似在可及范围内。在最低暗物质质量区，信号多集中于单电子区间，但本底同样巨大，导致现有氙实验要么不在此区间搜索，要么如 XENONnT 施加极严切选而仅保留约 10⁻⁴ 的曝光量。

Method: 提出利用探测器绝大部分暗电子率并进行年调制分析，使灵敏度按 √B 标度；并指出该数据已被现有数据采集系统（DAQ）记录——XENONnT 与 PandaX-4T 的无触发（triggerless）系统连续记录，LZ 则在触发窗口（trigger windows）内记录，无需新硬件。基于实测暗电子率估算 XENONnT、LZ、PandaX-4T、PandaX-xT 与 XLZD 的灵敏度。

Result: 基于实测暗电子率的估算显示，采用年调制搜索可获得显著的灵敏度提升。

Conclusion: 氙探测器应转向利用暗电子率的年调制搜索，该方案无需新增硬件即可大幅提升亚-GeV 暗物质探测灵敏度，并推动对现有与未来氙数据的重新分析。

Abstract: There is high interest in improving experimental sensitivity to sub-GeV dark matter (DM) that scatters with electrons, as compelling theoretical models seem within reach. At the lowest DM masses, most of the expected signal lies in the single-electron regime, but so do large backgrounds. Xenon experiments therefore either do not yet search there at all or, as in XENONnT, apply cuts so severe that only $\sim$$10^{-4}$ of the exposure is retained, with sensitivity set by the criterion $S \lesssim B$, where those are the signal and background counts. We advocate that xenon detectors instead utilize most of their dark-electron rate (i.e., the total raw electron hit rate) and perform an annual-modulation search, for which the sensitivity scales as $S \lesssim \sqrt{B}$. Modern DAQs already record this rate, continuously in triggerless systems such as the XENONnT and PandaX-4T DAQs, and within trigger windows in LZ, so no new hardware is required. Using measured dark-electron rates, we estimate the sensitivity of XENONnT, LZ, PandaX-4T, PandaX-xT, and XLZD. We find that dramatic gains in sensitivity are possible, motivating new analyses of present and future xenon data.

</details>


### [156] [Vector boson scattering in composite Higgs models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28214)
*Rosy Caliri,Manuel Kunkel,Werner Porod*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 以 WZjj 产生过程为探针，研究发现即使在共振态质量下界以下，复合希格斯（Composite Higgs）模型中纵向极化矢量玻色子的分布尾部仍显著偏离标准模型（Standard Model）预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矢量玻色子散射（vector boson scattering）是检验电弱对称性破缺（electroweak symmetry breaking）机制的有力工具；LHC 数据已对复合希格斯模型的共振态给出 2 TeV 以下的质量下界，需评估该情形下是否仍存在可观测的新物理效应。

Method: 采用具有费米子底层描述的复合希格斯模型，假设仅有一个赝 Nambu-Goldstone 玻色子（pseudo Nambu-Goldstone boson）参与电弱对称性破缺并对应标准模型希格斯态；以 WZjj 产生为基准过程，分析各分布尾部相对于标准模型预言的偏离。

Result: 即便共振态位于 LHC 下界之外，多个分布的尾部仍显示出相对标准模型的显著偏离，该效应在纵向极化（longitudinally polarised）矢量玻色子情形下尤为明显。

Conclusion: 分布尾部（尤其是纵向极化通道）可作为探测复合希格斯模型的敏感观测量，补充直接共振态搜寻的不足。

Abstract: Vector boson scattering is a powerful tool to test the mechanism of electroweak symmetry breaking. We focus on Composite Higgs models with an underlying fermionic description and assume that only one pseudo Nambu-Goldstone boson contributes to electroweak symmetry breaking, which we identify as the Higgs state of the Standard Model. In these scenarios, LHC data imply lower bounds on the predicted resonances below 2 TeV depending on the scenario. Using $WZjj$ production as a benchmark, we demonstrate that the tails of various distributions nevertheless show significant deviations from Standard Model predictions. This holds in particular for the case of longitudinally polarised vector bosons.

</details>


### [157] [Timelike electric dipole form factors of the baryon octet: short-distance kernels and baryonic rescattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28492)
*Chao-Qiang Geng,Xiang-Nan Jin,Chia-Wei Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用格点光锥分布振幅（light-cone distribution amplitudes, LCDAs）计算了夸克电偶极矩（electric dipole moments, EDMs）对 $J/\psi$ 质量处重子八重态（baryon octet）类时形状因子（timelike form factors）的领头扭度（leading-twist）贡献，并据此由 BESIII 数据给出奇异夸克 EDM 的上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夸克电偶极矩是检验标准模型之外 CP 破坏（CP violation）的重要探针，而重子类时形状因子在 $J/\psi$ 共振区可由 BESIII 等实验精确测量，因此有必要定量估计夸克 EDM 对这些可观测量的贡献，以从中提取对奇异夸克 EDM 的限制。

Method: 采用格点光锥分布振幅计算领头扭度夸克 EDM 对重子八重态类时形状因子的贡献；并结合介子交换模型（meson-exchange model）讨论 kaon 交换对 $Ξ$ 与 $Σ$ 道的耦合，以及采用 $1\,\mathrm{GeV}$ 截断的重散射（rescattering）效应和重整化标度（renormalization scale）变化进行分析。

Result: 对奇异夸克 EDM $d_s$，短距离 $Σ/Λ$ 比值为 $-0.080^{+0.037}_{-0.098}$，高于非相对论夸克模型（nonrelativistic quark model）的 $-1/3$；介子交换使 $Σ$ 与 $Λ$ 形状因子量级相当，短距离 $Λ$ 形状因子约为 $5\times10^{-4}d_s$。由此 BESIII 测量给出 $|d_s|<1.3\times10^{-15}\,e\,\mathrm{cm}$（95% 置信水平）。

Conclusion: 联合拟合投影的 $Λ$、$Σ^+$ 与 $Ξ$ 灵敏度可将仅用 $Λ$ 的上限在 BESIII 与超级陶粲装置（Super Tau-Charm Facility）上降低 25% 以上；重整化标度变化会使投影限制改变约两倍，是主要的理论不确定性来源之一。

Abstract: We calculate the leading-twist contribution of quark electric dipole moments (EDMs) to the timelike form factors of the baryon octet at the $J/ψ$ mass using lattice light-cone distribution amplitudes. For a strange-quark EDM $d_s$, the short-distance $Σ/Λ$ ratio is $-0.080^{+0.037}_{-0.098}$ over the lattice input ranges. This range lies above the nonrelativistic quark-model value $-1/3$. In the meson-exchange model, kaon exchange feeds the large $Ξ$ source into the $Σ$ channel and makes the $Σ$ and $Λ$ form factors comparable in magnitude. The short-distance $Λ$ form factor is about $5\times10^{-4}d_s$. The BESIII measurement then gives $|d_s|<1.3\times10^{-15}\,e\,\mathrm{cm}$ at 95% confidence level. With rescattering at a $1\,\mathrm{GeV}$ cutoff, joint fits to projected $Λ$, $Σ^+$ and $Ξ$ sensitivities lower the $Λ$-only upper limits by more than 25% at BESIII and the Super Tau-Charm Facility. Renormalization-scale variation changes the projected limits by about a factor of two.

</details>


### [158] [Quark and gluon gravitational form factors of the nucleon at next-to-leading order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30124)
*Ulaş Özdem*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在三圈（次领头阶 α_s）QCD 求和规则（QCD sum rules）中首次将夸克与胶子能量动量张量同阶处理，计算核子的引力形状因子 A、J、D，并系统检验求和规则适用性；O(α_s) 修正显著压低各总量，总量结果在 1–2.5 GeV² 与格点 QCD（lattice QCD）一致，其中 D 最可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核子的引力形状因子（gravitational form factors）A、J、D 是刻画其质量与自旋分布、能量动量张量（EMT）结构的关键量，但此前求和规则计算中夸克与胶子部分常不同阶处理，且求和规则的可信区间与系统误差来源缺乏清晰判定。

Method: 采用三点 QCD 求和规则，在 α_s 次领头阶（next-to-leading order）对夸克与胶子 EMT 于 MS-bar 重整化方案下同阶计算 A、J、D；引入反对称 Borel 矩（antisymmetric Borel moments）构造受保护估计量，并借助核子 EMT 检验方法本身。

Result: O(α_s) 修正使 A_tot 降低 32–40%、J_tot 降低 22–27%、|D_tot| 降低 21–27%；在 Q²=1.5 GeV² 得 A_tot=0.337±0.113、J_tot=0.206±0.100、D_tot=−0.206±0.121。总量形状因子在 1≤Q²≤2.5 GeV² 与格点 QCD 吻合；D 因关联函数以微扰贡献为主而最可靠，夸克与胶子分量均与格点一致。

Conclusion: A 与 J 中最大贡献来自不随 Q² 下降的局域胶子凝聚项，其胶子占比明显低于格点值，可能源于该阶胶子 EMT 仅通过胶子交换与凝聚耦合。有效工作区仅在 β≈1 附近，Borel 窗口内无法分离跃迁常数项；个别守恒恒等式偏差约两个标准差，提示 g_{μν} 结构缺少有限项，故 \bar c 仅在领头阶加凝聚给出。

Abstract: We compute the quark and gluon gravitational form factors $A$, $J$ and $D$ of the nucleon in three-point QCD sum rules at next-to-leading order in $α_s$ for the quark and gluon energy-momentum tensors (EMT), treating both at the same order in the $\overline{\rm MS}$ scheme. The nucleon EMT also tests the method, showing where the sum rules are reliable and why. Protected estimators of $D$ and of the trace form factor $\bar c$ require antisymmetric Borel moments, since none with equal Borel masses alone is protected. The $\mathcal{O}(α_s)$ corrections reduce $A_{\rm tot}$ by $32$--$40\%$, $J_{\rm tot}$ by $22$--$27\%$ and $|D_{\rm tot}|$ by $21$--$27\%$, since the perturbative share of the two-point function is much larger. The total form factors agree with lattice QCD for $1\le Q^2\le2.5$~GeV$^2$. At $Q^2=1.5$~GeV$^2$, $A_{\rm tot}=0.337\pm0.113$, $J_{\rm tot}=0.206\pm0.100$ and $D_{\rm tot}=-0.206\pm0.121$. $D$ is the most reliable result: its correlator is mostly perturbative, and its quark and gluon parts agree with the lattice. In $A$ and $J$, a local gluon-condensate term that does not fall with $Q^2$ is the largest, and their gluon shares are well below the lattice, possibly because the gluon EMT couples at this order only through gluon exchange and the condensate. A working region exists only near $β=1$, and the constant part of transitions cannot be separated within the Borel windows. The individual conservation identities deviate at one point by two standard deviations, consistent with a missing finite term in the $g_{μν}$ structure, and $\bar c$ is therefore given at leading order with condensates.

</details>


### [159] [Towards a Global CE$ν$NS Picture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30638)
*Heerak Banerjee,Philipp Eller*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出一个从实验数据中提取并解释相干弹性中微子-核散射（CE$\\nu$NS）微分截面的一般框架，基于广义参数基与贝叶斯推断（Bayesian inference），并实现为开源 Julia 包 Newtrinos.jl。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CE$\\nu$NS 实验分析主要给出归一化约束，对相关谱形状（correlated spectral shape）的约束有限，缺乏通用的、可直接从数据中约束微分截面形状与归一化的建模框架。

Method: 构建由广义中微子相互作用（generalized neutrino interaction）模型中反复出现的常见运动学项（kinematic terms）组成的广义参数基（generalized parametric basis），对观测到的 CE$\\nu$NS 响应的形状与归一化进行数值约束；随后以贝叶斯推断应用于 COHERENT 的 CsI 与液氩（liquid argon）数据集，并进行联合分析。

Result: 所得归一化约束与 COHERENT 合作组（Collaboration）报告的结果相当，同时额外给出对相关谱形状的约束；联合分析利用两个数据集的互补敏感性（complementary sensitivities），约束了单一数据集难以触及的微分截面特征。

Conclusion: 该框架可推广用于 CE$\\nu$NS 微分截面的提取与解释，并已以开源 Julia 包 Newtrinos.jl 形式发布，支持模块化前向建模（forward modeling）、并行计算与自动微分（automatic differentiation）。

Abstract: We present a general framework for extracting and interpreting the Coherent Elastic Neutrino-Nucleus Scattering (CE$ν$NS) differential cross-section from experimental data. A generalized parametric basis, constructed from common kinematic terms recurring in generalized neutrino interaction models, is employed to numerically constrain the shape and normalization of the observed CE$ν$NS response. The framework was applied to the COHERENT CsI and liquid argon datasets using Bayesian inference, yielding normalization constraints comparable to those reported by the Collaboration while providing additional constraints on the correlated spectral shape. A combined analysis was also performed, exploiting the complementary sensitivities of the two datasets to constrain features of the differential cross-section that are less accessible to either dataset individually. The framework has been implemented in the open-source Julia package Newtrinos.jl, which supports modular forward modeling, parallel computation, and automatic differentiation.

</details>


### [160] [Exact analytic solutions for the $n=1$ chameleon field in planar systems with an arbitrary number of uniform layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30712)
*Chad Briddon,Mario Pitschmann*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 针对 n=1 变色龙（chameleon）模型，在由 N 个离散均匀密度区构成的平面系统中给出精确解析解，并用于预测 CANNEX 实验对第五力（fifth force）的限制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变色龙等屏蔽标量场（screened scalar field）可规避观测限制，是暗能量（dark energy）候选者，但其非线性（non-linear）特性导致文献中精确解析解稀少，且以往对实验的预测常把平板与壁简化为单一均匀物体。

Method: 推导 N 区平面系统中 n=1 变色龙模型的精确解析解（exact analytic solution），与文献中的近似解（approximate solution）对比，并基于该解预测 CANNEX 实验对卡西米尔力（Casimir force）与第五力的限制。

Result: 采用 CANNEX 完整的十五层（fifteen-layered）构型而非将平板与壁视为单一均匀物体后，诱导的变色龙压力（chameleon pressure）最高增加约 7%。

Conclusion: 该压力提升幅度不足以在投影灵敏度（projected sensitivity）下探测新的参数空间（parameter space）区域。

Abstract: Scalar fields have long been investigated as a potential dark energy candidate. Of these models, screened scalar fields like the chameleon have received interest due to their ability to avoid observational constraints. However, the non-linear nature of these models has led to there being only a small number of exact analytic solutions within the literature. In this work, we present exact analytic solutions for the $n=1$ chameleon model in a planar system consisting of $N$ discrete regions of uniform density. We compare these to the relevant approximate solutions within the literature. We also use our solutions to forecast constraints from CANNEX, a recent experiment devised to measure Casimir forces and detect hypothetical fifth forces. Unlike in previous works, we consider the full fifteen-layered setup of CANNEX, as opposed to treating the plate and wall as single uniform objects. Doing so revealed an increase in the induced chameleon pressure of up to $\sim 7 \%$. However, the resulting gain is insufficient to probe new regions of parameter space at the projected sensitivities.

</details>


### [161] [A general formalism for electromagnetic production of two mesons on the nucleon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30755)
*Reddy Malik Priandika,Samson Clymton,Terry Mart*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个核子上双介子电磁产生的普适形式体系（formalism），将强子流按16个规范与洛伦兹不变矩阵展开，并映射为16个类CGLN的泡利振幅，从而直接给出五重微分截面及各响应函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单介子电生（electroproduction）已有Berends等人的分解框架，但双介子产生（double-meson production）缺乏可在宽能区、单道与耦合道分析中通用的协变描述体系，需要统一的振幅与观测量表述。

Method: 沿用Berends等的分解思路，将强子流（hadronic current）展开为16个规范不变（gauge-invariant）且洛伦兹不变（Lorentz-invariant）的矩阵，其中4个在实光子情形下消失；把任意反应模型给出的洛伦兹不变（狄拉克）振幅映射到16个类CGLN泡利振幅（Pauli amplitudes），进而得到五重微分截面（five-fold differential cross section）及横向、纵向和干涉响应函数（response functions）。并以核子树级Born项计算阈值附近的γp→π⁰π⁰p与γp→π⁺π⁰n作为示例。

Result: 该矩阵集合虽非全部线性独立，但直接源于流的协变分解，使振幅可直接从费曼图（Feynman diagrams）读出，观测量形式更简洁对称。示例计算显示：单个图破坏的规范不变性在总振幅中恢复，并在引入强子形状因子（hadronic form factors）后仍保持。

Conclusion: 该形式体系为双介子光生与电生（photo- and electroproduction）的后续理论与现象学研究提供了通用基础。

Abstract: We develop a general formalism for the electromagnetic production of two mesons on the nucleon, $γ_{\rm v}N\toΦ_1Φ_2B$, that can be used in single-channel as well as coupled-channel analyses over a wide range of energies. In the spirit of the decomposition introduced by Berends et al. for single-meson electroproduction, the hadronic current is expanded in a set of 16 gauge- and Lorentz-invariant matrices, four of which vanish for real photons. The corresponding Lorentz-invariant (Dirac) amplitudes can be obtained from any reaction model and are mapped onto 16 CGLN-like Pauli amplitudes, from which the five-fold differential cross section and its transverse, longitudinal, and interference response functions follow directly. Although these matrices are not all linearly independent, they follow directly from the covariant decomposition of the current, so the amplitudes can be read off from the Feynman diagrams and the observables take a simpler, more symmetric form. As a first application, we calculate the $γp\toπ^0π^0p$ and $γp\toπ^+π^0n$ reactions near threshold from tree-level nucleon Born terms. This example shows explicitly how the Dirac amplitudes are read off from Feynman diagrams and how gauge invariance, which is violated by the individual diagrams, is restored in the total amplitude and preserved when hadronic form factors are included. The formalism thus provides a versatile basis for future theoretical and phenomenological studies of double-meson photo- and electroproduction. A more complete abstract is provided in the paper.

</details>


### [162] [Light on GeV-scale seesaw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30780)
*Enrico Bertuzzo,Michele Frigerio,Salvador Urrea*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 下一代束流倾倒（beam-dump）与加速器中微子实验对GeV量级惰性中微子（sterile neutrino）具极高灵敏度，而最小跷跷板机制（minimal seesaw mechanism）所预言的活性-惰性混合过小。若两个惰性中微子N1、N2通过跃迁偶极（transition dipole）与光子耦合，可大幅增强其产生并开辟新衰变道，从而经离壳光子（off-shell photon）产生的带电轻子对（charged-lepton pairs）探测量跷跷板带（seesaw band）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小跷跷板机制预言的活性-惰性混合（active-sterile mixing）微弱，使下一代束流倾倒与加速器中微子实验难以企及。振荡数据（oscillation data）要求至少两个惰性中微子，其可能的跃迁偶极耦合为间接探测提供新途径。

Method: 以跃迁偶极耦合为出发点，分析其对惰性中微子产生与衰变的影响，并将离壳光子产生的带电轻子对确定为最具前景的实验信号；进而评估T2K近探测器与SHiP等实验的探测潜力。

Result: T2K近探测器（near detector）可探测m_N≲500 MeV的跷跷板带，SHiP可覆盖m_N≲5 GeV；当偶极强度d≳(10 TeV)^{-1}时，SHiP可选择性测量需非零活性-惰性混合的衰变道。

Conclusion: 足够大的跃迁偶极将产生特征性的信号层级（hierarchy of signatures），为跷跷板机制提供直接检验，并使原本不可及的参数区域进入实验可探测范围。

Abstract: The next generation of beam-dump and accelerator-neutrino experiments will probe GeV-scale sterile neutrinos with unprecedented sensitivity. However, the tiny active-sterile mixing predicted by the minimal seesaw mechanism is expected to remain out of reach. Oscillation data require at least two sterile neutrinos, $N_1$ and $N_2$, which may couple to the photon through a transition dipole. Such a coupling can radically change the picture, both by strongly enhancing sterile-neutrino production and by opening new decay channels. We identify charged-lepton pairs produced by an off-shell photon as the most promising experimental signature. We show that the seesaw band can be probed by the T2K near detector for sterile-neutrino masses $m_N\lesssim 500$ MeV, and by SHiP for $m_N\lesssim 5$ GeV. For a sufficiently large dipole, $d\gtrsim (10~\mathrm{TeV})^{-1}$, SHiP can selectively measure decay channels that require nonzero active-sterile mixing. This would give rise to a distinctive hierarchy of signatures, providing a direct test of the seesaw mechanism.

</details>


### [163] [Diffractive Photoproduction of High $p_T$ Photons in Ultraperipheral Collisions at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30966)
*João V. Bulhões,Victor P. Goncalves*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究 LHC 超周边碰撞中高横动量光子的衍射光生过程，比较了不同碰撞系统及 BFKL Pomeron 的多种描述，并估计了包含次领头阶（NLO）修正的截面与微分分布，指出该通道未来测量可约束 BFKL 动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于利用高能 QCD（quantum chromodynamics）中 BFKL 动力学的实验检验需求，探索超周边碰撞（ultraperipheral collisions）中高横动量光子衍射光生这一通道对约束 BFKL Pomeron 描述的潜力。

Method: 方法上考虑 LHC 下不同碰撞系统（pp、pPb、PbPb）的衍射光生过程，采用 BFKL Pomeron 的多种替代描述，并计入 BFKL 特征函数（BFKL characteristic function）的次领头阶（NLO）修正，估计截面与微分分布。

Result: 结果显示该过程截面相当可观（sizable），并给出了在超周边 pp、pPb 与 PbPb 碰撞中测量该过程的前景评估。

Conclusion: 结论认为该通道的未来测量有助于约束 BFKL 动力学，并提升对高能 QCD 的理解。

Abstract: We investigate diffractive photoproduction of large-transverse-momentum photons in ultraperipheral collisions at the LHC, considering different collision systems and alternative descriptions of the BFKL Pomeron. We estimate cross sections and differential distributions including NLO corrections to the BFKL characteristic function and assess the prospects for measuring this process in ultraperipheral pp, pPb, and PbPb collisions. We find that the cross sections are sizable, and that future measurements in this channel can help constrain BFKL dynamics and improve our understanding of high-energy QCD.

</details>


### [164] [A proposal for an LHC search for baryon number violation in complementarity with trilepton nucleon decay searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31142)
*Amit Bhoonah,Francis M. Burk,Da Liu,Tong Ou,Deepak Sathyan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究Super-Kamiokande（Super-K）三轻子核衰变（trilepton nucleon decay）限制与TeV能标重子数破坏（Baryon Number Violation, BNV）的相容性，并在有效场论（effective field theory, EFT）框架下提出利用LHC现有数据开展专门搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对三轻子核衰变的实验限制是否能与TeV能标的重子数破坏共存尚不明确，且LHC数据中可能已有可挖掘的互补探测潜力，需要系统性论证并推动专门分析。

Method: 在有效场论框架下推导与保持 B − L/3 结构的BNV高维算子相关的模型无关限制；比较Super-K环路级三轻子核衰变限制与树图级粲重子衰变限制；并针对一个s道双夸克共振（diquark resonance）退化为轻夸克—双轻子对的代表性紫外完备（UV completion）开展对撞机现象学分析，设计专门的选择判据（selection cuts）。

Result: Super-K对环路级三轻子核衰变的现有限制显著强于这些算子所诱导的树图级粲重子衰变限制，对应BNV能标上界为 O(1–30) TeV，从而为LHC搜索留下空间。对撞机分析显示，ATLAS与CMS的同号双轻子（same-sign dilepton）搜索已给出间接约束，但基于所设计判据的专门分析可显著扩展基准点（benchmarks）的探测范围，仅用Run 2数据即可与Super-K的三轻子核衰变限制及Hyper-Kamiokande的预期灵敏度形成互补。

Conclusion: TeV能标下 B − L/3 型BNV仍未被排除，Super-K与LHC搜索具有互补性；所提出的选择判据不仅适用于该代表性模型，还可推广至更广泛的情形。

Abstract: We investigate the compatibility of existing limits on trilepton nucleon decay by the Super-Kamiokande (Super-K) experiment with Baryon Number Violation (BNV) at the TeV scale and motivate a dedicated search using existing data at the LHC. Working within an effective field theory framework, we derive model-independent constraints on higher-dimensional operators associated with $B - L/3$ conserving texture of BNV. We demonstrate that existing Super-K limits on loop-level trilepton nucleon decay provide significantly stronger constraints compared to tree-level charm-baryon decays induced by these operators. These constraints translate into bounds of $O(1-30)$ TeV on the BNV scale, leaving open the possibility of an LHC search for $B - L/3$ type BNV. Motivated by this complementarity, we investigate the collider phenomenology of a representative UV completion featuring an $s$-channel diquark resonance that decays into a leptoquark--dilepton pair, producing a distinctive final state containing an anti-top quark, two charged anti-leptons, and missing transverse energy. Our results indicate that, although same-sign dilepton searches by ATLAS and CMS already place indirect constraints on this model, a dedicated analysis based on the selection cuts we develop significantly extends the reach for our chosen benchmarks. In particular, using only Run 2 data, we demonstrate complementary reach to limits on trilepton nucleon decay searches set by the Super-K experiment and to the projected reach of Hyper-Kamiokande. The selection cuts are applicable beyond our representative model.

</details>


### [165] [Scotogenic dark matter from gauged $L_μ+L_τ$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28223)
*J. Echeverria-Puentes,Sergey Kovalenko,Pablo San Martin Sartori,Carlos A. Vaquera-Araujo,Yoxara Villamizar,Jilberto Zamora Saa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于规范 \(L_\mu + L_\tau\) 对称性的 scotogenic 模型：该对称性被双单位自发破缺后残留物质宇称（matter parity）\(M_P\)，从而同时解释马约拉纳（Majorana）中微子质量与稳定暗物质候选者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 scotogenic 机制（辐射产生中微子质量并稳定暗物质）与 \(L_\mu + L_\tau\) 规范对称性结合，可为中微子质量起源与暗物质稳定性提供统一解释，并赋予区别于现有模型的味结构（flavor structure）。

Method: 构建规范 \(L_\mu + L_\tau\) 模型，令其由两个单位电荷的标量自发破缺，残留 \(M_P\) 宇称；分析以单态为主的 CP 偶标量暗物质候选者的现象学，并扫描与热遗迹丰度（thermal relic abundance）相符的参数区域，同时考察 \(Z'\) 玻色子、电弱精确测量（electroweak precision constraints）与动力学混合（kinetic mixing）的影响。

Result: 轻重子中微子为马约拉纳型；scotogenic 圈图（scotogenic loop）中最轻电中性中间态受 \(M_P\) 保护，可作为弱相互作用大质量粒子（WIMP）暗物质候选者。\(Z'\) 玻色子可兼容广泛暗物质相关参数，动力学混合达 \(ε\sim 10^{-2}\) 时电弱精确约束不产生显著张力。

Conclusion: 讨论现有对轻子亲和高规范玻色子（leptophilic gauge bosons）的搜寻限制，并给出可见（visible）与不可见（invisible）对撞机信号（collider signatures）变得相关的条件。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce the first scotogenic model based on a gauged $L_μ + L_τ$ symmetry whose spontaneous breaking by two units leaves a remnant matter parity $M_P$. The resulting light active neutrinos are of Majorana nature and the lightest electrically neutral state mediating the scotogenic loop is stabilized by $M_P$ and can be identified as a weakly interacting massive particle dark matter candidate. We analyze the phenomenology of a predominantly singlet-like CP-even scalar dark matter candidate and the representative regions of parameter space compatible with the observed thermal relic abundance. The $Z'$ boson is compatible with a broad range of parameters relevant for dark matter. In particular, electroweak precision constraints do not lead to significant tensions for kinetic mixing values up to $ε\sim 10^{-2}$. We discuss the implications of existing searches for leptophilic gauge bosons and the conditions under which visible and invisible collider signatures become relevant.

</details>


### [166] [Chiral and deconfinement thermodynamics of the Polyakov-quark linear sigma model with and without charm quark: an SU(3) versus SU(4) comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28429)
*Nourhan M. Rfeek,Hossam Wahid,Fatima M. Rfeek,Alexandra Friesen,Yuri Kalinovsky,Saleh O. Allehabi,Azzah A. Alshehri,Ashraf F. El-Sherif,Abdel Nasser Tawfik*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在Polyakov-loop扩展线性sigma模型（PLSM）下同口径对比三味（u,d,s）与四味（u,d,s,c）计算，分离粲夸克（charm quark）热贡献；结果表明mc>>Tc使粲夸克在相变区受到热压低，归一化后两味方案的状态方程（EoS）在T≲2Tc内几乎重合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究强相互作用物质在有限温度与重子密度下的性质时，粲夸克的热贡献是否重要尚需在与三味方案同等条件下（on equal footing）的系统比较来厘清；该工作即为分离粲夸克热效应而设计。

Method: 采用Polyakov-loop扩展线性sigma模型（PLSM），通过显式与自发对称性破缺描述手征动力学并再现标量与赝标量介子真空谱；以对数型Polyakov-loop势（logarithmic Polyakov-loop potential）引入禁闭并于连续极限格点QCD状态方程定标；在平均场近似（mean-field approximation）下计算轻、奇、粲夸克凝聚、Polyakov loop及压强、迹反常（trace anomaly）、熵密度等体热力学量，并确定手征与退禁闭赝临界边界。

Result: Polyakov部分按格点QCD压强与迹反常数据调参后，熵密度在交叉区（crossover region）与HotQCD 2+1(+1)味结果一致；零重子密度下非奇夸克凝聚在SU(3)与SU(4)中分别于Tc=210 MeV与218 MeV熔化，曲率参数κ分别为0.018与0.017；归一化至相应Stefan-Boltzmann极限后，SU(3)与SU(4)的EoS在T≲2Tc内几乎重合，粲夸克主要体现为更高的理想气体上限、逐渐的高温尾部及u,d→s→c的味序行为。

Conclusion: 因mc>>Tc，粲夸克在交叉区始终受到热压低，其贡献在归一化后主要为整体标度效应而非改变相变结构；在平均场理论可达的全部重子化学势范围内，强子—部分子转变（hadron-parton transition）保持平滑过渡（smooth crossover）。

Abstract: Using the Polyakov-loop extended linear-sigma model (PLSM), we investigate strongly interacting matter at finite temperature and baryon density by comparing the three-flavor (u,d,s) and four-flavor (u,d,s,c) formulations on equal footing, with the aim of isolating the thermal contribution of the charm quark. The model describes chiral dynamics through explicit and spontaneous symmetry breaking and reproduces the vacuum spectra of scalar and pseudoscalar mesons. Confinement is incorporated through a logarithmic Polyakov-loop potential calibrated to the continuum lattice-QCD equation of state (EoS). Within the mean-field approximation, we calculate light-, strange-, and charm-quark condensates, the Polyakov loop, and bulk thermodynamic observables, including pressure, trace anomaly, and entropy density, over broad ranges of temperature and baryon chemical potential. We also determine the chiral and deconfinement pseudocritical boundaries and represent them as phase boundaries and three-dimensional order-parameter surfaces. After tuning the Polyakov sector to lattice-QCD pressure and trace-anomaly data, the resulting entropy density agrees with HotQCD 2+1(+1)-flavor results throughout the crossover region. At zero baryon density, the non-strange condensate melts at Tc = 210 MeV in SU(3) and 218 MeV in SU(4), with curvature parameters kappa=0.018 and 0.017. Because mc>>Tc, charm remains thermally suppressed across the crossover. After normalization to the corresponding Stefan-Boltzmann limits, the SU(3) and SU(4) EoS nearly coincide below T~2Tc. Charm mainly contributes through a higher ideal-gas limit, a gradual high-temperature tail, and the flavor ordering u,d->s->c. For all baryon chemical potentials accessible within mean-field theory, the hadron-parton transition remains a smooth crossover.

</details>


### [167] [Compactification and non-zero density in (2+1)-dimensional four-fermion model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29823)
*K. G. Klimenko,A. N. Kozhakin,R. N. Zhokhov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该研究在具有一个紧致维的 (2+1) 维 Nambu–Jona-Lasinio（NJL）型模型中讨论了相图，考察紧致化与费米子密度对手征对称性破缺的影响，边界条件取为周期边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探明额外紧致维（compact dimension）与有限费米子密度（fermion density）如何共同影响 (2+1) 维 NJL 型模型中的手征对称性破缺（chiral symmetry breaking），并借此厘清该体系的相结构。

Method: 采用 (2+1) 维 NJL 型模型，在其中一个空间维度上做紧致化处理，并在周期边界条件（periodic boundary conditions）下计算相图，同时引入费米子密度作为参量。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果，仅指出相图已在该设定下得到研究，并明确了紧致化效应与费米子密度对手征对称性破缺的共同作用是该相图的核心内容。

Conclusion: 在含一个紧致维的 (2+1) 维 NJL 型模型中，周期边界条件下紧致化与费米子密度均被纳入手征对称性破缺的描述，构成该模型相图的基本要素；摘要未给出进一步的物理结论。

Abstract: The phase diagram of (2+1)-dimensional NJL type model has been studied with one compact dimension. The effect of compactification and fermion density on the chiral symmetry breaking is considered for periodic boundary conditions.

</details>


### [168] [Superheavy gravitational dark matter from runaway bubble collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30113)
*Cheng-Wei Chiang,Van Que Tran*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出一种仅通过引力耦合的稳定粒子可由失控气泡壁（runaway bubble walls）碰撞过程中的引力子交换（graviton exchange）成对产生，并给出其产额解析式，可在极高质量区间（约至 $10^{17}$ GeV）实现暗物质（dark matter）丰度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在冻结产生（freeze-in）机制失效的低温再加热（reheating）条件下，需要新的暗物质产生机制；论文考察仅靠引力相互作用的稳定粒子能否在相变（phase transition）气泡壁碰撞中被有效产生。

Method: 采用解析计算：在持续时间为 $1/\beta$ 的相变中，由失控气泡壁碰撞通过引力子交换产生一对仅引力耦合的Dirac费米子（Dirac fermion），导出其产额 $Y_X \simeq 6.8\times10^{-3}(H/\beta)(T_{\rm reh}/M_{\rm P})^3$，其中 $H$ 为真空能对应的哈勃率（Hubble rate），$T_{\rm reh}$ 为再加热温度，$M_{\rm P}$ 为约化普朗克质量（reduced Planck mass）。

Result: 产额在接近 $(H/\beta)M_{\rm P}/\nu^2$ 的质量之前保持平坦，其中 $\nu$ 为量级一的壁场位移（wall-field excursion）参数；由此，仅靠引力即可在再加热温度远低于粒子质量的条件下提供暗物质，质量上限约 $10^{17}$ GeV。

Conclusion: 引力子交换主导的气泡壁碰撞产生机制可突破冻结产生的限制，为高质量暗物质提供可行途径；该结论基于解析产额估算，未涉及具体数值模拟或实验约束。

Abstract: A stable particle that couples only through gravity is pair-produced in collisions of runaway bubble walls by graviton exchange. For a transition of duration $1/β$, a Dirac fermion reaches the yield $Y_X\simeq6.8\times10^{-3}(H/β)(T_{\rm reh}/M_{\rm P})^3$, where $H$ is the Hubble rate of the vacuum energy, $T_{\rm reh}$ the reheating temperature and $M_{\rm P}$ the reduced Planck mass. The yield is flat up to masses near $(H/β)M_{\rm P}/ν^2$, where $ν$ of order one sets the wall-field excursion. Gravity alone then gives dark matter up to about $10^{17}$~GeV at reheating temperatures far below its mass, where freeze-in fails.

</details>


### [169] [Two-loop correction to the complex-bion amplitude in a quasi-exactly solvable sextic oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29701)
*Adrian M. Escobar-Ruiz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文用两种图解（diagrammatic）计算方法分析准精确可解（quasi-exactly solvable）的六次势（sextic potential），并检验正、负耦合区域下微扰、瞬子（instanton）与聚束子（bion）贡献的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六次势的准精确可解性可通过因子化（factorization）精确固定基态能量，但这一结论是否能在微扰展开的图解层面被显式验证，以及解析延拓至负耦合后非微扰（nonperturbative）效应的结构，需要直接的计算检验。

Method: 正耦合下用费曼图（Feynman diagram）逐阶计算并显式核对微扰系数直至三阶的抵消；解析延拓到负耦合后分析零模（zero mode）、实瞬子（real instanton）与复中性聚束子（complex neutral bion）的贡献；并在一族精确约束解（exact constrained family）上通过两圈（two-loop）图解求和计算局域中位振幅。

Result: 正耦合下因子化精确给出基态能量 E0 并迫使所有微扰系数为零，图解计算显式验证了直至三阶的抵消。负耦合下形式零模不可归一化，实瞬子仍存在但其单瞬子贡献对 E0 为零，能量的领头移动来自复中性聚束子。直接图解求和给出 c_2L^diag = -0.750000(5)，与精确 WKB（exact-WKB）值 -3/4 吻合极佳。

Conclusion: 图解计算与因子化及精确 WKB 结果在数值上高度一致，说明该准精确可解六次势的非微扰结构可由瞬子—聚束子图像（instanton-bion picture）自洽描述，并为负耦合区域的非微扰能移提供了可靠的定量检验。

Abstract: In this study, a quasi-exactly solvable sextic potential is analyzed by two diagrammatic calculations. At positive coupling, factorization fixes the ground-state energy $E_0$ exactly and forces every perturbative coefficient to vanish. Using only Feynman diagrams, we verify this cancellation explicitly through third order. After continuation to negative coupling, the formal zero mode becomes nonnormalizable. A real instanton remains, but its one-instanton contribution to \(E_0\) vanishes. In particular, the leading shift in energy arises from a complex neutral bion. Taking an exact constrained family, we calculate its local median amplitude through two loops. The direct diagrammatic sum gives $c_{\rm 2L}^{\rm diag}=-0.750000(5)$, in excellent agreement with the exact-WKB value $-3/4$.

</details>


### [170] [Chiral crossover responses in a magnetic field: Finite-$μ_5$ matching in the NJL model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30535)
*Jingyi Chao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在Nambu-Jona-Lasinio（NJL）模型中，仅靠真空标定无法确定有限手征化学势（chiral chemical potential）$\\mu_5$ 附加相互作用的系数 $c_r$；通过匹配单位化手征微扰理论（unitarized chiral perturbation theory）给出的 $eB=0$ 标量磁化率峰值温度比，可锁定 $c_{\\mathrm{SC}}$ 与 $c_{\\mathrm{PV}}$，并显著压缩四种正则化处理在有限 $\\mu_5$ 下预临界温度响应的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限手征化学势下独立信息对NJL模型中预临界温度（pseudocritical-temperature）响应的约束作用，并检验不同真空正则化（soft-cutoff, SC 与 Pauli-Villars, PV）方案在有限 $\\mu_5$ 响应上的正则化依赖性（regularization dependence）能否被约束。

Method: 在磁场下采用NJL模型，比较软截断（SC）与Pauli-Villars（PV）两种真空正则化，各自结合直接处理（direct treatment, D）或磁有限差分方案（magnetic finite-difference scheme, MFDS），共四种处理；利用 $eB=0$ 时单位化手征微扰理论预测的标量磁化率峰值温度比来标定 $c_r$。

Result: 匹配得到 $c_{\\mathrm{SC}}=-0.07112$、$c_{\\mathrm{PV}}=0.22479$；固定系数后，在 $eB=0.1,0.2,0.4$ GeV$^2$ 下，归一化有限 $\\mu_5$ 诱导质量拐点温度变化的四处理间离散度分别缩小约 $7.5$–$15.3$ 倍（$\\mu_5=0.15$ GeV）与 $18.1$–$32.6$ 倍（$\\mu_5=0.30$ GeV）；标量磁化率峰值温度比在 $\\mu_5=0.15$ GeV 处的离散度分别缩小约 $74$、$27$、$14$ 倍。

Conclusion: 正则化依赖的有限 $\\mu_5$ 响应差异在采样的非零磁场下持续存在，且无需进一步匹配即可被有效压缩，说明 $eB=0$ 的独立信息可为有限磁场下的模型预测提供有效约束。

Abstract: We investigate how independent information at finite chiral chemical potential $μ_5$ constrains pseudocritical-temperature responses in the Nambu-Jona-Lasinio model under a magnetic field. Vacuum calibration alone leaves the coefficient $c_r$ of the additional interaction $δΩ_r=c_rμ_5^2(M-m_0)^2$ undetermined. We compare soft-cutoff (SC) and Pauli-Villars (PV) vacuum regularizations, each combined with the direct treatment (D) or the magnetic finite-difference scheme (MFDS). The reference SC and PV treatments exhibit opposite finite-$μ_5$ responses already at $eB=0$. Matching the $eB=0$ scalar-susceptibility peak-temperature ratio predicted by unitarized chiral perturbation theory gives $c_{\mathrm{SC}}=-0.07112$ and $c_{\mathrm{PV}}=0.22479$. Holding these coefficients fixed at $eB=0.1,0.2,0.4$ GeV$^2$ reduces the four-treatment spread of the normalized finite-$μ_5$-induced mass-inflection temperature change by factors of approximately $7.5$-$15.3$ at $μ_5=0.15$ GeV and $18.1$-$32.6$ at $μ_5=0.30$ GeV. For the distinct scalar-susceptibility peak-temperature ratio at $μ_5=0.15$ GeV, the spread decreases by factors of approximately $74$, $27$, and $14$ at the same magnetic fields, respectively. The reduction in regularization-dependent finite-$μ_5$ responses thus persists at the sampled nonzero magnetic fields without further matching.

</details>


### [171] [Fate of Vainshtein Screening for UV/IR Mixing with a $P(X)$ Higgs Classicalizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30764)
*Eléana Berteaux,Florian Nortier*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文检验了在规范化的k-essence与k-chameleon模型（P(X)类）中能否实现希格斯经典化（Higgs classicalization），发现由于屏蔽区域内部强耦合失效（strong-coupling breakdown），Vainshtein屏蔽（Vainshtein screening）无法围绕强局域源建立，故此类P(X)理论不适合作为希格斯经典化的框架；但结果不构成no-go定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典化（classicalization）是非Wilsonian框架，其有效场论在强耦合标度之上通过产生衰变为大量软量子的半经典物体实现紫外-红外混合（ultraviolet-infrared mixing），其关键要求是力载体轻于强耦合标度。将该机制嵌入电弱希格斯部门（electroweak Higgs sector）以缓解小等级问题（little hierarchy problem）是自然应用方向，故需检验具体模型中能否实现希格斯经典化。

Method: 在规范化的k-essence与k-chameleon模型的P(X)类中考察希格斯经典化的可能性；判据是理论须支持围绕强局域源（sharply localized source）的Vainshtein屏蔽，并尝试通过规范动能函数（gauge kinetic function）与chameleon屏蔽构建共生机制。

Result: 结果表明该类理论无法实现所需的Vainshtein屏蔽；即便借助规范动能函数尝试与chameleon屏蔽共生，仍因屏蔽区域内部出现强耦合失效（strong-coupling breakdown）而失败。

Conclusion: P(X)理论不能为希格斯经典化提供合适框架；但作者强调该结论仅针对特定模型类别，不应被解读为对希格斯经典化的no-go定理。

Abstract: Classicalization is a non-Wilsonian framework in which an effective field theory self-completes above its strong-coupling scale by generating semiclassical objects that decay into many soft quanta, thereby realizing ultraviolet-infrared mixing. A key requirement of this mechanism is that the force carrier be lighter than the strong-coupling scale. A natural application would be to embed classicalization in the electroweak Higgs sector to address the little hierarchy problem. In this work, we investigate whether Higgs classicalization can occur within the $P(X)$ class of gauged k-essence and k-chameleon models. For classicalization to occur, the theory must support Vainshtein screening around a sharply localized source. Our results show that it fails to do so, even when one attempts to engineer a symbiosis with chameleon screening through a gauge kinetic function, owing to a strong-coupling breakdown inside the screening region. These $P(X)$ theories therefore fail to provide a suitable framework for Higgs classicalization. Nevertheless, we emphasize that our results, obtained for a specific class of models, should not be interpreted as a no-go theorem for Higgs classicalization.

</details>


### [172] [New analytic constraints for the soft anomalous dimension bootstrap at three loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31168)
*Einan Gardi,Luke Lippstreu,Andrew J. McLeod,Laura Walsh,Zehao Zhu,Enrico Zunino*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用费曼积分的幺正切割与顺序不连续性的关系，推导出对软反常维度的新约束，并据此强化无质量情形、首次完成单质量情形的四极函数自举构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软反常维度（soft anomalous dimension）支配规范理论振幅的普适长程奇异性，其解析结构高度受限且适合自举方法（bootstrap methods）；本文旨在引入新约束以拓展可自举的范围。

Method: 重新审视幺正切割（unitarity cuts）与费曼积分顺序不连续性（sequential discontinuities）之间的关系，据此推导三圈（three loops）下非重叠双粒子道中某些顺序不连续性对每个贡献四极函数（quadrupole function）的 web 消失的约束，并用于自举。

Result: 证明上述约束对任意部分子质量成立，并在已知无质量（massless）与单质量（one-mass）情形显式验证；应用后显著强化无质量自举，并首次构造单质量四极函数，将每个四极函数在假设函数空间内确定至单一系数（该系数由匹配已有图解结果固定）。

Conclusion: 新约束与无物理分支点缺失、对称性及共线与 Regge 极限（Regge limits）所给约束 largely 互补，为自举多质量部分子振幅的三圈软反常维度开辟了途径。

Abstract: The soft anomalous dimension governs the universal long-distance singularities of gauge theory amplitudes. It exhibits a highly constrained analytic structure and has been shown to be amenable to bootstrap methods. In this paper we revisit the relation between unitarity cuts and sequential discontinuities of Feynman integrals and use it to derive new constraints on the soft anomalous dimension. At three loops, we show that certain sequential discontinuities in non-overlapping two-particle channels vanish for every web contributing to the quadrupole function that correlates four coloured partons. These constraints hold for arbitrary parton masses, and we explicitly verify them for the known massless and one-mass cases. We then demonstrate their constraining power by substantially strengthening the massless bootstrap and carrying out the first bootstrap construction of the one-mass quadrupole function. The new constraints are largely complementary to those arising from the absence of unphysical branch points, symmetries, and collinear and Regge limits. With their help, we determine each quadrupole function within the assumed function space up to a single coefficient, which we fix by matching to existing diagrammatic results. These results open the way to bootstrapping the three-loop soft anomalous dimension for amplitudes with several massive partons.

</details>

### [173] [Thermodynamics of QCD+QED from the lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29423)
*G. Endrődi,E. Garnacho-Velasco,G. Markó,L. Pethő*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 该会议论文分析有限温度下量子电动力学与量子色动力学耦合理论（QCD+QED）的结构及其格点模拟策略，并给出新的格点模拟结果，重点考察电磁耦合在系统热力学中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零温下QED修正已可微扰处理且为精确物理观测量所需，但文献迹象表明有限温度下引入QED可能引发非微扰效应，改变相图结构，因为耦合理论具有严格的中心对称性（center symmetry）。

Method: 分析QCD+QED耦合理论的结构及其在格点（lattice）上的多种模拟策略，并开展有限温度格点QCD+QED模拟；同时讨论耦合理论的新特征及模拟所面临的挑战，指出需发展新算法。

Result: 给出有限温度格点QCD+QED模拟的新结果，聚焦电磁耦合（electromagnetic coupling）在该系统热力学中的作用，并揭示耦合理论中出现的新特征与模拟挑战。

Conclusion: 要从第一性原理（first principles）理解QCD+QED相图，需针对有限温度耦合理论的特性开发新算法。

Abstract: QED corrections to observables in QCD are by now well-understood at zero temperature, where QED effects are perturbative and required for precision physics observables, such as the neutron-proton mass difference or the muon anomalous magnetic moment, among many others. However, there are indications in the literature that at finite temperature the introduction of QED can lead to non-perturbative effects, affecting the structure of the phase diagram, since the combined theory is exactly center-symmetric. In this proceedings article, we analyze the structure of the combined theory and the different strategies to simulate it on the lattice, as well as presenting novel results of lattice QCD+QED simulations at finite temperature, focusing on understanding the role of the electromagnetic coupling in the thermodynamics of this system. We also discuss new features that arise in the combined theory and the challenges that these simulations entail, which require the development of new algorithms in order to understand the phase diagram of QCD+QED from first principles.

</details>

### [174] [Optimal isotropic three-material conducting composites with an ideal conductor, in the plane and in space.Bounds and optimal structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29235)
*Andrej Cherkaev,Leonid Gibiansky*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一种三维各向同性三材料导电复合材料的最优设计，目标是在体积分数给定、其中一相为理想导体（ideal conductor）的条件下最小化等效电导率（effective conductivity），并给出了下界及其可达性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望确定给定材料属性与体积分数下等效电导率的极值，并刻画实现该极值的最优微结构（optimal structures），从而将能量下界与可构造的几何构型对应起来。

Method: 先以材料属性与体积分数为参数推导等效电导率的下界，利用受约束的多凸包络（constrained polyconvex envelope），并通过拉格朗日乘子（Lagrange multipliers）施加二次与三次零拉格朗日量（null Lagrangians）；随后假设最优结构中的场为正半定（positive semidefinite），并结合三次零拉格朗日量使该界成为精确界；最后通过构造最优结构证明该界可达。

Result: 所得界确定了极值电导率以及最优结构中相应的支撑场（support fields）集合，且依参数不同分为五个具有不同解析表达式的区域；通过构造球形原子（spherical atoms）——即中等导电材料核心外包覆缠绕的最优平面结构层，并在两个极端区域额外加入体积分数过量材料形成的外壳（crust）——实现了所有区域中该界的可达性。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了三材料三维各向同性导电复合材料等效电导率的一个精确下界，并通过显式的球形原子型最优微结构证明了界在每个参数区域内均可达到，从而完整刻画了极值电导率与相应的最优构型。

Abstract: The paper describes an optimal isotropic three-dimensional, three-material conducting composite. One material is an ideal conductor, the volume fractions are prescribed, and the objective is to minimize the effective conductivity over all admissible layouts.
  We first derive a lower bound for the effective conductivity as a function of the material properties and volume fractions. The composite energy is bounded below by a constrained polyconvex envelope, where quadratic and cubic null Lagrangians are imposed through Lagrange multipliers. We further assume that the fields in optimal structures are positive semidefinite. Together with the use of the cubic null Lagrangian, this assumption makes the bound exact. The resulting bound determines the extremal conductivity and the set of associated support fields in optimal structures. Depending on the parameters, it has five regions with different analytic expressions.
  We then prove the bound attainability by constructing optimal structures that realize the bound in every region. They are spherical atoms: a core of the intermediate-conducting material, surrounded by a mantle of wrapped optimal planar structures. In the two extreme regions, a crust is added from the material whose prescribed volume fraction is excessive.

</details>

### [175] [JAX-Nano: A GPU-Accelerated Differentiable Phonon Boltzmann Transport Solver for Nanoscale Heat Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28926)
*Bo Zhang,Amy Zhaomei Zhang*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: JAX-Nano 是一个 GPU 加速、端到端可微的声子玻尔兹曼输运方程（BTE）求解器，统一处理稳态与瞬态问题，并支持基于梯度的逆参数辨识与大规模三维纳米热输运仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当半导体器件尺寸接近声子平均自由程（phonon mean free path）时，热输运进入弹道与准弹道（ballistic/quasi-ballistic）区域，傅里叶定律（Fourier's law）失效，而声子 BTE 虽能刻画非平衡效应却计算代价高昂、且难以进行参数反演与灵敏度分析。

Method: 在统一的有限体积（finite-volume）框架下集成稳态与瞬态 BTE 格式，支持结构化/非结构化网格、按模态分辨（mode-resolved）的输运、声子散射、体热源及多种边界条件；借助自动微分（automatic differentiation）直接对输运性质、边界条件、生热率与几何参数求梯度，无需单独推导伴随方程。

Result: 数值结果在扩散—弹道（diffusive-to-ballistic）全区间与解析解及已有 BTE 求解器吻合；展示了单鳍、双鳍及阵列鳍式场效应晶体管（FinFET）构型的三维算例，并实现稳态与瞬态下单/多参数辨识，包括同时反演按模态分辨的声子弛豫时间（relaxation time）与空间分布生热率；同时评估了 GPU 在空间分辨率、角度离散与声子能带维度的性能与可扩展性，并与基于 MPI 的 BTE 求解器对比。

Conclusion: JAX-Nano 为三维声子输运、自动微分与逆参数辨识提供了统一计算框架，可支撑纳米尺度热系统的灵敏度分析与优化设计。

Abstract: As semiconductor device dimensions approach phonon mean free paths, heat transport enters ballistic and quasi-ballistic regimes where Fourier's law becomes inadequate. The phonon Boltzmann transport equation (BTE) describes these nonequilibrium effects but presents significant computational challenges. This work introduces JAX-Nano, a GPU-accelerated, end-to-end differentiable phonon BTE solver for nanoscale heat transport. The solver integrates steady-state and transient BTE formulations within a unified finite-volume framework, supporting structured and unstructured meshes, mode-resolved transport, phonon scattering, volumetric heat generation, and multiple boundary conditions. Automatic differentiation enables gradient computation with respect to transport properties, boundary conditions, heat-generation rates, and geometric parameters without separately derived adjoint equations. Numerical predictions are validated against analytical solutions and established BTE solvers across diffusive-to-ballistic regimes. Applications to complex three-dimensional geometries are demonstrated using single-fin, double-fin, and arrayed fin field-effect transistor (FinFET) configurations. Gradient-based inverse learning enables single- and multi-parameter identification, including simultaneous recovery of mode-resolved phonon relaxation times and spatially distributed heat-generation rates in steady-state and transient problems. GPU performance and scalability are evaluated with respect to spatial resolution, angular discretization, and phonon bands, including comparisons with an MPI-based BTE solver. JAX-Nano provides a unified computational framework for three-dimensional phonon transport, automatic differentiation, and inverse parameter identification, enabling sensitivity analysis and optimization of nanoscale thermal systems.

</details>

### [176] [Measuring the Neutrino Mass with $^{241}$Pu](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29122)
*Mustafa Anaam,Nicolo de Groot,Inge van Rens*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 该摘要提出以钚-241（$^{241}$Pu）替代氚（tritium）作为绝对中微子质量（absolute neutrino mass）测量的候选同位素，并讨论抑制铀-237（$^{237}$U）β衰变本底的策略，比较两者在不同本底抑制水平下的中微子质量灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氚（tritium）核质量低，反冲动能较大，使分子氚（$T_2$）及束缚于基底上的原子氚测量受限；$^{241}$Pu的β衰变端点能量与半衰期同氚相近，但核质量大得多，可能成为有吸引力的替代方案。

Method: 摘要未给出具体实验或模拟方法，仅说明将讨论抑制$^{237}$U β衰变本底的策略，并以本底抑制程度为变量，对比$^{241}$Pu与氚的预期中微子质量灵敏度（projected neutrino-mass sensitivity）。

Result: 摘要属于方案性讨论，未给出定量结果；作者指出$^{241}$Pu的主要挑战是$^{237}$U β衰变带来的本底，并给出随本底抑制变化的灵敏度比较框架。

Conclusion: $^{241}$Pu在大核质量方面具有潜在优势，但其可行性取决于$^{237}$U本底的抑制效果；其相对氚的灵敏度优势需在本底抑制达到一定水平后才可能显现（摘要未明确具体阈值）。

Abstract: Tritium has long been the isotope of choice for measurements of the absolute neutrino mass. Its low nuclear mass leads to substantial recoil energy, limiting measurements with both molecular tritium ($T_2$) and atomic tritium bound to a substrate. With a beta-decay endpoint energy and half-life similar to those of tritium, but a much larger nuclear mass, $^{241}$Pu may offer an interesting alternative. Its principal challenge is background from the beta decay of $^{237}$U. We discuss strategies for suppressing this background and compare the projected neutrino-mass sensitivities of $^{241}$Pu and tritium as a function of the background suppression.

</details>


### [177] [Probing radial flow via the differential flow coefficient $v_0(p_T)$ using CMS open data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30904)
*Allan E. F. G. Ferreira,Thiago S. Domingues,Matthew Luzum,Cesar A. Bernardes*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 利用 CMS 开放数据测量 Pb+Pb 碰撞中径向流系数 $v_0(p_T)$ 及其 $p_T$ 积分量 $v_0$，发现标度比 $v_0(p_T)/v_0$ 在低 $p_T$ 具有跨能量、跨中心度的近似普适性，并结合 Jetscape 模型与 Sobol 敏感性分析识别主导参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 径向流涨落（radial flow fluctuations）的能量依赖及模型参数来源尚不清晰，需要系统测量并与多阶段混合模型对比，以厘清各物理参数对 $v_0$ 相关观测量方差的贡献。

Method: 基于 CMS 开放数据测量 $\sqrt{s_{NN}}=2.76$ TeV 下 50--60% 与 60--70% 中心度（centrality）的 $v_0(p_T)$ 与 $v_0$，并与 ATLAS 5.02 TeV 结果比较；采用基于主成分分析（principal component analysis）训练的高斯过程仿真器（Gaussian process emulator）探索 Jetscape 的 17 维参数空间，并用 Sobol 指数进行全局方差敏感性分析（global variance-based sensitivity analysis）。
