Result: $v_0(p_T)/v_0$ 在 $p_T\lesssim3$ GeV 内近似与碰撞能量和中心度无关；敏感性分析显示核子涨落参数 $\sigma_k$ 是 $v_0$、$v_0(p_T)$ 及标度比方差的主导来源，其次为自由流参数 $\tau_R$、$\alpha$ 与核子宽度 $w$；对 $(\zeta/s)_{\max}$ 的敏感性在 $v_0$ 上较弱但在 $v_0(p_T)$ 与标度比上增强。

Conclusion: 在 $p_T$ 积分量与标度谱上标定的仿真器预测能较好描述低至中等 $p_T$ 数据，且 2.76 TeV 标定的参数亦可描述 ATLAS 5.02 TeV 的 $v_0(p_T)/v_0$，支持径向流涨落动量依赖的近似普适性。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive study of the differential radial flow coefficient $v_0(p_T)$ and its $p_T$-integrated counterpart $v_0$ in Pb+Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 2.76~\mathrm{TeV}$, using CMS open data for mid-peripheral centrality classes of 50--60\% and 60--70\%. The measured $v_0(p_T)$ distributions are compared with ATLAS measurements at $\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 5.02~\mathrm{TeV}$, revealing a clear energy dependence in the overall magnitude of radial flow fluctuations. Notably, the scaled ratio $v_0(p_T)/v_0$ exhibits an approximate universality that is largely independent of both collision energy and centrality at low $p_T$, persisting up to $p_T\sim3~\mathrm{GeV}$, suggesting that the integrated $v_0$ encodes most of the energy-dependent information. We compare the data with the \textsc{Jetscape} multi-stage hybrid model, using Gaussian process emulators trained on a principal component analysis of the model output to explore the full 17-dimensional parameter space. A global variance-based sensitivity analysis using Sobol indices shows that, for the Grad viscous correction, the nucleon fluctuation parameter $σ_k$ is the dominant source of variance for $v_0$, $v_0(p_T)$, and $v_0(p_T)/v_0$, followed by the free-streaming parameters $τ_R$ and $α$ and the nucleon width $w$. The sensitivity to the bulk viscosity peak $(ζ/s)_{\max}$ is weak for $v_0$ but increases for $v_0(p_T)$ and for the scaled ratio. Emulator predictions, calibrated on $p_T$-integrated observables and on scaled spectra, describe the data reasonably well at low and intermediate $p_T$. Parameters calibrated at $\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 2.76~\mathrm{TeV}$ also describe the ATLAS $v_0(p_T)/v_0$ data at $5.02~\mathrm{TeV}$, supporting a near universal momentum dependence of radial flow fluctuations across collision energies.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Higher-Order Modulational Instability in Spin-Orbit-Coupled Bose-Einstein Condensates: Finite-Raman Effects and Branch-Resolved Spectral Redistribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28514)
*Cui-Cui Ding,Boris A. Malomed,Qin Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 研究了等相互作用自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit-coupled, SOC）双组分玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose–Einstein condensate, BEC）中的高阶调制不稳定性（modulational instability）与非线性谱再分布，给出载波及任意阶边带的精确代数系数、几何功率谱及增益与最小谱衰减率间的精确关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清SOC与拉曼耦合（Raman coupling）如何组织多组分凝聚体中的高阶不稳定性与边带谱再分布，为相干非线性波动力学提供统一框架。

Method: 针对等相互作用SOC二元BEC，区分有限拉曼耦合（非可积，含非振荡与振荡不稳定区）与零拉曼极限两种情形，结合线性稳定性分析与直接数值模拟，并推导载波及任意阶边带的精确代数系数。

Result: 拉曼耦合重塑不稳定带并改变可增长谐波；零拉曼极限下SOC在固定载波波数处使相邻谐波在不稳定带与稳定间隙间切换，从而调控高阶不稳定性、呼吸子（breather）分支与频率跳变序列。所得精确几何功率谱统一描述载波耗尽、方向不对称性与边带衰减；高增益呼吸子分支可具有更小的最大载波耗尽、更慢的高阶谱衰减与更弱的方向不对称性；当SOC诱导的动量移动补偿载波波数时，即使k与k_L均非零谱仍可恢复对称。

Conclusion: SOC与拉曼耦合以可解析的方式组织高阶不稳定性与非线性谱再分布，所建立的统一框架适用于多组分凝聚体中的相干非线性波动力学研究。

Abstract: We investigate higher-order modulational instability and the nonlinear spectral redistribution in a spin-orbit-coupled (SOC) binary Bose--Einstein condensate with equal interactions. For finite Raman coupling, the system is nonintegrable and exhibits both nonoscillatory and oscillatory instability regimes. Raman coupling reshapes the instability bands and changes what harmonics can grow. Direct simulations show that this reorganization modifies the subsequent nonlinear sideband dynamics. In the zero-Raman limit, SOC shifts successive harmonics between instability bands and stability gaps at a fixed carrier wavenumber. It thereby controls higher-order instability, distinct breather branches, and frequency-hopping sequences. We derive exact algebraic coefficients for the carrier and arbitrary-order sidebands. This yields an exact geometric power spectrum for the complete infinite sideband sequence and a unified description of carrier depletion, directional imbalance, and sideband decay, including an exact relation between the instability gain and the minimum spectral decay rate. For two coexisting breather branches with the same background and modulation wavenumber, the higher-gain branch can have a smaller maximum carrier depletion, a slower high-order spectral decay, and a weaker directional imbalance. SOC can also restore a symmetric sideband spectrum when the SOC-induced momentum shift compensates the carrier wavenumber, even when both $k$ and $k_L$ are nonzero. These analytical and numerical results clarify how SOC and Raman couplings organize higher-order instability and nonlinear spectral redistribution, providing a unified framework for studying coherent nonlinear-wave dynamics in multicomponent condensates.

</details>


### [179] [A phenomenological decomposition of SRC-inspired occupation effects in imbalanced Fermi superfluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29791)
*Lucas A. Souza*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文提出一个按扇区分解（sector-resolved）的唯象框架，用受短程关联（short-range correlation, SRC）启发的动量分布分别修正配对项（模型 P）、阻塞项（模型 B）或两者（模型 P+B），并比较四种实现下非平衡双组分费米超流（imbalanced two-component Fermi superfluid）的相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对非平衡双组分费米超流的研究通常采用尖锐费米台阶（sharp Fermi-step）占据，但相互作用体系在费米面以下存在耗尽（depletion）、在高动量端存在尾巴，这种简化忽略了短程关联带来的占据修正。

Method: 引入扇区分辨的唯象框架，将预设的 SRC 启发式动量分布分别作用于反常配对贡献（模型 P）、失配诱导的阻塞项（模型 B）或两者（模型 P+B），并以理想情形（ideal）为基准，通过巨势景观（grand-potential landscape）与定密度重构，在三维和二维中确定配对/相分离到极化正常态的边界。

Result: 在两种维度下，配对扇区的修正几乎解释了临界极化（critical polarization）下降的全部幅度，而阻塞扇区的修正始终处于次要地位；因此相边界形成两对近乎简并的曲线：ideal ≃ B 与 P ≃ P+B。

Conclusion: 在所设定的唯象构造范围内，结果定量说明了预设的 SRC 启发式占据如何分别通过配对扇区与阻塞扇区传播到非平衡超流的相边界，其中配对扇区是主导通道。

Abstract: Imbalanced two-component Fermi superfluids are usually formulated with sharp Fermi-step occupations, although interacting systems exhibit depletion below the Fermi surface and a high-momentum tail. We introduce a sector-resolved phenomenological framework in which one prescribed short-range-correlation (SRC)-inspired momentum distribution modifies either the anomalous pairing contribution (model P), the imbalance-induced blocking term (model B), or both (model P+B). The four realizations---ideal, P, B, and P+B---are compared through the grand-potential landscape and through a fixed-density reconstruction of the paired/phase-separated to polarized-normal boundary in three and two dimensions. In both dimensionalities, the pairing-sector modification accounts for nearly all of the reduction in the critical polarization, whereas the blocking-sector correction remains subdominant. Consequently, the phase boundaries form two nearly degenerate pairs, ideal $\simeq$ B and P $\simeq$ P+B. These results quantify, within the stated phenomenological construction, how a prescribed SRC-inspired occupation propagates separately through the pairing and blocking sectors of an imbalanced superfluid.

</details>


### [180] [Universal thermodynamics and BKT transition of the planar Bose gas from weak to strong interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30407)
*Yaniss Rabahi,Ivan Balog,Adam Rançon*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 采用泛函重整化群（FRG）计算稀薄二维玻色气体在弱、强耦合下的普适热力学，发现状态方程对近似方案不敏感，而改进近似显著改善BKT转变的描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典场（classical-field）方法在强耦合区不再可靠，需要在弱至强耦合范围内定量描述二维玻色气体热力学与超流密度，并改进对Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless（BKT）转变的处理。

Method: 使用泛函重整化群（functional renormalization group, FRG），采用保留有效势（effective potential）完整场依赖及空间导数项的近似方案，对以往FRG计算加以扩展。

Result: 状态方程（equation of state）对近似方案不敏感，即使在强耦合下差异也仅百分之几；改进近似将BKT转变的描述提升至可再现超流密度的普适跃变（universal jump）与临界反常维度（critical anomalous dimension），精度在百分之几内。弱耦合下与经典场蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟吻合良好，较强耦合下偏离弱耦合成立的经典场标度（classical-field scaling）。

Conclusion: 该方案给出了跨越BKT转变的热力学与超流密度的定量描述，并覆盖经典场方法失效的强耦合区。

Abstract: We compute the universal thermodynamics of the dilute two-dimensional Bose gas at weak and strong coupling using the functional renormalization group (FRG). We employ an approximation that retains the full field dependence of the effective potential and spatial derivative terms, extending previous FRG calculations. We find that the equation of state is remarkably insensitive to the approximation scheme, with differences of only a few percent even at strong coupling. By contrast, the improved approximation substantially improves the description of the Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless (BKT) transition, reproducing the universal jump of the superfluid density and the critical anomalous dimension to within a few percent. At weak coupling, our results agree very well with classical-field Monte Carlo simulations, while at stronger coupling we observe deviations from classical-field scaling, valid at weak coupling. Our approach thus provides a quantitative description of the thermodynamics and superfluid density across the BKT transition, including in the strong-coupling regime where classical-field methods are no longer reliable.

</details>


<div id='physics.app-ph'></div>

# physics.app-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Analysis of Geometric Eigenmode Splitting in Planar Microwave Resonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29200)
*Hagen Papenburg,Jason Corn,Romain Speciel,Adham Naji*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 从第一性原理出发，提出一种解析方法，指导平面微波谐振器设计，实现仅分裂谐振模式而几乎保持中心频率不变的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面微波谐振器（planar microwave resonator）设计中的传统难题在于缺乏能够单独控制各谐振频率、或调节特定本征模式（eigenmode）间耦合、同时不影响中心频率等其他系统参数的几何机制；该能力对传感器、滤波器、天线及谐振网络设计具有变革意义。

Method: 基于第一性原理（first principles）建立解析方法（analytical method），通过建议特定微扰形状（perturbation shapes）来指导实际设计，以实现模式分裂（mode-splitting）的同时保持中心频率近乎不变。

Result: 独立数值仿真（numerical simulations）与带状线谐振器（stripline resonator）测量相互验证了该方法的有效性；理论给出的一阶估计（first-order estimate）在实践中于较宽的分裂带宽（超过中心频率的54%）内依然成立。

Conclusion: 所提方法可指导此前难以发现或优化的新颖设计，且其调谐效应在较宽带宽内保持有效，提升了方法的实用性。

Abstract: A traditional challenge in planar microwave resonator design is to find geometric mechanisms that can control individual resonant frequencies - or tune coupling between specific eigenmodes - without affecting other system parameters, such as the center frequency. Achieving this capability can be transformative for the design of microwave sensors, filters, antennas and resonant networks. Starting from first principles, we outline the development of an analytical method that guides practical design by suggesting specific perturbation shapes capable of achieving resonant mode-splitting while keeping the center frequency nearly stationary. This approach opens the door to novel designs that were previously difficult to discover or optimize. Independent numerical simulations and measurements of stripline resonators validate the effectiveness of this approach. The demonstrated tuning effect shows that the first-order estimate suggested by the theory persists in practice over a relatively wide splitting bandwidth (exceeding 54% of center frequency), increasing the utility of the proposed method.

</details>


<div id='nucl-th'></div>

# nucl-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Revealing Nuclear Shape through Identified-Hadron Flow Decorrelations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30429)
*Hadi Mehrabpour,Abhisek Saha,Li Yan*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 利用逐事件粘性流体力学（event-by-event viscous hydrodynamics）耦合强子输运，研究铀-铀与氖-氖碰撞中π介子、K介子、质子的动量依赖椭圆流退关联（elliptic-flow decorrelation），并提出交叉坐标（crossing coordinates）(P_cross, R_cross)以同时约束四极形变（quadrupole deformation）与三轴性（triaxiality）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论重离子碰撞通过集体流（collective flow）编码原子核形状，但识别粒子（identified hadrons）在区分不同形变模式方面的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 采用逐事件粘性流体力学耦合强子输运的混合框架，计算193 GeV的²³⁸U+²³⁸U中心碰撞与5.36 TeV的²⁰Ne+²⁰Ne碰撞中π介子、K介子和质子的动量依赖椭圆流退关联，并以π-K与π-p流因子化比（flow-factorization ratio）的交点定义交叉坐标（P_cross, R_cross）。

Result: 不同粒子种类呈现随动量和形变而异的响应，即使单一形变效应较弱，核形状信息仍保留在相对关联中；四极形变与三轴性的变化在交叉平面上产生不同轨迹，可对两类形状参数形成互补约束，该行为在高阶矩关联（higher-moment correlations）中持续存在，且Ne+Ne的能量比较揭示了碰撞动力学的影响。

Conclusion: 识别粒子的流退关联可作为探测与约束本征核几何（intrinsic nuclear geometry）的互补途径。

Abstract: Relativistic nuclear collisions encode nuclear shape in collective flow, yet the potential of identified hadrons to disentangle deformation modes remains largely unexplored. Using event-by-event viscous hydrodynamics coupled to hadronic transport, we investigate momentum-dependent elliptic-flow decorrelations of pions, kaons, and protons in central $^{238}$U+$^{238}$U collisions at 193 GeV and $^{20}$Ne+$^{20}$Ne collisions at 5.36 TeV. The identified species exhibit distinct momentum- and deformation-dependent responses, with nuclear-shape information persisting in their relative correlations even when individual deformation effects are weak. We introduce crossing coordinates $(P_{\rm cross},R_{\rm cross})$, defined by the intersection of pion-kaon and pion-proton flow-factorization ratios. Variations of quadrupole deformation and triaxiality generate distinct trajectories in the crossing plane, offering complementary constraints on both nuclear-shape parameters. The corresponding behavior persists in higher-moment correlations, while an energy comparison in Ne+Ne reveals the influence of collision dynamics. Our findings establish identified-particle flow decorrelations as a complementary approach to probing and constraining intrinsic nuclear geometry.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [Oscillatory superconducting proximity effects in a buried Pt interlayer detected by FMR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28313)
*L. Peridon,T. Courtois,S. A Hussain,J. B. Tjernshaugen,J. Linder,S. ElMeligy,B. Újfalussy,K. Dumesnil,S. Mangin,S. Petit-Watelot,C. Ciccarelli,T. Hauet*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作利用宽频铁磁共振（broadband ferromagnetic resonance, FMR）测量NbN/Pt/Py与NbN/Cu/Py三层膜中超导邻近效应，发现自旋泵浦（spin pumping）对Py阻尼的抑制可作为探测埋藏金属中间层内超导关联与电子态密度的灵敏手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要在埋藏金属中间层中探测超导邻近效应（superconducting proximity effect）及电子态密度变化，并厘清超导与磁性邻近效应共存时的行为，因此寻找一种灵敏且非局域的探测手段。

Method: 采用宽频铁磁共振测量NbN/Pt/Py和NbN/Cu/Py三层膜，以超导转变温度（Tc）以下自旋泵浦对Py阻尼贡献的降低作为探针；并结合准经典理论（quasiclassical theory）对结果进行建模解释。

Result: Cu中间层中阻尼降低至少可持续到20 nm，与Cu中超导相干长度（coherence length）大于100 nm相符；Pt中间层则表现出非单调的厚度依赖，并在5 nm以上被完全抑制，该振荡可由Pt中超导与磁性邻近效应共存所导致的超导序参量和电子态密度振荡解释。

Conclusion: 自旋泵浦阻尼被确立为探测埋藏金属层中超导邻近效应的灵敏探针（sensitive probe）。

Abstract: We investigate superconducting proximity effects in NbN/Pt/Py and NbN/Cu/Py trilayers using broadband ferromagnetic resonance (FMR). The reduction of the spin-pumping contribution to the Py damping below the superconducting transition temperature (Tc) is used to probe the superconducting correlations and electronic density of state in the buried metallic interlayer. For a Cu interlayer, the damping reduction remains finite up to at least 20 nm, in agreement with the large coherence length above 100nm in Cu. In contrast, the damping reduction for a Pt interlayer exhibits a non-monotonic thickness dependence and is fully suppressed above 5 nm. Using the quasiclassical theory, we are able to explain the oscillation in damping reduction below Tc with the coexistence of superconducting and magnetic proximity effects in Pt, which generates oscillations of the superconducting order parameter and electronic density of state in the exchange-splitted Pt interlayer. Our results establish spin-pumping damping as a sensitive probe of superconducting proximity effects in buried metallic layers.

</details>


### [184] [Curling Superconducting Vortices by Nematic Fluxon-Skyrmion Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28479)
*Xiaoqing Bao,Shiyuan Wang,Dekun Shen,Guibin Lan,Tianqi Wang,Yuelin Zhang,Guoqiang Yu,Ka Shen,Haiwen Liu,Jinxing Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在第二类超导体/手性磁体异质结中观测到约两个数量级增强的偶场类霍尔信号，且其极性随正交电流方向反转，源于向列型磁通子-斯格明子相互作用驱动的定向涡旋流动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米尺度上确定性调控涡旋动力学（vortex dynamics）是提升超导体临界阈值与设计下一代超导量子器件的关键，但超导体本征的涡旋钉扎势（vortex pinning landscapes）使该目标在科学上仍具挑战性。

Method: 构建第二类超导体/手性磁体异质结（type-II superconductor/chiral magnet heterostructure），在磁通流（flux flow）与磁斯格明子（magnetic skyrmions）共存的场-温窗口内测量横向输运响应，并借助扩展的涡旋黏度张量（extended vortex-viscosity tensor）分析向列型磁通子-斯格明子相互作用（nematic fluxon-skyrmion interaction）。

Result: 观测到约两个数量级增强的偶场类霍尔信号（even-in-field Hall-like signal），其极性在正交电流方向间反转，表明存在沿轨道方向的涡旋流动；对混合体系中斯格明子的纳米尺度确定性操控可实现对耗散行为的选择性与可逆调控。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了可重构的磁通子输运（reconfigurable fluxon transport），类比冬奥会冰壶的精确操控，为可编程拓扑与超导量子器件（programmable topological and superconducting quantum devices）的探索开辟新途径。

Abstract: Superconducting vortex represents a topological entity in type-II superconductors, where the prototypical manifestation of such a macroscopic quantum state holds great promise for applications in future quantum technologies. Deterministic control of the vortex dynamics at the nanoscale is central to enhance superconductors' critical thresholds and design next-generation superconducting quantum devices. However, it is still scientifically challenging due to the intrinsic nature of the vortex pinning landscapes in superconductors. Here, we observe an even-in-field Hall-like signal with about two order of magnitude enhancement in a type-II superconductor/chiral magnet heterostructure, which confines to the field-temperature window where the flux flow coexists with magnetic skyrmions. The polarity of such a giant even-in-field transverse response reverses between orthogonal current directions, suggesting an on-track vortex flow driven by a nematic fluxon-skyrmion interaction analyzed via the extended vortex-viscosity tensor. More critically, a deterministic manipulation of skyrmions at the nanoscale within the hybrid system enables selective and reversible controls over the dissipative behavior and therefore the reconfigurable fluxon transport, mimic the precise control of curling stone in winter Olympic, thereby opening a new avenue towards the exploration of programmable topological and superconducting quantum devices.

</details>


### [185] [A High-Frequency Flux-Driven DC-SQUID Threshold Detector at the Single-Microwave-Photon Energy Scale: A Numerical Feasibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28627)
*M. Y. Wang,Y. Q. Chai,S. N. Wang,L. F. Wei*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于高频磁通驱动直流超导量子干涉仪（DC-SQUID）的非平衡阈值探测器，通过数值求解RCSJ方程证明其可在单光子能量尺度上统计区分弱微波脉冲，且所需实验条件比低阻尼快速扫描方案更易实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前作表明快速扫描偏置电流可使电流偏置约瑟夫森结阈值探测器（CBJJ-JTD）脱离平衡并抑制热噪声，但所需极低阻尼（β≤10⁻⁴）与极快扫描（v/β≥1）在当前结器件与低通滤波低温测量线路中难以实现，故需更易实验落地的方案。

Method: 采用高频磁通驱动的DC-SQUID结构，通过数值求解电阻-电容-电感并联结（RCSJ）方程，计算有/无微波输入时驱动与非驱动SQUID的切换电流分布（SCD），并以接收者操作特性（ROC）曲线及曲线下面积（AUC）量化探测性能，同时估计相位选择性与动态范围。

Result: 外加磁通驱动循环超导电流并在整个偏置电流扫描过程中维持非平衡相位动力学，从而无需快速扫描或依赖初始相位决定的瞬态振荡；在典型实验参数下，高频磁通驱动降低热噪声影响，实现对单光子能量尺度弱微波脉冲的统计判别。

Conclusion: 高频磁通驱动的DC-SQUID构成一种实验可及的非平衡阈值探测器，可缓解热噪声限制并支持单光子量级微波探测，同时给出了相位选择性与动态范围等探测器特性的数值估计。

Abstract: In our recent work (Phys. Rev. B \textbf{113}, 224503 (2026)), we showed numerically that rapidly sweeping the bias current of a current-biased Josephson junction threshold detector (CBJJ-JTD) drives it out of equilibrium. This substantially reduces the effect of thermal noise on microwave detection and may enable threshold detection near the energy of a single microwave photon. The scheme requires very low damping ($β\leq 10^{-4}$) and a very fast bias-current sweep ($v/β\geq 1$), both of which are difficult to achieve with available junctions and low-pass-filtered cryogenic measurement lines. Here we propose a more experimentally accessible nonequilibrium threshold detector based on a high-frequency flux-driven direct-current superconducting quantum interference device (DC-SQUID) and investigate it by numerically solving the resistively and capacitively shunted junction (RCSJ) equations. The applied flux drives a circulating supercurrent and sustains nonequilibrium phase dynamics throughout the bias-current sweep, removing the need for a fast sweep or for transient oscillations set by the initial phase. Using typical experimental device parameters, we calculate switching-current distributions (SCDs) with and without a microwave input for both driven and undriven SQUIDs. We quantify detection performance using receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curves and their area under the curve (AUC). The simulations show that high-frequency flux drive reduces the impact of thermal noise and enables statistical discrimination of weak microwave pulses at the single-photon energy scale. We also numerically estimate detector characteristics such as phase selectivity and dynamic range.

</details>


### [186] [Minimal Three-Band Model and $d$-Wave Superconductivity in La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_8$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28747)
*Ao Zhang,Chao Deng,Wenfeng Wu,Run Yang,Zhixiang Shi,Liang Si*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文结合密度泛函理论（DFT）、动力学平均场理论（DMFT）、随机相位近似（RPA）与涨落交换近似（FLEX）研究La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_8$的电子结构与超导配对对称性，发现其低能态由三个Ni-3$d_{x^2-y^2}$轨道主导并呈现$d_{x^2-y^2}$波配对，计算$T_C\approx10\,$K与实验一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期在Pr、Y取代的La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_8$中观察到常压超导，促使本文系统考察该体系的电子结构与超导配对对称性，以厘清其与铜氧化物及无限层镍氧化物的关系。

Method: 采用多方法组合：DFT分析能带与轨道成分；多轨道DMFT计算$d_{x^2-y^2}$填充并对比仅$d_{x^2-y^2}$模型与完整Ni-3$d$模型；基于简化模型开展RPA与FLEX计算以判定配对不稳定性。

Result: DFT显示低能态主要来自三个Ni-3$d_{x^2-y^2}$轨道且$d$-$p$杂化较弱；DMFT给出平均填充约0.87，接近无限层镍氧化物最优值；两种模型电子性质几乎一致，验证了简化模型的有效性；RPA与FLEX一致给出主导的$d_{x^2-y^2}$波配对不稳定性，$T_C$约为10 K。

Conclusion: La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_8$的电子结构与空穴掺杂铜氧化物高度相似，提示无限层镍氧化物中$Γ$、$A$点附近的电子口袋对超导可能并非必需；计算得到的$T_C$与实验观测吻合良好。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent observation of ambient-pressure superconductivity in Pr- and Y-substituted La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_8$, we combined density-functional theory (DFT), dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT), random phase approximation (RPA), and fluctuation exchange approximation (FLEX) methods to study its electronic structure and superconducting pairing symmetry. DFT band analysis indicates that the low-energy states are mainly dominated by three Ni-3$d_{x^2-y^2}$ orbitals with weak $d$-$p$ hybridization. Multi-orbital DMFT calculations yield an average $d_{x^2-y^2}$ filling of about 0.87, close to the optimal value for infinite-layer nickelates. By comparing the DMFT results of $d_{x^2-y^2}$ only and full Ni-3$d$ models, we find that their electronic properties are almost completely consistent, demonstrating the effectiveness of the simplified $d_{x^2-y^2}$ model. RPA and FLEX calculations based on this model consistently indicate a dominant $d_{x^2-y^2}$-wave pairing instability. These results establish La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_8$ as a system whose electronic structure closely resembles that of hole-doped cuprates, suggesting that the electronic pockets near the $Γ$- and $A$-points, which are characteristic of infinite-layer nickelates, may not be essential for superconductivity. The calculated superconducting transition temperature ($T_C$) is approximately 10\,K, in good agreement with experimental observations.

</details>


### [187] [Thickness-Dependent Morphology and Electrical Transport in Ultrathin Amorphous MoSi Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28862)
*O. O. Leha,O. V. Zraichenko,S. I. Kryvonohov,V. Yu. Lyakhno*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 研究了磁控共溅射制备的超薄非晶 Mo50Si50 薄膜的厚度依赖表面形貌与电输运性质，发现表面粗糙度低于0.2 nm且在1.92 nm处出现最小值，而输运在约1.5–2 nm以下偏离逆厚度标度并呈现超导体-绝缘体转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究超薄非晶 Mo50Si50 薄膜在厚度缩减至纳米尺度时，其表面形貌与电学输运性质如何随厚度演化。

Method: 采用磁控共溅射（magnetron co-sputtering）沉积薄膜，结合原子力显微镜（atomic force microscopy）表征表面形貌，并通过室温方块电阻与变温电导测量分析输运行为。

Result: 全厚度范围内均方根粗糙度低于0.2 nm并呈非单调厚度依赖；约1.9–5 nm时方块电阻近似逆厚度标度，低于约1.5–2 nm时明显偏离；30 nm薄膜出现超导转变，1.54 nm薄膜降温电阻显著上升表明超导体-绝缘体转变（superconductor-insulator transition），且其方块电导在2–20 K呈可重复的对数温度依赖。

Conclusion: 超薄 MoSi 存在厚度敏感的输运区间，对应异质结构仍保持较平滑表面形貌；对数电导行为与强无序准二维导体中扩散输运的量子修正（quantum corrections）一致。

Abstract: We investigate the thickness-dependent surface morphology and electrical transport of ultrathin amorphous Mo50Si50 films deposited by magnetron co-sputtering. Atomic force microscopy of Si-capped heterostructures on Si/SiO2 reveals smooth surfaces throughout the investigated thickness range, with RMS roughness below 0.2 nm and a non-monotonic thickness dependence characterized by a minimum at 1.92 nm. Room-temperature sheet resistance follows an approximately inverse-thickness dependence for films with nominal thicknesses of about 1.9-5 nm, whereas a pronounced deviation from this scaling develops below approximately 1.5-2 nm. Temperature-dependent measurements further reveal a superconducting transition in the 30-nm film, while the 1.54-nm film shows a strong resistance increase upon cooling clearly indicates a superconductor-insulator transition. In the 2-20 K range, the sheet conductance of the 1.54-nm film exhibits a reproducible logarithmic temperature dependence, Gs(T)=G0+Bln(T/T0), consistent with quantum corrections to diffusive transport in a strongly disordered quasi-two-dimensional conductor. These results identify a thickness-sensitive transport regime in ultrathin MoSi while demonstrating that the corresponding heterostructures retain comparatively smooth surface morphology.

</details>


### [188] [Charge-fluctuation hot lines and superconductivity in Ba$_{1-x}$K$_x$BiO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28879)
*Xiangang Wan,Sergey Y. Savrasov*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文提出Ba$_{0.6}$K$_{0.4}$BiO$_3$中约30 K超导的"缺失耦合"来源于Q=(π,π,π)/a处的动态电荷涨落，并用以过阻尼玻色子模型描述；结合的LDA声子与过阻尼模的Eliashberg计算可复现T_c≈25–30 K，并在热线上预言3–4倍增强的能隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ba$_{0.6}$K$_{0.4}$BiO$_3$中约30 K超导的起源长期未解：隧穿实验给出电子-玻色子耦合λ≈0.7–1.2，而密度泛函计算仅给出λ≈0.3（集中于50–70 meV的呼吸模），两者间约0.7的耦合缺口缺乏解释。

Method: 提出缺失耦合来自母体电荷歧化化合物有序矢量Q=(π,π,π)/a处的动态电荷涨落，引入过阻尼玻色子模型；基于x=0.4的DFT导带紧束缚拟合，推导自能、准粒子残量与能带重构的闭式结果，并进行各向异性Eliashberg计算与扩展Hubbard模型的RPA分析。

Result: 该玻色子仅在费米面"热线"（⟨111⟩方向附近k与k+Q均位于费米能级的小环）上强烈散射电子；求和规则使费米面平均耦合与玻色子频率及关联长度无关。取ω_m≈25 meV、λ≈0.7并与LDA声子结合，在2–3个晶格常数的关联长度下得到T_c≈25–30 K，热线上能隙增强3–4倍；更长关联长度下s波与局限于热线的变号T_{2g}态接近竞争。

Conclusion: 在扩展Hubbard模型的RPA中，该玻色子自然出现在R点电荷不稳定性附近，但纯电子涨落过弱且程过长；与呼吸模晶格畸变的耦合是增强该涨落的一种可能途径。

Abstract: The origin of superconductivity near 30~K in Ba$_{0.6}$K$_{0.4}$BiO$_3$ has remained a long-standing puzzle. Tunneling experiments give an electron--boson coupling $λ\approx 0.7$--1.2, whereas density-functional calculations give $λ\approx 0.3$, concentrated in the breathing mode near 50--70~meV. We propose that the missing coupling of about 0.7 comes from dynamical charge fluctuations at Q=(pi,pi,pi)/a, the ordering vector of the charge-disproportionated parent compound, and introduce an overdamped boson model to describe them. Using a tight-binding fit to the conduction band found from density-functional calculations at $x=0.4$, we show that such a boson scatters electrons strongly only on hot lines of the Fermi surface, small loops around the $\langle 111\rangle$ directions where k and k+Q are both at the Fermi level. We derive closed-form self-energies, quasiparticle residues and band reconstructions, including shadow bands and hot-line pseudogaps for long-range correlations, and show that a sum rule fixes the Fermi-surface-averaged coupling independently of the boson frequency and correlation length. Combining the LDA phonon with an overdamped mode of frequency $ω_m\approx 25$~meV and coupling $λ\approx 0.7$, anisotropic Eliashberg calculations reproduce $T_c\approx 25$--30~K for correlation lengths of two to three lattice spacings and predict a gap enhanced by a factor of 3--4 on the hot loops. For longer correlation lengths an $s$-wave state competes closely with a sign-changing $T_{2g}$ state confined to the hot loops. In the random-phase approximation for an extended Hubbard model, the boson emerges naturally near a charge instability at $R$, but purely electronic fluctuations are too weak and too long-ranged; coupling to the breathing-mode lattice distortion is one possible way to strengthen them.

</details>


### [189] [Tuning of superconducting and physical properties of Mo$_{1-x}$Si$_x$ thin films for Superconducting Nanowire Single-Photon Detector Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28927)
*Oleh V. Zraichenko,Oleksandr O. Leha,Sergei V. Bengus,Valerii Yu. Lyakhno*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本研究系统表征了直流磁控共溅射（DC magnetron co-sputtering）制备的超薄非晶 Mo₁₋ₓSiₓ 薄膜的常态电学性质，覆盖宽成分与厚度范围，并给出超导转变温度随 Si 含量变化的趋势，为探测器制备中的化学计量选择提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在刻画该沉积工艺可达到的参数空间（parameter space），并支撑探测器（detector）制备中薄膜化学计量（stoichiometry）的选取。

Method: 采用直流磁控共溅射在 Si/SiO₂ 与 Si/Si₃N₄ 衬底上沉积 x = 0.14–0.5 的 Mo₁₋ₓSiₓ 薄膜，用 X 射线衍射（X-ray diffraction）确认非晶结构、X 射线反射（X-ray reflectometry）标定厚度，并测量方块电阻与超导转变温度。

Result: 室温方块电阻为 110–1990 Ω/□，对应电阻率 125–478 μΩ·cm；方块电阻总体随 Si 含量增加和厚度减小而升高；较厚参考膜的超导转变温度为 5.2–7.7 K，且随 Si 含量单调降低；Si/SiO₂ 与 Si/Si₃N₄ 衬底间无系统差异，而 Al₂O₃ 上低厚度样品方块电阻偏高。

Conclusion: 结果界定了该沉积工艺的可及参数范围，但由于成分与厚度协同变化，两者效应难以定量分离；所得数据可为探测器制备中薄膜成分选择提供基础。

Abstract: We investigate the normal-state electrical properties of ultrathin amorphous Mo$_{1-x}$Si$_x$ films deposited by DC magnetron co-sputtering over a broad range of compositions and thicknesses. Films with Si contents of $x = 0.14$ to $0.5$ were deposited on Si/SiO$_2$ and Si/Si$_3$N$_4$ substrates (monocrystalline Si with SiO$_2$ and S$_3$N$_4$ buffer layers respectfully); X-ray diffraction confirms an amorphous structure across the full composition range, while X-ray reflectometry is used to verify film thicknesses. The room-temperature sheet resistance ranges from 110 to 1990 $Ω/\square$, corresponding to resistivities of 125-478 $μΩ\cdot$cm. The sheet resistance generally increases with increasing Si content and decreasing film thickness, although the coupled variation of composition and thickness limits the quantitative separation of these effects. Additional measurements on thicker reference films show superconducting transition temperatures between 5.2 and 7.7 K, with $T_c$ decreasing monotonically at higher Si contents. A comparison of films on different substrate types shows no systematic difference between Si/SiO$_2$ and Si/Si$_3$N$_4$, while films on Al$_2$O$_3$ exhibit elevated sheet resistance at the lower thicknesses. These results characterize the accessible parameter space of our deposition process and provide a basis for the selection of film stoichiometry for detector fabrication.

</details>


### [190] [Auditing a superconducting database built by a large language model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29445)
*Wenjian Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文提出一套由大语言模型（large language model, LLM）驱动的文献抽取流程，用于从已发表文献中构建超导材料（superconducting materials）数据库，并以多级校验关卡对每条抽取结果进行溯源核查。流程从19,576条候选记录中经门控筛选后保留3,137条材料记录，抽样审计显示其机械性错误已被有效剔除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导材料数据散落于论文正文、表格与图形中，须逐篇阅读才能汇集；LLM虽可承担阅读工作，但核心难题在于信任（trust）——数值可能归属邻近化合物、计算得到的转变温度（transition temperature）未标明为计算值，或原文本身有误。

Method: 构建一条LLM文献抽取流水线（pipeline），将每个抽取值与其原文来源逐一对照，并设置级联式门控（cascade of gates）按证据类型拒收字段级抽取：逐字证据缺失、文本未将数值关联至该材料、源句读作计算结果。

Result: 流程共提出19,576条记录，门控拒收659,252条字段级抽取，其中644,438条因缺少逐字证据、13,121条因数值未与材料对应、1,693条因源句呈计算表述。最终数据库保留3,137条材料记录，每条附有来源句。两独立模型对按数据库比例抽取的500条记录进行审计：93.4%的受审记录其数值确实见于文本且文本所涉即记录所指化合物，91.8%为测量值而非预测值。

Conclusion: 门控机制可有效去除机械性错误（mechanical errors），但作者同时追问这种校验的效力边界（how far that checking goes），即溯源核查能覆盖到何种程度。

Abstract: The data on superconducting materials are published as measurements and scattered across the literature, in prose, in tables and in figures, so that gathering them into one place means reading the papers themselves. Large language models (LLMs) can read those papers, and databases built that way are appearing. The difficulty is not the reading but the trust: a number lifted out of a sentence can belong to a neighbouring compound, a computed transition temperature can be recorded without saying that it is computed, and the paper itself may be wrong. This paper describes a pipeline that extracts superconducting materials from the published literature with an LLM, checks every value against its source, and asks how far that checking goes. The pipeline proposed 19,576 records; a cascade of gates rejected 659,252 field-level extractions, 644,438 of them for want of verbatim evidence, 13,121 because the text does not attach the value to the material, and 1,693 because the source sentence reads as a calculation. The database that survives holds 3,137 material records, each with the sentence its value came from. An audit of the survivors by two independent models, on 500 records drawn in proportion to the database, shows that the gates remove the errors that are mechanical: in 93.4% of the audited records the value is in the text and the text is about the compound the record names, and 91.8% of them are measurements rather than predictions.

</details>


### [191] [Mixed-State Magnetotransport Below $T_c$ in Type-II Superconducting NbN Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29817)
*Sai Vaishnavi Kanduri,Danilo Bürger,Zichao Li,Mario Ziegler,Detlef Born,Sahitya V. Vegesna,Heidemarie Krüger*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文在NbN薄膜混合态下测量纵向与霍尔电阻，借助唯象电流分配模型分离耗散电流与超导电流贡献，并报告一个随驱动电流增大而增强的偶宇称横向信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第二类超导体（type-II superconductor）在临界温度$T_c$以下的混合态（mixed state）中，超导过程与耗散过程共存于磁场中，导致输运性质难以解读，需区分两类电流的贡献。

Method: 在蓝宝石与硅衬底上生长4 nm厚NbN薄膜，采用Van der Pauw构型在$T_c$以下以1 mA与2 mA驱动电流测量磁场依赖的薄层电阻（sheet resistance）$R^M_{xx}$与霍尔电阻（Hall resistance）$R^M_{xy}$；结合纵向响应与反对称化霍尔信号，用唯象电流分配模型（phenomenological current-partition model）估计耗散与超导电流贡献。

Result: 估计出随磁场变化的超导电流，以及由霍尔信号导出的有效耗散载流子密度（effective dissipative carrier density）与迁移率；并观测到一个偶场（even-in-field）横向信号，其幅度强烈依赖驱动电流。

Conclusion: 该偶场横向信号可能源于Abrikosov涡旋（Abrikosov vortex）的磁结构与超电流织构、涡旋动力学及库珀对电流重分布（Cooper-pair current redistribution）等混合态响应的组合；现有测量无法唯一区分涡旋、凝聚体或联合贡献。该模型提供了比较纵横向混合态响应的实用途径，但并不意味着两类电流通道在空间上直接分离。

Abstract: The transport properties of type-II superconductors in the mixed state below the superconducting transition temperature, $T_c$, remain difficult to interpret because superconducting and dissipative processes coexist in the presence of a magnetic field. To investigate this regime in NbN thin films, we measured the field-dependent sheet resistance ($R^M_{xx}$) and Hall resistance ($R^M_{xy}$) below $T_c$ in Van der Pauw geometry for 4-nm-thick NbN films on sapphire and silicon substrates at driving currents of 1 mA and 2 mA. By combining the longitudinal response with the antisymmetrized Hall signal, we use a phenomenological current-partition model to estimate the dissipative and superconducting current contributions. From this analysis, we estimate the field-dependent superconducting current together with the Hall-derived effective dissipative carrier density and mobility. We also observe an even-in-field transverse signal whose magnitude depends strongly on the driving current. We discuss this signal as a possible mixed-state response involving the magnetic and supercurrent texture of Abrikosov vortices, vortex dynamics, and Cooper-pair current redistribution. The present measurements do not uniquely distinguish vortex, condensate, or combined contributions. The model provides a practical way to compare the longitudinal and transverse mixed-state responses without implying a direct spatial separation of the two current channels.

</details>


### [192] [Valence band, electron correlation, and possible temperature dependent change in the electronic structure near $E_F$ of a candidate of ambient-pressure nickelate superconductors Sr$_3$Ni$_2$O$_5$Cl$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30088)
*Ryota Goban,Daiki Ootsuki,Kenichi Ozawa,Akira Yasui,Masayuki Ochi,Kazuhiko Kuroki,Ryo Matsumoto,Kensei Terashima,Yoshihiko Takano,Yuji Muraoka,Takayoshi Yokoya*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文利用光电子能谱（photoemission spectroscopy, PES）研究了常压双层镍酸盐超导体候选材料 Sr₃Ni₂O₅Cl₂（Sr3252）的电子结构，结合 DFT 与 DFT+U 计算确定 Ni 3d t₂g 主导的价带特征，并观测到费米能级附近显著的温变行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sr3252 被视为潜在的双层镍酸盐（bilayer nickelate）常压超导体，但其基础电子结构信息尚不充分，需要实验手段加以澄清以支撑对其超导电性的进一步探索。

Method: 采用光电子能谱测量价带谱并与密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）及 DFT+U 计算结果对比，同时进行费米能级（Fermi level, E_F）附近的高分辨变温 PES 测量。

Result: 价带谱显示以 Ni 3d t₂g 为主导的态位于约 0.6 eV 结合能处，并在约 0.2 eV 处出现可归因于 Ni 3d_{3z²−r²} 特征的肩峰；DFT+U（U = 3 eV）模拟谱整体上更好地再现了价带形状，从而给出电子关联强度（electron correlation）的实验估计。费米能级附近的高分辨谱呈现明显温度依赖，仅用光空穴—声子耦合导致的展宽无法完全解释，提示电子结构发生变化。

Conclusion: 研究提供了 Sr3252 的基础电子结构信息，为后续深入探究其超导电性提供依据。

Abstract: Photoemission spectroscopy (PES) studies of Sr$_3$Ni$_2$O$_5$Cl$_2$ (Sr3252), a candidate ambient-pressure bilayer nickelate superconductor, have been performed to investigate the electronic structure. Valence band spectra, which are compared with DFT and DFT+U calculations, reveal dominant Ni 3$d$ $t_{2g}$-derived states located around 0.6 eV binding energy with a shoulder around 0.2 eV that can be attributed to states with a dominant Ni 3$d_{3z2-r2}$ character. The overall valence band shape is better reproduced by the simulated spectrum of DFT+U (= 3 eV), providing an experimental estimation of the strength of electron correlation. High-resolution PES near the Fermi level ($E_F$) exhibits marked temperature dependence, which cannot be fully understood by a broadening due to coupling of a photo-hole to phonons alone and suggests change in electronic structure. The results provide fundamental electronic structure information of Sr3252, which is useful to further explore the superconductivity of Sr3252.

</details>


### [193] [Soliton Propagation of Zero Sound in Superfluid $^3$He-B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30108)
*J. A. Sauls*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文将氦-3 B相（$^3$He-B）视为由两能级“分子”（即库珀对，Cooper pairs）组成的介质，推导了零声（zero sound）与B相集体模（collective modes）相互作用的非线性方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在构建一个能统一描述零声与B相集体模耦合的非线性理论框架，并借此解释Polturak等人的相关实验现象。

Method: 把$^3$He-B建模为两能级“分子”（库珀对）介质，据此推导零声与集体模相互作用的非线性方程；同时验证在线性响应极限（linear response limit）下方程退化为已知的动理学理论（kinetic theory）结果。

Result: 所得非线性方程在线性响应极限下与动理学理论的已知结果一致，并讨论了该方程的孤子解（soliton solutions）。

Conclusion: 该理论框架借助孤子解与Polturak等人的实验相联系，为非线性零声与集体模相互作用的实验解释提供了可能途径。

Abstract: Nonlinear equations for zero sound interacting with the B-phase collective modes are derived by treating $^3$He-B as a medium of two-level ``molecules'' (Cooper pairs), The nonlinear equations reduce to known results from kinetic theory in the linear response limit. Soliton solutions to the nonlinear equations are discussed in context with the experiments of Polturak et al.

</details>


### [194] [Spin-controlled thermophase and noise-optimized thermometry in multiorbital quantum dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30315)
*Markus Plautz,Jeroen Danon,Jacob Linder*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文研究双轨道量子点约瑟夫森结中的热相位效应（thermophase effect），并评估其作为超导电路中亚开尔文温度差相位式温度计的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导电路中的准粒子谱（quasiparticle spectrum）接近粒子-空穴对称，热电响应通常微弱，因此需要探索将温度差转换为超导相位差的新机制，以满足低温量子技术中的热管理需求。

Method: 构建由带 Rashba 自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）与塞曼劈裂（Zeeman splitting）的双轨道量子点构成的约瑟夫森结，分析其热相位效应，并基于包含读出噪声、电流噪声以及轨道能量与磁场涨落的噪声预算（noise budget）评估温度计性能。

Result: 塞曼劈裂通过抑制库珀对输运增强热相位，自旋轨道杂化可移动轨道共振并反转响应符号；最优工作点在于平衡大准粒子响应与对参数涨落的敏感性之间的权衡。在实验启发的噪声假设下，投影噪声等效温度（noise-equivalent temperature）约为 $10\,\mu\mathrm{K}/\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$，并确定了线性标定保持准确的参数范围。

Conclusion: 栅极可调的量子点结为超导电路中亚开尔文温度差的传感提供了一条基于相位的途径。

Abstract: Converting temperature differences into electrical signals underlies both thermoelectric energy conversion and thermometry, which is essential for managing heat in cryogenic quantum technologies. Superconducting circuits typically show a weak thermoelectric response, since their quasiparticle spectrum is nearly particle-hole symmetric. In a Josephson junction, a temperature difference can instead be converted into a superconducting phase difference in an open circuit. We investigate this thermophase effect in a setup where the junction consists of a two-orbital quantum dot with Rashba spin-orbit coupling (SOC) and Zeeman splitting. A second orbital near the superconducting coherence peaks supports quasiparticle transport over a broad gate range. Zeeman splitting can enhance the thermophase by suppressing pair transport, while spin-orbit hybridization shifts the orbital resonances and can reverse the sign of the response. We assess the junction as a phase-based thermometer using a noise budget that includes readout and current noise together with fluctuations of the orbital energies and the magnetic field. The optimal operating points do not maximize the thermophase but balance a large quasiparticle response against sensitivity to parameter fluctuations. Under experimentally motivated noise assumptions, we obtain projected noise-equivalent temperatures of order $10\,μ\mathrm{K}/\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$ and determine the range over which a linear calibration remains accurate. Gate-tunable quantum-dot junctions thus offer a phase-based route to sensing temperature differences in superconducting circuits at sub-kelvin temperatures.

</details>


### [195] [Neutron Spin Resonance as Probe of Triplet Pair Density Waves in CeCoIn$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30395)
*David W. Tam,Ilaria Maccari,Stéphane Raymond,Barry Winn,Paul Steffens,Arno Hiess,Jacques Ollivier,Dariusz Jakub Gawryluk,Ekaterina Pomjakushina,Aline Ramires,Michel Kenzelmann*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 结合非弹性中子散射与对称性分析，研究重费米子超导体 CeCoIn5 面内磁场下自旋共振模的演化；在高场接近上临界场处发现第三个共振模，并用量子凝聚体集体激发（涉及跃迁至激发超导态）统一解释共振劈裂、新模式及相对强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋共振模（spin resonance modes）是非传统超导体最具特征的集体激发之一，但其微观起源尚未解决；传统上将其视为纯磁激发，无法解释重费米子超导体中观察到的丰富磁场演化行为。

Method: 结合非弹性中子散射（inelastic neutron scattering）与对称性分析（symmetry analysis），研究重费米子超导体 CeCoIn5 在面内磁场（in-plane magnetic field）下自旋共振模的演化，并将实验共振支与超导凝聚体的集体模（涉及跃迁至激发超导态）相对应。

Result: 在高场、接近超导上临界场（upper critical field）处发现一个强度较高的第三个共振模；所提框架可统一解释共振的场致劈裂（field-induced splitting）、新观测的高场模式以及各模式的中子散射相对强度。

Conclusion: 该工作首次提供直接证据，表明自旋共振模可与超导体的单-三重态激发（singlet-triplet excitation）相关联，为共振模的非纯磁起源提供统一解释。

Abstract: Spin resonance modes are among the most characteristic collective excitations of unconventional superconductors, yet their microscopic origin remains unresolved. Although commonly interpreted as purely magnetic excitations, this picture does not explain the rich magnetic field evolution of these modes observed in heavy-fermion superconductors. Here we combine inelastic neutron scattering and symmetry analysis to investigate the evolution of the spin resonance modes in the heavy-fermion superconductor CeCoIn$_5$ under an in-plane magnetic field. At high fields, approaching the upper critical field of the superconductor, we discover a third resonance mode with relatively high intensity. We demonstrate that the resonance branches observed experimentally can be interpreted as collective modes of the superconducting condensate involving transitions to excited superconducting states. This framework provides a unified interpretation of the field-induced splitting of the resonance, the newly observed mode at high fields, the relative neutron scattering intensities of each mode, and provides the first direct evidence that a spin resonance mode can be associated with a singlet-triplet excitation of a superconductor.

</details>


### [196] [Perfect Spin-to-Charge Signal Conversion via Inverse Spin Hall Effect in a Superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30781)
*Rodrigo de las Heras,Ilya V. Tokatly,F. Sebastian Bergeret*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 超导态下准粒子电导被强烈抑制，可使较小的自旋霍尔角（spin Hall angle）也实现接近1的自旋-电荷（spin-to-charge）信号转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自旋霍尔（spin Hall）机制中自旋霍尔角偏小，本质上限制了自旋-电荷转换效率，论文旨在寻找突破该限制的途径。

Method: 采用非局域自旋阀（nonlocal spin-valve）结构，将自旋积累注入超导体，并通过逆自旋霍尔效应（inverse spin Hall effect）转换为横向电荷信号；推导了包含有限谱展宽与有限温度的非平衡响应。

Result: 在超导态下，能隙以下被强烈抑制的准粒子电导（quasiparticle conductance）使电荷不平衡产生的电压可与注入自旋偏压（spin bias）相当，即使自旋霍尔角很小。

Conclusion: 超导性可克服自旋霍尔角偏小的限制，实现接近1的自旋-电荷转换；论文提出通过温度和自旋偏压依赖的探测器电压测量来检验预测。

Abstract: Spin-to-charge conversion is central to spintronics, but conventional spin Hall mechanisms are intrinsically limited by the small spin Hall angle. Here we show that superconductivity can overcome this limitation and enable near-unity spin-to-charge signal conversion. We consider a nonlocal spin-valve geometry in which a spin accumulation is injected into a superconductor and converted into a transverse charge signal through the inverse spin Hall effect. In the superconducting state, the strongly suppressed quasiparticle conductance below the gap allows the resulting charge imbalance to generate a voltage comparable to the injected spin bias, despite a small spin Hall angle. We derive the full nonequilibrium response including finite spectral broadening and finite temperature, and propose temperature and spin-bias-dependent measurements of the detector voltage to test our predictions.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [197] [Spherical orbits around a Newman-Janis-generated rotating Bronnikov black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28074)
*F. Subandi,H. S. Ramadhan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究由磁荷 Bronnikov 质量轮廓经 Newman–Janis 生成（Newman–Janis generation）的旋转正则几何中的球面测地线（spherical geodesics）：Kretschmann 标量在中心指数压低，黑洞情形下视界外球面光子轨道全部径向不稳定，决定出比 Kerr 更小且畸变的阴影，并检验能量条件与 Maeda 的五项几何判据 C1–C5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在考察该旋转正则（非奇异）度规在强场区是否可作为物理上合理的黑洞候选，通过球面光子轨道、阴影（shadow）、类时 ISSO 与能量条件（energy conditions）系统检验其几何与因果性质，并与 Kerr 情形对比。

Method: 采用解析几何方法：计算 Kretschmann 标量以判断正则性；分析球面光子轨道的径向稳定性；以不稳定光子轨道确定阴影轮廓（edge-on 观测者）；对类时运动求边缘稳定球面轨道与最内稳定球面轨道（ISSO）；检验零、弱、强、主导能量条件（null/weak/strong/dominant energy conditions）及稳定因果性（stable causality）与曲率界。

Result: Kretschmann 标量在中心指数压低，并在单叶域 r≥0 内跨盘与环光滑；黑洞情形下视界外物理允许的球面光子轨道均径向不稳定，而无视界构型可存在稳定球面光子轨道；不稳定轨道给出的边缘观测阴影较 Kerr 阴影更小且畸变；强场区能量条件出现违反，但渐近区及每个黑洞构型外部四类能量条件均满足。

Conclusion: 结合稳定因果性与曲率界，该度规满足 Maeda 提出的物理合理非奇异黑洞（nonsingular black holes）五项几何判据（C1–C5），支持该旋转正则几何作为物理上可接受的黑洞描述的候选。

Abstract: We investigate spherical geodesics in a Newman--Janis-generated rotating regular geometry constructed from the magnetically-charged Bronnikov mass profile. The Kretschmann scalar is exponentially suppressed at the center, and is smooth across the disk and ring within the single-sheeted domain $r\geq0$. We show that all physically admissible spherical photon orbits outside the event horizon are radially unstable in the black-hole regime, whereas stable spherical photon orbits can exist in horizonless configurations. The unstable photon orbits determine the shadow, which for an edge-on observer is smaller and distorted compared with the Kerr shadow. For timelike motion, we identify marginally stable spherical orbits and determine the innermost stable spherical orbit (ISSO). We further examine the null, weak, strong, and dominant energy conditions, finding violations in the strong-field region but satisfaction of all four conditions asymptotically and throughout the exterior of every black-hole configuration. Together with stable causality and the curvature bound, these results show that the metric satisfies the five geometric criteria C1--C5 proposed by Maeda for physically reasonable nonsingular black holes.

</details>


### [198] [Curvature-history tensor memory in inflationary cosmology: Component closure, ghost-free dynamics, and CMB BipoSH likelihood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28106)
*G. M. Rhythm*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个广义协变（generally covariant）的暴胀模型：曲率历史激活空间对称无迹张量记忆序参量，产生原初统计各向同性的标度依赖四极偏离。核心结论是，可逆dressing保持解数与物理辛范数符号，且代表性解远离Ostrogradsky奇异性阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文试图在广义协变框架下解释原初统计各向同性（statistical isotropy）的破缺，特别是标度依赖的四极（quadrupolar）偏离，由此引入由曲率驱动的张量记忆机制。

Method: 曲率源采用Kretschmann不变量的四维Gauss–Bonnet完成；用局部(C,χ)表示实现推迟理论（retarded theory）；投影张量扇区由dressing场Q_μν=ℰ_C Q̂_μν描述，导出完整二次Hessian，经精确块Schur补（block Schur complement）消去ADM约束与推迟响应变量，并做非线性ADM Legendre分析。

Result: 原始场Hessian呈精确同余形式ℰ_C†(□_Q−M²)ℰ_C，即便[B(C),□_Q]≠0，可逆dressing仍保持解数与物理辛范数（symplectic norm）符号；获得领头各向异性曲率作用，代表性暴胀解远离Ostrogradsky分支阈值；曲率依赖相势给出近似常数的激活平台，产生红外正则的振荡原初四极与精确标度依赖T/E BipoSH协方差。

Conclusion: 模型在广义协变框架下实现曲率激活的张量记忆机制，避免Ostrogradsky不稳定性并给出可观测的标度依赖四极各向异性；论文同时给出对应的cut-sky高斯与二次最大似然（quadratic maximum-likelihood）估计量。

Abstract: We present a generally covariant inflationary model in which curvature history activates a spatial symmetric-tracefree tensor-memory order parameter and generates a scale-dependent quadrupolar departure from primordial statistical isotropy. The curvature source is the four-dimensional Gauss--Bonnet completion of the Kretschmann invariant. A local $(C,χ)$ representation is used only to implement the retarded theory: fixed retarded Cauchy data remove the homogeneous response modes, so the negative-sign localized combination is not an independent phase-space degree of freedom. The projected tensor sector is described by the dressed field $Q_{μν}=\mathcal E_C\widehat Q_{μν}$ with $\mathcal E_C=\exp[B(C)\Box_{\widehat Q}/2]$. Its raw-field Hessian has the exact congruence form $\mathcal E_C^\dagger(\Box_Q-\mathsf M^2)\mathcal E_C$, so an invertible dressing preserves the solution count and the sign of the physical symplectic norm even when $[B(C),\Box_Q]\ne0$.
  We derive the full quadratic Hessian, eliminate the ADM constraints and retarded response variables through an exact block Schur complement, and obtain the leading anisotropic curvature action. A nonlinear ADM Legendre analysis shows that the representative inflationary solution remains far from the singular threshold associated with an Ostrogradsky branch. A smooth curvature-dependent phase potential gives an approximately constant activated plateau with subdominant memory energy. Finite activation then produces an infrared-regular oscillatory primordial quadrupole and an exact scale-dependent $T/E$ BipoSH covariance. We also give the corresponding cut-sky Gaussian and quadratic maximum-likelihood estimators.

</details>


### [199] [Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity corrections in Dark Dimension scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28132)
*George K. Leontaris,George Prampromis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究暗维度（Dark Dimension）场景中含一至两个微米级额外维时 Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet（EGB）黑洞的热力学、动力学稳定性及其现象学含义。核心结论是：仅在 D=5 存在正比热分支，其黑洞遗迹在紫外截断保护下可充当全部冷暗物质，并在 33 THz 处留下标量诱导引力波信号，同时 6D 情形给出可与广义相对论（General Relativity, GR）区分的对撞机判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗维度场景中微米级额外维可能改变黑洞的量子与热力学行为，而 EGB 修正项在 D≥5 时对引力动力学产生非平凡影响。论文旨在检验此类修正黑洞能否作为稳定遗迹解释冷暗物质（cold dark matter），并寻找可观测与实验判别信号。

Method: 针对渐近平直、中性的 Boulware-Deser 解（正 Gauss-Bonnet 耦合 α），在任意 D≥5 维推导质量、霍金温度（Hawking temperature）、Wald 熵与热容。结合 G_5=(8πM_*^3)^{-1}、α=M_*^{-2} 的参数化，分析弦紫外截断（string ultraviolet cutoff）对高阶谐波不稳定性的抑制，并采用体热辐射（bulk blackbody emission）模型计算寿命修正，最后给出对撞机基准。

Result: 正热容分支仅在 D=5 出现，温度在 r_h=2√α 处峰值 T_max=1/(8π√α)，零半径极限给出 M_lim≃1.06×10^{-12} g（M_*∼10^{10} GeV）。该分支可与经典不稳定区重叠，但弦紫外截断可安全抑制相应高阶模，故遗迹黑洞可构成全部冷暗物质；其观测特征包括 33 THz 的标量诱导引力波峰与霍金暴发（Hawking burst）的压制。D=6 时所有半径热容为负，小半径寿命标度为 τ∝M_0^3（对比 τ_GR∝M_0^{5/3}）；M_*∼10 TeV 时给出产生截面能量标度更陡、衰变多重数抑制（⟨N⟩∼4）的对撞机判据。

Conclusion: 在 D=5 EGB 暗维度框架下，受紫外完备性保护的遗迹黑洞可作为冷暗物质候选，并对应特征引力波与霍金辐射信号；D=6 情形因热容恒为负而不具备该稳定分支，但其寿命标度与对撞机特征提供了区分 6D EGB 引力与经典 GR、并标记向弦标度过渡的实验途径。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamics, dynamical stability, and phenomenological implications of Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet (EGB) black holes in Dark Dimension scenarios with one or two micron-sized extra dimensions. For the asymptotically flat, neutral Boulware-Deser solution with positive Gauss-Bonnet coupling $α$, we derive the mass, Hawking temperature, Wald entropy, and heat capacity in arbitrary dimension $D\ge 5$. A positive-heat-capacity branch arises only in $D=5$, where the temperature peaks at $T_{\max}=1/(8π\sqrtα)$ at $r_h=2\sqrtα$ and vanishes in the zero-radius limit at finite mass $M_{\rm lim}=3πα/(4G_5)$. For $G_5=(8πM_*^3)^{-1}$, $α=M_*^{-2}$, and $M_*\sim 10^{10}$ GeV, this yields $M_{\rm lim}\simeq 1.06\times 10^{-12}$ g. While this branch formally overlaps with classical instability zones, the string ultraviolet cutoff safely suppresses the responsible high-harmonic modes. Assuming a UV completion stabilizes these relics, we show they can constitute all of cold dark matter. We map their observational signatures, highlighting a scalar-induced gravitational-wave peak at 33 THz and the suppression of Hawking bursts. In $D=6$, the heat capacity is negative at all radii. Within a bulk blackbody emission model, we derive the leading large-mass lifetime correction and the small-radius scaling $τ\propto M_0^3$, compared to $τ_{\rm GR}\propto M_0^{5/3}$. For $M_*\sim 10$ TeV, we identify distinct collider benchmarks: a steeper energy scaling for the production cross section and a suppressed decay multiplicity ($\langle N \rangle \sim 4$). These provide clear experimental signatures distinguishing 6D EGB gravity from classical General Relativity, mapping the transition toward the string scale.

</details>


### [200] [Generalization of the Theory of Relativity Based on Finsler Geometry, Incorporating a Fundamental Mass at High Energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28141)
*Rustam Ibadov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文将狭义与广义相对论推广：除光速 c 外引入不变量基本质量 M，为有质量粒子能量设上限，并以 Finsler 几何（Finsler geometry）给出闭式表述与可观测修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是把 Kadyshevsky 的基本质量假设（fundamental-mass hypothesis）几何化，使"存在第二个不变量 M、有质量粒子能量有界"这一设想成为可检验的理论框架。

Method: 对投影变形的质量壳（mass shell）作精确 Legendre 变换，得到闭式 Finsler 函数 F_μ（μ=m/M），即由度规 g 与单位类时余矢量 u 构造的 Lorentzian Randers 度规（Randers metric）；其指标线（indicatrix）为平移 W=μu/(1+μ) 的 Minkowski 双曲面，故属 Zermelo 导航度规。

Result: 光线传播与爱因斯坦理论完全相同，而有质量粒子满足 E<M、时间膨胀规律被变形、自由落体依赖质量组成（η≃Δm/M）；Finsler–Einstein 张量方程在空间平坦宇宙中精确求解给出含 ∝μḢ 修正的 Friedmann 方程，de Sitter 空间保持精确成立。

Conclusion: 粒子能量观测、等效原理检验（equivalence-principle tests）与电子电偶极矩（electron EDM）上限可用于约束 M，从而把基本质量假设转为可实验检验的几何理论。

Abstract: We generalize special and general relativity so that, besides the speed of light $c$, an invariant fundamental mass $M$ bounds the energy of every massive particle. The exact Legendre transform of a projectively deformed mass shell gives, for a particle of mass $m$, a closed-form Finsler function $F_μ$, $μ=m/M$: a Lorentzian Randers metric built from the spacetime metric $g$ and a unit timelike covector $u$. Its indicatrix is the Minkowski hyperboloid displaced by $W=μu/(1+μ)$, so $F_μ$ is a Zermelo navigation metric whose symmetry group, the Lorentz group centred at $W$, realizes a relativity principle with two invariants, $c$ and $M$. Light propagates exactly as in Einstein's theory, while massive particles obey $E<M$, a deformed time-dilation law and a composition-dependent free fall, $η\simeqΔm/M$. The Finsler--Einstein tensor equation, solved exactly for a spatially flat universe, gives Friedmann equations with corrections $\proptoμ\dot H$ and leaves de~Sitter space exact. Observed particle energies, equivalence-principle tests and electron-EDM limits constrain $M$. The construction makes Kadyshevsky's fundamental-mass hypothesis geometric and testable.

</details>


### [201] [Imprints of perfect fluid dark matter distributions and topological defects on Born--Infeld AdS black hole signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28143)
*Faizuddin Ahmed,Ahmad Al-Badawi,İzzet Sakallı*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文构造了携带拓扑缺陷（整体单极子（GM）或宇宙弦（CS））并浸没于幂律暗物质晕（PFDM）中的静态球对称 Born–Infeld（BI）反德西特（AdS）黑洞解，系统分析其成像、轨道动力学、准周期振荡、准正规模与热力学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将四类独立形变——拓扑缺陷的立体角亏缺、BI 非线性电磁、PFDM 晕强度与 AdS 曲率——统一纳入同一黑洞度规，考察它们对可观测量的联合影响。

Method: 给出解析度规并进行极限检验；计算视界、光子球与阴影；求解最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）与周转频率，结合双峰准周期振荡（QPO）的相对论进动预言，用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）拟合五个吸积源；对标量扰动做高阶 WKB 准正规模（QNM）与灰体因子（GBF）计算，并在扩展相空间中分析范德瓦尔斯型 P–V 结构。

Result: 视界、光子球与阴影随缺陷强度 ξ 单调增大；标量势、QNM 谱与灰体因子随 ξ 增大而软化；临界点随缺陷、BI 标度与晕强度移动；四个扇区在成像、计时、衰减与热力学通道留下各自印记。

Conclusion: 四类形变具有可区分但不是彼此独立的观测敏感性，作者将其解读为参数敏感度而非观测上的分离，需联合多通道拟合方可判定。

Abstract: We construct a static, spherically symmetric \BI{} \AdS{} \BH{} that carries a topological defect, either a \GM{} or a \CS{}, and is immersed in \PFDM{}. The metric function collects four independent deformations of the Schwarzschild geometry: a constant solid-angle deficit $(1-ξ)$ from the defect, the nonlinear charge sector controlled by the \BI{} scale $β$, the logarithmic \PFDM{} term set by the intensity $λ$, and the \AdS{} curvature $Λ$. We verify that the solution reduces to the charged \AdS{}--\PFDM{} defect geometry as $β\to \infty$ and to Schwarzschild when all deformations switch off. The horizon, photon sphere, and shadow all grow monotonically with the defect strength $ξ$. We compute the innermost stable circular orbit and the epicyclic frequencies, read off the relativistic-precession predictions for twin-peak quasi-periodic oscillations, and confront them with five accreting sources through a Markov chain Monte Carlo fit. For spin-0 perturbations we obtain the scalar potential, the higher-order WKB \QNM{} spectrum, and the \GBF{}, all of which soften as $ξ$ rises. The extended phase space analysis yields a \VdW{}-type $P$--$V$ structure whose critical point shifts with the defect, the \BI{} scale, and the halo intensity. Throughout, the four sectors leave distinct imprints on imaging, timing, ringdown, and thermal channels, {\color{black} which we read as parameter sensitivities rather than as an observational separation of the four, a distinction a joint multichannel fit would be needed to settle}.

</details>


### [202] [Stationary branches of non-canonical spinor theories and their cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28149)
*Ugo Moschella,Mario Novello,Oliver F. Piattella*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究最小耦合引力的非规范（non-canonical）经典旋量场，揭示纯动能情形下场方程解分裂为两支：正则支满足无质量狄拉克方程并贡献由拉格朗日量加性常数决定的真空能，驻定支则使旋量场方程恒等满足、能动张量恒等于宇宙学常数。引入具非零极小值的势后，驻定组态成为宇宙晚期吸引态，可精确再现ΛCDM背景膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于考察非规范旋量拉格朗日量（依赖于费米动能标量与标量双线性项）在引力耦合下的解结构，尤其是场方程是否唯一确定旋量演化，以及此类场能否充当宇宙学常数或暗能量来源。

Method: 采用经典场论分析：对纯动能理论分解场方程，辨识出正则支（regular branch）与驻定支（stationary branch）；在空间平坦均匀宇宙中考察两支的宇宙学表现，并给出显含标量双线性项时的稀释律（dilution law）；再引入具非零极小值的势并分析晚期渐近行为与简单二次模型。

Result: 纯动能情形下解分为两支：正则支中旋量满足无质量狄拉克方程（massless Dirac equation），真空能由拉格朗日量中的加性常数确定；驻定支中旋量场方程恒等满足，能动张量对任意旋量位形、任意时空均严格等价于宇宙学常数，故旋量演化不唯一。平坦均匀宇宙中两支均表现为宇宙学常数源。引入具非零极小值势后，驻定组态成为晚期吸引态，趋近过程产生无压分量与有效宇宙学常数；二次模型中背景膨胀精确再现标准ΛCDM，真空能数值仍由加性常数控制。

Conclusion: 非规范旋量场的场方程存在解不唯一性，驻定支可提供不依赖具体旋量位形的宇宙学常数型能量，并在含势情形下成为晚期吸引子，从而在背景层面模仿ΛCDM，而真空能标度由拉格朗日量中的加性常数设定。

Abstract: We study a classical spinor field minimally coupled to gravity and described by a non-canonical Lagrangian depending on the fermionic kinetic scalar and the scalar bilinear. For purely kinetic theories, we show that the field equations split the solutions into two distinct branches. On the regular branch, the spinor satisfies the massless Dirac equation and contributes a vacuum energy determined by an additive constant in the Lagrangian. On the stationary branch, the spinor field equations become identically satisfied, while the energy-momentum tensor is exactly that of a cosmological constant for any spinor configuration and in any spacetime. The spinor evolution is therefore not uniquely determined by the field equations. In a spatially flat homogeneous universe, both branches act as cosmological-constant sources. We also give the dilution law for homogeneous configurations in theories with explicit dependence on the scalar bilinear. When a potential with a nonzero minimum is introduced, the stationary configuration becomes the late-time state of the cosmological evolution, and the approach to it generates a pressureless component in addition to an effective cosmological constant. For a simple quadratic model, the background expansion reproduces exactly that of the standard $Λ$CDM cosmology, while the value of the vacuum energy remains controlled by an additive constant in the Lagrangian.

</details>


### [203] [Primordial black holes production in Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28150)
*Ekaterina O. Pozdeeva,Sergey Yu. Vernov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文提出将Gauss-Bonnet项与标量场函数相乘的暴胀模型，可同时解释原初黑洞形成与暗物质来源，并与ACT观测数据相符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有暴胀模型缺乏在原初黑洞（primordial black holes, PBHs）形成与暗物质（dark matter）来源之间的统一机制解释，作者据此引入Gauss-Bonnet（Gauss-Bonnet）项与标量场函数耦合的建模思路。

Method: 考虑Gauss-Bonnet项乘以标量场函数（scalar field）的模型，并在此框架下构造一个新的暴胀（inflationary）模型，通过选取特定参数值计算其对原初黑洞形成的预言及PBH质量估计。

Result: 在特定模型参数取值下，该模型的预言与Atacama Cosmology Telescope（ACT）的观测数据一致；基于PBH质量的估计，这些原初黑洞可视为暗物质全部或部分的可能来源。

Conclusion: 该Gauss-Bonnet耦合暴胀模型为原初黑洞形成提供了机制，并支持PBHs作为暗物质候选者的可能性。

Abstract: We consider models with the Gauss-Bonnet term multiplied to a function of the scalar field and propose a new inflationary model which provides a mechanism for the formation of primordial black holes (PBHs). For certain values of the model parameters, the predictions of this model agree with observational data from the Atacama Cosmology Telescope (ACT). Due to estimation of possiable PBHs masses, we demonstrate that they can be considered as a possible source for all or part of dark matter.

</details>


### [204] [Optical appearance of a non-commutative black hole surrounded by an accretion disk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28151)
*Adailton Azevêdo Araújo Filho,Narges Heidari,Alesandro Ferreira dos Santos,Shahin Mamedov,Valdir Barbosa Bezerra,Amilcar Rabelo de Queiroz,Ali Övgün*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究非对易施瓦西黑洞（non-commutative Schwarzschild black hole）在透明薄发射盘下的光学外观，定量给出非对易参数θ对临界碰撞参数、最内稳定圆轨道及观测强度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非对易几何（non-commutative geometry）修正的时空可能改变强引力场中的光子运动与辐射可观测特征，需要系统性刻画其对黑洞图像（black hole image）的定量影响。

Method: 采用洛伦兹型物质分布（Lorentzian matter distribution）给出等效外部度规，计算光子轨迹、盘传递函数（disk transfer function）及静态与圆周运动发射体的观测热辐射强度（bolometric intensity），并计入引力红移（gravitational redshift）、轨道时间膨胀与多普勒调制（Doppler modulation）。

Result: 固定渐近质量下，θ=Θ/M²由0增至0.03时临界碰撞参数（critical impact parameter）减小约16.1%，最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）半径减小23.7%；高阶窄特征随之收缩，而宽直接峰位移小于1%。相同源归一化下，峰值直接强度与屏幕孔径积分信号分别增大约6.5%和4.3%；不同径向发射分布在同一临界曲线处产生不同的中心亮度凹陷，轨道运动造成倾斜图像中迎向侧与背离侧的不对称。

Conclusion: 非对易参数使光子环与轨道结构整体收缩并增强观测通量，且径向发射分布与轨道运动显著影响中心亮度分布与图像不对称性，可作为区分该时空模型的观测特征。

Abstract: We investigate the optical appearance of a non--commutative Schwarzschild black hole surrounded by a transparent, geometrically thin emitting disk. A Lorentzian matter distribution supplies the effective exterior metric. We compute photon trajectories, disk transfer functions, and the observed bolometric intensity for static and circular emitters, taking into account gravitational redshift, orbital time dilation, and Doppler modulation. At fixed asymptotic mass, increasing $θ=Θ/M^2$ from zero to $0.03$ reduces the critical impact parameter by approximately $16.1\%$ and the innermost stable circular orbit radius by $23.7\%$. The narrow higher-order features follow this contraction, while the broad direct peak shifts by less than $1\%$ for a fixed emission profile. With the same source normalization, the peak direct intensity and the signal integrated over the screen aperture increase by approximately $6.5\%$ and $4.3\%$, respectively. Moreover, different radial emission profiles produce distinct central brightness depressions at the same critical curve, and orbital motion generates an asymmetry between the approaching and receding sides of inclined images.

</details>


### [205] [Spin-Torsion Coupling: Vector and Scalar Torsional Potentials of Chiral Spinor Currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28182)
*Elisa Varani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 论文在弱场、Ricci-flow 启发的几何框架下研究手征旋量流（chiral spinor currents）所产生扭转场（torsional fields）的积分表示，构建屏蔽格林核（screened Green-kernel）解并考察环向构型、混合手征源与中微子自旋动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将旋量源、有限程扭转势（finite-range torsional potentials）、环向几何与中微子自旋动力学联系起来，为后续扭转输运（torsional transport）与可观测效应的研究提供数学基础。

Method: 从旋量流与轴扭转（axial torsion）的耦合出发，构造屏蔽格林核解，并分析其与反对称标架扰动（antisymmetric tetradic perturbations）及扭率（contorsion）的关系；重点处理稳态环向（toroidal）构型与混合手征源（mixed-chirality source）对应的翻转扇区积分。

Result: 对稳态环向构型，磁型偶极矩为零，而非零环向矩（toroidal moment）与有效环流（effective circulation）仍存在；这些量随 Yukawa 屏蔽的依赖关系被分析，并讨论了其与类 Aharonov-Bohm 几何结构的关联。此外，在轴背景中考察了大质量 Majorana 中微子的动力学，区分手征混合（chiral mixing）与螺旋度振荡（helicity oscillations），并识别横向扭转分量的作用。

Conclusion: 所提框架整合了旋量源、有限程扭转势、环向几何与中微子自旋动力学，为扭转输运及可观测效应的进一步研究奠定数学基础。

Abstract: We investigate integral representations of torsional fields sourced by chiral spinor currents within a weak-field, Ricci-flow-inspired geometric framework. Starting from the coupling between spinorial currents and axial torsion, we construct screened Green-kernel solutions and examine their relation to antisymmetric tetradic perturbations and contorsion.
  Particular attention is devoted to stationary toroidal configurations, for which the magnetic-type dipole moment vanishes while a nonzero toroidal moment and effective circulation remain. The dependence of these quantities on Yukawa screening is analyzed, together with their implications for an Aharonov-Bohm-like geometric structure.
  We further introduce a mixed-chirality source describing an effective connection between oppositely polarized domains and investigate the corresponding flip-sector integral. The dynamics of massive Majorana neutrinos in an axial background is then examined, distinguishing chiral mixing from helicity oscillations and identifying the role of transverse torsional components.
  The resulting framework connects spinorial sources, finite-range torsional potentials, toroidal geometry, and neutrino spin dynamics, while providing a mathematical basis for further investigations of torsional transport and observable effects.

</details>


### [206] [Kinetic moment estimates and a one-sided Lyapunov barrier for the relativistic fermionic Michie-King equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28278)
*Dorota Bors,Robert Stańczy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文沿固定 Tolman–Klein 不变量（Tolman–Klein invariants）的平衡族研究截止 RAR（cutoff Ruffini–Argüelles–Rueda，即相对论费米 Michie–King）状态方程，借助 Cauchy–Schwarz 不等式与 Bors–Stańczy 型 Lyapunov 屏障（Lyapunov barrier）证明致密度比 2Gm(r)/(rc^2) 的显式 Lambert 上界，并修正既有线性比较公式中的对数系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于为相对论费米气体的状态方程给出严格的质量—半径型约束，并说明线性状态方程 P=ρc^2/√3 在物理楔形区域内的向量场支配地位；同时纠正文献中已使用的显式线性比较公式里的对数系数错误。

Method: 方法基于三个动力学矩（kinetic moments）导出压强—密度比及其导数的精确公式，由 Cauchy–Schwarz 得到二者间的整体关系，进而推出 TOV（Tolman–Oppenheimer–Volkoff）系统的垂直向量场支配（vertical vector-field domination）；并构造填满的单侧 Bors–Stańczy Lyapunov 屏障，逐项检验各边界条件。

Result: 该屏障控制所有向外偏离，包括自 Lyapunov 子水平集下界离开后的情形；对正则构型得到显式 Lambert 界 2Gm(r)/(rc^2)<0.6879212521，在比值取驻值的对角线上收紧至 0.6734385929。

Conclusion: 论证以正则 TOV 构型（regular TOV configuration）为条件，不需要单独的有限半径存在性定理；同时识别并修正了此前所用显式线性比较公式（linear-comparator formula）中的一个对数系数。

Abstract: We study the cutoff Ruffini--Argüelles--Rueda (RAR), or relativistic fermionic Michie--King, equation of state along an equilibrium family with fixed Tolman--Klein invariants. Three kinetic moments give exact formulas for both the pressure-to-density ratio and the derivative of pressure. Cauchy--Schwarz yields a global relation between these two quantities. It implies vertical vector-field domination of the associated Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff system by the linear equation of state $P=ρc^2/\sqrt3$ in the physical wedge $0<y\leq3x$. We construct a filled, one-sided version of the Bors--Stańczy Lyapunov barrier and check every boundary. This controls all outward excursions, including those after a lower exit from a Lyapunov sublevel. For regular configurations the resulting explicit Lambert bound is $2Gm(r)/(rc^2)<0.6879212521$. Using the diagonal where this ratio has a stationary value sharpens the bound to $0.6734385929$. The argument is conditional on a regular TOV configuration and does not require a separate finite-radius existence theorem. We also identify and correct a logarithmic coefficient in the previously used explicit linear-comparator formula.

</details>


### [207] [Radial stability of nonlinearly scalarized black holes in Einstein-phantom scalar-Gauss-Bonnet theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28523)
*De-Cheng Zou,Yun Soo Myung,Meng-Yun Lai,Hyat Huang,Wen-Cong Gan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究爱因斯坦—幽灵标量—高斯—博内理论中标量化黑洞的径向稳定性，发现低支分支不稳定、原支分支在指数耦合下可稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对含多项式与指数耦合的爱因斯坦—幽灵标量—高斯—博内（Einstein–phantom scalar–Gauss–Bonnet）理论中所出现的非线性标量化黑洞（nonlinearly scalarized black holes），其径向稳定性（radial stability）尚不明确，故系统考察其扰动与稳定性质。

Method: 将耦合的标量—度规扰动（scalar–metric perturbations）在消去冗余度规扰动后约化为薛定谔型方程（Schrödinger-type equation），并借助 S-形变方法（S-deformation method）分析正零能解；同时对比两支分支的径向稳定性与热力学分析（thermodynamic analysis）。

Result: 幽灵标量化黑洞存在低支（lower branch）与原支（primary branch）两支；低支（含纯四次解，pure quartic solutions）存在一个增长模式（growing mode），而原支的正零能解可通过 S-形变方法确立稳定性，指数耦合下可得到稳定的原支解。

Conclusion: 径向稳定性与热力学分析在两类分支间存在联系；此外，多项式耦合的爱因斯坦—标量—高斯—博内理论所得正则标量化黑洞（canonical scalarized black holes）呈现低支不稳定、原支稳定的结构。

Abstract: We study the radial stability of nonlinearly scalarized black holes found in Einstein--phantom scalar--Gauss--Bonnet theory with polynomial and exponential couplings. Two branches of lower and primary are found for phantom scalarized black holes. The coupled scalar--metric perturbations reduce to a Schrödinger-type equation, after eliminating redundant metric perturbations. The lower branch including the pure quartic solutions has one growing mode, while positive zero-energy solutions for the primary branch could establish stability through the $S$-deformation method. Stable primary solutions are found for the exponential coupling. Also, we explore the connection between radial stability and thermodynamic analysis for two branches. Furthermore, we show that canonical scalarized black holes obtained from Einstein--scalar--Gauss--Bonnet theory with polynomial coupling reveals unstable lower and stable primary branches.

</details>


### [208] [Numerical Integration of Geodesic Motion in Non-Schwarzschild Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28694)
*Jan Pijáček,Georgios Lukes-Gerakopoulos,Ondřej Zelenka*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文提出一种在 Wolfram Mathematica 中数值求解测地线运动（geodesic motion）的方法，用于处理施瓦西黑洞（Schwarzschild black hole）叠加薄 Vogt-Letelier 盘的系统，并重点解决粒子穿越薄盘时度规导数不连续带来的数值稳定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯施瓦西时空因高度对称性而完全可积，但加入薄盘后球对称性被破坏，系统变为弱可积（weak-integrable）动力学系统；薄盘处度规导数的不连续会影响数值稳定性与运动积分的守恒，需专门处理。

Method: 在 Wolfram Mathematica 中实现测地线数值求解，比较多种薄盘穿越（disk crossings）的数值处理方式，最终采用基于四维速度重正化（re-normalization of the four-velocity）的方法；并利用庞加莱截面（Poincaré sections）与旋转数谱（rotation number spectra）考察系统相空间。

Result: 实现了基于四维速度重正化的盘穿越处理方案，并借助庞加莱截面与旋转数谱对系统相空间结构进行了分析。

Conclusion: 四维速度重正化是处理薄盘穿越不连续性的有效数值方案，可用于弱可积系统中测地线运动的稳定数值求解与相空间刻画。

Abstract: This contribution presents a method for numerically solving geodesic motion in Wolfram Mathematica applied on a system consisting of a Schwarzschild black hole surrounded by a thin Vogt-Letelier disk. While geodesic motion in the Schwarzschild spacetime is fully integrable due to its high degree of symmetry, the addition of a disk breaks the spherical symmetry and leads to a weak-integrable dynamical system. Particular attention is paid to the treatment of particle trajectories near the thin disk, where discontinuities in the derivatives of the metric may affect numerical stability and the conservation of integrals of motion. Several numerical treatments of disk crossings were implemented, arriving to a method based on the re-normalization of the four-velocity. The phase-space of the system is investigated using Poincaré sections and rotation number spectra.

</details>


### [209] [Gravitomagnetic Frame Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28731)
*Ruggiero Matteo Luca,Astesiano Davide*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在线性阶下，检验空间分离的自旋检验粒子对有限时长平面引力波的响应，发现非零引力磁脉冲面积会导致自旋的残余相对转动，可视为一种局部参考系记忆效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限时长平面引力波对分离自旋检验粒子的作用，旨在寻找对时空曲率积分量的直接操作性探测手段。

Method: 在探测器静止参考系中采用费米法坐标（Fermi normal coordinates）与局部度规的引力电磁分解（gravitoelectromagnetic decomposition），在波振幅线性阶展开；在距参考世界线的领头阶，自旋演化由黎曼张量（Riemann tensor）的磁部分主导。

Result: 具有非零引力磁脉冲面积（gravitomagnetic pulse area）的有限曲率剖面会引起自旋的残余相对转动，其大小正比于局部引力磁场的时间积分；该变化在曲率消失后仍然保留。

Conclusion: 该效应可解释为局部参考系记忆可观测量，具有有限平面波曲率剖面的速度型记忆（velocity-type memory）结构，为积分磁部分曲率提供了直接的操作性探针。

Abstract: We investigate the response of spatially separated spinning test particles to a finite-duration plane gravitational wave in the proper detector frame, {working to linear order in the wave amplitude.}
  Using Fermi normal coordinates and the gravitoelectromagnetic decomposition of the local metric, the spin evolution is governed, at leading order in the distance from the reference world-line, by the magnetic part of the Riemann tensor. A finite curvature profile with a non-vanishing gravitomagnetic pulse area induces a residual relative rotation of the spins, proportional to the time integral of the local gravitomagnetic field. The resulting change persists after the curvature has vanished and may be interpreted as a local frame-memory observable. This effect has the structure of a velocity-type memory of finite plane-wave curvature profiles and provides a direct operational probe of the integrated magnetic part of spacetime curvature.

</details>


### [210] [Exactly solvable Brill waves: explicit gravitational-wave initial data with trapped surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28758)
*Piotr Koc*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在环面坐标（toroidal coordinates）下给出了 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 上纯引力波（Brill 波）真空初始数据的首个精确解析单参数族解，并刻画其质量与视界结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 上纯引力波（Brill waves）的真空初始数据仅能通过数值方法获得，缺乏精确解析解（exact solution），难以严格分析其质量与视界性质。

Method: 在环面坐标下使约束方程（constraint）分离变量，选取合适的种子函数（seed function）使各模式方程（mode equation）化为超几何方程（hypergeometric equation），从而精确求解单参数族；并利用标度对称性（scaling symmetry）生成更多可解族。

Result: 所得数据实解析（real analytic）且渐近平坦（asymptotically flat），仅当 $k<k_c=0.8048\ldots$ 时存在；质量可写成显式级数且在 $k_c$ 处有极点。在 $k_c$ 附近可证明数据包含陷获面（trapped surfaces）与视界（apparent horizon）。

Conclusion: 该工作首次给出 Brill 波真空初始数据的精确解析族，并通过标度对称性得到进一步可解族（其中一个为初等解），为近临界区的视界形成提供严格解析依据。

Abstract: Vacuum initial data for pure gravitational waves on $\mathbb{R}^3$ (Brill waves) have so far been obtained only numerically. Here a one-parameter family is solved exactly: in toroidal coordinates the constraint separates, and a suitable seed function makes every mode equation hypergeometric. The data are real analytic and asymptotically flat and exist precisely for $k<k_c=0.8048\ldots$; their mass is an explicit series with a pole at $k_c$. Near $k_c$ the data provably contain trapped surfaces and an apparent horizon. A scaling symmetry yields further solvable families, one of them elementary.

</details>


### [211] [Anti-de Sitter charges and maximal Cauchy hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28989)
*A. Rod Gover,Jarosław Kopiński,Andrew Waldron*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文从极大共形紧致柯西超曲面（maximal conformally-compact Cauchy hypersurfaces）上的哈密顿约束与动量约束的 Neumann 数据出发，构造负宇宙学常数真空时空渐近对称性（asymptotic symmetries）的守恒荷，为具有类时共形边界（timelike conformal boundary）的真空时空提供一般且典范的荷的初值处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对负宇宙学常数真空时空（渐近 AdS 型）的渐近对称守恒荷，现有处理多依赖边界或渐近展开，缺乏统一的初值表述；本文旨在给出不依赖特定渐近展开的一般、典范（canonical）的荷定义框架。

Method: 采用初值（initial value）方法：在极大且共形紧致的柯西超曲面上取哈密顿约束（Hamiltonian constraint）与动量约束（momentum constraint）的 Neumann 数据作为基本输入，据此构造渐近对称性对应的守恒荷（conserved charges）。

Result: 得到负宇宙学常数真空时空渐近对称性守恒荷的一种一般且典范的初值构造，使其直接由约束方程的 Neumann 数据表达，适用于具有类时共形边界的真空时空。

Conclusion: 该构造为含类时共形边界的真空时空提供了统一的荷的初值处理方案，可作为研究渐近对称性与守恒量的一般框架。摘要未给出具体度规算例或显式荷表达式。

Abstract: We construct conserved charges of asymptotic symmetries for negative-cosmological-constant vacuum spacetimes from the Neumann data for the Hamiltonian and momentum constraints on maximal conformally-compact Cauchy hypersurfaces. This gives a general and canonical initial value treatment of charges for vacuum spacetimes with timelike conformal boundary.

</details>


### [212] [The shadow of rotating FJNW metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28990)
*Mina Shokrollahi,Behrouz Mirza,Mahnaz Tavakoli Kachi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究旋转 Fisher-Janis-Newman-Winicour（FJNW）度规的天体物理效应，利用 Hamilton-Jacobi 方程得到其黑洞阴影，并分析度规参数对阴影大小及光线偏折角的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期文献导出了旋转 FJNW 度规，但其天体物理观测特征（如黑洞阴影与光线偏折）尚缺乏系统研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用 Hamilton-Jacobi 方程（Hamilton-Jacobi equation）求解旋转 FJNW 时空中的光子轨道，以获得黑洞阴影轮廓；并进一步计算该时空中的光线偏折角（deflection angle）。

Result: 结果表明，增大角动量参数 a 与度规参数 γ 均会导致阴影尺寸增大；同时对旋转 FJNW 时空中的光线偏折角进行了研究。

Conclusion: 旋转 FJNW 度规的阴影特征对参数 a 与 γ 敏感，这为其在天体物理观测中的潜在检验提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate the astrophysical aspects of the rotating Fisher-Janis-Newman-Winicour (FJNW) metric recently derived in [1]. The Hamilton-Jacobi equation is used to obtain the shadow of the rotating FJNW metric. We find that increasing the angular momentum parameter a and the metric parameter γleads to an increase in the size of the shadow. Finally, we study the deflection angle of light in the rotating FJNW spacetime.

</details>


### [213] [Unruh-like effects and correlation harvesting between particle detectors in uniform motion in (2+1)-dimensional Minkowski spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29038)
*Siwei Han,Zeinab Imani,Jun Feng,Robert B. Mann*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究(2+1)维闵可夫斯基真空中高斯开关型 Unruh-DeWitt 探测器沿线性、悬链线、尖点与圆周四类匀加速轨迹运动时的跃迁概率、有效温度及双探测器纠缠采集行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过扭转-加速度比这一连续参数统一刻画四类加速轨迹，并厘清轨迹几何如何影响单探测器的热响应与双探测器的真空纠缠采集。

Method: 计算高斯开关（Gaussian switching）Unruh-DeWitt 探测器与无质量标量场（massless scalar field）耦合下的单探测器跃迁概率（transition probability）与有效温度（effective temperature），并考察两个探测器沿水平位移的同型轨迹运动时的纠缠采集（entanglement harvesting）协议，分析 concurrence 与量子互信息（quantum mutual information）。

Result: 跃迁概率与有效温度总体随加速度增大而升高，但对扭转-加速度比 $\bar{b}$ 的依赖随能隙（energy gap）呈现非单调性；加速度或间距增大通常抑制采集到的 concurrence 与量子互信息，抑制程度强烈依赖轨迹类别；四类轨迹均存在真正纠缠采集（genuine entanglement harvesting）区间。

Conclusion: 存在真空关联单独即可纠缠探测器、而通信贡献无法克服局域噪声的参数区间，表明轨迹几何是调控加速探测器量子关联采集的关键因素。

Abstract: We study Gaussian-switched Unruh-DeWitt detectors coupled to a massless scalar field in the (2+1)-dimensional Minkowski vacuum and following four classes of uniformly accelerated trajectories: linear, catenary, cusped, and circular motion. We first determine how the single-detector transition probability and effective temperature depend on acceleration and the torsion-to-acceleration ratio $\bar{b}$, whose continuous variation connects these four trajectory classes. We find that both quantities generally increase with acceleration, while their dependence on $\bar{b}$ can become nonmonotonic depending on the energy gap. We also examine a correlation-harvesting protocol by two detectors following horizontally displaced copies of the same trajectory. Increasing acceleration or separation generally suppresses the harvested concurrence and quantum mutual information, but the extent of suppression at fixed acceleration depends strongly on the trajectory class. For each of the four trajectory classes, we identify regimes of genuine entanglement harvesting in which vacuum correlations alone suffice to entangle the detectors, while the communication contribution alone cannot overcome local noise.

</details>


### [214] [Quasinormal Modes and Excitation Factors of a Parity-Symmetric Beyond-Horndeski Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29052)
*Roman A. Konoplya,Milena Skvortsova*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文系统计算了宇称对称的超越 Horndeski 引力（beyond-Horndeski gravity）中渐近平直黑洞对标量、电磁与 Dirac 扰动的准正规模（quasinormal modes）谱，并用多种独立数值方法交叉验证，指出前人六阶 WKB 近似在部分参数区间不足，且强形变下激发因子（excitation factors）的巨大偏离主要源于龟坐标（tortoise coordinate）中势垒峰位的位移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对宇称对称超越 Horndeski 黑洞的扰动稳定性与准正规模缺乏高精度、可交叉验证的结果，并检验文献中六阶 WKB 近似（第六阶展开）在形变参数空间中的可靠性。
