Method: 采用甚高阶 WKB 展开（very-high-order WKB expansions）结合 Padé 重求和（Padé resummation），以 Cesàro 求和（Cesàro means）诊断内部收敛性；主导模由时域演化加 Prony 提取（Prony extraction）与 Chebyshev 伪谱配点（Chebyshev pseudospectral collocation）独立校验，后者还给出以度规标度和物理 ADM 质量两种归一化的频率扫描；激发因子由格林函数（Green-function）留数经复围道积分单独获得。

Result: 在固定度规标度归一化下，趋近 Schwarzschild 通常同时提高振荡频率与阻尼率，而更强形变使模式移向低频与弱阻尼；在 ADM 质量单位下，轻度形变解接近 Schwarzschild，最强形变区域因渐近质量本身变化剧烈而呈现增强且非单调的行为。各独立方法吻合良好，但文献中六阶 WKB 近似在部分参数区域不足：基模（fundamental mode）阻尼率偏差达 9%，第一泛音（first overtone）偏差可达量级一。Schwarzschild 激发因子被复现；强形变区其幅度在渐近龟坐标约定下可与 Schwarzschild 值相差两个量级以上，该效应主要来自势垒在龟坐标中的形变依赖位移，若以势垒极大值处为原点则偏差显著变小。

Conclusion: 高阶 WKB 加 Padé 重求和配合时域与伪谱方法可为准正规模提供可靠的高精度谱；六阶 WKB 在超越 Horndeski 参数空间局部不可靠；激发因子的大幅偏离主要是坐标约定效应而非本征物理放大。

Abstract: We study scalar, electromagnetic, and Dirac perturbations of an asymptotically flat black hole in parity-symmetric beyond-Horndeski gravity. The quasinormal spectrum is computed with very-high-order WKB expansions and Padé resummation, with Cesàro means used to diagnose internal convergence. The dominant modes are independently tested by time-domain evolution with Prony extraction and by Chebyshev pseudospectral collocation; the latter also provides systematic frequency scans in both the metric-scale and physical ADM-mass normalizations. Excitation factors are obtained separately from Green-function residues by complex-contour integration. In the fixed metric-scale normalization, approaching Schwarzschild generally increases both the oscillation frequency and damping rate, whereas stronger deformation shifts the modes toward lower frequencies and weaker damping. In ADM-mass units, the mildly deformed solutions remain close to Schwarzschild, while the most deformed sector becomes enhanced and nonmonotonic because the asymptotic mass itself varies strongly. The independent methods agree very well, however the sixth-order WKB approximation used in [G. Antoniou, T. D. Pappas, P. Kanti, Phys. Rev. D 112, 084013 (2025)] is insufficient in certain regions of the parameter space: the fundamental-mode discrepancy reaches nine percent in the damping rate and the first-overtone discrepancy can be of order unity. The Schwarzschild excitation factors are recovered. In the strongly deformed regime, their magnitudes can differ from the Schwarzschild values by more than two orders of magnitude in the asymptotic tortoise-coordinate convention. We show that this large effect is predominantly due to the deformation-dependent displacement of the potential barrier in the tortoise coordinate: when its maximum is used as the origin, the excitation factors exhibit much more modest deviations.

</details>


### [215] [The Schwarzschild Ringdown Time Prescription on Critical Kerr Plunges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29054)
*Jingxu Wu,Junyi Zhang,Daniil Stepanenko,Maxim Khlopov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文将史瓦西（Schwarzschild）坠落中准正规模（quasinormal-mode）等幅收敛的时间起点（$9.435M$，位于 $(2,2)$ 应变峰之后）与源的提前时间（advanced time）$v$ 在 $r=0$ 处的取值联系起来，并推广到真实顺行临界克尔（Kerr）坠落情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在史瓦西情形中，准正规模等幅收敛起点与源在 $r=0$ 处的提前时间相关，但该关系的物理起源及其在旋转（克尔）时空中的推广尚不明确。

Method: 对真实顺行临界克尔坠落，$P(r_-)<0$ 使入射时间 $v$ 在内视界（inner horizon）处对数发散而 $u_K$ 保持有限；在唯一内部零点 $P(r_0)=0$ 处将 $v$ 与 $-u_K$ 匹配，从而构造无自由参数的起始时间层级 $(u_{\rm H},u_{r_0},u_{\rm C})$。

Result: 该层级在 $a\to 0$ 极限下回到 $9.435M$，并对 $a/M=0.9$ 的偏心坠落预测两阶段残余衰减（residual decay）及在 $(6.85M,11.35M,13.35M)$ 处的频率锁定（frequency locking）。

Conclusion: 结果表明准正规模收敛起始时间可由视界时间与内部零点匹配无参数地确定，并给出克尔情形下可检验的两阶段衰减与频率锁定预言。

Abstract: For a Schwarzschild plunge, constant-amplitude quasinormal-mode convergence has been tied to the source's advanced time at $r=0$, lying $9.435M$ after the $(2,2)$ strain peak. On real prograde critical Kerr plunges, $P(r_-)<0$ forces the ingoing time $v$ to diverge logarithmically at the inner horizon while $u_K$ stays finite. Matching $v$ to $-u_K$ at the unique interior zero $P(r_0)=0$ yields a parameter-free onset hierarchy $(u_{\rm H},u_{r_0},u_{\rm C})$ that recovers $9.435M$ as $a\to 0$ and predicts two-stage residual decay and frequency locking at $(6.85M,11.35M,13.35M)$ on an $a/M=0.9$ eccentric plunge.

</details>


### [216] [QTG minimons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29109)
*Valeri P. Frolov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究准拓扑引力（quasitopological gravity, QTG）中的静态球对称、无事件视界的正则解（称为 QTG minimons），并耦合 Polyakov 作用量以引入真空极化，得到四维 Hayward 型与 Born–Infeld 型解及候选的 $\mathrm{AdS}_2\times S^{D-2}$ 喉道极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究低于正则黑洞（regular black hole）阈值的小质量分支解，并考察真空极化（vacuum polarization）这类量子效应对该分支结构的影响。

Method: 将球面约化后的 QTG 作用量与二维共形物质的 Polyakov 作用量耦合，对任意特征函数 $h(p)$ 导出静态渐近空态下的约束一阶系统，给出 $D\ge4$ 的中心展开，并在四维下数值分析 Hayward 型与 Born–Infeld 型模型。

Result: 两类模型均保留类 de Sitter（de Sitter-like）核心；靠近临界分支时渐近质量随量子耦合减小，而中心蓝移（blueshift）即使在小耦合下也可变得很大，数值结果表真空极化在临界极限附近是非均匀扰动。在固有距离下假设光滑的常半径极限，得到候选的 $\mathrm{AdS}_2\times S^{D-2}$ 喉道几何（QTG extremons）。

Conclusion: QTG minimons 构成正则黑洞阈值以下的小质量解分支，其临界极限附近真空极化表现为非均匀扰动，并可能趋近于具有 $\mathrm{AdS}_2\times S^{D-2}$ 喉道结构的 extremon 几何。

Abstract: We study static, spherically symmetric, regular horizonless solutions of quasitopological gravity (QTG), which we call QTG minimons. These configurations form the small-mass branch below the threshold for regular black holes. We include vacuum polarization by coupling the spherically reduced QTG action to the Polyakov action for two-dimensional conformal matter. For an arbitrary characteristic function $h(p)$, we derive a constrained first-order system in the static, asymptotically empty state. We obtain central expansions in $D\ge4$ and analyze four-dimensional solutions in the Hayward-type and Born--Infeld-type models. Both models retain a de Sitter-like core. Along the near-critical branch, the asymptotic mass decreases with the quantum coupling, while the central blueshift can become large even for a small coupling. The numerical results indicate that vacuum polarization is a nonuniform perturbation near the critical limit. Assuming a smooth constant-radius limit in proper distance, we derive a candidate $\mathrm{AdS}_2\times S^{D-2}$ throat geometry for the limiting configurations, which we call QTG extremons.

</details>


### [217] [Quantum coherence and entanglement of mixed states in relativistic settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29322)
*Xing-Le Zhao,Zhen Yang,Shu-Min Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究处于一般两量子比特X态（two-qubit X state）的两个Unruh-DeWitt探测器在其中一个经历匀加速并与无质量标量场局域相互作用时的纠缠（entanglement）与相干（coherence），发现纠缠在有限加速度处发生突然死亡，而相干在阈值之后仍保持非零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室制备的初态通常因制备不完美与环境残余耦合而为混合态（mixed state），纯态（pure state）只是理想化极限；因此有必要在更一般的混合初态下考察相对论量子信息（relativistic quantum information）中纠缠与相干的演化差异。

Method: 设定两个Unruh-DeWitt探测器初态为一般两量子比特X态，其中一个保持惯性且不耦合，另一个匀加速并局域耦合Minkowski真空（Minkowski vacuum）中的无质量标量场（massless scalar field）；在微扰处理（perturbative treatment）下求得约化探测器态，并计算concurrence、相干性的$l_1$范数（$l_1$ norm of coherence）与相对熵相干（relative entropy of coherence）。

Result: 在所考察的初态族中，纠缠在有限加速度处出现突然死亡（entanglement sudden death），且初始纠缠越大对应阈值越高；两种相干度量在阈值之外仍保持非零，并在表达式形式上的无限加速度极限下趋于零。此外还考察了这些量子资源对探测器能隙（energy gap）与相互作用时长的依赖。

Conclusion: 结果在混合态探测器模型中区分了相干与纠缠的持续性差异，并表明初始混合度（initial mixedness）是相对论量子信息中的相关要素。

Abstract: States prepared in laboratory experiments are generally mixed because of imperfect preparation and residual coupling to the environment, whereas pure initial states represent an idealized limit. Motivated by this distinction, we investigate quantum entanglement and coherence in a pair of Unruh-DeWitt detectors initially prepared in a general two-qubit X state. One detector remains inertial and uncoupled, while the other undergoes uniform acceleration and interacts locally with a massless scalar field in the Minkowski vacuum. We obtain the reduced detector state within a perturbative treatment and evaluate its concurrence, $l_1$ norm of coherence, and relative entropy of coherence. For the initial-state families considered, the resulting expressions exhibit entanglement sudden death at finite acceleration, with larger initial entanglement associated with a higher threshold. Both coherence measures remain nonzero beyond this threshold and approach zero in the formal infinite-acceleration limit of these expressions. We also examine the dependence of these resources on the detector energy gap and interaction duration. The results distinguish the persistence of coherence from that of entanglement in the mixed-state detector model and identify initial mixedness as a relevant ingredient in relativistic quantum information.

</details>


### [218] [Regge calculus for $f(R)$ gravity with torsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29447)
*Xiao-Kan Guo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在Regge演算（Regge calculus）框架下构造含时空挠率（torsion）的f(R)引力，证明不必引入挠率的额外几何定义：挠率的轴矢扇区由无质量Kalb–Ramond二形式携带，在四维对偶于单个标量场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在避免为挠率（torsion）强加临时的几何定义（ad hoc geometric definition），并在离散Regge格点上以纯引力加标量场的方式刻画挠率效应，从而免去完整的离散微分演算（discrete differential calculus）。

Method: 采用D'Adda带坐标的单纯形引力表述，将f(R)引力与时空挠率离散化；推导格点作用量（lattice action）与完整离散运动方程，并检验连续极限（continuum limit）；对均匀各向同性格点得到离散Friedmann–Raychaudhuri方程组，并对S^3正则三角剖分计算精确的动能与曲率系数。

Result: 离散理论等价于纯Regge引力耦合两个标量场——标量子（scalaron）与轴子（axion），格点上无需挠率的几何定义；所得离散方程在演化时间步长的一阶精度上重现连续闭合解。

Conclusion: 该情形构成一个特殊案例：挠率效应可由Regge格点上的标量场完全描述，无需完整的离散微分演算。

Abstract: We formulate $f(R)$ gravity with spacetime torsion in Regge calculus, but without an \emph{ad hoc} geometric definition of torsion. Working in D'Adda's formulation of simplicial gravity with coordinates, we show that the axial sector of the torsion is carried by a massless Kalb--Ramond two-form, which in four dimensions is dual to a single scalar on the dual vertices. The discretized theory is therefore pure Regge gravity coupled to two scalar fields, the scalaron and the axion, without a geometric definition of torsion on the Regge lattice. We derive the lattice action and the complete set of discrete equations of motion, and we verify the continuum limit. For a homogeneous and isotropic lattice we obtain the discrete Friedmann--Raychaudhuri system, compute the exact kinetic and curvature coefficients for a regular triangulation of $\mathbb S^3$, and show that the discrete equations reproduce the continuum closed solution at first order in the evolution time step. The results therefore present a very special case in which the torsional effects are completely described by scalar fields on the Regge lattice and there is no need of a full discrete differential calculus.

</details>


### [219] [Birefringent Imaging and Interferometry with Periodic Orbits and Accretion in Einstein-nonlinear-electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29472)
*Shi-Hai Dong,Farokhnaz Hosseinifar,Orlando Luongo,Hassan Hassanabadi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在爱因斯坦-非线性电动力学（Einstein-nonlinear electrodynamics）框架下，采用Euler-Heisenberg型模型研究磁荷黑洞（magnetically charged black hole）的双折射成像（birefringent imaging）、干涉可见度（interferometric visibility）、中性周期测地线（neutral periodic geodesics）与薄盘辐射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将非线性电动力学耦合作为自由模型参数处理，系统考察磁荷与非线性修正对光子传播、轨道结构及辐射观测特征的影响，并厘清不同源构型下结论的适用条件。

Method: 利用两个有效光子度规（effective photon metrics）分析不稳定光环（unstable light rings）与捕获阈值；将像序强度转为带符号傅里叶可见度（Fourier visibilities）；以有理轨道频率（rational orbital frequencies）分类中性束缚轨道；并进行稳态薄盘（stationary thin-disk）的通量、温度、辐射效率与偏振分辨热谱计算。

Result: 两个有效度规给出不同的光环、捕获阈值与图像；可见度呈现各自的临界曲线振荡及未分辨总和中的窄环调制尺度，且该双模对应不要求光子在两势垒间运动。在采样参数范围内，磁荷主导变化，匹配的Maxwell对比可分辨出较小的非线性修正。

Conclusion: 静态光学薄发射体与轨道运动的光学厚热盘属于不同源构型，偏振与亮度的比较仅在各自构型内使用同一物质源，相应结果以该构型为条件。

Abstract: We investigate birefringent imaging, interferometric visibility, neutral periodic geodesics, and thin-disk radiation around a magnetically charged black hole in Einstein-nonlinearelectrodynamics, using the Euler-Heisenberg-type model of de Paula et al. [14]. The nonlinear coupling is treated as a free model parameter. Two effective photon metrics give rise to distinct unstable light rings, capture thresholds, and images. Transforming their image-order intensities into signed Fourier visibilities reveals separate critical-curve oscillations and a narrow-ring modulation scale in the unresolved sum. This two-mode correspondence does not require photon motion between two potential barriers. Rational orbital frequencies are then used to classify neutral bound trajectories and provide conservative gravitationalwave snapshots relevant to extreme-mass-ratio inspirals. A stationary thin-disk calculation supplies flux, temperature, emitted efficiency, and polarization-resolved thermal spectra. Across the sampled parameters, magnetic charge dominates the changes, while matched Maxwell comparisons resolve smaller nonlinear corrections. The static, optically thin emitters and the orbiting, optically thick thermal disk are distinct source configurations. Polarization comparisons use the same material source within each configuration; their brightness and spectral results are conditional on that configuration.

</details>


### [220] [Viscous damping of axisymmetric oscillation modes of uniformly rotating relativistic stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29617)
*Óscar H. Petit,José A. Font*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 基于BDNK一阶粘性流体力学，在Cowling近似下用二维数值代码研究了体粘性与剪切粘性对均匀旋转相对论星轴对称振荡的阻尼作用，覆盖288个扰动星模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清体粘性（bulk viscosity）与剪切粘性（shear viscosity）在旋转相对论星振荡阻尼中的相对贡献，并考察其随自转速率与致密度（compactness）的变化规律。

Method: 采用Bemfica–Disconzi–Noronha–Kovtun（BDNK）一阶粘性流体力学（first-order viscous hydrodynamics）方程，在Cowling近似下假设流体演化于旋转星的固定时空（fixed spacetime）中，构建二维数值代码；建立两族多方体（polytropic）星模型，研究准径向l=0 F模与l=1,2,3基频模，数据集共288个扰动模型。

Result: 所有模式与所选粘性下，BDNK结果中振荡在数毫秒内衰减，远快于理想流体（perfect fluid）情形。F模阻尼由体粘性主导，与其整体压缩性质一致；l=2、l=3模主要由剪切粘性阻尼；l=1模对两种粘性系数均不敏感，其衰减率由数值耗散（numerical dissipation）主导。旋转使F模对剪切粘性变得敏感。

Conclusion: 由于固有数值阻尼不影响衰减率斜率（slopes），粘性敏感性估计可可靠识别各模式的主导阻尼机制（剪切或体粘性），并揭示随自转速率与致密度的稳健趋势。

Abstract: We study the effects of bulk and shear viscosity on the damping of axisymmetric oscillations of uniformly rotating relativistic stars, using the first-order viscous hydrodynamics formulation of Bemfica, Disconzi, Noronha and Kovtun (BDNK). The equations of motion are implemented in a two-dimensional numerical code under the Cowling approximation, assuming the fluid evolves on the fixed spacetime of a rotating star. We build two sequences of polytropic stellar models and investigate how damping depends on rotation rate (from the non-rotating case to the mass-shedding limit) and compactness. Our study focuses on the quasi-radial $l=0$ $F$ mode and the $l=1,2,3$ fundamental modes, covering a dataset of 288 perturbed stellar models. For all modes and chosen viscosities, the BDNK runs show that oscillations decay within a few milliseconds, much faster than in the perfect fluid case. The $F$ mode damping is dominated by bulk viscosity, consistent with its global compression of the star, while the $l=2$ and $l=3$ modes are mainly damped by shear viscosity. The $l=1$ mode is largely insensitive to both viscosity coefficients, and its decay rate is dominated by numerical dissipation. Rotation renders the $F$ mode sensitive to shear viscosity. Since the inherent numerical damping does not affect the slopes of the decay rates, our viscous sensitivity estimates faithfully identify the dominant damping mechanism for each mode, shear or bulk viscosity, and reveal robust trends with rotation rate and compactness.

</details>


### [221] [Gravitational waves in hierarchical triples with lensing and relativistic orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29638)
*Brian C. Seymour,Fabiano Feleppa,Luka Vujeva,Johan Samsing*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层三体（hierarchical triple）引力波波形建模方法，将内双星视为后牛顿（post-Newtonian）信号，并利用零测地线（null geodesics）光线追踪与偏振平行移动精确计算第三体导致的环境调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系核附近可能形成双黑洞（binary black holes, BBH），若存在第三体，则时间延迟、透镜效应（lensing）与角动量进动会对引力波波形产生调制，从而可用于测量第三体的存在。

Method: 在几何光学极限（geometric optics limit）下，通过光线追踪零测地线并平行移动其偏振，纳入新的相对论性透镜、像差（aberration）与偏振项，无需弱场展开；内双星按后牛顿信号演化，环境调制则借助椭圆积分（elliptic integrals）高效计算。

Result: 与先前的后牛顿近似相比，该方法显著提升了此类三体波形的精度，并实现了调制的数值高效计算。

Conclusion: 该工作为未来的分层三体波形代码库奠定基础，可应用于 LISA 的参数估计（parameter estimation）与可探测性研究。

Abstract: Binary black holes (BBH) may be formed near galactic nuclei. If such BBH systems exist, modulations to the gravitational waveform through time delays, lensing, and angular momentum precession could allow the presence of the third body to be measured in this system. In this letter, we advance the modeling for hierarchical triples by carefully including new relativistic lensing, aberration, and polarization terms. We show that the gravitational waves from this system can be computed exactly in the geometric optics limit by ray tracing the null geodesics and parallel transporting their polarization without a weak-field expansion. Our construction evolves the inner binary as if it were a post-Newtonian signal, and computes the environmental modulations efficiently with elliptic integrals. We find that this method improves the accuracy of such a triple waveform significantly compared to previous post-Newtonian approximations, and allows numerically efficient computation of the modulations. This lays the groundwork for future hierarchical triple waveform codebases, which can be used in LISA parameter estimation and detectability.

</details>


### [222] [Ionization of gravitational atoms by a companion-sourced Yukawa potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29939)
*Yi Wang,Jiayi Yao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究伴星（companion）来源的汤川相互作用（Yukawa interaction）如何改变化子超辐射云（superradiant cloud）的连续态电离（continuum ionization）过程，指出连续态电离可在远小于束缚态混合所需的耦合强度下终止超辐射增长，并计算其对引力波（gravitational waves）相位的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转黑洞（rotating black holes）周围的超辐射不稳定性（superradiant instability）会产生超轻玻色子云，伴星可通过驱动跃迁至1/r引力势的连续态来电离该云并消耗轨道能量；既有研究多关注纯引力通道，伴星汤川相互作用的角色尚不明确。

Method: 在弱耦合下提取引力与汤川跃迁振幅（transition amplitudes）之间的干涉项作为电离功率（ionization power）的主导修正；在较大耦合下考察纯汤川贡献，并将连续态电离与非共振束缚态混合（off-resonant bound-state mixing）通道进行对比；进一步计算引力波相位修正及啁啾率（chirp-rate）响应。

Result: 在所涉轨道频率下，吸引性相互作用增强连续态电离并加速旋进（inspiral），排斥性相互作用效果相反；较大耦合时纯汤川贡献可与引力贡献竞争，对较轻的媒介子（mediator）尤为显著。连续态电离能在显著更小的汤川耦合下终止超辐射增长，且在两通道各自的临界耦合处，汤川相互作用均主导云耗竭（cloud depletion）。

Conclusion: 伴星汤川相互作用可显著改变化子云的连续态电离与耗竭，并在临界耦合附近主导该过程；由此产生的引力波相位修正与啁啾率响应在相邻轨道谐波开启连续态电离通道的阈值附近呈现频率相关特征。

Abstract: Superradiant instabilities around rotating black holes can produce clouds of ultralight bosons bound by the black hole's gravitational field. A binary companion can ionize these clouds by driving transitions into continuum states in the black hole's 1/r gravitational potential, extracting energy and angular momentum from the orbit. We investigate how a companion-sourced Yukawa interaction modifies this process. At weak coupling, the interference term between the gravitational and Yukawa transition amplitudes provides the leading correction to the ionization power. For the orbital frequencies here, an attractive interaction enhances continuum ionization and accelerates the inspiral, whereas a repulsive interaction has the opposite effect. At larger coupling, the pure Yukawa contribution to the ionization power becomes increasingly important and can compete with the gravitational contribution, particularly for lighter mediators. Comparing continuum ionization with off-resonant bound-state mixing, we find that continuum ionization can halt superradiant growth at a substantially smaller Yukawa coupling. At their respective critical couplings, the Yukawa interaction provides the dominant contribution to cloud depletion in both channels. Finally, we calculate the phase correction to gravitational waves due to continuum ionization and characterize how the chirp-rate response varies with frequency near the thresholds at which successive orbital harmonics open continuum-ionization channels.

</details>


### [223] [Void inside the horizon: connecting the third-quantized vacuum to a classical spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29963)
*Dong-han Yeom*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 论文在黑洞内部应用第三次量子化（third quantization），通过对超空间（superspace）的欧氏解析延拓，说明量子引力真空如何涌现出经典时空几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Kantowski–Sachs 时空的正则量子化下构造的波包可描述 Schwarzschild 黑洞经典内部，但该波包会延伸至经典奇点，需要一种机制避免奇点并说明经典几何的涌现。

Method: 引入对超空间（而非时空本身）的解析延拓与欧氏波函数（Euclidean wave function），并借助第三次量子化（third quantization）赋予其物理意义，使欧氏延拓对应连接第三次量子化真空态与相干经典几何的在壳轨迹（on-shell trajectory）。

Result: 欧氏解析延拓的超空间提供了连接第三次量子化真空态与经典相干几何的在壳轨迹解释；文中还讨论了需将非线性自相互作用与婴儿宇宙虫洞耦合（baby-universe wormhole couplings）纳入第三次量子化作用量。

Conclusion: 经典时空的涌现可理解为第三次量子化框架下由真空态向相干经典几何的动力学转变，该转变需通过在作用量中加入非线性自相互作用与虫洞耦合来驱动。

Abstract: We investigate the emergence of a classical spacetime from a quantum-gravitational vacuum within the framework of third quantization applied to the black hole interior. Following the canonical quantization of the Kantowski--Sachs spacetime, one can construct a wave packet that explains the classical interior of a Schwarzschild black hole. This wave packet should be extended to the classical singularity. To avoid this, one can introduce an analytic continuation and the Euclidean wave function. This is not an analytic continuation of the spacetime, but of the superspace. To give this a suitable meaning, it is reasonable to introduce the concept of the third quantization. In this aspect, the Euclidean analytic continuation of the superspace explains the on-shell trajectory connecting the third-quantized vacuum state to a coherent classical geometry. Finally, we discuss how nonlinear self-interactions and baby-universe wormhole couplings must be incorporated into the third-quantized action to dynamically drive this transition.

</details>


### [224] [The type-II double copy for relativistic Rindler fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30242)
*Yu-Cheng Wang,Jing-Rui Zhang,Yun-Long Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在Weyl双拷贝（Weyl double copy）框架下，对一阶相对论性Rindler流体（relativistic Rindler fluids）的Petrov type-II几何构造了混合单拷贝（mixed single-copy）表示，并在梯度展开一阶内检验其Maxwell方程与零拷贝标量方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将Weyl双拷贝推广至相对论性Rindler流体的Petrov type-II几何，探索该几何是否存在自洽的单拷贝/零拷贝描述，并厘清相对论情形与非相对论情形的差异。

Method: 在统一的固定平直Rindler背景（flat Rindler background）上，采用一致的旋量约定（spinor conventions）与实场强表示，将Weyl旋量代数分解为两个对称二阶Maxwell旋量；把退化参数（degeneration parameter）与梯度展开参数分离处理，并沿连续退化路径检验方程精度。

Result: Maxwell方程与零拷贝标量方程在相对论梯度展开（relativistic gradient expansion）的一阶精度内得到满足；随复剪切（complex shear）与涡度（vorticity）趋于零，可实现向Petrov type-D与type-N分支的连续退化，且各路径上方程精度保持。

Conclusion: 结果表明相对论性Rindler流体的Petrov type-II几何支持混合单拷贝构造，其退化结构与双拷贝精度具有一致性；并给出与非相对论（nonrelativistic）情形的对比。

Abstract: Within the Weyl double copy framework, we study a mixed single-copy construction for the Petrov type-II geometry of first-order relativistic Rindler fluids. Working on a common fixed flat Rindler background with consistent spinor conventions and a real field strength representation, we algebraically factorize the Weyl spinor into two symmetric rank-two Maxwell spinors and test the Maxwell equations and the zeroth copy scalar equation up to the first order in the relativistic gradient expansion. We separate the degeneration parameter from the gradient expansion parameter and analyze the continuous degeneration to the Petrov type-D and type-N branches as the complex shear and vorticity tend to zero, together with the preservation of the accuracy of the Maxwell and scalar field equations along each path. We also compare the relativistic construction with the nonrelativistic case.

</details>


### [225] [Segre Classification as a Framework for Generating Exact Solutions in Static Cylindrical Spacetimes with Levi-Civita Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30258)
*Wardah Aroosh Afzal,Tooba Feroze*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于Segre分类系统分析柱对称静态时空（cylindrically symmetric static spacetime），将各Segre型对应到Newman-Penrose标量表示的Ricci旋量规范形式，并针对各存活类型构造解、检验物理与稳定性条件，同时计算C-能量与曲率不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在以Segre分类（Segre classification）为框架，理解柱对称静态时空中无迹Ricci张量的代数结构，并借此刻画相应类型中物质与能量的分布特征。

Method: 确定柱几何下无迹Ricci张量（trace-free Ricci tensor）的存活Segre型，并用Newman-Penrose标量表示对应Ricci旋量的规范形式；对各项同性型、各向异性型（引入新度规函数）及三压强互异型（采用Krori-Barua函数）分别求解，并对带电情形与非零电磁场（electromagnetic field）耦合求解。

Result: 各向同性与各向异性解满足Zeldovich条件、因果性条件（causality condition）、能量条件（energy conditions）与绝热指数（adiabatic index）等物理与稳定性判据；[(1,1)11]与[(1,11)1]型给出ρ=-P_r的类暗能量（dark energy）构型，核心处违反强能量条件，后者能动量张量呈快子流体（tachyonic fluid）形式；[(1,1)(11)]型获得带电解并与电真空外部（electrovac exterior）衔接。

Conclusion: 与Levi-Civita外部解的匹配表明边界处径向压强为零等价于Darmois条件；各解均给出C-能量（C-energy）与曲率不变量（curvature invariants），并与既有工作进行了比较。

Abstract: The main objective of this article is to analyze cylindrically symmetric static spacetime based on the Segre classification, where each Segre type corresponds to a set of canonical forms of Ricci spinors represented by Newman-Penrose scalars. The surviving Segre types of trace-free Ricci tensor in the cylindrical geometry are $[1,(111)]$, $[1,1(11)]$, $[(1,1)11]$, $[1,111]$, $[(1,11)1]$, $[(1,1)(11)]$ and $[(1,111)]$. Different combinations of eigenvalues and eigenvectors formed via Segre classification aid in understanding the distribution of matter and energy within that particular kind. Solutions are presented for the isotropic type $[1,(111)]$, for the anisotropic type $[1,1(11)]$, where a new metric function is introduced, and for type $[1,111]$, where all three pressures differ and the Krori-Barua functions are used. They satisfy the physical and stability criteria, namely the Zeldovich condition, causality condition, energy conditions and adiabatic index. The types $[(1,1)11]$ and $[(1,11)1]$ have $ρ=-P_r$ and give dark energy like configurations in which the strong energy condition is violated in the core, the latter having the tachyonic fluid form of the energy momentum tensor. For type $[(1,1)(11)]$, a charged solution in the presence of a non-null electromagnetic field is obtained and joined to an electrovac exterior. Matching with the Levi-Civita exterior shows that the vanishing of the radial pressure at the boundary is equivalent to the Darmois conditions. The C-energy and the curvature invariants are evaluated for each solution, and the results are compared with earlier works.

</details>


### [226] [Imprints of quantum space in the ringdown phase of Kerr black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30391)
*Nikola Herceg,Tajron Jurić,A. Naveena Kumara,Andjelo Samsarov,Ivica Smolić,Kumar S. Gupta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在非交换几何（noncommutative geometry）框架下，利用伪半Killing Drinfeld twist，首次系统研究了非交换Kerr黑洞时空的引力度规扰动，给出准正规模（QNMs）频率与阻尼率的量子修正及其在引力波铃宕中的潜在观测印记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有非交换几何研究多局限于Schwarzschild情形，缺乏对旋转黑洞度规扰动的系统分析；本文旨在将Drinfeld twist方法从Schwarzschild推广至Kerr黑洞，考察量子形变对引力QNMs的影响。

Method: 采用伪半Killing Drinfeld twist（pseudo-semi-Killing Drinfeld twist），在保持经典Kerr几何为精确背景解的同时引入对线性化引力扰动的量子修正；在自旋与量子形变参数的一阶（含混合项）展开下，导出轴向扰动的类Schrödinger主方程及含频率与自旋依赖的有效势，并用WKB近似与Leaver连分数法（continued-fraction method）计算基频QNM。

Result: 结果显示，旋转、纯量子效应及其耦合作用均会修正QNM的振荡频率与阻尼率，其中自旋-量子耦合（spin-quantum coupling）引入额外的自旋依赖修正。基于Kerr锚定的唯象方案，对GWTC-3与GWTC-5.0中若干并合残余黑洞估算了频率与阻尼时间的分数偏移。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个使用Drinfeld twist分析非交换Kerr黑洞时空引力度规扰动的工作，将先前研究拓展至Schwarzschild之外；量子形变效应可同时改变振荡与阻尼特性，其估计偏移展示了在引力波铃宕（ringdown）中探测量子修正的可能性。

Abstract: We investigate quantum-space corrections to the gravitational quasinormal modes (QNMs) of Kerr black holes within the framework of noncommutative geometry. The analysis employs a pseudo-semi-Killing Drinfeld twist, which preserves the classical Kerr geometry as an exact background solution while introducing nontrivial quantum corrections to linearized gravitational perturbations. Working to first order in the black hole spin and the quantum deformation parameter, including their mixed contribution, we derive a Schrödinger-like master equation for axial gravitational perturbations with a frequency- and spin-dependent effective potential. We compute the fundamental QNM frequencies using the WKB approximation and Leaver's continued-fraction method.Our results reveal corrections to the QNM oscillation frequencies and damping rates arising from rotation, pure quantum effects, and their interplay.To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic analysis of gravitational metric perturbations in a noncommutative Kerr black hole spacetime using a Drinfeld twist, extending prior work beyond the Schwarzschild case. Our findings demonstrate that quantum deformation effects alter both the oscillation frequencies and damping rates of quasinormal modes, with spin-quantum coupling introducing additional spin-dependent corrections to the Kerr QNM spectrum. Using a Kerr-anchored phenomenological prescription, we estimate the fractional frequency and damping-time shifts for selected merger remnants from the GWTC-3 and GWTC-5.0 catalogs. These estimates illustrate the potential signatures of quantum corrections in gravitational-wave ringdown.

</details>


### [227] [Extreme Mass Ratio Inspirals: accessing generic orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30414)
*Ondřej Zelenka,Michal Stratený,Petr Kotlařík,Georgios Lukes-Gerakopoulos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文提出一种机器学习方法，用于预测极端质量比旋进（EMRI）通量计算中占主导的径向模数，从而减少不必要的模式计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 频域形式求解 Teukolsky 方程需计算大量模式振幅，其中许多可以忽略，但事先无法判断哪些可安全跳过，造成计算浪费。

Method: 采用机器学习方法，将主导径向模数（dominant radial mode number）建模为其余模式数的函数进行估计，并应用于 EMRI 通量计算流程。

Result: 该方法可用于剔除不必要的计算，降低 EMRI 通量计算中的冗余模式数量；摘要未给出具体量化精度或加速比。

Conclusion: 机器学习预测主导模数可作为削减 EMRI 波形建模计算成本的可行手段，服务于 LISA 相关引力波数据处理需求。

Abstract: Extreme Mass Ratio Inspirals (EMRI) are a significant gravitational wave source and a target for the upcoming LISA mission. Computations of the fluxes of conserved quantities, such as energy and angular momentum, are a critical part of modeling EMRI waveforms and can be performed using a frequency domain formalism to solve the Teukolsky equation. This requires computations of large numbers of mode amplitudes, many of which are negligible. However, it is unknown ex ante which can be safely skipped. We present a machine learning approach to estimate the dominant radial mode number as a function of the remaining mode numbers and apply it to reduce the number of unnecessary computations in EMRI flux calculation.

</details>


### [228] [Post-Newtonian expansion of the energy and angular momentum flux radiated to infinity: Eccentric-equatorial orbits about a Kerr black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30437)
*Jezreel C. Castillo,Christopher Munna,Zachary Nasipak,J. David Brown,Charles R. Evans*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文给出无自旋点质量在Kerr黑洞偏心赤道轨道上辐射至无穷远的能量与角动量通量的解析表达式。采用Teukolsky形式体系与Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi方法，在$1/p$与$e$上做双重展开至10PN、$e^{20}$，并以数值结果验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作仅涉及Schwarzschild黑洞的偏心轨道，对具有自旋的Kerr黑洞尚缺高精度的解析通量结果，而这类通量对极端质量比旋近（EMRI）波形建模至关重要。

Method: 在一阶黑洞微扰理论（black hole perturbation theory）中采用Teukolsky形式体系（Teukolsky formalism），借助Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi方法获得Teukolsky方程的无限级数解析解，并用Mathematica生成对后牛顿紧致参数$1/p$与偏心率$e$的双重展开。

Result: 展开达到10PN与$e^{20}$，超过已有的6PN、$e^{16}$结果；刻画了通量系数对自旋$a$与$e$的依赖，并找到一组可关于$e$重整为闭式表达式的自旋依赖分量。与独立Teukolsky代码的高精度结果及插值通量数据集对比，在$a=0.9M$、$p\gtrsim20$至75、$e$由0.1到0.7范围内，10PN通量与现有插值网格吻合约$10^{-7}$。

Conclusion: 解析通量表达式得到数值验证，在较大$p$范围内可替代Teukolsky代码结果，适用于EMRI等需要高精度偏心Kerr轨道通量的场景。

Abstract: We present analytical expressions for the energy and angular momentum fluxes radiated to infinity by a (nonspinning) point mass in an eccentric-equatorial orbit about a Kerr black hole. These results build upon our previous work involving point masses on eccentric orbits about a Schwarzschild black hole. We now adopt the Teukolsky formalism in first-order black hole perturbation theory to extend our methods to Kerr. We obtain the expressions using the method of Mano, Suzuki, and Takasugi, which provides infinite-series analytical solutions to the Teukolsky equation that we generate using Mathematica. The expressions presented are obtained as a double expansion in the post-Newtonian (PN) compactness parameter $1/p$, where $p$ is the dimensionless semi-latus rectum, and in the eccentricity $e$, up to 10PN and $e^{20}$, extending beyond prior 6PN and $e^{16}$ results available for generic orbits. We characterize how the PN flux coefficients depend on the black hole spin $a$ and $e$, and find a set of spin-dependent components that can be resummed as closed-form expressions in $e$. The analytical expressions are then validated with numerical comparisons against high-precision sample results from an independent Teukolsky code and with a broader set of numerically interpolated values derived from a flux data set produced with the same code. For $a=0.9M$, the numerically evaluated 10PN fluxes match current interpolated flux grids ($\sim10^{-7}$) for $p\gtrsim20$ to $75$ as $e$ ranges from $0.1$ to $0.7$, where they could replace Teukolsky-code results.

</details>


### [229] [Dissipative Tidal Love Numbers of Neutron Stars from Viscous Heating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30466)
*Nikola Savić,Filippo Vernizzi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在世界线有效场论框架下计算了非旋转相对论中子星的领头低频耗散潮汐响应，并给出线性耗散Love数的两种互补推导。结果表明，对冷中子星而言剪切黏性潮汐耗散对引力波相位的影响可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星双星引力波波形中潮汐效应的精确建模需要了解耗散性潮汐响应，而此前研究多集中于保守（非耗散）潮汐Love数，低频耗散贡献的定量大小尚不明确。

Method: 采用世界线有效场论（worldline effective field theory）描述潮汐响应，通过两条互补途径确定线性耗散Love数：一是基于星体内部红移黏性熵产生（redshifted viscous entropy production），将计算化为对静态平衡潮（static equilibrium tide）的积分；二是利用外部度规的虚数、频率奇次部分作为独立校验。随后结合公开物态方程（equation of state）评估对双星引力波相位的修正。

Result: 对等质量1.4+1.4 M⊙双星，在红移温度T∞=10^5 K与标准电子剪切黏性下，20 Hz至1 kHz频段内潮汐耗散引起的引力波相位偏移在所考虑的四组物态方程下均小于10^-3周；若用简单热模型计入星体升温，偏移进一步降至10^-6周以下。

Conclusion: 在所采用的黏性模型下，冷中子星的剪切黏性潮汐耗散对引力波相位演化可忽略，意味着可观测的耗散潮汐信号需源于其他物理机制。

Abstract: We compute the leading low-frequency dissipative tidal response of nonrotating relativistic neutron stars. We formulate the response in a worldline effective field theory. We determine the linear dissipative Love number in two complementary ways: from the redshifted viscous entropy production inside the star and from the imaginary, frequency-odd part of the exterior metric. The first method reduces the calculation to an integral over the static equilibrium tide; the second provides an independent check. We apply our approach to publicly available equations of state and assess the corresponding correction to the gravitational-wave phase of neutron-star binaries. For an equal-mass $1.4+1.4M_\odot$ binary with both stars at a redshifted temperature $T_\infty=10^5\,\mathrm K$ and a standard electron shear viscosity, tidal dissipation shifts the gravitational-wave phase by less than $10^{-3}$ cycles between $20\,{\rm Hz}$ and $1\,{\rm kHz}$ for the four equations of state considered. Including the resulting rise in the stars' temperature in a simple thermal model reduces the shift below $10^{-6}$ cycles. For the viscosity model considered here, shear-viscous tidal dissipation in cold neutron stars is negligible for gravitational-wave phasing. This suggests that a measurable dissipative tidal signature would have to come from a different mechanism.

</details>


### [230] [Geometric distinguishability of two-source gravitational-wave signals from the extrinsic geometry of the single-source waveform manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30560)
*Cezar Valentin Ionescu,Paul Adrian Gogîţă,Laurentiu Ioan Caramete,Ana Caramete*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文以几何视角刻画重叠引力波信号的“源混淆（source confusion）”问题：将单源波形族视为探测噪声加权希尔伯特空间中的嵌入流形，提出“到流形距离（distance-to-manifold）”统计量，并从理论上与数值上验证其可表征多源可分辨性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重叠的引力波信号可能出现强简并，使多源波形被单源“有效源”精确表示，从而难以分辨；现有方法缺乏坐标无关、可解析的诊断量来定位混淆发生的参数方向。

Method: 将单源波形族建模为探测器希尔伯特空间中的嵌入流形，定义到流形的最小残差统计量；在无噪声极限下证明其平方与双源/单源假设的极大似然差相关，并用微扰最小化分析近邻双源情形。数值上采用六参数频域 inspiraling 模型与类 LISA 时间延迟干涉响应进行直接优化与二维混淆区映射。

Result: 有效单源拟合可吸收全部领头切空间形变，第一不可约残差位于流形法空间并由第二基本形式（second fundamental form）支配；数值优化在可分辨区间重现预测的四次标度关系，二维混淆区图显示高度各向异性的可分辨性边界。

Conclusion: 外在波形流形曲率（extrinsic waveform-manifold curvature）可作为局部多源混淆的坐标无关诊断量，并为识别最难分辨的参数空间方向提供实用框架。

Abstract: Overlapping gravitational-wave signals can produce strong degeneracies in which a multi-source waveform is accurately represented by a single effective source. We formulate this source-confusion problem geometrically by treating the family of single-source waveforms as an embedded manifold in the noise-weighted detector Hilbert space. We introduce the distance-to-manifold statistic, defined as the minimum residual obtained by fitting a waveform with the single-source model, and show that its squared value is directly related to the maximized likelihood difference between two-source and single-source hypotheses in the noiseless limit. For two sources with nearby parameters, perturbative minimization demonstrates that the effective single-source fit absorbs all leading tangent-space deformations. The first irreducible residual lies in the normal space of the waveform manifold and is governed by its second fundamental form. We validate our approach using a six-parameter frequency-domain inspiral model with a LISA-like time-delay-interferometry response. Direct optimization reproduces the predicted quartic scaling over the numerically resolved regime, while two-dimensional confusion-zone maps reveal strongly anisotropic distinguishability boundaries. These results establish extrinsic waveform-manifold curvature as a coordinate-independent diagnostic of local multi-source confusion and provide a practical framework for identifying parameter-space directions in which overlapping gravitational-wave sources are most difficult to resolve.

</details>


### [231] [The Science Chapter of the Einstein Telescope Geometry Comparison Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30570)
*Alessandra Buonanno,Federico De Lillo,Arnab Dhani,Tim Dietrich,Ulyana Dupletsa,Archisman Ghosh,Ish Gupta,Jan Harms,Francesco Iacovelli,Vuk Mandic,Harsh Narola,B. S. Sathyaprakash,Chris Van den Broeck,Haowen Zhong*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该章节比较爱因斯坦望远镜（Einstein Telescope, ET）三种几何构型（单址10 km三角形Δ、两址15 km L形2L，近对齐与错位），结果显示三者均可实现ET科学目标但各有所长：错位2L最利于源定位、多信使与亮警笛宇宙学，近对齐2L最利于随机背景，Δ在连续源与爆发源上表现相当。Fisher信息矩阵（Fisher information matrix, FIM）预测与贝叶斯推断（Bayesian inference）对照总体不改变构型排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究由ET董事会（ET Board of Governmental Representatives）委托，旨在为构型选择提供"科学附加价值"依据，即量化不同几何布局对ET广泛科学目标（致密双星、致密物质、宇宙学、随机背景、多信使、基础物理、连续源与爆发源）的影响。

Method: 采用代表ET科学目标的指标，对单址10 km三角形（Δ）与两址15 km L形（2L，近对齐/错位）三种构型进行对比；并以约550个双黑洞（binary black hole）与中子星-黑洞（neutron star-black hole）信号的Fisher信息矩阵预测对照贝叶斯推断进行验证，同时评估加入宇宙探索者（Cosmic Explorer）后的网络效果。

Result: 多数灵敏度驱动的科学目标中2L优于Δ；错位2L在单源定位与刻画、多信使天文学及亮警笛宇宙学上最佳，近对齐2L在随机背景上最佳；Δ定位优于对齐2L，并在连续源、爆发源及千赫兹频段与两种2L相当；30°的2L取向在随机背景灵敏度与定位间取得良好折中。FIM对内禀参数未表现出系统性构型偏好，但对距离与天区定位因无法刻画多模态与长程相关性而偏差较大，却不影响排序；加入Cosmic Explorer后各构型覆盖范围提升、几何差异缩小而排序基本不变。

Conclusion: 三种ET构型均能实现ET科学目标，但优势领域不同，构型选择需在随机背景灵敏度与源定位/多信使能力之间权衡，30°取向的2L可作为折中方案；FIM虽在距离与天区定位上存在偏差，但足以可靠支撑构型排序结论。

Abstract: This is the Scientific Added Value Chapter of the Einstein Telescope (ET) Geometry Comparison Report, commissioned from the ET Organization by the ET Board of Governmental Representatives. We compare three configurations: a single-site 10-km triangle ($Δ$) and two 15-km L-shaped detectors (2L) at widely separated sites, either nearly aligned or misaligned. We use metrics representative of the ET science case: compact-binary populations, dense matter, cosmography, stochastic backgrounds, multimessenger astronomy, fundamental physics, and continuous and burst sources. All three configurations deliver the ET science case, but they excel in different areas. For most sensitivity-driven science, the 2L configurations outperform the $Δ$. The misaligned 2L is best for localizing and characterizing individual sources, multimessenger astronomy, and bright-siren cosmology. The nearly aligned 2L is best for stochastic backgrounds. The $Δ$ localizes sources better than the aligned 2L and performs comparably to both 2L configurations for continuous and burst sources and at kilohertz frequencies. A 2L orientation of $30^\circ$ is a good compromise between stochastic-background sensitivity and localization. We validate Fisher information matrix (FIM) forecasts against Bayesian inference for $\sim550$ binary black hole and neutron star-black hole signals. Despite substantial differences, the FIM shows no systematic preference for either configuration for intrinsic parameters. For distance and sky localization, the FIM cannot capture multimodality and long-range correlations, leading to larger discrepancies, which however do not affect the ranking. Adding Cosmic Explorer to the network improves the reach of all three ET configurations, especially in volume localization and characterization of high-redshift sources. Differences between geometries shrink, but the ranking is largely unchanged.

</details>


### [232] [Constraining Regular Black Hole Models with the Effective Field Theory treatment of General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30616)
*Sami Viollet*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 论文提出以“广义相对论作为有效场论（effective field theory）”的微扰预言为基准，检验黑洞背景下的非微扰量子引力模型，并以 Hayward 度规为例说明其不符合微扰结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非微扰量子引力模型（如正则黑洞（regular black holes））缺乏与已知微扰结果对照的检验手段，需要一个可操作的判据来评估其合理性。

Method: 先回顾该有效场论框架下关于黑洞附近静态粒子相互作用能（interaction energy）一阶量子修正的已知结果，再据此构造检验以修正 Schwarzschild 度规形式表述的非微扰量子黑洞模型的方法。

Result: 对 Hayward 度规（Hayward metric）的应用表明其与微扰预言不一致；同时给出了能够重现该预期结果的最一般度规形式。

Conclusion: 微扰有效场论结果可作为筛选非微扰量子黑洞模型的约束条件，并可用于反推符合要求的度规类别。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a method for using perturbative predictions from treating general relativity as an effective field theory to constrain non-perturbative approaches to quantum gravity in black hole contexts. We start by reviewing a known result from this framework, which estimates the first quantum corrections to the interaction energy of a static particle near a black hole. We then propose a way to test non-perturbative quantum black hole models - specifically those formulated as modified Schwarzschild metrics, also known as regular black holes - against this perturbative result. To illustrate, we apply our method to the Hayward metric, showing that it does not align with the perturbative prediction, and we identify the most general metric that can reproduce the expected result.

</details>


### [233] [Central charges as black-hole hair in weakly coupled trace-anomaly gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30708)
*Francisco Tello-Ortiz,A. Vásquez,Kazuharu Bamba,Ghulam Mustafa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究Gabadadze弱耦合引力迹反常（gravitational trace anomaly）有效理论中的黑洞，证明常数anomalyon解仅在Euler与Weyl两个中心荷（central charges）相等时适用于任意Ricci平直几何（含Kerr族），否则黑洞携带由表面重力、视界拓扑与中心荷之差唯一确定的标量毛发（scalar hair）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是考察引力迹反常有效理论中Euler与Weyl中心荷并存时黑洞解的存在性与性质，特别是标量场（anomalyon）能否在Ricci平直背景上自洽存在，以及物质部门与电荷如何影响黑洞毛发。

Method: 采用Gabadadze弱耦合有效理论框架，保留Euler与Weyl两个中心荷，通过解析证明常数anomalyon解的条件，并对高阶导数Weyl算符作阶约化（order-reduction）以分析背景、anomalyon-引力子混合（anomalyon-graviton mixing）及传播速度。

Result: 毛发荷仅由表面重力、视界拓扑与中心荷之差决定：最大超对称物质使黑洞无毛，标准模型与光子主导部门给出确定符号的毛发，共形对撞机界（conformal collider bounds）将其限制在普适窗口内。电荷使毛发在临界荷质比处变号，极端值仅保留Weyl荷，Euler荷主导anomalyon的近视界增长；所有幺正物质部门中视界正则的毛发在Cauchy视界发散。

Conclusion: 阶约化后，背景、混合与传播速度仅依赖中心荷之差，从而将无鬼（no-ghost）与Laplacian稳定性条件推广至完整反常理论；结果表明反常毛发是中心荷不等时的普遍特征并受物质部门与电荷结构约束。

Abstract: We study black holes in Gabadadze's weakly coupled effective theory of the gravitational trace anomaly, keeping both the Euler and the Weyl central charges. We prove that a constant anomalyon on any Ricci-flat geometry, the Kerr family included, solves the full theory if and only if the two central charges coincide. Otherwise, the black holes continuously connected to this branch carry scalar hair whose charge is fixed by the surface gravity, the horizon topology and the difference of central charges alone: maximally supersymmetric matter leaves black holes bald, the Standard Model and photon-dominated sectors produce hair of definite sign, and conformal collider bounds confine it to a universal window. Electric charge separates the two anomaly invariants: the hair reverses sign at a critical charge-to-mass ratio set by the ratio of central charges, its extremal value retains only the Weyl charge, and the Euler charge governs the near-horizon growth of the anomalyon. For every unitary matter sector, the hair regular at the event horizon diverges at the Cauchy horizon. Finally, once the higher-derivative Weyl operator is order-reduced, the background, the anomalyon-graviton mixing and all propagation speeds depend only on the difference of central charges, which extends the no-ghost and Laplacian stability conditions to the full anomaly.

</details>


### [234] [Detecting Frame Dragging Effect with Next-Generation Spaceborne Clocks in a LEO-DRO Satellite Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30726)
*Li-Fang Li,Hanlin Song,Xing Bian,Tong Liu,Shao-Jiang Wang,Song He,Yang Li,Jiaxing Leng,Ziren Luo,Peng Xu,Rong-Gen Cai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本工作提出由一颗低地球轨道（low Earth orbit, LEO）卫星与一颗远距离逆行轨道（distant retrograde orbit, DRO）卫星组成的星座方案，利用下一代光钟的频率比对测量约 $10^{-20}$ 量级的参考系拖曳（frame-dragging）信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在提升对地球自转引起的参考系拖曳信号的探测灵敏度，同时获得稳定且引力隔离良好的频率参考，并借光钟稳定性进展实现对同量级拖曳信号的直接测量。

Method: 建立 LEO 与 DRO 之间频率比对的完整相对论公式，纳入后牛顿度规（post-Newtonian metric）、地球高阶多极矩、日月潮汐势（tidal potentials）以及引力磁场矢量势（gravitomagnetic vector potential）。

Result: 参考系拖曳信号量级为 $10^{-20}$；零死时间（zero-dead-time）锶光晶格钟（strontium optical lattice clocks）已实现 20,000 s 平均时间下 $2.9\times10^{-19}$ 的分数频率不稳定度，估计一天稳定性为 $2.5\times10^{-19}$。

Conclusion: 随着钟稳定性持续提升，近期有望达到 $10^{-20}$ 量级的分数频率灵敏度，从而直接测量同量级参考系拖曳信号，约束地球引力磁场（gravitomagnetic field），并为爱因斯坦钟同步（Einstein clock synchronization）中引力磁效应的未来研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Our approach employs a satellite constellation comprising one satellite in low Earth orbit (LEO) and one satellite in distant retrograde orbit (DRO). This configuration is designed to enhance the sensitivity to the Earth's rotation-induced frame-dragging signal while providing a stable and gravitationally isolated frequency reference. We derive a complete relativistic formulation for the frequency comparison between LEO and DRO, incorporating the post-Newtonian metric, higher-order Earth multipoles, lunar and solar tidal potentials, and the gravitomagnetic vector potential. The frame-dragging signal is of order $10^{-20}$, a level that is becoming increasingly accessible with next-generation optical clocks. Recent advances in zero-dead-time strontium optical lattice clocks have demonstrated a fractional frequency instability of $2.9\times10^{-19}$ at an averaging time of 20,000~s, with an estimated one-day stability of $2.5\times10^{-19}$. With continued advances in clock stability, achieving fractional-frequency sensitivity at the $10^{-20}$ level appears feasible in the near term, potentially enabling the direct measurement of a frame-dragging signal of the same order. Such a measurement would constrain Earth's gravitomagnetic field, enabling future investigations of gravitomagnetic effects on Einstein clock synchronization.

</details>


### [235] [Analytic $k$-essence Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30846)
*V. K. Oikonomou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个完全解析的 k-essence 暴胀解（k-essence inflation），在无需将慢滚假设（slow-roll）动态代入场方程的前提下仍满足观测约束，且压强奇点（pressure singularity）在引入辐射与冷暗物质后被证明可经典规避。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有暴胀模型多依赖慢滚近似（slow-roll approximation）简化场方程，本文旨在给出不依赖该动态假设、同时与观测约束相符的解析暴胀方案。

Method: 取动能项形式 K(X)=X^n/X_s^{n-1} 并施加追踪条件（tracking condition）φ̇²=γH^{-m}，求得哈勃参数、标度因子与标量场的解析表达式；该理论中原初曲率扰动（primordial curvature perturbations）的声速（sound speed）为常数，故不存在梯度不稳定性（gradient instabilities）。

Result: 暴胀观测指数仅依赖参数 n 与 m，且所得演化确为暴胀性质；暴胀结束后若干 e-fold 出现压强奇点，但经定性与定量数值论证，在计入辐射与冷暗物质（cold dark matter）等物质流体后该奇点被经典地避免。

Conclusion: 该解析 k-essence 暴胀解在满足观测约束的同时保持声速恒定与稳定性，其晚期压强奇点问题可由实际宇宙的物质组分自然消除。

Abstract: In this work we develop a fully analytic $k$-essence inflation solution which is compatible with the observational constraints on inflation, without the need for the slow-roll assumption being imposed dynamically in the field equations. The analytic solution is obtained for a kinetic scalar sector of the form $K(X)= \frac{X^n}{X_s^{n-1}}$ and for the scalar field satisfying a tracking condition $\dotφ^2=γH^{-m}$. For this class of theories, the sound speed of the primordial curvature perturbations is constant, so no gradient instabilities occur in the theory. The Hubble rate, the scale factor and the scalar field can be analytically expressed as functions of the cosmic time, and the observational indices of inflation depend solely on the parameters $n$ and $m$. The resulting evolution is inflationary and it develops a pressure singularity a few $e$-foldings after the end of inflation. However, using qualitative and quantitative numerical arguments, we show that the pressure singularity is classically avoided when the matter fluids of radiation and cold dark matter are taken into account.

</details>


### [236] [Schwarzschild-de Sitter space is not rigid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31019)
*Philippe G. LeFloch,Luc Rozoy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在任意紧致亚极端 Schwarzschild–de Sitter 区域基础上，构造了一族含两个额外黑洞视界（面积可指定）的静态轴对称真空解，并分析其极限与误差函数（lapse）极大集几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是在正宇宙学常数背景下，于给定参考几何中实现多个指定面积黑洞视界的可控构造，并刻画其解析性质与极限行为。

Method: 方法为跨尺度粘合（gluing at separated scales）结合一致椭圆估计（uniform elliptic estimates）；用积分条件消除各 Schwarzschild 模型的无穷小平移，用局部张量源恢复线性化方程可解性，并以反射对称与径向位置调节消去源系数；再用径向 Green 函数与球面热方程确定误差函数角向变化。

Result: 所得解的所有视界与对称轴正则，度规与 lapse 到边界实解析；质量参数趋零时度规与 lapse 光滑收敛到参考解（除极大 lapse 球面两对径点外），在新增视界附近重标度得到 Schwarzschild 空间度规；小质量时 lapse 极大集恰为一赤道圆，其 Hessian 横向负定，近邻正则水平集为环面。

Conclusion: 结论是可通过粘合方法构造具有规定面积多视界的静态轴对称真空解，且其极限、局部几何与极大集结构均得到严格刻画。

Abstract: Starting from any compact sub-extremal Schwarzschild-de Sitter region with positive cosmological constant, we construct a family of static axisymmetric vacuum solutions with two additional black-hole horizons, each of prescribed area. All horizons and the symmetry axis are regular, and the metric and lapse are real analytic up to the boundary. As the mass parameter tends to zero, the metric and lapse converge smoothly to the reference solution away from two antipodal points on its sphere of maximal lapse. Rescaling near either added horizon yields the Schwarzschild spatial metric in the limit. For small prescribed mass, the lapse attains its maximum precisely on one equatorial circle. Its Hessian is negative definite in the transverse plane, with the smaller eigenvalue magnitude asymptotic to a positive constant times the added mass. Nearby regular level sets are tori. The proof combines gluing at separated scales with uniform elliptic estimates. An integral condition removes infinitesimal translations of each Schwarzschild model, while a localized tensor source restores solvability of the linearized equation. Matching the local Schwarzschild corrections to a global Green tensor identifies the leading source coefficients. Reflection makes these coefficients equal, and varying the common radial position of the centers makes them vanish. A radial Green function and the heat equation on the sphere determine the angular variation of the lapse and the geometry of its maximum set.

</details>


### [237] [Lyapunov Stability of a Schwarzschild Black Hole Surrounded by the Kiselev Anisotropic Fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31035)
*Saswati Roy,Paramita Debnath,Shubham Kala,Hemwati Nandan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究嵌入 Kiselev 各向异性流体（Kiselev anisotropic fluid）的 Schwarzschild 黑洞周围的测地线结构与圆轨道稳定性，通过有效势与坐标时 Lyapunov 指数（coordinate-time Lyapunov exponent）确定光子球、ISCO 与 OSCO，并给出任意 ω 下闭合形式的 Lyapunov 指数平方表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kiselev 各向异性流体为描述黑洞周围暗能量类或额外物质环境提供参数化框架，而此类环境中圆轨道的稳定性边界（光子球、ISCO、OSCO）尚需系统刻画，且需要与已知度规（Schwarzschild–de Sitter、Reissner–Nordström 等）建立联系。

Method: 采用零测地线与类时测地线的有效势（effective potential）方法，取状态方程参数 ω = -1, -2/3, -1/3, 0, 1/3, 1 及由视界结构限定的归一化参数 N 的容许范围；用坐标时 Lyapunov 指数检验稳定性，并推导 (λ_t^T)^2 的闭合表达式。

Result: 光子球在全部 (ω, N) 下均不稳定：ω ≤ 0 时不稳定度随 N 增大而减弱，ω = -1、-2/3 在极端极限处消失；ω = 1/3 时随 N 非单调变化，ω = 1 时增强。ω = -1、-2/3 时稳定圆轨道局限于 ISCO 与 OSCO 之间，二者在临界 N 处合并，超过后不存在稳定圆轨道；ω ≥ -1/3 时 ISCO 之外所有半径均存在稳定圆轨道。(λ_t^T)^2 的符号恰在 ISCO 与 OSCO 处变号，与稳定性边界一致。

Conclusion: Lyapunov 判据重现了稳定圆运动的边界，验证了方法的自洽性；Schwarzschild–de Sitter、质量平移 Schwarzschild 与 Reissner–Nordström 度规作为特例被回收，结果对黑洞阴影（black-hole shadow）、薄吸积盘（thin accretion disc）与准周期振荡（quasi-periodic oscillation）具有潜在意义。

Abstract: We investigate the geodesic structure and the stability of circular orbits around a Schwarzschild black hole surrounded by a Kiselev anisotropic fluid, characterised by the equation-of-state parameter $ω$ and the normalisation parameter $N$. We consider six representative values, $ω=-1,-2/3,-1/3,0,1/3$ and $1$, over the admissible range of $N$ determined by the horizon structure, including the corresponding extremal limits. Using the effective potentials for null and timelike geodesics, we determine the photon sphere, the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) and the outermost stable circular orbit (OSCO), and examine their stability through coordinate-time Lyapunov exponents. The photon sphere is unstable for all values of $ω$ and $N$ considered. Its instability weakens with increasing $N$ for $ω\leq0$ and vanishes at extremality for $ω=-1$ and $-2/3$, whereas it varies non-monotonically with $N$ for $ω=1/3$ and strengthens for $ω=1$. For $ω=-1$ and $-2/3$, stable circular orbits are confined between the ISCO and the OSCO, which merge at a critical value of $N$ beyond which no stable circular orbits exist. In contrast, for $ω\geq-1/3$ stable circular orbits exist at all radii beyond the ISCO, whose location varies with $N$. We also derive a closed-form expression for the squared coordinate-time Lyapunov exponent $(λ_t^T)^2$, valid for arbitrary $ω$, whose sign changes exactly at the ISCO and OSCO, so that the Lyapunov criterion reproduces the boundaries of stable circular motion. The Schwarzschild--de Sitter, mass-shifted Schwarzschild and Reissner--Nordström geometries are recovered as special cases, and the results may have implications for black-hole shadows, thin accretion discs and quasi-periodic oscillations.

</details>


### [238] [Relativistic dynamical friction and accretion for Kerr black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31061)
*Nicholas Speeney,Soumodeep Mitra,Emanuele Berti,Andrea Maselli,Sumanta Chakraborty*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在Kerr黑洞（Kerr black hole）嵌入无碰撞暗物质（dark matter, DM）分布的设定下，计算非自旋致密天体在圆赤道轨道上的相对论性动力学摩擦（dynamical friction）与吸积（accretion），揭示自旋导致摩擦减弱甚至反向的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA观测的极端质量比旋近（extreme-mass-ratio inspirals, EMRIs）可用于探测大质量黑洞周围环境，包括预期形成的暗物质超密度区；此前结果限于Schwarzschild时空，需推广至旋转黑洞。

Method: 将DM建模为从初始Hernquist轮廓（Hernquist profile）绝热增长的尖峰（spike），通过对相对论散射与俘获截面（capture cross sections）在次天体所见的一般各向异性相空间（phase space）上积分，得到动量转移量。

Result: 远离主黑洞时摩擦随局部DM密度变化且随自旋增大；在快速自旋（a≳0.37）黑洞的最内稳定圆轨道（innermost stable circular orbit, ISCO）附近，参考系拖曳（frame dragging）迫使束缚DM粒子共转，摩擦减弱并在密度峰值处反向；非旋转黑洞若初始晕具有大净角动量也可模拟该反向，但所需各向异性超出宇宙学模拟结果。

Conclusion: 常用的Chandrasekhar型（Chandrasekhar-like）局部密度处方可能高估摩擦力；建模DM对EMRIs的影响时必须纳入环境的完整相空间结构。

Abstract: Extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) observed by LISA will probe the environments of massive black holes (BHs), including the dark matter (DM) overdensities expected to form around them. We compute the relativistic dynamical friction and accretion experienced by a non-spinning compact object on circular equatorial orbits around a Kerr BH embedded in a collisionless DM distribution, extending previous results in Schwarzschild. The DM is modeled as a spike grown adiabatically from an initial Hernquist profile, and the momentum transfer is obtained by integrating relativistic scattering and capture cross sections over the full, generically anisotropic phase space seen by the secondary. Far from the primary the friction tracks the local DM density, which increases with spin. Near the innermost stable circular orbit of rapidly spinning BHs $(a\gtrsim 0.37)$, however, frame dragging forces bound DM particles to co-rotate, so that the secondary is overtaken by the surrounding medium: the friction weakens and eventually reverses sign where the DM density peaks. We show that a similar reversal can be mimicked around a non-rotating BH if the initial halo carries large net angular momentum, although this requires anisotropies beyond those found in cosmological simulations. We also show that commonly used Chandrasekhar-like prescriptions, which depend only on the local density, can overestimate the friction force. Our results demonstrate that the full phase-space structure of the environment must be included when modeling DM effects on EMRIs.

</details>


### [239] [Enhancing eccentricity through tidal resonance in binary neutron star systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31172)
*Ivan Markin,Natalie Williams,Tim Dietrich*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 数值相对论模拟显示，高度自旋的双中子星系统中，逆行自旋星的 f 模共振可在晚期旋近阶段引入轨道偏心率，该机制与双星形成历史无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道偏心率（orbital eccentricity）通常被视为双中子星形成历史的探针：孤立形成的双星在并合前应早已圆化，可测偏心率暗示致密星环境中的动力学形成。本文旨在检验偏心率是否可能另有非形成历史的来源。

Method: 采用数值相对论（numerical relativity）模拟两颗高度自旋中子星的并合，其中一颗为顺行自旋（prograde）、另一颗为逆行自旋（retrograde），并分析 f 模（f-mode）共振与角动量转移过程，辅以共振锁定模型（resonance locking model）解释自旋演化。

Result: 模拟中观测到逆行自旋星 f 模的持续潮汐共振（tidal resonance），伴随轨道向其转移角动量导致自旋逐渐改变；该自旋变化可由考虑 f 模频率自旋依赖偏移的简单共振锁定模型很好地解释，且初始准圆轨道（quasicircular）在晚期旋近中出现偏心率。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一种与形成历史无关的双中子星偏心率起源，并为快速自旋双中子星动力学建模提供了重要依据。

Abstract: Orbital eccentricity of binary neutron star systems is a key parameter to reveal their formation history. Since binaries formed in isolation are expected to circularize long before merger, measurable eccentricity suggests dynamical formation in dense stellar environments. However, using numerical-relativity simulations, we show that eccentricity can be introduced in the late inspiral of an initially quasicircular binary, due to the $f$-mode resonance of one of the stars. We simulate the merger of two highly spinning neutron stars, one star spinning prograde and the other retrograde. Persistent tidal resonance with the $f$ mode of the retrograde-spinning star is observed, accompanied by a gradual change of its spin due to angular momentum transfer from the orbit. This spin change can be well explained by a simple resonance locking model that accounts for the spin-dependent shift of the $f$-mode frequency. These findings reveal an origin of eccentricity in binary neutron stars that is independent of their formation history and provide important information for modeling binary neutron star dynamics with rapidly spinning stars.

</details>


### [240] [Optimal Statistic for a Stochastic Gravitational Wave Background using Pulsar-Timing--Astrometry Cross Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31196)
*Kimberly K. Boddy,Jeffrey S. Hazboun,Kris Pardo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文将天体测量（astrometry）视为探测引力波（GWs）的可行手段，类比脉冲星计时（pulsar timing）发展出恒星角向偏折的成对相关统计量，并进一步构建天体测量与脉冲星计时残差的交叉相关统计量，给出弱信号与中等信号情形下的信噪比及标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星计时是既有的引力波探测途径，但天体测量可提供与之重叠且更高频段的覆盖，因此需要建立与脉冲星计时相对应的统计分析方法，以搜索随机引力波背景（stochastic GW background）。

Method: 基于引力波同时引起脉冲到达时间变化与恒星视位置横向角偏折这一共同物理机制，采用天文学测量与脉冲星计时的统一形式（common formalism），构造恒星偏折成对之间的相关统计量，并导出恒星偏折与脉冲星计时残差之间的交叉相关统计量（cross-correlation statistic）。

Result: 由相关统计量估计了信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio），并给出弱信号（weak-signal）与中等信号（intermediate-signal）两种情形下的标度律（scaling laws）。

Conclusion: 天体测量可作为脉冲星计时搜索引力波的补充手段，二者共享统一的形式框架，并可支持联合的相关与交叉相关分析，其探测能力可由所导出的信噪比标度律刻画。

Abstract: Astrometric measurements have emerged as a viable probe of gravitational waves (GWs), complementing pulsar timing searches by providing overlapping and higher GW frequency coverage. At its core, the search for GWs using astrometry is very similar to the search with pulsar timing data: GWs induce a change in pulse arrival times along the line of sight to a pulsar and a transverse angular deflection in the apparent position of a star. In analogy to pulsar timing, we develop a correlation statistic between pairs of stellar deflections for astrometry to search for a stochastic GW background. With a common formalism for both astrometry and pulsar timing, we also develop the cross-correlation statistic between stellar deflections and pulsar timing residuals. We estimate the signal-to-noise ratios from the correlation statistics and provide scaling laws in the weak- and intermediate-signal regimes.

</details>


### [241] [On the existence of constants of motion for multipolar test-body dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31200)
*Paul Ramond*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 延伸试验体在自旋诱导与绝热潮汐多极矩作用下的守恒运动方程可表述为哈密顿量 H=-½μ²+μU 的流；在Kerr时空中，Carter常数与Rüdiger常数的推广仅对Kerr黑洞多极矩存在（至十六极阶），但在自旋五次阶不再可延拓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带自旋与潮汐多极矩的延伸试验体（extended test body）的守恒动力学，并考察Kerr时空中隐藏对称性（hidden symmetry）及其守恒常数对物体多极结构的依赖。

Method: 将任意阶自旋诱导与绝热潮汐多极矩产生的力与力矩统一为协变标量势 U，证明运动方程等价于哈密顿量 H=-½μ²+μU 的哈密顿流；随后在Kerr背景下构造Carter常数与Rüdiger常数的推广（至十六极阶、自旋四次阶），并在自旋五次阶进行数值实现的解析判据检验。

Result: 存在精确守恒的质量概念与自旋范数（spin norm），适用于任意多极阶与任意背景；Carter与Rüdiger常数的推广当且仅当多极矩为Kerr黑洞多极矩时存在，使Kerr中自旋试验黑洞动力学在该阶微扰可积（perturbatively integrable）；在自旋五次阶，两者均不可延拓。

Conclusion: 在守恒试验体极限下，黑洞双星（black-hole binaries）具有其他致密天体所不具备的动力学隐藏对称性，该对称性可延续至很高多极阶但最终破缺。

Abstract: We show that the conservative equations of motion of an extended test body, with force and torque sourced by spin-induced and adiabatic tidal multipoles of arbitrary order, are the flow of the Hamiltonian $H=-\tfrac12μ^2+μU$, where $μ$ is the body's kinematic mass and all multipoles enter through a covariant scalar potential $U$. For such bodies, a certain notion of mass and a spin norm are shown to be exactly conserved, at all multipolar orders and in any background. We then apply our framework to the Kerr spacetime, and construct extensions of the Carter and Rüdiger constants through hexadecapolar order (quartic in spin). They are found to exist if, and only if, the body's multipoles are those of a Kerr black hole. Together with the energy and angular momentum, they make the dynamics of a spinning test black hole in Kerr perturbatively integrable through that order. Lastly, at the next, quintic-in-spin order, an analytic criterion implemented numerically reveals that neither constant admits a continuation, for any conservative multipoles. In the conservative, test-body limit, black-hole binaries thus carry a dynamical hidden symmetry shared by no other compact objects, which persists to high multipolar order but ultimately breaks.

</details>


<div id='math.OA'></div>

# math.OA [[Back]](#toc)

### [242] [Invariant subspaces and nest algebras in left-right operator splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28903)
*Mark Spivack*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该短文以算子代数视角分析粗糙面散射边界积分方程中的左右（left–right, L-R）分裂法：下三角（L 型）算子的弱闭包构成套代数（nest algebra），并在 Hilbert–Schmidt 核情形下给出可逆性、Arveson 距离公式及数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L-R 分裂法将边界积分算子分解为下三角与严格上三角部分，但此前缺乏对下三角部分所成算子代数结构的系统刻画，故需厘清其代数性质并评估前向截断近似的理论最优性。

Method: 考察 L 型算子构成的算子代数：证明其弱闭包为套代数（nest algebra），证明具 Hilbert–Schmidt 核的第二类 L 型算子存在 L 型逆，并用 Arveson 距离公式（Arveson's distance formula）以单一切口处的后向耦合刻画最优前向近似误差；辅以界估计与二维双层核（2D double-layer kernel）的数值实验。

Result: 数值与界估计表明截断近似接近最优；最坏情形输入场主要是左行掠射波（grazing wave），而非右行入射场的后向散射（backscatter）。对硬表面，逆 L 算子至二阶修正由镜像面 L 算子的四倍给出。

Conclusion: L-R 分裂的下三角部分具有清晰的套代数结构，支持截断近似的最优性判断，并给出硬表面逆算子的显式近似形式；文中另提出若干开放问题（open questions）。

Abstract: The left--right (L-R) splitting method for boundary integral equations in rough-surface scattering writes the integral operator as the sum of its lower- and strictly upper- triangular parts. This note examines the operator algebra formed by the lower-triangular ($L$-type) parts.Their weak closure is a nest algebra; second-kind $L$-type operators with Hilbert--Schmidt kernels have $L$-type inverses; and Arveson's distance formula expresses the error of the best possible forward-only approximation in terms of the backward coupling across a single cut. Bounds and numerical results for the 2D double-layer kernel suggest that truncation is close to optimal, and that the worst-case input field is a predominantly left-going grazing wave rather than backscatter of a right-going incident field. For a hard surface the inverse $L$-operator is given, up to a second-order correction, by four times the $L$-operator of the mirror-image surface, and several open questions are posed.

</details>


<div id='hep-ex'></div>

# hep-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [243] [Regge-eikonal description of high-energy proton-proton elastic scattering: a single-channel Gaussian benchmark confronted with TOTEM data at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28140)
*Ji Li*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该文将具有高斯动量转移依赖的单坡密子（Pomeron）Regge Born 振幅在碰撞参数空间做程函化（eikonalization），得到闭式的单通道纯吸收振幅，并用于检验其能否同时描述 13 TeV 的积分截面与衍射凹陷（diffractive dip）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在检验最简单的单高斯不透明度（Gaussian opacity）模型能否同时再现 TOTEM 在 √s=13 TeV 测得的总截面、弹性/非弹性截面及衍射凹陷位置，并厘清模型失配的来源。

Method: 对单坡密子 Regge Born 振幅作程函化，在碰撞参数空间解析求出单通道纯吸收振幅的闭式，用整指数积分 Ein(a) 与相关级数 I₁(a) 表达总/弹/非弹截面与前向斜率（forward slope），并以直接数值积分和弱耦合 Born 极限校验；随后以两组参数与 TOTEM 数据对比，并考察符号相位（signature phase）、双分量不透明度及双通道 Good-Walker 程函等修正。

Result: 采用 Donnachie-Landshoff 轨迹并在 7 TeV 处固定归一化时，模型给出 σ_tot=139.2 mb（测量值 110.6±3.4 mb），首个衍射零点位于 |t|=0.244 GeV²（测量凹陷在 0.47 GeV²）；对 σ_tot 与 σ_el/σ_tot 双参数标定后，|t|≲0.1 GeV² 内微分截面误差在 5% 以内，但零点移至 0.315 GeV² 且凹陷处截面高估逾一个量级。

Conclusion: 单高斯不透明度无法同时容纳积分截面与衍射凹陷位置；恢复符号相位给出 ρ=0.098（与 TOTEM、ATLAS 一致）并填充零点但不移动其位置；双分量不透明度把凹陷处超出由 15 倍降至 2 倍，却不能同时再现凹陷-峰结构与大 |t| 尾部；高斯轮廓上的双通道 Good-Walker 程函使极小点进一步偏离测量凹陷。

Abstract: A single-Pomeron Regge Born amplitude with a Gaussian dependence on the momentum transfer is eikonalized in impact-parameter space, and the resulting single-channel, purely absorptive amplitude is evaluated in closed form. The total, elastic and inelastic cross sections and the forward slope are expressed through the entire exponential integral Ein(a) and a related series $I_1(a)$, and the closed forms are verified against direct numerical integration and against the weak-coupling Born limit. Two parameter choices are confronted with the TOTEM measurements at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV. With the Donnachie-Landshoff trajectory and a normalization fixed at 7 TeV, the model gives $σ_{tot}=139.2$ mb against the measured $110.6\pm3.4$ mb and places the first diffractive zero at $|t|=0.244$ GeV$^2$ against the measured dip at 0.47 GeV$^2$. Calibration of the two parameters to the measured $σ_{tot}$ and $σ_{el}/σ_{tot}$ describes the differential cross section to within five per cent for $|t|\lesssim0.1$ GeV$^2$, but places the zero at 0.315 GeV$^2$ and overestimates the cross section at the measured dip by more than an order of magnitude. A single Gaussian opacity therefore cannot simultaneously accommodate the integrated cross sections and the position of the diffractive dip. Restoring the signature phase gives $ρ=0.098$, in agreement with the TOTEM and ATLAS measurements, and fills the zero without moving it; a two-component opacity reduces the excess at the dip from a factor of 15 to a factor of 2 without reproducing the dip-bump structure and the large-$|t|$ tail simultaneously; a two-channel Good-Walker eikonal on a Gaussian profile moves the minimum further away from the measured dip.

</details>


### [244] [Design of a Resonator Array Axion Haloscope and its Projected Sensitivity from 2 to 4 GHz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28500)
*ADMX Collaboration,Taj A. Dyson,Mohamed H. Awida,Chelsea L. Bartram,Christian Boutan,Thomas Braine,James H. Buckley,Thomas J. Caligiure,Madeleine Carhart,Gianpaolo Carosi,Aaron S. Chou,Nicholas Christman,John Clarke,Edward J. Daw,Nick Du,Leanne D. Duffy,Sean Durham,Kieran Enzian,Graeme Flower,Chandrashekhar Gaikwad,Joseph R. Gleason,Cyrus Goodman,Maxim Goryachev,Charles Hanretty,Michael T. Hatzon,Alexander T. Hipp,Julia Hoffman,Matthew Hollister,Mark Jones,Rakshya Khatiwada,On Kim,Stefan Knirck,Daria Kowsari,Chao-Lin Kuo,Erik W. Lentz,Morgan J. Lynn,Ben T. McAllister,Donald V. Mitchell,Claude Mostyn,Kater W. Murch,Trinh N. L. Nguyen,Noah S. Oblath,Emma C. I. Paterson,Mitchell G. Perry,Aaron Quiskamp,Sephora Ruppert,B. Jordan Russell,Gray Rybka,Pierre Sikivie,Raj A. Singh,James Sinnis,Mitchell E. Solano,Andrew Sonnenschein,Neil S. Sullivan,David B. Tanner,Matthew S. Taubman,Jonathan R. Tedeschi,Rick J. Tesarek,Michael E. Tobar,Matthew O. Withers,Andrew K. Yi,Barkotel F. Zemenu,Dan Zhang,Zijun C. Zhao,Kaiwen Zheng*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: ADMX扩展频率范围（EFR）项目设计了一种含18个1米长谐振腔、独立低噪声放大链与数字相干信号合成的卤振器（haloscope），预计在36个月内将轴子耦合灵敏度推进至8–16 μeV（2–4 GHz），低于DFSZ基准模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCD轴子（QCD axion）既是暗物质（dark matter, DM）候选粒子，又可解决长期存在的强电荷-宇称（strong CP）问题。此前ADMX已覆盖约2.7–4.2 μeV，在建与规划运行拟延伸至8 μeV（2 GHz），更高频段尚待覆盖。

Method: 提出ADMX扩展频率范围（ADMX extended frequency range, EFR）卤振器设计：18个长1米的谐振腔，分别由独立低噪声放大链读出，并采用数字相干信号合成（digital coherent signal combination）。结合磁体与低温基础设施、腔体设计与冷电子学细节，利用腔体与电子学模拟及以往ADMX运行的诊断数据估算频率相关的瞬时扫描速率，并结合停机时间研究给出灵敏度投影。

Result: 在所述假设下，预计36个月内于8–16 μeV（2–4 GHz）区间达到低于DFSZ轴子基准模型的投影灵敏度。

Conclusion: EFR设计通过多腔体与相干合成扩展ADMX的频率覆盖与扫描效率，若按计划实施，可将QCD轴子暗物质探测的耦合灵敏度上限延伸至DFSZ模型以下的高频区间。

Abstract: The QCD axion is a dark matter (DM) candidate particle which can also solve the long-standing strong charge-parity problem. The Axion Dark Matter eXperiment (ADMX) searches for resonantly-enhanced conversion of axion DM into photons in a strong magnetic field. Previous versions of the experiment probed most QCD axion DM from 2.7 $μ$eV to 4.2 $μ$eV, with ongoing and planned runs poised to cover up to 8 $μ$eV (2 GHz). We present the design of a haloscope with 18 cavities 1 m in length read out using individual low-noise amplifier chains and digital coherent signal combination called ADMX extended frequency range (EFR). We discuss details on magnet and cryogenic infrastructure, cavity design, cold electronics and efficiency of coherent signal combination. We use detailed cavity and electronics simulations, as well as diagnostic data from previous ADMX runs, to estimate frequency-dependent instantaneous scan rate. When paired with a comprehensive study of experiment downtime, a projection of final axion coupling sensitivity is produced. Under the stated assumptions, the projected sensitivity is projected to reach below the DFSZ axion benchmark model from 8 $μ$eV to 16 $μ$eV (2 GHz to 4 GHz) in 36 months.

</details>


### [245] [Lorentz-invariant fiducial decay selection for the ATLAS $H\to ZZ^{*}\to 4\ell$ STXS measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30272)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 将ATLAS的H→ZZ*→4ℓ产生截面（σ·B）与STXS测量重述到单一洛伦兹不变基准衰变区（LIF），把依赖EFT的基准接受度吸收进信号参数化，使现有测量可直接用于EFT解释而无需依赖Wilson系数的重建效率修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有σ·B与简化模板截面（STXS）测量中的选择因子包含以标准模型衰变运动学计算的衰变侧基准接受度（fiducial acceptance），而在有效场论（effective field theory, EFT）解释下该接受度本身依赖于威尔逊系数（Wilson coefficients），导致EFT解释需引入额外修正。

Method: 以H→4ℓ衰变定义洛伦兹不变基准区（Lorentz-invariant fiducial, LIF），将EFT依赖的接受度并入信号参数化，并检验剩余重建效率修正是否与EFT无关；基于√s=13.6 TeV、积分亮度165 fb⁻¹的希格斯信号蒙特卡罗模拟进行数值评估。

Result: 标准模型中LIF接受度为A_SM^LIF=0.8546，其产生模式无关性优于0.07%，在STXS各区间内稳定性为0.19%；重建信号事件中来自LIF区外的比例小于1%，区内事件的重建效率在所考察的Wilson系数范围内变化不超过6%，与已发表分析基准相空间的表现相当。

Conclusion: 现有包容性STXS截面测量可重标度（rescale）至相应的LIF基准相空间，从而在无需EFT依赖的重建效率修正情况下实现EFT解释。

Abstract: ATLAS measurements of Higgs boson production-mode cross-sections and Simplified Template Cross Sections (STXS), $σ\cdot B$, in the $H\to ZZ^{*}\to 4\ell$ decay channel rely on selection factors that fold in a decay-side fiducial acceptance evaluated with Standard Model decay kinematics. In an effective-field-theory interpretation that acceptance depends on the Wilson coefficients themselves. We recast these production-side $σ\cdot B$ results into a single Lorentz-invariant fiducial (LIF) decay region defined by the $H\to 4\ell$ decay, absorbing the EFT-dependent fiducial acceptance into the signal parametrisation, while the remaining reconstruction-efficiency corrections are shown to be largely EFT-independent. Using the Higgs boson signal Monte Carlo simulation at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$ TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 165 fb$^{-1}$, the LIF acceptance in the Standard Model is found to be $A^{LIF}_{SM}=0.8546$, production-independent to 0.07% and stable across STXS bins to 0.19%. Less than 1% of the reconstructed signal events originate from outside the LIF region, and the reconstruction efficiency for events in the region changes by at most 6% over the probed ranges of the EFT Wilson coefficients, as in the fiducial phase space of the published analysis. Consequently, the existing inclusive STXS cross-section measurements can be rescaled to the corresponding LIF fiducial phase space, allowing for EFT interpretations without EFT-dependent corrections for the reconstruction efficiency.

</details>


### [246] [Geometry-conditioned differentiable surrogate with adjoint Monte Carlo sampling for scintillator detector co-design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30580)
*Lorenzo Pepa,Mohammad Ali Marashli,Nicolas Babio Elguero,Tommaso Dorigo,Irene Fagnani,Carlo Gulizia,Pablo Martinez Ruiz del Arbol,Fredrik Sandin,Pietro Vischia*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该工作从模块化事件驱动蒙特卡洛模型与互易（伴随）蒙特卡洛构造出发，训练了几何条件化的可微神经网络代理模型，用于快速预测闪烁体探测器中光子收集概率与条件输运时间分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化基于闪烁体的成像探测器光导几何需反复进行光子输运评估，直接蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟计算代价高，故需可微代理模型（differentiable surrogate）加速评估并支撑读出协同设计。

Method: 构建模块化、事件驱动的蒙特卡洛模型生成并输运闪烁光子，并用从光导读出面发射加权光子轨迹的互易蒙特卡洛（reciprocal Monte Carlo，即伴随模型 adjoint model）构造空间与时间分辨的光学响应场。针对含两个镜面弯曲单包层光导的闪烁体单元，用伴随模型训练几何条件化神经代理模型。

Result: 代理模型可预测受限光导设计族内的收集概率与条件输运时间分布，且原生可微（natively differentiable），可作为基于梯度的可微优化流程（differentiable pipeline）组件；正向—互易基准与留出几何验证确认了精度并识别了局限。

Conclusion: 所开发工具为快速光学响应研究与探测器读出协同设计（detector readout co-design）提供了基础。

Abstract: Optimizing light-guide geometries for scintillator-based imaging detectors requires repeated photon-transport evaluations, making direct Monte Carlo simulation computationally costly. Differentiable surrogates can accelerate these evaluations and support detector readout co-design aimed at improving information extraction from particle interactions. In this work we describe a surrogate model for photon transport. We develop it starting from a modular, event-driven Monte Carlo model for the generation and transport of scintillation photons, and a reciprocal Monte Carlo construction (an adjoint model) of a spatially and temporally resolved optical response field. The latter evaluates the collectible light output and its temporal structure from a scintillator volume by launching weighted photon trajectories from the light-guide readout face. We apply this approach to a scintillator cell with two mirrored, curved, single-clad light guides. We employ the adjoint model to train a geometry-conditioned neural surrogate that predicts collection probabilities and conditional transport-time distributions across a constrained guide-design family. The surrogate is natively differentiable, lending itself to be an element of a differentiable pipeline for gradient-based optimization. A forward-reciprocal benchmark and validation on held-out geometries establish the accuracy of the trained model and identify its limitations. The developed tools provide a foundation for rapid optical-response studies and detector readout co-design.

</details>


### [247] [Unbinned Likelihoods for Probability Densities with Poles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30877)
*Méril Reboud,Danny van Dyk,Matteo Verzeletti*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 提出一种“逐共振态减去单极点项”的方法，将窄共振（narrow resonance）对非共振振幅（nonresonant amplitude）的干涉贡献以闭式表示，仅对光滑余项按探测器分辨率（detector resolution）网格作数值卷积，从而大幅降低非分箱似然拟合（unbinned likelihood fit）的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当窄共振与近邻非共振振幅发生干涉时，真值层面概率密度（truth-level probability density）与探测器分辨率的卷积必须分辨宽度远小于分辨率的共振峰，导致非分箱似然拟合（unbinned likelihood fit）计算代价过高甚至不可行。

Method: 对每个共振态从概率密度中减去一个单极点项（simple-pole term），该共振贡献可解析闭式给出；剩余光滑部分采用网格数值卷积，网格间距仅由分辨率决定。方法适用于多维密度及仅在实轴上已知的密度。

Result: 通过两个基准问题（benchmark problems）定量评估了该方法的精度、计算成本及统计影响（statistical impact）。

Conclusion: 极点减除（subtraction）是处理概率密度含极点时非分箱似然计算问题的通用手段，可推广至实验粒子物理（experimental particle physics）中的大量类似问题。

Abstract: Unbinned likelihood fits to detector-level data become prohibitively expensive when narrow resonances interfere with a nonresonant amplitude. The convolution of the truth-level probability density with the detector resolution must then resolve the resonances, whose widths are much smaller than the resolution. We propose a simple approach that subtracts one simple-pole term per resonance from the probability density. This term captures the resonant contribution in closed form, and the smooth remainder is convolved numerically on a grid whose spacing is set by the resolution alone. The approach applies to multidimensional densities and to densities known only on the real axis. Using two benchmark problems, we quantify the accuracy, computational cost, and statistical impact of the approach. We emphasise that the subtraction is a general approach to the computational problems of unbinned likelihoods in the presence of poles in their probability density and that it is applicable to a wide variety of problems in experimental particle physics.

</details>


### [248] [Distillation of a Normalising Flow into a Compact Symbolic Expression for Real-Time Anomaly Detection at the LHC Level-1 Trigger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30896)
*Justin Bollhalder,Florencia Canelli,Valentina Guglielmi,Roope Oskari Niemi,Maurizio Pierini,Chang Sun,Francesco Vaselli*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该工作将基于归一化流（normalising flow）的异常检测（anomaly detection）模型进一步压缩，蒸馏为显式数学公式，通过符号回归（symbolic regression）实现特征选择与压缩，使其可在ATLAS/CMS的Level-1 Trigger微秒级延迟内运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为避免触发系统（trigger system）实时事例选择引入的偏置，异常检测需在Level-1 Trigger之前实现；但该层级仅有数微秒延迟预算，排除了表达力最强的异常检测模型。

Method: 采用SymbolNet符号回归框架结合自适应剪枝（adaptive pruning），将归一化流异常检测模型蒸馏（knowledge distillation）为一个基于输入子集的显式数学表达式，同时完成特征选择与压缩；并将其部署于Xilinx Virtex UltraScale+ VU13P FPGA。

Result: 最终表达式含577个运算，在测试集上匹配教师模型的判别能力，并可泛化至四个基准新物理（new physics）场景；FPGA实现为九周期延迟，在后布线（post-route）最高时钟频率下对应42.8 ns，占用0.81%的LUT与0.44%的DSP切片。

Conclusion: 结果表明，符号化蒸馏可将异常检测模型压缩为极轻量的显式公式，在满足Level-1 Trigger严格延迟与资源约束的同时保持判别性能，为触发前端的异常检测部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: At the CERN Large Hadron Collider, anomaly detection techniques are becoming popular alternatives to traditional signature-aware new physics searches. For this approach to avoid any bias induced by the real-time event selection applied in the trigger system of ATLAS and CMS, it should be implemented before the first stage of selection, the Level-1 Trigger. This requires deciding to accept or reject each collision within a few microseconds, a latency budget that excludes the most expressive anomaly-detection models. Knowledge distillation has successfully addressed this issue by replacing a complex anomaly-detection model with a lighter student network. This work takes the compression one step further, distilling an anomaly detection model based on a normalising flow into an explicit mathematical formula. Using the SymbolNet symbolic-regression framework with adaptive pruning, the student is a learned functional form based on a chosen subset of the inputs, providing at once feature selection and compression through knowledge distillation. The final expression contains 577 operations and matches the teacher's discrimination on a test dataset, generalising to four benchmark new physics scenarios.
