Implemented on a Xilinx Virtex UltraScale+ VU13P FPGA, the symbolic student has a nine-cycle latency, corresponding to 42.8\,ns at the post-route maximum clock frequency, and uses 0.81\% of the device's LUTs and 0.44\% of its DSP slices.

</details>


### [249] [Underlying-event properties in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$ TeV measured with the ALICE detector at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31077)
*Jesús Eduardo Muñoz Méndez*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: ALICE 利用 LHC Run 3 的 pp 对撞数据（√s = 13.6 TeV）测量了中快度区底层事件（underlying event）的带电粒子数与横向动量密度随领头粒子横动量变化的分布，并与其他能量结果及 PYTHIA 8、EPOS4 模型预言进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 底层事件（underlying event）是理解 pp 碰撞中多部分子相互作用（multi-parton interactions）与软强子产生的重要窗口，需要在更高碰撞能量下获得新的实验约束。

Method: 基于 ALICE 在 LHC Run 3 采集的 √s = 13.6 TeV pp 碰撞数据，以领头带电粒子的方位角方向为参考划分 toward、transverse 与 away 三个区域，测量带电粒子数密度与横向动量密度随领头粒子横动量 p_T^leading 的变化。

Result: transverse 区域的粒子密度在低 p_T^leading 处迅速上升，随后在 p_T^leading > 5 GeV/c 时趋于平台（plateau）；结果与更低质心能量下的测量以及 PYTHIA 8、EPOS4 的预言进行了对比。

Conclusion: 该测量展示了底层事件活动的能量依赖性，并为检验 PYTHIA 8 与 EPOS4 等蒙特卡罗事件产生器（Monte Carlo event generator）模型提供了新的实验依据。

Abstract: This contribution reports measurements characterizing the underlying event at midrapidity in pp collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$ TeV, based on LHC Run 3 data collected by ALICE. The charged-particle number and summed transverse-momentum densities are measured as a function of the leading-particle transverse momentum ($p_{\rm T}^{\rm leading}$) in the toward, transverse, and away regions, defined relative to the azimuthal direction of the leading charged particle. The particle densities in the transverse region are observed to increase steeply at low $p_{\rm T}^{\rm leading}$, followed by a plateau for $p_{\rm T}^{\rm leading}>5$ GeV/$c$. The results are compared with measurements at lower center-of-mass energies to illustrate the energy dependence of the underlying-event activity, and with predictions from PYTHIA 8 and EPOS4.

</details>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [250] [Particle separation in a time-correlated Gaussian velocity field: a numerical study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28147)
*Mario Maurelli,Silvia Morlacchi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过数值方法研究二维无散度、时间相关高斯速度场中被动示踪粒子对（passive tracer pairs）的扩散行为，发现在粗糙度指数（roughness exponent）α=0.2、时间去相关指数（temporal decorrelation exponent）β=0.5（即 α+β<1 区域）时，外推的均方位移暗示粒子在有限时间内获得正的均方间距，与初始间距无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在时间相关的湍流速度场中，粒子对分离是否具有与初始间距无关的有限时间行为尚不清楚。本文关注 α+β<1 参数区域下粒子对扩散的定性特征。

Method: 构建由稀疏傅里叶模态（Fourier modes）组成的二维无散度、时间相关高斯速度场，各模态按独立 Ornstein–Uhlenbeck 过程演化，其空间正则性与尺度依赖的时间去相关特性可与幂律高斯速度系综（power-law Gaussian velocity ensemble）相比拟；据此对被动示踪粒子对进行数值模拟。

Result: 在 α=0.2、β=0.5（满足 α+β<1）条件下，外推的均方距离（mean-square distance）与正时刻的粒子间距（均方意义）相容：无论初始间距多小，两粒子都会在有限时间内达到正的均方距离。

Conclusion: 结果表明显式时间相关性在 α+β<1 区域可导致粒子对分离的"瞬时"有限时间行为，为理解时间相关速度场中的粒子对扩散提供了数值证据。

Abstract: We numerically investigate the dispersion of passive tracer pairs in a two-dimensional, divergence-free, time-correlated Gaussian velocity field. The field is constructed from a sparse set of Fourier modes evolving as independent Ornstein--Uhlenbeck processes, with spatial regularity and scale-dependent temporal decorrelation comparable to those of a power-law Gaussian velocity ensemble. We show that, for spatial roughness exponent $α=0.2$ and temporal decorrelation exponent $β=0.5$, corresponding to the regime $α+β<1$, the extrapolated mean-square distance is compatible with particle separation (in mean-square) at positive time: two particles reach a mean-square positive distance at a finite time, no matter how close they are initially.

</details>


### [251] [Nonequilibrium fluctuations could explain the hot mitochondria paradox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28225)
*Matthew P. Leighton,Benjamin B. Machta*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文针对“线粒体内部比细胞内其他区域高出约10 K”的实验观测与热传导理论之间的矛盾，提出一种基于非平衡涨落与频率选择性温度探针的表观温差解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项实验报道线粒体内部与细胞环境间存在高达约10 K的明显温差，但热传导物理在缺乏远超生物学能量预算的前提下，不允许细胞内存在微开尔文（microkelvin）量级以上的稳态温差；该悖论自首次测量报道近十年仍未获解释，构成研究的核心动机。

Method: 作者提出理论性解释框架：细胞环境中的非平衡涨落（nonequilibrium fluctuations）与对特定频段敏感、具有频率选择性的分子温度探针（molecular temperature probes）相结合，可在生物学上可获得的功率预算（power budget）内产生表观的10 K温差。

Result: 所提机制使升高的温度测量结果与“真实10 K稳态温差在理论上不可能”这一结论得以共存，即在无需超出生物学可得能量预算的条件下解释表观10 K温差的来源。

Conclusion: 表观温差可能源于测量机制而非真实稳态热梯度，从而调和实验观测与热传导理论之间的长期悖论；该解释为细胞微观温度测量提供了以非平衡涨落与探针频率响应为核心的新视角。

Abstract: Several different experiments have found apparent temperature differences of up to 10 K between the interior of mitochondria and the rest of the cellular environment. The physics of heat conduction, however, precludes the steady-state existence of a temperature difference greater than the microkelvin scale within the cell, absent a far greater energy budget. Nearly a decade after the first reported measurement, this apparent paradox remains unexplained. Here, we propose an explanation which allows the elevated temperature measurements to coexist with the theoretical impossibility of a true 10 K steady-state temperature difference. We suggest that nonequilibrium fluctuations within the cellular environment, combined with molecular temperature probes sensitive to specific ranges of frequencies, can lead to apparent 10 K temperature differences with a biologically attainable power budget.

</details>


### [252] [Learning free energy across a differentiable temperature range by deep generative modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28810)
*Yaowen Zhang,Chen Chen,Wanyu Lin,Shuo-Hui Li,Ding Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出可学习温度微分自由能微扰（temperature-differentiable free energy perturbation, TD-FEP），一种基于深度生成、以能量为目标且无需数据的框架，可跨越连续温度区间一次性完成状态生成与自由能计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由能（free energy）决定物理、化学与生物转变的方向性、稳定性与温度敏感性，但传统方法如热力学积分（thermodynamic integration）和自由能微扰（free energy perturbation）通常需逐温度单独计算，且采样大量热力学态代价高昂。

Method: TD-FEP 通过学习以温度为条件、在参考体系与目标体系之间的可逆映射（invertible mapping），并采用无数据（data-free）、基于能量（energy-based）的优化进行训练，从而将一次性状态生成与自由能计算统一于连续温度范围。

Result: 在孤立分子（isolated molecules）、周期性材料（periodic materials）与化学反应（chemical reactions）等多类应用中完成验证，表明框架具有较广的适用性。

Conclusion: TD-FEP 使自由能计算可在连续温度范围内一次性完成，克服了传统方法逐温度计算与热力学态采样成本高的限制，具备跨体系类型的通用性。

Abstract: Free energy governs the directionality, stability, and temperature sensitivity of physical, chemical, and biological transformations. Conventional free energy calculations, such as thermodynamic integration and free energy perturbation, often have to be performed at individual temperatures and are limited by the high cost of sampling an extensive set of thermodynamic states. Here, we introduce learned temperature-differentiable free energy perturbation (TD-FEP), a deep generative framework that learns a temperature-conditioned, invertible mapping between reference and target systems through data-free, energy-based optimization. TD-FEP thereby unifies one-shot state generation and free energy calculation across a continuous temperature range. We validated TD-FEP across a broad range of applications, including isolated molecules, periodic materials, and chemical reactions.

</details>


### [253] [Large Deviations and Critical Scaling in the Finite-Population Granovetter Threshold Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28811)
*José F. Fontanari*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文对Granovetter阈值模型的有限人口精确解作系统渐近分析，将传播概率表述为次序统计量（order statistic）的边界穿越事件，并把宏观大偏差率（large-deviation rate）写成受约束的相对熵极小化问题；对Beta分布阈值给出全部形状参数下的显式解与四类最优经验阈值剖面，并由数值模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阈值驱动的级联过程中，宏观级联等稀有集体事件的发生概率与典型构型缺乏统一刻画，尤其缺乏对有限人口精确解的严格渐近分析。

Method: 将传播概率表示为次序统计量的边界穿越事件，进而把宏观大偏差率表述为受约束的相对熵极小化问题；对Beta分布阈值解析求解各形状参数α、β下的最优经验阈值剖面，并用重要性采样（importance sampling）与序贯蒙特卡洛（sequential Monte Carlo）进行验证。

Result: 对Beta分布阈值得到全参数范围的显式解，识别出全自由、全钉扎、钉扎–自由、自由–钉扎四类最优经验阈值剖面，它们同时决定宏观级联的指数概率与触发构型；数值估计确认了率函数并独立重现最优剖面。临界标度方面，确认均匀阈值下平均活跃比例按N^{-1/2}衰减，而α=1、0<β<1时按N^{-1}衰减，并由微观分支过程（branching process）极限导出对数临界窗口的显式标度函数。

Conclusion: 结果为确定阈值驱动级联过程中稀有集体事件的概率与特征构型提供了通用框架。

Abstract: We develop a systematic asymptotic analysis of the exact finite-population solution of Granovetter's threshold model, in which a single initial instigator triggers a cascade among agents with independently distributed adoption thresholds. By expressing the propagation probability as an order-statistic boundary-crossing event, we formulate its macroscopic large-deviation rate as a constrained relative-entropy minimization problem. For Beta-distributed thresholds, we obtain explicit solutions for all shape parameters $α$ and $β$, identifying four possible optimal empirical threshold profiles: fully free, fully pinned, pinned--free, and free--pinned. These profiles determine both the exponential probability of macroscopic cascades and the threshold configurations responsible for their occurrence. Importance-sampling estimates confirm the analytical rate functions, while sequential Monte Carlo simulations independently reproduce the predicted optimal profiles. We also revisit the critical scaling near $α_c=1$ and confirm the principal finite-size scaling laws: the mean active fraction decays as $N^{-1/2}$ for uniform thresholds and as $N^{-1}$ for $α=1$ and $0<β<1$. In the latter regime, we further derive an explicit scaling function for the logarithmic critical window from the microscopic branching-process limit. Our results provide a general framework for determining the probabilities and characteristic configurations of rare collective events in threshold-driven cascade processes

</details>


### [254] [Time-Reversed Task Events and Thermodynamic Bounds for Stochastic Thermal Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28874)
*Kaiyuan Cao,Bei Xiao,Jian Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于任务（task-based）的框架，将有限时间（finite-time）性能与随机热机及制冷机中的热力学不可逆性（thermodynamic irreversibility）联系起来，并利用前向与逆向任务概率间的二元相对熵（binary relative entropy）给出平均熵产生（mean entropy production）下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究缺乏将有限时间任务的成功/失败统计与热力学不可逆性直接联系的理论工具，本文旨在建立这一联系，以约束有限时间热任务的能量代价。

Method: 以在规定时间内达到累积功（accumulated-work）阈值为任务定义，通过精确轨迹时间反演（exact trajectory time reversal）构造逆事件；以前向与逆任务概率间的二元相对熵导出平均熵产下界，并结合热力学第一定律（first law）与熵平衡（entropy balance）约束热机的不可逆功损失与制冷机的超额输入功；以三能级随机系统（three-level stochastic system）为例并与全动力学对照检验。

Result: 所导出的下界在三能级随机系统的全动力学检验中成立，表明仅凭任务成功或失败的统计即可揭示不可逆性，并约束有限时间热任务的能量成本。

Conclusion: 任务成功/失败统计可作为探测不可逆性的信息源；论文并提出了利用受监测三能级系统耦合工程化热库（engineered thermal reservoirs）与功通道（work channel）的实验验证路径。

Abstract: We develop a task-based framework that relates finite-time performance to thermodynamic irreversibility in stochastic heat engines and refrigerators. For tasks defined by reaching an accumulated-work threshold within a prescribed time, we construct the reverse event through exact trajectory time reversal. The binary relative entropy between forward and reverse task probabilities provides a lower bound on mean entropy production. Combined with the first law and entropy balance, this bound constrains irreversible work loss in engines and excess input work in refrigerators. We illustrate the framework with a three-level stochastic system and test the bounds against its full dynamics. These results show how success-or-failure statistics alone can reveal irreversibility and constrain the energetic costs of finite-time thermal tasks. We also outline an experimental route to testing the framework using a monitored three-level system coupled to engineered thermal reservoirs and a work channel.

</details>


### [255] [An informational interpretation of the reverse nonequilibrium molecular dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29174)
*Yuxing Jiao,Mingcheng Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文为反非平衡分子动力学（reverse nonequilibrium molecular dynamics, RNEMD）的“麦克斯韦妖式”非局域交换操作建立信息储库（information reservoir）解释：信息储库的统计偏置定义了一种“信息亲和力”，可在稳态下维持浓度梯度与扩散通量，并导出相应的线性输运系数、熵产生的涨落定理（fluctuation theorem）与热力学不确定性关系（thermodynamic uncertainty relation），并用计算机模拟加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNEMD通过看似人为的非局域交换操作施加通量，再由通量诱导热力学梯度，表面上颠倒了通常的力—通量因果关系；尽管该方法作为模拟技术已被充分研究，但缺乏信息—热力学层面的物理解释。作者希望为这类算法建立坚实的物理基础。

Method: 针对一个用于测定自扩散系数（self-diffusivity）的代表性RNEMD方案，构建信息储库解释；证明信息储库的统计偏置定义了信息亲和力（information affinity），并据此导出线性响应区域（linear response regime）内的线性输运系数。进一步利用稳态密度与电流的大偏差泛函（large deviation functional）推导熵产生的涨落定理及关联熵产生与粒子流涨落的热力学不确定性关系，并以计算机模拟佐证理论结果。

Result: 信息亲和力可维持稳态下的浓度梯度与扩散通量；导出了与信息亲和力对应的线性输运系数；建立了熵产生的涨落定理，以及联系熵产生与粒子电流涨落的热力学不确定性关系。计算机模拟结果与理论预测相符（据摘要表述）。

Conclusion: 该工作将RNEMD的“麦克斯韦妖式”操作解释为信息储库机制，从而为“力—通量因果反转”的表象提供信息—热力学物理解释，并为这类算法提供理论支撑；不过摘要未给出具体结论性延伸。

Abstract: Reverse nonequilibrium molecular dynamics (RNEMD) is an efficient simulation method for determining transport coefficients. In this method, a flux is imposed by a seemingly artificial nonlocal exchange operation and the corresponding thermodynamic gradient is induced as a result, apparently reversing the usual force-flux causality. Although this method is well studied as a simulation technique, developing an information-thermodynamic interpretation of its Maxwell-demon-type operation and the associated apparent reversal of force-flux causality would provide a solid physical foundation for algorithms of this type. Here, we develop an information-reservoir interpretation for a representative RNEMD scheme for determining self-diffusivity. We show that the statistical bias of the information reservoir defines an information affinity that sustains a concentration gradient and a diffusive flux in the steady state. We further derive the linear transport coefficient corresponding to the information affinity in the linear response regime. Using the large deviation functional for the steady-state densities and currents, we also establish a fluctuation theorem for entropy production and the thermodynamic uncertainty relation linking entropy production to the fluctuations of the particle current. Computer simulations are performed to corroborate our theoretical results.

</details>


### [256] [Renormalization-group analysis of two-dimensional long-range XY models with $p$-fold anisotropy: the $σ=2$ boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29362)
*Xianzhi Pan,Chengxiang Ding,Youjin Deng*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究 $1/r^{2+\sigma}$ 二维经典 XY 模型在 $\sigma=2$ 边缘情形下受 $p$ 重场 $h_p\cos(p\theta)$ 扰动后的临界行为，构建统一重整化群方程并在 $p<4$、$p=4$、$p>4$ 三种情形下给出不同结论，支持 $\sigma_*=2$ 边界而非 Sak 判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明 $\sigma=2$ 的边缘情形存在进入对数准长程序（log-QLRO）的连续相变，但该图像在加入 $p$ 重场扰动后如何改变尚不清楚，需厘清对称性破缺场对 log-QLRO 相与相变性质的影响。

Method: 以物理涡旋（vortex）与 $p$ 重场（$p$-fold field）逸度（fugacity）为变量，构造一套统一的重整化群（RG）方程，通过长程耦合参数 $\kappa$ 在短程（SR）与长程（LR）情形之间插值。

Result: $p<4$ 时小微扰处理失效、临界行为超出当前 RG 处理范围；$p>4$ 时该场在纯不动点处为无关（irrelevant），但低温下长程增强使刚度上升，log-QLRO 仅表现为有限尺度的渡越（crossover），唯一相变为无序到 $p$ 重有序相的连续相变；$p=4$ 时不动线偏离短程 Ashkin-Teller 形式，进入四重有序相的相变具有依赖 $\kappa$ 与 $h_4$ 的非普适关联长度指数。

Conclusion: 结果支持 $\sigma_*=2$ 作为边界，与 Sak 判据（Sak's criterion）的预言相悖。

Abstract: The two-dimensional classical XY model with $1/r^{2+σ}$ interactions has recently been shown to undergo, at the marginal case $σ=2$, a continuous transition into a logarithmic quasi-long-range-ordered (log-QLRO) phase where the correlation decays as $C(r) \sim (\ln r)^{-η_\ell}$. We study how a $p$-fold field $h_p\cos(pθ)$ modifies this picture. Using the physical vortex and $p$-fold field fugacities, we construct a unified set of renormalization-group (RG) equations that interpolate between the short-range (SR) and long-range (LR) cases through an LR coupling parameter $κ$. For $p<4$, the small-$h_p$ perturbative treatment breaks down and the critical behavior lies beyond the present RG treatment. For $p>4$, the field is irrelevant at the pure fixed point, but at low temperature the LR enhancement drives the stiffness upward: log-QLRO appears only as a finite-scale crossover, and the only transition is the continuous one from the disordered into the $p$-fold-ordered phase. For $p=4$, the fixed line shifts from the SR Ashkin-Teller form, and the transition into the fourfold-ordered phase has a nonuniversal correlation-length exponent depending on both $κ$ and $h_4$. These results support the $σ_*=2$ boundary, in contrast to Sak's criterion.

</details>


### [257] [Exact superdiffusion exponents and hull exploration of chiral random walkers on critical percolation clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29537)
*Joshua Uhlig,Ralf Metzler,Erik Kalz*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 将de Gennes“迷宫中的蚂蚁”概念推广到手性随机行走者（chiral random walker），在临界渗流簇（percolation cluster）上得到由渗流几何决定的超扩散指数89/84，并给出手性概率χ<1时超扩散向亚扩散转变的判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典“迷宫中的蚂蚁”描述无序介质中的亚扩散，但手性（chirality）这一自由度此前未被纳入；作者希望考察完全手性的行走者在临界渗流簇上的输运行为，并寻找可由渗流几何直接预测的输运规律。

Method: 构造完全手性的行走者，使其贴附于障碍边界并沿渗流簇的分形外壳（fractal hulls）运动，其轨道以精确的行走维度（walk dimension）7/4扩展直至闭合；进而分析部分手性（概率χ<1）情形，并用大规模数值模拟进行验证。

Result: 由于渗流簇包含所有尺度的孔洞，系综呈超扩散（superdiffusion）⟨r²⟩~t^{89/84}，由轨道尚未闭合且比例递减的行走者主导；两个指数（7/4与89/84）均由渗流几何唯一确定，与大规模数值模拟吻合。χ<1时超扩散转为经典蚂蚁的亚扩散（subdiffusion），交叉时间（crossover time）由两个指数共同设定，扩散系数被因子(1-χ)^{-2/d_w}增强。

Conclusion: 交叉时间与扩散系数之间的关系不依赖于手性的具体实现方式，因而可作为无序介质中手性示踪粒子（chiral tracers）的直接检验判据。

Abstract: Generalizing de Gennes' ant-in-the-labyrinth concept, we consider a chiral random walker on a percolation cluster at criticality. The fully chiral ant clings to obstacle boundaries and traces the fractal hulls of the cluster, spreading with the exact walk dimension $7/4$ until its orbit closes. Because the cluster contains holes on all scales, the ensemble spreads superdiffusively, $\langle r^2\rangle\sim t^{89/84}$, carried by a diminishing fraction of walkers whose orbits are still open. Both exponents follow from percolation geometry alone, and agree well with large-scale numerical simulations. When the walker turns chirally only with probability $χ<1$, superdiffusion gives way to the subdiffusion of the ant at a crossover time set by both exponents, with a diffusion coefficient enhanced by $(1-χ)^{-2/d_w}$. The relation between crossover time and diffusion coefficient does not depend on how chirality is realized, offering a direct test for chiral tracers in disordered media.

</details>


### [258] [Multipartite entanglement structure of crystal quenches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30795)
*Paul Gaudin,Clément Berthière*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究紧束缚链（tight-binding chain）中晶体淬火（crystal quenches）产生的准粒子多重态（quasiparticle multiplets），识别出不存在不可约三体纠缠（irreducible tripartite entanglement）的“零构型”（zero-configurations），并证明马尔可夫间隙（Markov gap）是晶体淬火中三体纠缠的可靠见证量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非平衡量子多体系统（out-of-equilibrium quantum many-body systems）中纠缠如何产生与传播是基础性问题；本文聚焦量子淬火（quantum quenches）后的多体纠缠结构。

Method: 研究紧束缚链中晶体淬火所生成准粒子多重态的纠缠结构与动力学，界定零构型，并构建马尔可夫间隙的准粒子图像（quasiparticle picture），同时与精确格点数值（exact lattice numerics）结果对比。

Result: 存在由三方共享、但仅含成对分布的两体纠缠态（bipartite-entangled states）、无不可约三体纠缠的多重态构型；此类零构型可进一步延迟三体纠缠的产生；马尔可夫间隙被证明为晶体淬火下不可约三体纠缠的可靠见证量，且在流体力学区域（hydrodynamic regime）与精确数值完全吻合。

Conclusion: 零构型的存在对晶体淬火的纠缠结构与动力学具有重要影响；马尔可夫间隙为准粒子图像下刻画三体纠缠动力学提供了有效工具，并获数值验证。

Abstract: Understanding how entanglement emerges and spreads in out-of-equilibrium quantum many-body systems is a fundamental problem. In this paper, we investigate multipartite entanglement after quantum quenches. In particular, we study the entanglement structure and dynamics of quasiparticle multiplets generated by crystal quenches in the tight-binding chain. We identify zero-configurations for which a multiplet shared between three parties has no irreducible tripartite entanglement---these configurations consist entirely of pairwise distributed bipartite-entangled states. The existence of zero-configurations has important implications on the entanglement structure and dynamics of crystal quenches. For example, we show that it can further delay the production of tripartite entanglement. We build the quasiparticle picture for the Markov gap, and prove that it is a faithful witness of irreducible tripartite entanglement for crystal quenches. We discuss several key aspects of their tripartite entanglement dynamics through the lens of the Markov gap. We confront our results with exact lattice numerics and find perfect agreement in the hydrodynamic regime.

</details>


### [259] [Trajectory arclength reveals thermodynamic criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30053)
*Jiménez-López,Javier,García-Garrido,V. J*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出微观哈密顿轨迹弧长（Hamiltonian trajectory arclength）可解析地刻画热力学临界性（thermodynamic criticality），在吸引型哈密顿平均场模型（attractive Hamiltonian mean-field model）中于相变点出现折点，并给出 N^{-1/2} 的有限尺寸修正与数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续相变（continuous phase transitions）中的非解析行为通常通过平衡热力学量刻画，而微观动力学轨迹本身是否携带可解析预测的临界信号尚不明确，论文旨在建立微观哈密顿运动与集体热力学临界性之间的定量联系。

Method: 对吸引型哈密顿平均场模型推导平衡平均弧长（equilibrium-averaged arclength）的热力学极限，将其表述为归一化拉格朗日描述符（normalized Lagrangian descriptor），并将非解析性追溯到自发磁化（spontaneous magnetization），解析导出主导临界有限尺寸修正。

Result: 该描述符在相变点精确出现折点，有限尺寸修正按 N^{-1/2} 标度且具显式解析前因子；最高 10^5 自由度的模拟在无可调参数下同时证实两项预测。

Conclusion: 轨迹弧长既不需要切空间演化（tangent-space evolution），也无需预先指定序参量（order parameter），为微观哈密顿运动与集体热力学临界性之间提供了简单而定量的联系。

Abstract: Thermodynamic criticality arises at continuous phase transitions, where collective interactions produce non-analytic behavior in equilibrium thermodynamic quantities in the thermodynamic limit. We show that this criticality also leaves an analytically predictable signature in the arclength of microscopic Hamiltonian trajectories. For the attractive Hamiltonian mean-field model, we derive the thermodynamic limit of the equilibrium-averaged arclength, formulated as a normalized Lagrangian descriptor, and demonstrate that it develops a kink precisely at the phase transition. We trace this non-analyticity to spontaneous magnetization and derive the leading critical finite-size correction, scaling as $N^{-1/2}$ with an explicit analytical prefactor. Simulations with up to $10^5$ degrees of freedom confirm both predictions without adjustable parameters. Requiring neither tangent-space evolution nor prior specification of an order parameter for its computation, trajectory arclength provides a simple, quantitative link between microscopic Hamiltonian motion and collective thermodynamic criticality.

</details>


### [260] [Exact solution of the long-range voter model in arbitrary dimensions: correlations and effective-dimensional scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30474)
*Fabrizio Rippa,Jacopo Alexander Garofalo,Giuseppe Petrillo,Eugenio Lippiello*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对任意空间维度下具有长程相互作用（long-range interactions）的选民模型（voter model），给出了空间关联函数的精确解析解，并据此建立了一个精确的有效维度对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程相互作用可深刻改变集体有序与临界行为，但非平衡系统（non-equilibrium systems）中此类相互作用的精确解析结果仍然稀少。本文旨在填补这一空白，对长程选民模型给出精确可解的分析。

Method: 从傅里叶模式（Fourier modes）的演化方程出发，识别由相互作用核（interaction kernel）产生的长波色散关系（long-wavelength dispersion），并推导相应的粗化（coarsening）与稳态标度区域。

Result: 解给出了区分各标度区域的临界条件、特征粗化长度以及稳态关联的渐近衰减。结果显示长程模型在维度 d、相互作用指数 β 下，与有效维度 d_eff = 2d/β 的短程选民模型具有相同的标度指数（scaling exponents）。

Conclusion: 该有效维度对应关系在粗化、边缘（marginal）与稳态区域均成立，但相应的标度函数（scaling functions）未必一致。这为非平衡长程系统的精确分析提供了范例。

Abstract: Long-range interactions can profoundly alter collective ordering and critical behavior, yet exact ana- lytical results for non-equilibrium systems with such interactions remain scarce. We obtain an exact analytical solution for the spatial correlation function of the voter model with long-range interac- tions in arbitrary spatial dimension. Starting from the evolution equation for the Fourier modes, we identify the long-wavelength dispersion generated by the interaction kernel and derive the resulting coarsening and stationary scaling regimes. The solution yields the critical condition separating these regimes, the characteristic coarsening length, and the asymptotic decay of stationary correlations. These results establish an exact effective-dimensional correspondence: the long-range model in dimen- sion d, with interaction exponent \b{eta}, exhibits the same scaling exponents as a short-range voter model in the effective dimension deff = 2d/\b{eta}. The correspondence holds across the coarsening, marginal, and stationary regimes, while the associated scaling functions need not coincide

</details>

### [261] [The replication-energy limit of PQG reduced-density-matrix theory from a single-fragment semidefinite program](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29835)
*Maho Nakata*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明在PQG（P、Q、G三条件）下，变分双电子约化密度矩阵（variational two-electron reduced-density-matrix）计算在复制的非相互作用碎片上能量每碎片收敛到单碎片半定规划（semidefinite program, SDP）的最小值，并揭示该非尺寸广延性（size extensivity）缺陷源于局域电子数涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分双电子约化密度矩阵方法不满足尺寸广延性：复制相同的严格非相互作用碎片时，每碎片能量会下降。此前仅有数值反例，缺乏对PQG条件下该现象的机制性刻画，本文旨在给出严格证明与物理解释。

Method: 在P、Q、G条件下，利用G条件（Garrod–Percus）使单体算符的方差与协方差在无需表示态的情况下保持半正定，从而可应用柯西–施瓦茨不等式（Cauchy–Schwarz inequality）构造显式的复制点；并结合单碎片半定规划与碎片间协方差的1/(k−1)标度进行分析。

Result: 证明五点结论：(i) 每碎片能量收敛到单碎片SDP最小值，通过k个复制品间量级为1/(k−1)的负碎片间协方差实现，并与全局单态（global singlet）一致地抵消局域电子数与自旋涨落；(ii) 每碎片能量随复制数非增；(iii) 在有限复制数下精确达到极限；(iv) 缺陷与局域电子数方差相关，尖锐的局域电子数可在无自旋约束下恢复可加性；(v) 相同单元链中每单元能量存在极限，并在耦合趋于零时趋于同一最小值。

Conclusion: PQG允许显式构造的复制点，其补偿的局域涨落使每碎片能量低于孤立值，从而系统解释了该方法的非尺寸广延性来源。Be、CH₄、N₂、H₂O（STO-6G）及四格点Hubbard碎片示例表明，在三指标条件T₁与T₂下与孤立值的差距仍然存在。

Abstract: Variational two-electron reduced-density-matrix calculations are not size extensive: the energy per fragment can decrease when identical, strictly noninteracting fragments are replicated [M. Nakata and K. Yasuda, Phys. Rev. A 80, 042109 (2009)]. For the $P$, $Q$, and $G$ conditions (PQG) we prove the following. (i) The energy per fragment converges to the minimum of a semidefinite program (SDP) on one fragment. The limit is reached through negative interfragment covariances of order $1/(k-1)$ for $k$ replicas, which cancel the local electron number and spin fluctuations consistently with a global singlet. (ii) The energy per fragment is nonincreasing in the replica count. (iii) It reaches the limit exactly at a finite replica count. (iv) The defect is tied to the local electron-number variance: a sharp local electron number restores additivity without a spin constraint. (v) In chains of identical units the energy per unit has a limit that tends to the same minimum as the coupling vanishes. The key tool is that the $G$ condition [C. Garrod and J. K. Percus, J. Math. Phys. 5, 1756 (1964)] keeps the variances and covariances of one-body operators positive semidefinite without a representing state, so that the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality applies. PQG therefore admits explicitly constructed replicated points whose compensated local fluctuations lower the energy per fragment below the isolated value. Be, CH$_4$, N$_2$, and H$_2$O (STO-6G) and a four-site Hubbard fragment illustrate the results; in these examples the gap to the isolated value persists with three-index conditions $T_1$ and $T_2$.

</details>

### [262] [A $C_2$-cofinite non-rational principal $W$-algebra of type $D_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28661)
*Dražen Adamović,Ivana Vukorepa*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文证明存在既满足 C₂-有限性（C₂-cofiniteness）又非有理（non-rational）的简单主 W-代数（principal W-algebra），并以中心荷为 −50 的 W = W_{−38/7}(D₄, f_reg) 为例，显式构造其 17 个不可约模与不可分解对数模（logarithmic module）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有认知常将 C₂-有限性与有理性相联系，本文旨在给出反例，验证 D. Xie 关于该 W-代数存在十七个不可约模及一个最低共形权为 −2 的对数模的预测。

Method: 确定 Zhu 代数 A(W) 与 Poisson 代数 R_W = W/C₂(W)（二者维数均为 18），并利用 W 在 L_{−14/3}(D₄) ⊗ L₁(D₄) 中的对角陪集（diagonal coset）实现及此前关于 category O 中不可约 L_{−14/3}(D₄)-模的分类，构造全部十七个不可约 W-模。

Result: 得到 A(W) ≅ ℂ^{16} × ℂ[ε]/(ε²)；构造出一个不可分解 L_{−14/3}(D₄)-模，并经陪集实现得到不可分解对数 W-模，其最低广义 L(0)-特征空间为二维，L(0) 以特征值 −2 的非平凡 Jordan 块作用其上。

Conclusion: 简单主 W-代数可以同时是 C₂-有限且非有理的，表明 C₂-有限性并不蕴含有理性；对数结构由不可分解模与 L(0) 的 Jordan 块体现。

Abstract: We show that a simple principal $W$-algebra can be $C_2$-cofinite and non-rational. For $\mathcal W=\mathcal W_{-38/7}(D_4,f_{\mathrm{reg}})$ of central charge $-50$, D. Xie predicted seventeen irreducible modules and a logarithmic module of lowest conformal weight $-2$. We determine the Zhu algebra $A(\mathcal W)$ and the Poisson algebra $R_{\mathcal W}=\mathcal W/C_2(\mathcal W)$. Both have dimension $18$, and $A(\mathcal W)\cong\mathbb{C}^{16}\times\mathbb{C}[\varepsilon]/(\varepsilon^2)$. We construct all seventeen irreducible $\mathcal W$-modules using the realization of $\mathcal W$ as a diagonal coset in $L_{-14/3}(D_4)\otimes L_1(D_4)$ and our previous classification of irreducible $L_{-14/3}(D_4)$-modules in the category $\mathcal O$. We also construct an indecomposable $L_{-14/3}(D_4)$-module and use the coset realization to obtain an indecomposable logarithmic $\mathcal W$-module. Its lowest generalized $L(0)$-eigenspace is two-dimensional, and $L(0)$ acts on this space by a nontrivial Jordan block with eigenvalue $-2$.

</details>


### [263] [Möbius Fusion Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29234)
*Ippo Orii,Yuji Tachikawa*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 该文从带反单对象对合（anti-monoidal involution）的伪酉模张量范畴（pseudo-unitary modular tensor category）出发，构造了所谓 Möbius 融合代数（Möbius fusion algebra），证明其半单性并给出其单模维数公式，该公式预期是非可定向情形的 Verlinde 公式（Verlinde formula）类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 Verlinde 公式与 Reshetikhin-Turaev 型拓扑量子场论（topological quantum field theory）主要针对可定向流形；本文旨在为非可定向流形上的相应理论提供代数工具，因而需要构造一族新的融合型代数。

Method: 以伪酉模张量范畴（pseudo-unitary modular tensor category）及其反单对象对合（anti-monoidal involution）P 为输入，借助画在 Möbius 带（Möbius band）上的带状图（ribbon graphs）定义 Möbius 融合代数；Möbius 带的方向反转通过 P 作用于这些图。

Result: 证明该 Möbius 融合代数半单（semisimple），并给出其单模（simple modules）维数公式，公式由模 S-矩阵（modular S-matrix）以及从 P 的作用中提取的一组量共同表达。

Conclusion: 所得到的维数公式被预期充当 Verlinde 公式的非可定向类比（non-orientable analogue），服务于非可定向流形上的 Reshetikhin-Turaev 型拓扑量子场论框架。

Abstract: Given a pseudo-unitary modular tensor category $\mathcal{C}$ with an anti-monoidal involution $\mathsf{P}$, we define a generally non-commutative algebra from this data, which we call the Möbius fusion algebra $\mathfrak{M}$ of $(\mathcal{C},\mathsf{P})$, in terms of ribbon graphs drawn on the Möbius band, where the orientation-reversal of the Möbius band acts on the graphs by $\mathsf{P}$. We show that this algebra is semisimple, and give a formula for the dimensions of its simple modules, in terms of the modular $S$-matrix of $\mathcal{C}$ and a certain set of quantities extracted from the action of $\mathsf{P}$. This formula is expected to play the role of a non-orientable analogue of the Verlinde formula, in the context of Reshetikhin-Turaev-type topological quantum field theories on non-orientable manifolds.

</details>
