### [264] [Horizon mergers and initial test-charge acceleration in charged black-hole geometries with anisotropic sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28095)
*Saeed Noori Gashti,Mohammad Ali S. Afshar,Mohammad Reza Alipour*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在含弦云（string cloud）、Kiselev 幂律（power-law）成分或对数型（logarithmic）环境项的静态球对称黑洞时空中，研究视界并合（horizon merger）条件与带电试验粒子的初始径向加速度。核心结果是：环境应力张量各向异性（anisotropic），Kiselev 分支中内事件视界并合电荷仅给出上限，额外还需正的下限才能出现三个正视界；并给出外向加速的局域荷质比阈值（如对数情形 λ_*≈1.251125）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是澄清此类带环境成分的黑洞几何中视界并合的几何约束与试验粒子初始运动，并纠正将有效环境应力张量等同于均匀精质（quintessence）或无压冷暗物质（cold dark matter）的惯常做法。作者强调仅给出几何与粒子动力学结果，不建立双黑洞力律或宇宙加速机制。

Method: 分离 Maxwell 贡献后，作者采用统一的质量函数（mass-function）表述推导视界并合条件并对 Kiselev 分支分类。对相对静态观者静止释放的试验粒子，推导其外向初始加速度的局域荷质比阈值，令 λ=q/μ、z=Q/Q_ext（Q_ext 为内事件视界并合电荷），并分析亚极端（subextremal）弦云与对数剖面的阈值性质及中性平衡半径的稳定性。

Result: 环境应力张量呈各向异性而非均匀精质；Kiselev 分支中内事件并合电荷仅提供上限，三正视界还需与事件—宇宙学并合（event-cosmological merger）相关的正下限。亚极端弦云阈值下确界为 1/z，仅在无穷远处趋近；亚极端对数剖面阈值在有限外部区域处处大于 1 并有有限全局最小值，α/M=0.4、Q/M=1.2 时 λ_*≈1.251125。非退化 Kiselev 静态区中中性平衡半径唯一且径向不稳定；密度匹配只给出条件性剖面归一化，对数剖面的积分参数与由圆轨道推断的质量组合不同。

Conclusion: 结论限于几何视界界与试验粒子动力学：给出视界并合的荷电边界及初始加速阈值，但未建立双黑洞间作用力定律或宇宙加速机制，亦不构成对弱引力猜想（Weak Gravity Conjecture）的检验。

Abstract: We study horizon mergers and the initial radial acceleration of charged test particles in static, spherically symmetric black-hole geometries with a string cloud, a Kiselev power-law component, or a logarithmic profile conventionally. After separating the Maxwell contribution, the effective environmental stress tensors are anisotropic and are not identified with homogeneous quintessence or pressureless cold dark matter. Using a common mass-function formulation, we derive the horizon-merger conditions and classify the Kiselev branches. In this family, the inner-event merger charge provides only an upper bound: three distinct positive horizons can additionally require a positive lower charge bound associated with the event-cosmological merger. For a test particle released from rest relative to a static observer, we derive the local charge-to-mass threshold for outward initial acceleration. Writing $λ=q/μ$ and $z=Q/Q_{\rm ext}$, where $Q_{\rm ext}$ is the inner-event merger charge, the subextremal string-cloud threshold has infimum $1/z$, approached only at infinity. For the subextremal logarithmic profile, the threshold exceeds unity throughout the finite exterior and attains a finite global minimum. For $α/M=0.4$ and $Q/M=1.2$, this minimum is $λ_*\simeq1.251125$. In a nondegenerate Kiselev static patch, the neutral balance radius is unique and radially unstable. Density matching supplies conditional profile normalizations, not observational fits. For the logarithmic profile, the integration parameter is distinguished from the mass combination inferred from circular motion. These results characterize geometric horizon bounds and test-particle dynamics; they do not establish a two-black-hole force law or a mechanism for cosmic acceleration, nor do they constitute a test of the Weak Gravity Conjecture.

</details>


### [265] [Level repulsion and the quantum viscosity of near-extremal black branes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28203)
*Panos Betzios,Olga Papadoulaki,Maria Myrto Pegioudi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在近极端黑膜（near-extremal black branes）的 JT 引力盘近似下，一阶剪切黏度（shear viscosity）发散；引入矩阵积分（matrix integral）作为非微扰完备后，通过 Kubo 公式可将该发散转化为能级关联的普适行为，从而在双重标度极限下得到有限且普适的黏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JT 引力盘近似下，低于能隙标度（gap scale）的量子涨落使剪切黏度按 $(CT)^{-1/2}$ 增长，导致单圈 $\eta/s$ 发散；需引入非微扰完备以解决该发散并理解能级关联对输运系数的刻画。

Method: 采用矩阵积分形式的 JT 引力非微扰完备，用 Kubo 公式（Kubo formula）探测能级关联（level correlations）；考察有限极端熵 $S_0$、双重标度极限（$	\omega$ 与能级间隔标度 $\omega_{\rm sp}\equiv e^{-S_0}/C$ 保持固定比值），并在极低温 $CT\sim e^{-2S_0/3}$ 处将问题约化为 Airy 核（Airy kernel）；同时与弦方程（string equation）定义的显式完备作比较。

Result: 有限 $S_0$ 下能级排斥（level repulsion）使直流黏度消失；双重标度极限中存在有限普适黏度，固定 $\omega$ 且 $\omega_{\rm sp}\ll\omega\ll e^{S_0/3}\omega_{\rm sp}$ 时 Schwarzian 增长饱和于 $\eta_{\max}\simeq0.174\,(\omega/\omega_{\rm sp})\,\eta_{sc}$，更低温度下黏度下降；极低温处回路展开（genus expansion）与单圈熵计算完全失效，问题精确化为 Airy 核；淬火平均（quenched averages）的 $\eta$ 与 $s$ 之比在任何非零温度下有限且为正。

Conclusion: 与弦方程定义的完备比较，体结果（bulk results）一致，淬火边缘结果仅变化百分之几，而退火量（annealed quantities）在阶一量级上依赖于非微扰完备的选择。

Abstract: Below the gap scale of the AdS$_2$ throat, quantum fluctuations make the shear viscosity of near-extremal black branes grow as $(CT)^{-1/2}$ in the disk approximation of JT gravity, and the one-loop $η/s$ diverges. To cure this divergence, we include the non-perturbative completion of JT gravity as a matrix integral. The Kubo formula then probes universal features of level correlations. At finite extremal entropy $S_0$, level repulsion makes the DC viscosity vanish. A finite, universal viscosity survives in a double-scaling limit in which the probe frequency $ω$ is held in a fixed ratio to the level-spacing scale $ω_{\rm sp}\equiv e^{-S_0}/C$. At fixed $ω$, with $ω_{\rm sp}\llω\ll e^{S_0/3}ω_{\rm sp}$ the Schwarzian growth then saturates at $η_{\max}\simeq0.174\,(ω/ω_{\rm sp})\,η_{sc}$, where $η_{sc}$ is the semiclassical viscosity, and the viscosity decreases at lower temperatures. At the extremely low temperature scale $CT\sim e^{-2S_0/3}$ the genus expansion and the one-loop entropy computations completely break down. There we start probing the edge of the spectrum and the problem reduces exactly to the Airy kernel. We find that the ratio of the quenched averages of $η$ and $s$ is finite and positive at every non-zero temperature. We compare our results with explicit completions defined by the string equation, in which the spectrum is bounded from below. The bulk results are the same in these completions and the quenched edge results change only by a few per cent, while the annealed quantities depend on the non-perturbative completion at order one.

</details>


### [266] [The index from a super-point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28204)
*Dhanvarsh Annamalai,Hyunook Hur,Thomas Hussel,Monica Jinwoo Kang,Craig Lawrie,Jaewon Song*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将可编码4d $\mathcal{N}=2$超共形场论Higgs分支与Macdonald指数的滤过分次环的代数几何构造，推广至Schur扇区含费米型生成元的理论，此时环为$\mathbb{Z}_2$分次交换环，指数由广义弧空间（generalized arc space）的Hilbert级数重现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有代数几何构造仅适用于Schur扇区不含费米型生成元的理论，需推广以覆盖含费米生成元的$\mathcal{N}=2$超共形场论，并厘清其环结构与指数的弧空间刻画。

Method: 构造$\mathbb{Z}_2$分次交换的滤过分次环，用定义理想可能含生成元与其导数之间关系的广义弧空间Hilbert级数重现指数；并具体分析自由$\mathcal{N}=2$矢量多重态、$\mathfrak{su}(2)$与$\mathfrak{su}(3)$规范代数的$\mathcal{N}=4$超杨–米尔斯、秩一$\mathcal{N}=3$ SCFT及$\widehat{E}_6(SU(2))$ SCFT。

Result: 秩一$\mathcal{N}=3$ SCFT的理想为单个超势及其两个超对称变分的Jacobian理想之和；$\mathcal{N}=4$超杨–米尔斯的理想类似地由单个$\mathcal{N}=4$超场各分量的Jacobian理想之和给出，该超场在$\mathfrak{su}(2)$情形下为味对称群单态、在$\mathfrak{su}(3)$情形下为三重态。$\widehat{E}_6(SU(2))$理论的相伴VOA为Feigin–Feigin–Tipunin $\mathcal{A}(6)$代数。

Conclusion: $\mathcal{A}(6)$的Zhu $C_2$代数的弧空间不能重现真空特征标，故$\mathcal{A}(6)$不是经典自由（classically free）的；在第一jet环中为Zhu $C_2$代数补充三个关系后，广义弧空间至少在$q^{51}$阶重现$\widehat{E}_6(SU(2))$的Macdonald指数。

Abstract: We extend the algebro-geometric construction of filtered graded rings that encode both the Higgs branch and the Macdonald index of 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ superconformal field theories to theories whose Schur sector contains fermionic generators. The rings are then $\mathbb{Z}_2$-graded-commutative, and the index is reproduced by the Hilbert series of a generalized arc space, whose defining ideal may contain relations among the generators and their derivatives. We study the free $\mathcal{N}=2$ vector multiplet, $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang--Mills with gauge algebras $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ and $\mathfrak{su}(3)$, the rank-one $\mathcal{N}=3$ SCFTs, and the $\widehat{E}_6(SU(2))$ SCFT. For the rank-one $\mathcal{N}=3$ SCFTs, the ideal is the sum of the Jacobian ideals of a single superpotential and of its two supersymmetry variations. For $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang--Mills, the ideal is similarly the sum of the Jacobian ideals of the components of a single $\mathcal{N}=4$ superfield, which is a singlet of the $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ flavor symmetry for gauge algebra $\mathfrak{su}(2)$, and a triplet for $\mathfrak{su}(3)$. The associated VOA for the $\widehat{E}_6(SU(2))$ theory is given by the Feigin--Feigin--Tipunin $\mathcal{A}(6)$ algebra. The arc space of Zhu's $C_2$-algebra of $\mathcal{A}(6)$ does not reproduce the vacuum character, and therefore $\mathcal{A}(6)$ is not classically free. Supplementing Zhu's $C_2$-algebra by three relations in the first jet ring, the generalized arc space reproduces the Macdonald index of $\widehat{E}_6(SU(2))$ up to, at least, order $q^{51}$.

</details>


### [267] [The Gradient Structure of Perturbative RG Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28205)
*Lorenzo Benfatto,William H. Pannell,Andreas Stergiou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文证明在球面上的共形Ward恒等式（conformal Ward identity）允许通过重整化方案（scheme）的选择消除Weyl一致性条件（Weyl consistency conditions）中的反对称阻碍，从而在微扰论各阶将所有维度的重整化群流（RG flows）化为梯度流，并由此导出微扰意义下的F̃定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自Jack与Osborn工作三十余年来，Weyl一致性条件所允许的反对称度规贡献——阻碍2、4、6维微扰重整化群流成为梯度流（gradient flows）的项——一直无法被去除，构成长期未解问题。

Method: 利用球面上的共形Ward恒等式，证明可通过方案选择消去耦合空间度规（metric on the space of couplings）的反对称部分；并考察相关算子源（sources of relevant operators）在球面上与曲率的混合是否影响结论。

Result: β函数成为势A关于对称度规的梯度，适用于任意维度d及有无Weyl反常（Weyl anomaly）的理论；领头阶度规由扰动算符两点函数确定，弱耦合下由幺正性（unitarity）保证正定；在不动点处A等于F̃=sin(πd/2)·ln Z_{S^d}，故F̃定理对微扰流在任意圈阶成立。

Conclusion: 相关算子源不影响上述结果，唯一例外是幺正理论中接近d=6,10,…的自由标量场源，因此φ³理论在六维附近的梯度流性质仍为未决问题。

Abstract: Weyl consistency conditions show that perturbative renormalisation group flows in two, four and six dimensions are almost gradient flows. The obstruction is an antisymmetric contribution to the metric on the space of couplings, which has resisted removal since the work of Jack and Osborn more than 35 years ago. We show that a conformal Ward identity on the sphere allows it to be removed by a choice of scheme. The beta function is then the gradient of a potential $A$ with respect to a symmetric metric, to all orders in perturbation theory. The Ward identity gives this result in any dimension $d$, in theories with and without a Weyl anomaly. At leading order the metric is fixed by the two-point functions of the perturbing operators, so unitarity makes it positive definite at weak coupling. At fixed points $A$ equals $\widetilde{F}=\sin\tfrac{πd}{2}\,\ln Z_{S^{d}}$, so the $\widetilde{F}$-theorem follows for perturbative flows to all loop orders. We show that sources of relevant operators, which on the sphere mix with the curvature, do not affect these results. In unitary theories the only exception is the source of a free scalar field near $d=6,10,\ldots\,$, so that for $φ^3$ theory near six dimensions the gradient property remains open.

</details>


### [268] [An all-orders proof of gradient flow for multiscalar $φ^4$ theory in four dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28206)
*Marten Reehorst*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明四维多标量 φ⁴ 理论（multiscalar φ⁴ theory）的重整化群流（renormalisation group flow）在所有微扰阶（to all orders in perturbation theory）上均为梯度流（gradient flow）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标量耦合空间中重整化群流是否为梯度流尚有争议；Pannell 与 Stergiou 仅在六圈（six loops）给出使梯度流成立所需的若干系数关系，缺乏一般性证明。

Method: 基于 Osborn 与 Jack 的外尔一致性条件（Weyl consistency conditions），并假设带局域耦合（local couplings）的生成泛函有限、以及大动量下大质量费曼积分（massive Feynman integrals）展开的余项有界；由此导出含反对称部分的梯度公式，并用 Ward 恒等式（Ward identity）将反对称贡献改写为对称度规与势的贡献。

Result: 所得度规（metric）在所有微扰阶上正定（positive definite）；该证明蕴含五圈与六圈场旋转系数之间的四条关系，并经显式计算验证其成立。

Conclusion: 四维 φ⁴ 理论的重整化群流确为梯度流，其梯度结构由外尔异常系数与 Ward 恒等式所确定；伴随论文（companion paper）以球面上的 Ward 恒等式处理 d=4-ε 及其他临界维度。

Abstract: We prove that the renormalisation group flow of multiscalar $φ^4$ theory in four dimensions is a gradient flow to all orders in perturbation theory. The proof uses the Weyl consistency conditions of Osborn and Jack, and it assumes two properties of perturbation theory: that the generating functional with local couplings is finite (also assumed by Osborn and Jack), and that the known expansion of massive Feynman integrals at large momenta holds with a bound on its remainder at each momentum. The consistency conditions give a gradient formula in which the tensor that multiplies the beta function is allowed to have an antisymmetric part, determined by coefficients of the Weyl anomaly. We derive a Ward identity for the two-point function of the marginal operators at constant couplings away from fixed points, with sources for the currents of field rotations and for the mass operator. It re-expresses one of the anti-symmetric contributions in terms of two-point functions and the first moment of the three-point function. We show that, after this rewrite, each of the resulting terms is equivalent to a symmetric contribution to the metric and a contribution to the potential, and we give both explicitly. The metric is positive definite to all orders in perturbation theory. The proof implies in particular the four relations among five- and six-loop coefficients of the field rotation, which Pannell and Stergiou derived as the conditions for a gradient flow at six loops. We check by explicit computation that these relations indeed hold. A companion paper treats $d=4-\varepsilon$ and other critical dimensions with a Ward identity on the sphere.

</details>


### [269] [Bring Your Own Instanton: Monopole Catalysis from Sourced SU(2) Instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28208)
*Csaba Csáki,Larissa Kiriliuk,Zhihan Liu,Rotem Ovadia*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文指出，'t Hooft-Polyakov 磁单极场中质子衰变的 Rubakov-Callan 催化（Rubakov-Callan catalysis）由被费米子“源化”的 SU(2) 瞬子（sourced SU(2) instantons）主导，此类拓扑规范组态不产生指数压低，且催化属红外（IR）效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上磁单极催化质子衰变与真空 BPST 瞬子（BPST instantons）的图像存在张力：真空瞬子给出指数压低（exponentially suppressed）的速率，难以解释催化过程的显著效应，因此需要新的拓扑机制刻画该过程。

Method: 作者引入由费米子源化的 SU(2) 瞬子（sourced SU(2) instantons）这一新拓扑规范组态，其携带单位瞬子数（unit instanton number）；进一步将绕数（winding number）与费米子零模（fermionic zero modes）数目关联，推测存在相应的指标定理（index theorem），并计算磁单极核内外费米子插入的主导阶关联函数（leading-order correlator）的闭式表达。

Result: 与真空 BPST 瞬子不同，源化瞬子不导致指数压低的速率；绕数与费米子零模数目之间存在对应关系；磁单极核内外费米子插入的主导阶关联函数均得到闭式表达，且结果表明散射费米子并不进入磁单极核。

Conclusion: Rubakov-Callan 催化被判定为红外（IR）效应，其中散射费米子（scattering fermions）不抵达磁单极核（monopole core），从而为磁单极催化质子衰变提供非指数压低的新图像。

Abstract: We show that Rubakov-Callan catalysis of proton decay in the field of a 't Hooft-Polyakov monopole proceeds through "sourced SU(2) instantons", which are novel topological gauge configurations sourced by fermions and carrying unit instanton number. Unlike vacuum BPST instantons, these sourced instantons do not give rise to exponentially suppressed rates. We relate the winding number to the number of fermionic zero modes, suggesting an underlying index theorem for sourced instantons. We also calculate the closed-form expression for the leading-order correlator for fermion insertions both outside and inside the monopole core. Our results indicate that Rubakov-Callan catalysis is an IR effect where the scattering fermions do not reach the monopole core.

</details>


### [270] [Wormholes do not contribute to supersymmetric partition functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28210)
*Jan Boruch,Douglas Stanford*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文论证连接多个渐近边界（asymptotic boundaries）的虫洞（wormhole）必然自发破缺边界条件所保留的部分对称性；对费米型对称性（fermionic symmetry），各轨道贡献相消，故虫洞对超对称配分函数（supersymmetric partition function）的矩无贡献，但配分函数仍可有非零系综方差（ensemble variance）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 Iliesiu、Kologlu 与 Turiaci 关于超对称指标（supersymmetric index）的结果推广到更一般的超对称配分函数矩，澄清虫洞对系综平均与方差的贡献问题。

Method: 从对称性角度分析多边界虫洞：考察其构型在边界条件所保留对称群下的变换行为，指出构型构成破缺对称性的非平凡轨道（orbit），并讨论费米型对称性下轨道贡献的相消。

Result: 多边界虫洞总是自发破缺至少部分边界对称性；费米型对称性下每个轨道贡献为零，因此虫洞对超对称配分函数的矩不贡献。但在所分析的类 SYK 模型（SYK-like）中，配分函数仍可具有非零系综方差，源自保留全部连续对称性的“非几何虫洞（non-geometric wormhole）”构型。

Conclusion: 虫洞不贡献超对称配分函数的矩，推广了超对称指标方面的既有结果；同时指出非零系综方差可由非几何虫洞产生，说明几何虫洞缺失并不排除系综方差。

Abstract: We show that wormholes connecting multiple asymptotic boundaries always spontaneously break at least some of the symmetries preserved by their boundary conditions. Their configurations then form nontrivial orbits of the broken symmetry, and for the case of a fermionic symmetry, the contribution of each orbit vanishes. We conclude that wormholes do not contribute to moments of supersymmetric partition functions. This extends results on supersymmetric indices by Iliesiu, Kologlu, and Turiaci. We emphasize that supersymmetric partition functions can still have a nonzero ensemble variance. In SYK-like examples that we analyze, this arises due to the contribution of ``non-geometric wormhole'' configurations that preserve all continuous symmetries.

</details>


### [271] [The chiral $M^{3,2}$ theory: partition functions beyond large-$N$ and quantum holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28594)
*Xuao Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文识别出手征 M^{3,2} 理论中自由能按 N^{3/2} 增长的 Bethe 真空族，给出其全阶微扰 1/N 展开的闭合形式，并提出全阶 Airy 公式，为全息对偶提供高精度检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究手征 M^{3,2} 理论中 Bethe 真空的自由能标度行为，并利用三球配分函数的高精度计算检验全息对偶（holography），特别是四导数修正（four-derivative correction）与单圈修正（one-loop correction）的精确性。

Method: 识别自由能以 N^{3/2} 增长的 Bethe 真空族，利用高精度数据拟合其全阶微扰 1/N 展开的闭合形式；在特定混合 Chern–Simons 耦合（mixed Chern–Simons coupling）下以重子磁通（baryonic magnetic flux）区分真空族成员；并在 't Hooft 极限与 Cardy 极限下数值研究三球配分函数（three-sphere partition function）。

Result: 高精度数据支持该真空族全阶 1/N 展开的闭合形式；在特定混合 Chern–Simons 耦合处重子磁通可区分该族真空；数值结果支持一个全阶 Airy 公式（Airy formula）。

Conclusion: 四导数与单圈修正可对全息对偶构成精度检验（precision tests），并支持所提出的全阶 Airy 公式对三球配分函数的描述。

Abstract: We identify families of Bethe vacua in the chiral $M^{3,2}$ theory whose individual free energies grow as $N^{3/2}$. High-precision data support closed forms for their all-order perturbative $1/N$ expansions. At a distinguished mixed Chern--Simons coupling, the baryonic magnetic flux distinguishes members of this family of vacua. We also study the three-sphere partition function numerically in the 't Hooft limit and Cardy limit, and propose an all-order Airy formula. The four-derivative and one-loop corrections are precision tests of holography.

</details>


### [272] [Relativistic Hydrodynamics as an Intersection Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28735)
*Nikita Nekrasov,Paul Wiegmann*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文将理想相对论流体力学表述为与时空相关的辛空间（symplectic space）中的相交问题：约束矩映射（moment map）的零点集与由度规和状态方程确定的拉格朗日子流形（Lagrangian submanifold）之交集恰为 Lichnerowicz--Carter 系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想相对论流体力学已有协变表述，但需要一个能统一刻画输运方程、对称性与守恒律的几何框架，从而揭示其约束结构与不变量的来源。

Method: 以 0、1、3、4 次微分形式（differential forms）为独立场，构造时空关联的辛空间；时空微分同胚（diffeomorphisms）与动量一形式的平移作用为哈密顿作用，其矩映射的零点集构成余等向子簇（coisotropic subvariety）；再由度规与状态方程给出拉格朗日子流形并取其相交。

Result: 该相交恰为 Lichnerowicz--Carter 系统；可由约束直接导出环流不变量（circulation invariants）与 Ertel 不变量，阐明正则流参数（canonical flow parameter）的几何含义，并将能量--动量守恒（energy--momentum conservation）等同于本构拉格朗日量的协变性。

Conclusion: 该框架给出理想相对论流体力学的几何统一描述，并简要指出非相对论极限（nonrelativistic limit）以及熵层面的螺旋度（helicity）与分裂 Calabi--Yau 几何的关联。

Abstract: Ideal relativistic hydrodynamics admits a covariant description as an intersection problem in a symplectic space associated with spacetime. The independent fields are differential forms of degrees 0,1,3,4. Spacetime diffeomorphisms and shifts of the momentum one-form act Hamiltonianly; the zero locus of their moment map is a coisotropic subvariety encoding the transport equations. The spacetime metric and equation of state determine a Lagrangian submanifold, whose intersection with the coisotropic subvariety is precisely the Lichnerowicz--Carter system. We explain the geometric meaning of the canonical flow parameter, derive circulation and Ertel invariants directly from the constraints, and identify energy--momentum conservation with covariance of the constitutive Lagrangian. The nonrelativistic limit and a relation of entropy-level helicity to split Calabi--Yau geometry are briefly indicated.

</details>


### [273] [Massive Particles in Carrollian Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28780)
*Bin Chen,Zezhou Hu,Reiko Liu,Wen-Jie Ma*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在四维平坦时空（flat spacetime）中为有质量标量场（massive scalar）构造了三维 Carrollian 全息描述（Carrollian holography），并给出显式的体-边界积分变换（bulk-to-boundary integral transform），证明后代的约束关系等价于零态解耦（null-state decoupling）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有质量粒子（massive particles）如何在边界算符（boundary operators）中被编码，是 Carrollian 全息中的核心问题；本文意在为此提供具体的构造出发点。

Method: 构造三维 Carrollian 描述及显式体-边界积分变换；分析非零质量所需无穷多线性独立原初态（primary states）与无体自旋（bulk spin）所导致的后代（descendants）关系，并将其与零态解耦及边界场的局域约束对应；计算两点与三点函数及形式算符乘积展开（operator product expansion, OPE）系数。

Result: 所构造的积分变换保持完整庞加莱对称性（Poincaré symmetry），并将体标量数据映射为满足该约束的边界场；得到两点、三点函数及相应的形式 OPE 系数；积分变换在复动量（complex momenta）上求值，故洛伦兹号差（Lorentzian signature）下解析延拓（analytic continuation）不可或缺。

Conclusion: 该构造为将有质量散射振幅（scattering amplitudes）与 Carrollian 关联函数（Carrollian correlators）相联系、以及由边界数据重构体场（bulk fields）提供了具体起点。

Abstract: Understanding how massive particles are encoded in boundary operators is a central problem in Carrollian holography. We construct a three-dimensional Carrollian description of a massive scalar in four-dimensional flat spacetime, together with an explicit bulk-to-boundary integral transform. In this construction, nonzero mass requires infinitely many linearly independent primary states, while the absence of bulk spin imposes relations among their descendants. We show that these relations are equivalent to null-state decoupling and give a local constraint on the boundary fields. The integral transform preserves the full Poincaré symmetry and maps bulk scalar data to fields satisfying this constraint. We also obtain two- and three-point functions and the associated formal operator product expansion coefficients. The integral transform evaluates bulk data at complex momenta, making analytic continuation essential in Lorentzian signature. This construction provides a concrete starting point for relating massive scattering amplitudes to Carrollian correlators and for reconstructing bulk fields from boundary data.

</details>


### [274] [Thermodynamics of hard wall model of AdS/QCD at finite chemical potential with Gauss-Bonnet corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28884)
*Dharm Veer Singh,Shobhit Sachan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在五维 Einstein–Maxwell–Gauss–Bonnet 引力下研究 AdS/QCD 硬墙模型（hard wall model）的禁闭/去禁闭相变，包含夸克物质对几何的反作用，给出固定化学势与固定密度下的相变线，并计算致密禁闭相中 ρ 介子及其径向激发态的质量与衰变常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 AdS/QCD 硬墙模型的禁闭/去禁闭相变推广到有限夸克密度，并在包含反作用（back-reaction）与 Gauss–Bonnet 修正的框架下考察相变行为及介子性质随密度的变化。

Method: 采用五维 Einstein–Maxwell–Gauss–Bonnet 引力；去禁闭相取为带电 Gauss–Bonnet 黑洞（charged Gauss–Bonnet black hole），禁闭相取为在红外截断的热带电 AdS。将 Lee–Park–Sin 为满足夸克数一致性而固定规范场红外值改为 Gauss–Bonnet 耦合的函数，并作 WKB 分析。

Result: 得到固定夸克化学势与固定夸克数密度下的解析相变线；固定密度时相变经过两相共存区。负 Gauss–Bonnet 耦合提高相变温度并把禁闭相延伸至更高密度；因果带上界内的取值使零密度相变温度接近格点（lattice）结果。致密禁闭相中 ρ 介子及其径向激发态质量均随密度增加，WKB 分析将其归因于有效禁闭区尺寸变短，核密度下 ρ 介子质量上升约 1%。

Conclusion: Gauss–Bonnet 耦合与夸克物质反作用显著影响硬墙模型的相变结构，负耦合可取与格点相符的零密度相变温度；介子质量的密度依赖性可通过有效禁闭区尺寸缩减解释。

Abstract: We study the confinement/deconfinement transition of the hard wall model of AdS/QCD at finite quark density in five-dimensional Einstein--Maxwell--Gauss--Bonnet gravity, with the back-reaction of the quark matter on the geometry included. The deconfined phase is a charged Gauss--Bonnet black hole, and the confined phase is thermally charged AdS cut off in the infrared. The infrared value of the gauge field, which Lee, Park and Sin fixed by requiring a consistent quark number, becomes a function of the Gauss--Bonnet coupling. We obtain the transition line in closed form at fixed quark chemical potential and at fixed quark number density. At fixed density the transition passes through a region where the two phases coexist. A negative Gauss--Bonnet coupling raises the transition temperature and extends the confined phase to higher density. A value inside the causality bound gives a transition temperature at zero density close to the lattice value. In the dense confined phase we compute the masses and decay constants of the $ρ$ meson and its radial excitations. All masses increase with density. A WKB analysis relates the increase to a shorter effective size of the confining region, and at nuclear density the $ρ$ mass rises by about one percent.

</details>


### [275] [Finite-Temperature Stabilization of a KKLT Modulus in Three-Dimensional Lattice Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29000)
*Saya Mahmoudi,Furkan Utku Biber,Hyukjung Kim,Heeseung Zoe*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 通过包含热浴、耗散、空间梯度与随机涨落的三维朗之万格点模拟（three-dimensional Langevin lattice simulations），研究KKLT模量（KKLT modulus）的宇宙学稳定化，发现空间平均模量被俘获并不意味着所有空间区域都留在稳定势垒内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有分析多基于均匀演化，难以刻画阈附近涨落与空间梯度对模量俘获（modulus capture）的影响，因此需考察涨落是否会驱动局部区域跨越不稳定势垒。

Method: 采用三维朗之万格点模拟，纳入演化热浴（evolving thermal bath）、耗散、空间梯度与随机涨落，并与线性朗之万理论（linear Langevin theory）对照，同时进行收敛性检验以确定离散化依赖较弱的随机层。

Result: 远离俘获阈值时空间平均模量接近均匀演化；近临界轨迹在非稳定势垒附近滞留约一个e-fold（e-fold），长波涨落经快子放大（tachyonic amplification）驱动局部区域穿越势垒，即使平均场已折返稳定真空；线性朗之万理论可重现涨落增长，收敛检验识别出穿越比例对离散化无系统依赖的狭窄随机层。

Conclusion: 空间平均模量的俘获不保证每个空间区域都处于稳定化势垒之内，局部势垒穿越是近临界情形下不可忽略的现象。

Abstract: We investigate the cosmological stabilization of a KKLT modulus through three-dimensional Langevin lattice simulations incorporating an evolving thermal bath, dissipation, spatial gradients, and stochastic fluctuations. While the spatially averaged modulus closely follows homogeneous evolution away from the capture threshold, near-critical trajectories linger near the unstable potential barrier for approximately one e-fold, allowing long-wavelength fluctuations to undergo tachyonic amplification and drive localized regions across the barrier even as the mean field turns back toward the stabilized vacuum. Linear Langevin theory reproduces the fluctuation growth, while convergence tests identify a narrow stochastic layer in which the barrier-crossing fraction shows no systematic dependence on the discretization at a fixed effective distance from the threshold. These results demonstrate that capture of the spatially averaged modulus does not guarantee that every spatial region remains within the stabilizing barrier.

</details>


### [276] [Exact stress-tensor fluctuations from conformal symmetry and black hole radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29060)
*José Navarro-Salas,Roberto Balbinot,Alessandro Fabbri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用二维共形对称性，将半经典引力微扰的方差化为闭式表达，并证明相对涨落正比于 1/c，从而在中心电荷 c 较大时验证半经典近似的自洽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半经典引力以应力张量期望值为源，其可靠性依赖于该值附近涨落足够小；二维情形中共形对称性可精确确定霍金辐射的均值，但涨落是否同样受其控制尚待澄清。

Method: 通过算符乘积展开（operator product expansion）定义应力张量平方，其中含标定点分裂（point-splitting）方案的参数 α；再借助共形映射至局部惯性坐标（locally inertial coordinates）的变换律，求得零点分量的闭式方差。

Result: 相对涨落按 1/c 标度。史瓦西黑洞（Schwarzschild black hole）情形下，无穷远处霍金通量为 22/5c，与平直时空热辐射相同；视界处出射通量为 −44α/3c、入射通量为 (238+720α)/5c，当 α=−3/10（Möbius 协变方案）时两者均约化为 22/5c。

Conclusion: 在大中心电荷极限下涨落相对均值可忽略，结果支持半经典近似；同时涨落对点分裂方案（α）的依赖性表明该方案选择在视界处具有物理意义。

Abstract: Semiclassical gravity is sourced by the expectation value of the stress tensor, and it is reliable only if the fluctuations around this value are small. In two dimensions, conformal symmetry captures the essential physics of Hawking radiation in its s-wave sector and determines the mean value exactly, for any background and any quantum state. We show that it also determines the local fluctuations of the null components $T_{\pm\pm}$. The square of the stress tensor is defined by the operator product expansion, up to a multiple $α$ of $\partial_\pm^2T_{\pm\pm}$ that labels point-splitting prescriptions. Its transformation law under the conformal map to locally inertial coordinates gives the variance in closed form. The relative fluctuation scales as $1/c$, with $c$ the central charge. For the Schwarzschild black hole it is $22/5c$ for the Hawking flux at infinity, that of flat-space thermal radiation. At the horizon it is $-44α/3c$ for the outgoing flux and $(238+720α)/5c$ for the ingoing flux that drives the backreaction. Both reduce to $22/5c$ for $α=-3/10$, which corresponds to the Möbius-covariant prescription. For large $c$ these results support the semiclassical approximation.

</details>


### [277] [The Generalized Second Law from Fluctuation Theorems in a Quantum Reference Frame](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29415)
*Feng-Li Lin,Sayid Mondal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在固定的半有限代数（semifinite algebra）上，对经历幺正驱动（unitary driving）与弛豫（relaxation）的黑洞外部证明了广义第二定律（generalized second law, GSL），将 GSL 重构为开放外部的量子信道涨落定理（fluctuation theorem, FT）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wall 的经典证明依赖半经典几何（semiclassical geometry），且将固定母态（parent states）限制在逐次视界截断（horizon cuts）对应的代数上；本文旨在摆脱这一限制，在固定代数框架下给出更一般的 GSL 证明。

Method: 在固定的修饰外部代数（dressed exterior algebra）上演化状态，借助态–算符对偶（state–operator duality）类比海森堡与薛定谔绘景；以引力荷（gravitational charge）作量子钟构造模交叉乘积（modular crossed product），使密度算符的迹熵在计入钟贡献后重现广义熵。发展三种互补表述：分离的幺正驱动与弛豫、时间局域描述、两时刻 surprisal 涨落定理。

Result: 分离表述结合模功（modular work）、将视界吸收与面积增加相联系的物理过程第一定律，以及通过数据处理实现的相对熵收缩（relative-entropy contraction）；时间局域表述可处理同时发生的幺正驱动与耗散；两时刻 surprisal FT 处理完整信道，识别非单位性修正（nonunital correction），并在不解析中间动力学的情况下给出幺正修饰演化的 GSL 界。不混合不同修饰荷扇区且保持完整修饰参考态的弛豫是单位的（unital）。

Conclusion: 单位性是该 FT 证明的充分条件，但对特定演化路径上的 GSL 并非必要；熵界在受控慢钟极限（slow-clock limit）下确立 GSL，并量化了有限宽度钟修正（finite-width clock corrections）。

Abstract: We prove the generalized second law (GSL) for black-hole exteriors undergoing unitary driving and relaxation on a fixed semifinite algebra. Wall's influential proof assumes semiclassical geometry and restricts fixed parent states to algebras of successive horizon cuts. We instead evolve the state on a fixed dressed exterior algebra. With complete characteristic data, the same semiclassical exterior supports both descriptions, analogous to the Heisenberg and Schrödinger pictures through state--operator duality. Using a gravitational charge as a quantum clock, we construct a modular crossed product with density operators whose trace entropy reproduces generalized entropy up to clock contributions. Our approach parallels thermodynamics in regulated field theory and formulates the GSL through a quantum channel fluctuation theorem (FT) for the open exterior. We develop three complementary formulations under specified dynamical assumptions. Separated unitary driving and relaxation combine modular work, the physical-process first law relating horizon absorption to area gain, and relative-entropy contraction through data processing. A time-local description accommodates simultaneous unitary driving and dissipation. A two-time surprisal FT treats the complete channel, identifies the nonunital correction, and yields a GSL bound for unital dressed evolution without resolving intermediate dynamics. Relaxation that does not mix distinct dressed-charge sectors and preserves the full dressed reference state is unital. Unitality is sufficient for this FT proof but is not necessary for the GSL along a particular evolution. The entropy bounds establish the GSL in a controlled slow-clock limit and quantify finite-width clock corrections.

</details>


### [278] [Symmetry resolved spread complexity for BMN matrix model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29425)
*Dibakar Roychowdhury*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究 BMN 矩阵模型在横向 SO(6) 标量扇区全局 U(1) 对称性下的对称分辨扩散复杂度（symmetry-resolved spread complexity），通过将时间演化的带电热场双态（cTFD）分解为固定电荷正交扇区，分析返回振幅、谱矩、Lanczos 系数与早期复杂度。低温柔禁闭相映射为退耦位移谐振子并给出复杂度非均分与正性下界；有限温度退禁闭相给出高斯谱密度下的普适平方根标度与二次扩散。结果在全息上区分无视界 LM 液滴中的相干弦涨落与带电 D0 膜视界附近的耗散型置乱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有扩散复杂度（spread complexity）研究多针对无电荷分辨的整体态，缺乏对全局对称性（此处为 U(1) 电荷）下各固定电荷扇区复杂度结构及相依赖行为的刻画。本文旨在考察 BMN 矩阵模型在两个热力学相中对称分辨复杂度的扇区分解特征及其全息对应。

Method: 将时间演化的带电热场双态（charged thermofield double, cTFD）按固定电荷正交扇区分解，计算返回振幅、谱矩、三对角 Lanczos 系数（a_n^{(q)}、b_n^{(q)}）与早期扩散复杂度。低温柔禁闭区（βμ≫1）以退耦位移谐振子加 Casimir 型零点偏移建模；有限温度退禁闭区（T≫μ）将平面大 N 谱密度取为热高斯分布。

Result: 低温柔禁闭相：扇区动力学等效于带 Casimir 型零点偏移的退耦位移谐振子，给出因子化谱矩、对角 Lanczos 系数线性漂移、非对角项 √n 亚线性增长及热压低的初始跃迁；电荷相关 Boltzmann 权重、真空位移与退化破坏复杂度均分（complexity equipartition），扇区间相干与电荷弥散强制早期正性下界 ΔC(t)=C_total(t)−C̄(t)≥0。有限温度连续相：a_n^{(q)}=Ē_q 为常数、b_n^{(q)}=σ_E√n 呈普适平方根标度、早期扩散为 C^{(q)}(t)=σ_E^2(q,T)t^2+O(t^4)，由反作用后的扇区能量方差支配。

Conclusion: 两相在复杂度标度上呈现定性差异，并在全息上分别对应无视界 Lin-Maldacena（LM）液滴几何中相干弦涨落，以及带电十维黑 D0 膜视界附近的耗散型置乱（scrambling）。对称分辨复杂度可作为区分几何与相结构的诊断量。

Abstract: We investigate the symmetry-resolved spread complexity of the Berenstein-Maldacena-Nastase (BMN) matrix model under a global $U(1)$ symmetry acting within the transverse $SO(6)$ scalar sector. Decomposing the time-evolved charged thermofield double (cTFD) state into orthogonal fixed-charge sectors, we examine the return amplitude, spectral moments, tridiagonal Lanczos coefficients, and early-time spread complexity across the low-temperature confined ($βμ\gg 1$) and finite-temperature deconfined ($T \gg μ$) phases. In the low-temperature gapped regime, effective sector dynamics map onto decoupled, displaced harmonic oscillators with a Casimir-like zero-point offset, yielding factorized moments, linear drift in diagonal Lanczos coefficients $a_n^{(q)}$, and sub-linear $\sqrt{n}$ off-diagonal growth with thermally suppressed initial hopping. Charge-dependent Boltzmann weights, vacuum shifts, and degeneracies break complexity equipartition, while inter-sector coherence and charge dispersion enforce an early-time positivity bound $ΔC(t) = C_{\text{total}}(t) - \bar{C}(t) \ge 0$. In the \emph{finite}-temperature continuum phase, modeling the planar large-$N$ spectral density as a thermal Gaussian gives constant diagonal coefficients $a_n^{(q)} = \bar{E}_q$, universal square-root scaling $b_n^{(q)} = σ_E \sqrt{n}$, and a quadratic spread launch $\mathcal{C}^{(q)}(t) = σ_E^2(q,T)\,t^2 + \mathcal{O}(t^4)$ governed by the backreacted sector energy variance. Holographically, these regimes distinguish coherent string fluctuations in horizonless Lin-Maldacena (LM) droplet geometries from dissipative scrambling near charged ten-dimensional black D0-brane horizons.

</details>


### [279] [Scattering amplitudes in AdS$_5\times$ S$^5$ from the pure spinor superstring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29622)
*Luis F. Alday,Thiago Fleury,Tobias Hansen,Lucas N. S. Martins,Dennis Zavaleta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文为纯旋量超弦在 AdS_5×S^5 背景下的零模积分提出了一个保持 BRST 不变性的方案，并据此首次直接在世界面上计算了半 BPS 超引力态的两点和三点振幅，其中三点振幅与平面 N=4 超杨-米尔斯理论结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 AdS_5×S^5 背景下进行纯旋量超弦（pure spinor superstring）的振幅计算时，零模（zero mode）积分缺乏良定义的测度，构成核心障碍。

Method: 构建保持 BRST 不变性（BRST-invariant）的零模积分方案，其测度包含平直空间（flat space）中所缺失的贡献，并可用图变换算符（picture changing operators）或非极小调节子（non-minimal regulator）予以描述；该方案适用于任意多重数与任意外态的亏格为零（genus-zero）振幅。

Result: 该方案消除了该背景下振幅计算的核心障碍，并用于对半 BPS 超引力（half-BPS supergravity）态的两点与三点振幅进行首次直接的世界面（worldsheet）计算；经适当归一化的三点振幅精确重现平面 N=4 超杨-米尔斯（planar N=4 super Yang-Mills）理论的结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的 BRST 不变零模积分方案在亏格为零情形下普遍适用，为 AdS_5×S^5 纯旋量超弦的振幅计算奠定基础，并给出了与规范理论对偶结果相符的验证。

Abstract: We formulate a BRST-invariant prescription for the integration of zero modes in the pure spinor superstring on $AdS_5\times S^5$. The measure includes contributions absent in flat space and admits a description in terms of picture changing operators or a non-minimal regulator. The prescription is valid for genus-zero amplitudes with arbitrary multiplicity and external states and removes a central obstacle to amplitude computations in this background. We use it to perform the first direct worldsheet computation of two and three-point amplitudes of half-BPS supergravity states. The properly normalised three-point amplitudes exactly reproduce the results of planar ${\mathcal N}=4$ super Yang-Mills theory.

</details>


### [280] [Quadratic-in-spin scattering of compact bodies at fourth post-Minkowskian order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29670)
*Taushif Ahmed,Syed Mehedi Hasan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文给出对齐自旋致密天体在四阶后闵可夫斯基（fourth-post-Minkowskian）近似下的散射角（scattering angle），自旋精确到二次项，适用于任意速度与质量比，并包含自旋诱导四极矩（spin-induced quadrupole）、辐射反作用（radiation reaction）及曲率平方自旋-潮汐项（curvature-squared spin-tidal terms）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 更精确地刻画自旋致密双体的散射过程，特别是将自旋二次效应、四极矩与潮汐效应对散射角的贡献纳入高阶后闵可夫斯基框架，以改进黑洞散射的重求和预言。

Method: 采用三圈世界线积分（three-loop worldline integrals），结果以多重对数（polylogarithms）与椭圆积分（elliptic integrals）的闭合形式表达；红外极点（infrared poles）精确相消，从而确定尾对数（tail logarithm）。

Result: 得到四阶后闵可夫斯基散射角的闭合表达式；给出一个新的后牛顿（post-Newtonian）系数，以及适用于一般四极矩的三阶角动量损失（third-order angular-momentum loss）。

Conclusion: 新结果修正了黑洞散射的重求和预言（resummed predictions），表明自旋二次与潮汐相关贡献在高阶散射角计算中不可忽略。

Abstract: We present the fourth-post-Minkowskian scattering angle of aligned spinning compact bodies through quadratic spin at arbitrary velocity and mass ratio. We include spin-induced quadrupoles and radiation reaction, supplemented by curvature-squared spin-tidal terms. Three-loop worldline integrals yield closed forms in polylogarithms and elliptic integrals. Infrared poles cancel exactly, fixing the tail logarithm. We obtain a new post-Newtonian coefficient and third-order angular-momentum loss for general quadrupoles. The new input shifts resummed predictions for black-hole scattering.

</details>


### [281] [A note on Holographic Renormalization for Polarized IKKT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29677)
*Matteo Guglielmetti*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文为极化 IKKT 矩阵模型（polarized IKKT matrix model）真空所对应的 IIB 型超引力（type IIB supergravity）解构造了在壳作用量（on-shell action）的边界抵消项（boundary counterterm），并验证重整化后的作用量与矩阵模型结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于：超引力在壳作用量通常发散，需通过边界抵消项实现全息重整化（holographic renormalization），以便与对偶矩阵模型的可观测量进行定量比较。

Method: 基于变分原理（variational principle）构造抵消项，所用离壳涨落（off-shell fluctuations）的衰减条件由渐近运动方程（asymptotic equations of motion）确定；并通过在离壳层面将 RR 二形式势（RR two-form potential）对偶化，转入固定 D1-膜电荷（fixed D1-brane charges）的系综。

Result: 超对称鞍点（supersymmetric saddles）的重整化在壳作用量在转到固定 D1-膜电荷系综后，重现了矩阵模型的结果。

Conclusion: 结果表明该边界抵消项构造可实现 IIB 型超引力解的正确全息重整化，并支持极化 IKKT 矩阵模型与其超引力对偶之间在固定 D1-膜电荷系综下的定量对应。

Abstract: We construct a boundary counterterm for the on-shell action of type IIB supergravity solutions dual to vacua of the polarized IKKT matrix model. The construction is based on the variational principle, with off-shell fluctuations whose fall-off conditions are based on the asymptotic equations of motion. The renormalized on-shell action of the supersymmetric saddles reproduces the matrix- model result once we pass to the ensemble of fixed D1-brane charges, which we implement off-shell by dualizing the RR two-form potential.

</details>


### [282] [Infrared finite scattering theory: Non-relativistic Coulomb scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29691)
*Kartik Prabhu,Shivam Upadhyay*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视库仑势中的非相对论量子散射，发展Dollard框架下的微扰表述，证明库仑发散在各阶相消，并在海森堡绘景中构建适配修正渐近动力学的LSZ约化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Born级数因库仑相互作用的长程性（long-range interaction）产生发散积分；Dollard虽构造了良定义的散射算符，但显式微扰计算稀少，且缺乏向量子场论（quantum field theory）推广的框架。

Method: 建立Dollard散射算符（Dollard scattering operator）的微扰表述，使库仑发散在全阶（to all orders）相消；显式计算至第二Born阶，并通过含时正则变换（time-dependent canonical transformation）消去渐近相空间坐标中的对数库仑修正，在海森堡绘景（Heisenberg picture）中以多种互补途径构造Heisenberg S矩阵。

Result: 库仑发散在第二Born阶显式抵消，所得振幅重现已知的精确分布型库仑振幅（distributional Coulomb amplitude），含前向散射贡献；由此得到适配修正渐近动力学的LSZ约化（Lehmann–Symanzik–Zimmermann reduction）表述，并与Dollard散射算符建立联系。

Conclusion: 该构造为将LSZ形式推广至含长程相互作用与无质量场（massless fields）的相对论量子场论中的红外发散（infrared divergences）提供了框架。

Abstract: We revisit non-relativistic quantum-mechanical scattering in a Coulomb potential. The long-range nature of the interaction leads to divergent integrals in conventional S-matrix calculations using the Born series. Dollard showed that a well-defined scattering operator can be constructed by replacing the free Hamiltonian $ H_0 $ with an appropriate asymptotic Hamiltonian $ H_{\rm D} $. However, explicit perturbative calculations within Dollard's framework have remained scarce. We develop a perturbative formulation of the Dollard scattering operator in which the Coulomb divergences cancel to all orders. Perturbatively, we demonstrate this cancellation explicitly through second Born order and show that the resulting amplitude reproduces the known exact distributional Coulomb amplitude, including its forward-scattering contribution. With the aim of generalizing our analysis to quantum field theory, we reformulate the problem in the Heisenberg picture. We construct a new set of canonical variables through a time-dependent canonical transformation that removes the logarithmic Coulomb correction from the asymptotic phase-space coordinates. Using these variables, we construct the corresponding Heisenberg S-matrix through several complementary approaches. This leads to a formulation of the Lehmann--Symanzik--Zimmermann (LSZ) reduction formalism adapted to the modified asymptotic dynamics, which we relate to the Dollard scattering operator. This construction provides a framework for extending the LSZ formalism to infrared divergences in relativistic quantum field theories with long-range interactions and massless fields.

</details>


### [283] [Bounds on entanglement fluctuations at holographic interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29699)
*Pallab Basu,Vishnu Jejjala*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究固定熵（entropy）下纠缠涨落（entanglement fluctuations）的最小尺度问题，对一类由带势标量场支持的共形界面（conformal interfaces），证明了三者满足不等式 $c_\text{eff}\leq c_\text{cap}\leq c_\text{eff}^2/c_{LR}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注固定熵条件下纠缠涨落能有多小这一基本问题，并试图厘清熵、容量（capacity）与能量透射（energy transmission）之间的定量关系。

Method: 在全息框架下考察由标量场及势函数支持的一类共形界面；将耦合的引力响应（gravitational response）表述为一项输运项（transport term）减去正的标量贡献，并利用背景爱因斯坦方程（background Einstein equation）约束该减法项，从而导出相关不等式。

Result: 证明了 $c_\text{eff}\leq c_\text{cap}\leq c_\text{eff}^2/c_{LR}$；此外发现，较长的 Janus 喉部（Janus throat）可在熵系数有限的情况下使容量无界，而标量响应保持有能隙（gapped）。

Conclusion: 结果表明熵、容量与能量透射之间存在受引力约束的层级关系，且容量在特定几何（长 Janus 喉部）下可被显著放大，而标量响应仍受能隙保护。

Abstract: How small can entanglement fluctuations be at fixed entropy? For a class of holographic conformal interfaces supported by a scalar with a potential, we prove $c_\text{eff}\leq c_\text{cap}\leq c_\text{eff}^2/c_{LR}$, relating entropy, capacity, and energy transmission. The coupled gravitational response is a transport term minus a positive scalar contribution; the background Einstein equation bounds that subtraction. A long Janus throat allows unbounded capacity at a finite entropy coefficient while the scalar response remains gapped.

</details>


### [284] [Lie Algebra Cohomology and Seiberg-Witten Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29713)
*Ahsan Z. Khan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文计算了规范群为 SU(2) 的纯 N=2 规范理论中微扰全纯-拓扑局部算子代数，证实作者先前猜想：它是由中心荷为零的 Virasoro 元素 X 与权重为三的奇主元素 Y 生成、仅受 X^2=0 约束的 Poisson 顶点代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在证明作者此前提出的猜想，即纯 N=2 SU(2) 规范理论中微扰全纯-拓扑局部算子代数的具体结构，并将其与相对 Lie 代数上同调及凝聚 Satake 范畴中的不变量联系起来。

Method: 证明使用仿射直线上点构型空间（configuration spaces of points on the affine line）的因子化（factorization）、深度论证（depth argument），以及系数代数 C[z,w]/(w^2) 的两种形变（deformations）。

Result: 所得代数由中心荷（central charge）为零的 Virasoro 元素 X 和权重为三的奇主元素（odd primary）Y 生成，仅满足关系 X^2=0；等价地给出了相对 Lie 代数上同调 H*(sl_2[z,w]/(w^2), sl_2) 以及 SL_2 与 PGL_2 的凝聚 Satake 范畴（coherent Satake category）中单位对象（unit）的自 Ext 群（self-Ext groups）。

Conclusion: 该结果证实了作者的猜想，并建立了纯 N=2 SU(2) 规范理论局部算子代数、相对 Lie 代数上同调与凝聚 Satake 范畴中单位自 Ext 群这三种描述之间的等价关系。

Abstract: We compute the algebra of perturbative holomorphic-topological local operators of pure $\mathcal N=2$ gauge theory with gauge group $SU(2)$, proving a conjecture of the author: it is the Poisson vertex algebra generated by a Virasoro element $X$ of central charge zero and an odd primary $Y$ of weight three, subject only to $X^2=0$. Equivalently, we compute the relative Lie algebra cohomology $H^*(\mathfrak{sl}_2[z,w]/(w^2),\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ and the self-Ext groups of the unit in the coherent Satake category for $SL_2$ and $PGL_2$. The proof uses factorization on configuration spaces of points on the affine line, a depth argument, and two deformations of the coefficient algebra $\mathbb C[z,w]/(w^2)$.

</details>


### [285] [Boundary spectroscopy of hidden near-horizon reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30092)
*Yiling Chen,Jiayue Yang,Zhen Zhong,Robert B. Mann,Jie Jiang,Ming Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在旋转 BTZ 黑洞外部标量场中，推导出视界附近共转被动壁（corotating passive wall）的精确边界响应，并用 Airy 滤波器（Airy filter）刻画其谱；正能条件排除增长模式，响应可同时确定壁阻抗（wall impedance）与壁位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典黑洞视界（black hole horizon）被视为完全吸收体，任何偏离都会被体（bulk）所隐藏；本文旨在考察这种偏离在边界响应上是否可观测。

Method: 在旋转 Bañados-Teitelboim-Zanelli（BTZ）外部对标量场（scalar field）求解，得到视界附近共转被动壁的精确边界响应；以 Airy 滤波器给出其频谱，并借助正能条件（positive energy）分析模式稳定性。

Result: 在任意频带上，响应可识别出常数壁阻抗与壁位置，二者以正交方式进入，其权重由谱函数（spectral function）决定；增长模式被正能条件排除。

Conclusion: 边界所“看到”的是一个反射壁（reflecting wall），而完美吸收体（perfect absorber）则被指数式隐藏。

Abstract: A classical black hole horizon absorbs completely, and any deviation would be hidden in the bulk. For a scalar field in a rotating Bañados-Teitelboim-Zanelli exterior, we derive an exact boundary response of a corotating passive wall near the horizon. An Airy filter gives its spectrum, and positive energy excludes growing modes. On any frequency band the response identifies a constant wall impedance and the wall position, which enter orthogonally with weights determined by the spectral function. The boundary thus sees a reflecting wall, whereas a perfect absorber is exponentially hidden.

</details>


### [286] [Charged spin-$\frac32$ in a chiral Feshbach--Villars model: hyperbolic propagation, Landau-level instability, and exceptional points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30225)
*Haryanto M. Siahaan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究(3/2,0)⊕(0,3/2)表示上Pauli耦合二阶波动方程在自由旋磁比g下的谱性质，指出精确Landau谱在|g|j≤1时始终为实，g=2时临界自旋3/2通道在|qB|=m²/2处出现二阶例外点并丧失正定守恒量，同时区分了谱不稳定性与双曲性丧失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对高阶自旋（spin-3/2）相对论波动方程在电磁背景下的谱稳定性与量子化可行性问题，需厘清谱不稳定性（spectral instability）是否等价于方程双曲性（hyperbolicity）丧失，且不预设协变的物理单物种约化（single-species reduction）。

Method: 将Pauli耦合二阶波动方程改写为Feshbach–Villars形式的十六分量赝厄米（pseudo-Hermitian）哈密顿系统，求解精确自旋j的Landau谱；并引入有界高斯衰减的谐振静电势，将退化流形上的一阶问题约化为V±iC，在最低两态自旋3/2流形上分析非实本征值分支。

Result: 精确Landau谱当且仅当|g|j≤1时对所有场强保持实；g=2时自旋3/2临界通道在|qB|=m²/2处每手征块出现二阶例外点（second-order exceptional point），并伴随正定守恒二次型丧失。微扰下当m²/6<|qB|<m²/2且幅度足够小时出现非实本征值分支，唯特殊阱宽处一阶劈裂消失；自旋1/2的g=2极限通过Dirac谱实性检验，g=1/j则无虚部一阶修正。

Conclusion: 结果将谱不稳定性与双曲性丧失区分开来，表明二者的判据并不重合；作者明确指出这些结论并未确立物理的费米子量子化（fermionic quantization）。

Abstract: We study a Pauli-coupled second-order wave equation on $(\frac32,0)\oplus(0,\frac32)$ with a free gyromagnetic ratio $g$. Its Feshbach--Villars form is a sixteen-component pseudo-Hermitian Hamiltonian system with lightlike covariant characteristics in smooth electromagnetic backgrounds. The solution space is chirality doubled; a covariant physical single-species reduction is not assumed. The exact spin-$j$ Landau spectrum remains real at every field strength precisely when $|g|j\le1$. At $g=2$, the critical spin-$\frac32$ channel reaches zero at $|qB|=m^2/2$, where each chiral block develops a second-order exceptional point. This threshold also marks the loss of a time-independent positive-definite conserved quadratic form for the channel. We also include a bounded, Gaussian-damped harmonic electrostatic potential and its associated electric field. The complete first-order problem on a degenerate manifold reduces to $V\pm iC$, with $V$ and $C$ the projected potential and spin--electric coupling. On the lowest two-state spin-$\frac32$ manifold, nonreal eigenvalue branches arise for $m^2/6<|qB|<m^2/2$ at sufficiently small nonzero perturbation amplitude, except at a special well range where the first-order splitting vanishes. The spin-$\frac12$, $g=2$ limit satisfies the Dirac spectral-reality check, whereas $g=1/j$ has no imaginary first-order correction for this perturbation. These results separate spectral instability from loss of hyperbolicity; they do not establish a physical fermionic quantization.

</details>


### [287] [Matter-decorated trumpets in double-scaled SYK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30296)
*Matteo Beccaria*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文在双标度SYK（double-scaled SYK, DSSYK）模型中构造了带物质场传播子的虫洞振幅的粘合（gluing），将 sine-dilaton gravity 中的双喇叭（double trumpet）粘合从 JT 极限推广到超出 JT 的情形，并逐扇区精确证明其可由带物质插入的世界末端膜（end-of-the-world brane）迹构造的喇叭粘合而成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无物质的双喇叭虫洞振幅可通过沿离散长度闭合测地线粘合两个喇叭并对长度求和得到，但带维度 Δ 物质场传播子的虫洞粘合此前仅在 Jackiw--Teitelboim（JT）极限下已知，缺乏超出 JT 的显式构造。

Method: 以 DSSYK（sine-dilaton gravity 的候选边界对偶，可重现其 disk 振幅）为框架，采用高斯物质（Gaussian matter）的 ETH 双矩阵模型定义带物质虫洞；由带物质插入的世界末端膜迹构建带物质喇叭，并按穿过颈部的弦数（number of chords）逐扇区证明虫洞可精确粘合，权重为正并计入弦的扭曲、缠绕及绕颈闭合圈。

Result: 喇叭与权重均获得闭合形式，前者表为 Rogers 多项式的变换，并讨论了构造的唯一性；在 JT 极限下二者分别（而非仅组合后）重现已知粘合；在经典极限下，每个喇叭呈现体（bulk）预期形式，即纯引力 sine-dilaton 喇叭被探针物质测地线在边界处贡献的因子修饰。

Conclusion: 研究给出了超出 JT 极限的带物质虫洞粘合的精确构造，验证了 DSSYK 与 sine-dilaton gravity 对偶在含物质情形下的一致性，并为该构造的闭合形式与唯一性提供了依据。

Abstract: In sine-dilaton gravity, the double trumpet, the wormhole amplitude without matter, is obtained by gluing two trumpets along a closed geodesic of discrete length and summing over this length. When the wormhole carries the propagator of a matter field of dimension $Δ$, joining the two asymptotic boundaries and crossing the neck, such a gluing is known only in the Jackiw--Teitelboim (JT) limit. We construct the gluing beyond JT in double-scaled SYK (DSSYK), the proposed boundary dual of sine-dilaton gravity, whose disk amplitudes it reproduces. In this context, we adopt the definition of the matter-decorated wormhole in terms of the ETH two-matrix model with Gaussian matter. We build matter-decorated trumpets from an end-of-the-world brane trace with a matter insertion, and prove that the wormhole is glued from them exactly, sector by sector in the number of chords through the neck, with a positive weight that accounts for the twists and windings of these chords and for the closed loops around the neck. Trumpets and weight are obtained in closed form, the former as transforms of Rogers polynomials, and we discuss the uniqueness of the construction. In the JT limit, both reproduce the known gluing separately, and not only in combination. In the classical limit, each trumpet takes the form expected in the bulk: a pure-gravity sine-dilaton trumpet dressed by the factor that a probe matter geodesic contributes at its boundary.

</details>


### [288] [Rotating Black Holes from Scaling Transformations in Horava Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30337)
*Deniz O. Devecioglu,Mu-In Park*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究低能Hořava引力中改变解物理参数的标度变换，提出一种简洁的标度变换，可从已知相对论解生成旋转黑洞解，并据此构造了"Doran-like"与"Kerr-Schild-like"两类旋转解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在低能Hořava引力（low-energy Hořava gravity）框架下系统获得旋转黑洞解，作者寻求通过标度变换（scaling transformations）从已知相对论解（relativistic solutions）出发生成新解，以扩展该类引力理论中旋转解的形式与坐标系表述。

Method: 提出一种简洁的标度变换，用于生成低能Hořava引力中的旋转黑洞解；并以已知相对论解为基础，具体构造出"Doran-like"（Doran型）与"Kerr-Schild-like"（Kerr-Schild型）两类旋转解作为显式示例。

Result: 两类解均具有可穿透视界（horizon-penetrating）的性质，但其零视界（null horizons）并非Killing视界（Killing horizons）；两解的奇点结构（singularity structures）彼此不同，但在两者中时序（chronology）均受到保护，与此前Boyer-Lindquist坐标下得到的类Kerr解（Kerr-like solutions）一致。

Conclusion: 低能Hořava引力中由标度变换生成的旋转解可具备视界穿透性且保持时序保护，同时其零视界不具Killing性质；不同坐标系表述下（Doran型、Kerr-Schild型、Boyer-Lindquist型）的类Kerr解在时序保护上表现出共性。

Abstract: We study the scaling transformations that change the physical parameters of solutions in low-energy Horava gravity. In particular, we present a simple scaling transformation that generates rotating black-hole solutions in low-energy Horava gravity from known relativistic solutions. As explicit examples, we obtain "Doran-like" and "Kerr-Schild-like" rotating solutions in Horava gravity. We find that both solutions are horizon-penetrating, but their null horizons are not Killing horizons. We also find that, although their singularity structures differ from each other, chronology is protected in both solutions, as in the previously obtained Kerr-like solutions in Boyer-Lindquist coordinates.

</details>


### [289] [Darboux transformation for the ASDYM equations: Solitons and instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30433)
*Shangshuai Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文借助一重Darboux变换（one-fold Darboux transformation）将反自对偶Yang–Mills方程的Yang–Mills拉格朗日密度用τ函数表示，并分别处理指数型soliton与ADHM instanton两类解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是在反自对偶Yang–Mills（anti-self-dual Yang–Mills）框架下，用τ函数统一刻画Yang–Mills拉格朗日密度，并把分类上看似不同的soliton解与instanton解纳入同一变换语言。

Method: 对反自对偶Yang–Mills方程采用一重Darboux变换（one-fold Darboux transformation）；对指数函数构造的soliton，将相应τ函数以Grassmannian Gr(N,M;ℂ)中的任意点进行参数化；对ADHM构造给出的instanton，用一重Darboux变换重新解释ADHM构造。

Result: 得到τ函数形式的Yang–Mills拉格朗日密度；对指数型soliton给出由Grassmannian任意点参数化的τ函数并构造相应Yang–Mills场；对ADHM instanton重新导出Osborn的Yang–Mills拉格朗日密度公式，并获得相应的规范联络（gauge connection）。

Conclusion: 一重Darboux变换为反自对偶Yang–Mills的τ函数表述提供了统一视角，可同时覆盖Grassmannian参数化的soliton解与ADHM instanton解，并复现Osborn公式。

Abstract: Using a one-fold Darboux transformation for the anti-self-dual Yang--Mills equations, we express the Yang--Mills Lagrangian density in terms of $τ$-functions. For solitons constructed from exponential functions, we show that the associated $τ$-functions admit a parametrization by arbitrary points in the Grassmannian Gr(N,M;$\mathbb{C}$), and construct the corresponding Yang--Mills fields. For instantons arising from the Atiyah--Drinfeld--Hitchin--Manin (ADHM) construction, we reinterpret the ADHM construction in terms of a one-fold Darboux transformation, recover Osborn's formula for the Yang--Mills Lagrangian density, and obtain the corresponding gauge connection.

</details>


### [290] [Non-invertible Duality Defects in Four-Dimensional $\mathbb{Z}_N$ Lattice Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30489)
*Satoshi Yamaguchi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文针对四维 $\mathbb{Z}_N$ 格点规范理论（lattice gauge theories）的广义面作用量，通过为每个构建块赋权并证明拓扑不变性，构造出实现对偶性（duality）的拓扑界面（topological interfaces）；在自对偶耦合（self-dual couplings）处这些界面成为表示非可逆对称性（non-invertible symmetries）的对偶缺陷（duality defects）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四维 $\mathbb{Z}_N$ 格点规范理论的广义面作用量已知存在对偶性，但如何在对偶之间建立显式的拓扑对象并刻画其与中心对称性（center symmetry）的关联，尚需系统构造；本文即为在格点层面显式实现这些对偶性及其相关缺陷结构。

Method: 为每个构建块（building block）赋予权重，并证明所得界面具有拓扑不变性（topological invariance），从而构造实现对偶性的拓扑界面；随后考察自对偶耦合处界面的退化行为，并研究中心对称缺陷及其与对偶界面的交汇（junctions）。

Result: 构造出实现对偶性的拓扑界面并验证其拓扑不变性；在自对偶耦合处界面成为对偶缺陷，对应非可逆对称性；同时得到中心对称缺陷与对偶界面交汇的若干交叉关系（crossing relations）。

Conclusion: 格点 $\mathbb{Z}_N$ 规范理论的广义面作用量可通过对构建块赋权的方式显式实现拓扑对偶界面，其在自对偶点上升格为承载非可逆对称性的对偶缺陷，并与中心对称缺陷形成满足特定交叉关系的交汇结构。

Abstract: We consider four-dimensional $\mathbb{Z}_N$ lattice gauge theories with generalized plaquette actions. These theories are known to exhibit dualities. We construct topological interfaces implementing these dualities by assigning a weight to each building block and proving topological invariance. At self-dual couplings, these interfaces become duality defects representing non-invertible symmetries. We also study center symmetry defects and their junctions with the duality interfaces and derive several crossing relations.

</details>


### [291] [Dirichlet walls beyond JT gravity and their quantum group interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30567)
*Luca Griguolo,Jacopo Papalini,Lorenzo Russo,Domenico Seminara,Alex Tarana*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文对带有限Dirichlet边界条件的sinh膨胀子引力（sinh dilaton gravity）进行正则量子化，得到由q-超几何函数（q-hypergeometric functions）描述的宇宙波函数，并揭示其与U_q(sl(2,R))表示论的深刻联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将JT引力的超几何波函数推广到有限截断情形，理解有限Dirichlet边界条件下sinh膨胀子引力的量子性质及其代数结构。

Method: 以有效AdS₂几何中双侧类空测地线（two-sided spacelike geodesic）长度及其共轭动量构造约化相空间，进行正则量子化，得到有限差分薛定谔方程（finite-difference Schrödinger equation）。

Result: 宇宙波函数由q-超几何函数给出，构成JT引力超几何波函数的量子形变（quantum deformation）；有限截断哈密顿量可解释为U_q(sl(2,R))在双曲矩阵元上的约化Casimir；两个散射波解为模双（modular double）U_q(sl(2,R))⊗U_q̃(sl(2,R))的双曲矩阵元。

Conclusion: 宇宙波函数是两个散射波解在长度趋零时正则（regular）的唯一线性组合，表明有限截断sinh膨胀子引力具有q-形变的代数结构。

Abstract: We canonically quantize sinh dilaton gravity with finite Dirichlet boundary conditions. The reduced phase space is formulated in terms of the length of a two-sided spacelike geodesic in an effective AdS$_2$ geometry and its conjugate momentum. Canonical quantization leads to a finite-difference Schrödinger equation whose wavefunctions of the universe are given by $q$-hypergeometric functions. These furnish a quantum deformation of the hypergeometric wavefunctions of JT gravity. We further show that the finite cutoff Hamiltonian derived from the bulk analysis admits a natural interpretation as the reduced Casimir of $U_q(\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{R}))$ acting on hyperbolic matrix elements. The two scattering wave solutions are hyperbolic matrix elements of the modular double $U_q(\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb R))\otimes U_{\tilde q}(\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb R))$, and the wavefunction of the universe is their unique combination regular at vanishing length.

</details>


### [292] [Conservative tidal effects to third post-Minkowskian order in scalar-tensor theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30762)
*Zhengwen Liu,Hongbin Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文利用世界线有效场论，在含标量-高斯-博内耦合的无质量标量-张量理论中，计算了自旋为零致密双星在3PM阶的守恒有限尺寸（潮汐）贡献，包括冲量与散射角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密双星潮汐响应对引力波波形精度至关重要，而标量-张量理论及标量-高斯-博内耦合下的潮汐效应尚缺乏系统的高阶后闵可夫斯基描述。

Method: 采用世界线有效场论（worldline effective field theory），保留潮汐响应系数线性阶的完整速度依赖，覆盖标量偶极与四极以及电型、磁型与混合四极和八极，计算至三阶后闵可夫斯基（3PM）精度。

Result: 标量偶极在3PN阶即进入动力学，比引力四极提前两阶；3PM阶散射角潮汐修正还依赖响应的标量一阶导数并含高斯-博内耦合线性项；结果正确退化为广义相对论潮汐角与5PN阶后牛顿潮汐动力学，并经受检验粒子计算验证。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了标量-张量引力中致密双星潮汐效应的3PM解析结果，并通过广义相对论极限与检验粒子极限的验证，确认了结果在质量比领头阶上的正确性。

Abstract: Using worldline effective field theory, we compute the conservative finite-size contributions to the impulse and scattering angle of nonspinning compact binaries in massless scalar-tensor theories with a scalar-Gauss-Bonnet coupling through third post-Minkowskian (3PM) order. We retain the full velocity dependence at linear order in the tidal response coefficients. The calculation covers the scalar dipole and quadrupole and the electric, magnetic, and mixed quadrupoles and octupoles. Among them, the scalar dipole enters the dynamics already at third post-Newtonian (3PN) order, two orders before the gravitational quadrupole. At 3PM, the tidal correction to the angle also depends on the first scalar derivatives of the responses and contains terms linear in the Gauss-Bonnet coupling. The results reproduce the general-relativistic tidal angle and the post-Newtonian tidal dynamics through 5PN, and a test-body calculation confirms their leading order in the mass ratio, including the Gauss-Bonnet terms for a companion with vanishing sensitivity.

</details>


### [293] [Gradient formula from the sphere for perturbative RG flows near critical dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31050)
*Marten Reehorst*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究 $d=d_c-\varepsilon$ 维微扰重整化群（renormalization group）流的梯度性质，在球面 $S^d$ 上建立带局域耦合的重整化泛函性质，并导出无反常的 Ward 恒等式及其一阶变分形式的梯度公式，在多类标量/费米子理论中由有限性假设推出该公式对所有阶成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于考察微扰重整化群流是否具有梯度（gradient）结构，即 beta 函数能否写为某势的梯度，从而为固定点性质与球面自由能之间建立联系，并厘清反常与相关算符源项对此结构的影响。

Method: 在球面 $S^d$ 上研究带局域耦合的重整化泛函，陈述其有限性、局域外尔反常（local Weyl anomaly）、共形 Killing 矢量（conformal Killing vector）的 Ward 恒等式、旋转性质及自由理论性质，并由此构造泛函与其反常组合的无反常 Ward 恒等式；随后通过构造特定重整化方案推导梯度公式。

Result: 导出梯度公式，其对称度规（symmetric metric）包含球面上的两点函数，固定点处势在扩展形式下正比于球面自由能 $\tilde F$（up to a constant）；相关算符（relevant operators）源通过其外尔变分进入，在所求方案中梯度公式得以保持，且当自由理论某矩阵可逆时度规非退化。对多标量 $\phi^4$ 理论、$d=4-\varepsilon$ 中含 Yukawa 与四次耦合的标量与 Dirac 费米子、$d=3-\varepsilon$ 六次标量及 $d=6-\varepsilon$ 三次标量，均由有限性假设推出上述性质，梯度公式对球面上定义的 beta 函数在所有阶成立。

Conclusion: 梯度性质在所述理论类别中可由有限性假设普遍建立；对相互作用规范理论（interacting gauge theories），这些性质仍属假设。

Abstract: We study the gradient property of perturbative renormalisation group flows in $d=d_c-\varepsilon$, with a potential and a metric regular at $\varepsilon=0$ up to logarithms of gauge couplings. We state properties of the renormalised functional with local couplings on the sphere $S^d$: finiteness, a local Weyl anomaly, the Ward identity of a conformal Killing vector, rotation properties and statements on the free theory. From them we derive a Ward identity without anomaly for a combination of the functional and its anomaly. Its first variation is a gradient formula whose symmetric metric contains the two-point function on the sphere and whose potential at fixed points is, in an extended form of the properties, proportional to the sphere free energy $\tilde F$ up to a constant. Sources of relevant operators enter through their Weyl variation, and in a scheme which we construct the gradient formula persists, with a nondegenerate metric if a matrix of the free theory is invertible. For multiscalar $φ^4$ theory and for scalars and Dirac fermions with Yukawa and quartic couplings in $d=4-\varepsilon$, for sextic scalars in $d=3-\varepsilon$ and for cubic scalars in $d=6-\varepsilon$, we derive all these properties from a finiteness hypothesis. The gradient formula then holds to all orders for the beta function defined on the sphere. For interacting gauge theories the properties remain assumptions.

</details>


### [294] [Near-boundary and near-horizon momentum--Krylov response in noncommutative-inspired BTZ spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31106)
*F. A. Brito,F. G. Costa,J. A. V. Campos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算大质量探针粒子落入非对易启发（noncommutative-inspired）、经 Lorentzian 涂抹（Lorentzian-smeared）的 BTZ 黑洞时的固有径向动量，并将其用作动量-Krylov 复杂度（momentum-Krylov complexity）的增长率，从而研究涂抹参数对复杂度演化的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 Krylov 复杂度作为探针落入黑洞时的动力学诊断量，考察非对易涂抹（smearing）如何改变复杂度增长，并检验变形结果是否只是 BTZ 情形的重标度版本。

Method: 在固定渐近质量条件下，以探针的固有径向动量作为复杂度增长率；解析推导早期增长系数与晚期增长指数，并辅以数值计算进行验证，同时比较高斯型质量分布（Gaussian mass profile）与同霍金温度的 BTZ 参照。

Result: BTZ 极限下累积复杂度为 K0 = X(coshτ − 1)；含涂抹时早期增长系数为 F'_α(X)/4，晚期指数为表面引力 F'_α(x_+)/2。二者对涂抹参数的依赖不同，故变形响应并非 BTZ 曲线的重标度。远距离释放点下早期趋近 BTZ 而晚期速率不趋近；同霍金温度的 BTZ 可在约百分之十以内复现响应。临界涂抹处视界半径与表面引力以 1/2 和 3/2 的指数消失。固定质量下边界应力张量与 BTZ 一致，但 z^3 Fefferman–Graham 系数与边界锚定测地线长度不同。

Conclusion: 涂抹导致的复杂度响应无法由单一重标度描述，早期与晚期的失配是残余差异的来源；尽管边界应力张量不变，高阶 Fefferman–Graham 数据与测地线长度已能区分该非对易启发几何。

Abstract: We compute the proper radial momentum of a massive probe falling into a Lorentzian-smeared, noncommutative-inspired BTZ black hole at fixed asymptotic mass, and use it as the growth rate of momentum--Krylov complexity. In the BTZ limit the accumulated complexity is $\mathcal{K}_0 = X(\coshτ- 1)$. With smearing, the initial growth coefficient is $F'_α(X)/4$, while the late-time exponent is the surface gravity $F'_α(x_+)/2$. The two depend differently on the smearing parameter, so the deformed response is not a rescaled BTZ curve; we show this analytically and confirm it numerically. For a distant release point the early response approaches BTZ, whereas the late-time rate does not. A BTZ black hole with the same Hawking temperature reproduces the response to within about ten percent, and the remaining difference is set by the early/late mismatch. A Gaussian mass profile gives the same picture. The horizon radius and the surface gravity vanish at the critical smearing with exponents $1/2$ and $3/2$. At fixed mass the boundary stress tensor coincides with that of BTZ, but the $z^3$ Fefferman--Graham coefficient and the lengths of boundary-anchored geodesics do not.

</details>


### [295] [Electromagnetic Memory as a Collider Observable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31107)
*Felipe Diaz,Hernán A. González,Oriana Labrin,Jakob Salzer*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文指出电磁记忆（electromagnetic memory）与电荷关联函数（charge correlators）虽由不同探测器测量，但可由大规范对称性（large gauge symmetry）的Ward恒等式作为包含性可观测量（inclusive observables）联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁记忆探测软辐射，电荷关联函数探测硬带电径迹，二者分属不同探测器与不同的物理量。研究动机在于揭示这两类看似独立的可观测量之间存在由对称性所保证的内在联系。

Method: 利用大规范对称性的Ward恒等式建立两类量之间的关系；针对电磁流（electromagnetic current）产生的轻子对（lepton pairs），计算全部无质量多点记忆关联函数（massless multipoint memory correlators）以及有质量的两点函数（massive two-point function）。

Result: 关联函数可由天球自由玻色子表示（celestial free-boson representation）描述；其重合极限（coincident limit）给出软光子数（soft photon number）；在一阶辐射阶（first radiative order）关联函数保持有限，其中反冲（recoil）产生背对背的Sudakov对数（back-to-back Sudakov logarithms）。

Conclusion: Ward恒等式将软辐射型与硬径迹型可观测量统一为包含性可观测量，且所计算的记忆关联函数在天球自由玻色子框架下具有良好定义、一阶辐射修正下仍有限，反冲效应以Sudakov对数形式体现。

Abstract: Electromagnetic memory and charge correlators are measured by different detectors, sensitive to soft radiation and to hard charged tracks. We show that the Ward identity of large gauge symmetry relates these quantities as inclusive observables. For lepton pairs produced by an electromagnetic current, we compute all massless multipoint memory correlators and the massive two-point function, whose coincident limit gives the soft photon number. The correlators admit a celestial free-boson representation and remain finite at first radiative order, where recoil generates back-to-back Sudakov logarithms.

</details>


### [296] [Tensionless M5-brane in light-cone gauge. The spinor moving frame method is wanted](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31189)
*Igor Bandos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文对近期获得的 M 理论 5-膜无张力极限作用量完成了完整的光锥规范固定，并借助旋量动标架方法解决了局域费米 κ-对称性规范固定中的非平凡困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无张力极限下规范场强须非零且非平凡，导致 κ-对称性投影算符构造复杂，局域费米 κ-对称性的规范固定因此变得相当困难；同时需与不含世界体规范场的普通无张力 p-膜（null-p-branes）作对比。

Method: 采用旋量动标架方法（spinor moving frame method）：该动标架建立在规范场强的二次向量与 PST（Pasti-Sorokin-Tonin）标量导数之上，并要求二者在无张力极限下非零且非退化；据此完成光锥规范（light-cone gauge）固定，并简要讨论规范固定后作用量与运动方程的对称性，同时给出普通无张力 p-膜的旋量动标架表述以作比较。

Result: 实现了 M 理论 5-膜无张力极限作用量的完整光锥规范固定，并通过适配的旋量动标架化解了 κ-对称性规范固定的障碍；同时建立了普通无张力 p-膜（null-p-branes）的旋量动标架表述。

Conclusion: 旋量动标架方法是处理该类无张力极限理论中 κ-对称性规范固定的有效途径，其构造依赖于规范场强二次向量与 PST 标量导数；该框架亦可推广用于无世界体规范场的普通无张力 p-膜。

Abstract: We perform the complete light-cone gauge fixing for the tensionless limit of the M-theory 5-brane the action for which was recently obtained in [1]. The problem of gauge fixing of local fermionic $κ$-symmetry becomes quite nontrivial because the projector of that is constructed from the gauge field strength which in its turn is required to be nonvanaishing and nontrivial in this limit. To resolve this problem the spinor moving frame method has been adapted. The corresponding moving frame is constructed on a vector quadratic in the gauge field strength, which is required to be nonvanishing and nondegenerate in the tensionless limit, and the derivative of the PST (Pasti-Sorokin-Tonin) scalar. The symmetries of the gauge fixed action and equations of motion are briefly discussed. To make a comparison with the case of usual tensionless p-branes (null-p-branes), those which do not carry worldvolume gauge fields, we also elaborate their spinor moving frame formulation.

</details>


### [297] [Massive de Sitter correlators at one loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31214)
*Amara McCune*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文基于将大质量de Sitter关联函数表示为有限Mellin-Barnes积分，精确计算了原初关联函数中成对耦合圈图信号的挤压极限系数及其偏离挤压极限的修正，发现精确结果比晚期估计大10至30倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米子及带电荷大质量场仅成对与暴胀子耦合，其原初关联函数信号从单圈（one loop）开始，而现有信号模板多为晚期估计（late-time estimates），可能系统性低估信号强度。

Method: 将大质量de Sitter关联函数表示为有限Mellin-Barnes积分，精确计算挤压系数（squeezed coefficients）及其偏离挤压极限的修正；领头系数分解为硬部分（hard part）与软顶点（soft vertex），两者均精确求解。

Result: 无化学势时，精确系数与晚期估计相差一个仅依赖圈内质量与软顶点耦合的普适因子；重标准模型圈（heavy Standard Model loops）的精确信号比晚期估计大10至30倍。含化学势时比值依模型而异，螺旋矢量（helical vectors）基准达约30倍，费米子三角图达约100倍，与近期精确结果一致。

Conclusion: 晚期估计会显著低估此类圈图信号；偏离挤压极限时领头项很快失效，主要源于振荡相位的漂移（phase drift），文中对标量与费米子圈给出了该漂移的推导。

Abstract: Fermions and massive fields charged under an unbroken symmetry couple to the inflaton only in pairs, so their signals in primordial correlators begin at one loop. Templates for these signals are mostly late-time estimates. Starting from a representation of massive de Sitter correlators as finite Mellin-Barnes integrals, we compute the exact squeezed coefficients of these signals and their corrections away from the squeezed limit. The leading coefficient factorizes into a hard part and a soft vertex. Late-time estimates treat the hard part exactly but approximate the soft vertex by its late-time limit, whereas we compute both exactly. Without a chemical potential, the exact coefficient differs from the late-time one by a universal factor that depends only on the masses in the loop and the coupling at the soft vertex. For the heavy Standard Model loops, the exact signals are ten to thirty times larger than their late-time estimates. With a chemical potential, the ratio depends on the model, and at our benchmarks it reaches a factor of thirty for helical vectors and about a hundred for the fermion triangle, where we agree with recent exact results. Away from the squeezed limit the leading term soon fails, mainly through a drift in the phase of the oscillation, which we derive for scalar and fermion loops.

</details>


### [298] [Nonabelianization map for the $W_{3,c=2}$ conformal blocks. Part I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31232)
*Qianyu Hao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作启动了对 \(c=2\) 的 \(W_3\)-代数共形块（conformal blocks）的非阿贝尔化映射（nonabelianization map）的定义，利用 \(\mathfrak{gl}_3\) 谱网络（spectral network）数据，将黎曼曲面 \(C\) 上的 \(W_3\) 共形块用分支三重覆盖（branched triple cover）\(\tilde{C}\) 上的 Heisenberg 块表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受 Virasoro 共形块非阿贝尔化构造的启发，希望将这一构造推广到 \(c=2\) 的 \(W_3\)-代数共形块，从而建立其与三重覆盖上 Heisenberg 块之间的联系。

Method: 定义推广的非阿贝尔化映射，其依赖 \(C\) 上的 \(\mathfrak{gl}_3\) 谱网络数据；研究发现该映射还依赖谱网络对应的孤子退化度（soliton degeneracies），这一点在 Virasoro 情形中并未显式出现。由此给出 \(W_3\) 共形块以 \(\tilde{C}\) 上 Heisenberg 块所确定的费米子关联函数（fermion correlation functions）表示的新公式。

Result: 对三重覆盖中才出现的更具挑战性的 \((123)\) 型分支点（branch points）进行了检验；在最简例子（仅含两个 \((123)\) 型分支点）中，该非阿贝尔化构造给出了所期望的两点关联函数（two-point correlator）与 \(\tau\)-函数。

Conclusion: 该构造为 \(W_3\)-代数共形块提供了新的表述方式，并通过 \((123)\) 型分支点的最简例子获得验证，表明孤子退化度在三重覆盖推广中是必要的数据。

Abstract: This work is inspired by the nonabelianization construction of Virasoro conformal blocks. We start the process of defining the nonabelianization map which expresses $c=2$ $W_3$-algebra conformal blocks on a Riemann surface $C$ from Heisenberg blocks on a branched triple cover $\tilde{C}$ of $C$. This generalized nonabelianization map uses the datum of a $\mathfrak{gl}_3$ spectral network on $C$ and we find that this map also depends on the soliton degeneracies corresponding to the spectral network even though the soliton data didn't show up explicitly in the Virasoro case. This nonabelianization construction defines new formulas for $W_3$-algebra conformal blocks on $C$ in terms of fermion correlation functions determined by the Heisenberg blocks on $\tilde{C}$. We provide a check of the proposed construction associated to the more challenging $(123)$ type branch points which start to show up in three-fold covers. To be more precise, the simplest example that we focus on has only two of the $(123)$ type branch points and we show that in this example, our nonabelianization construction gives the desired two-point correlator and $τ$-function.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [299] [Corrections to the IPR in the localized phase of the Anderson model on the Bethe lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28705)
*Tommaso Rizzo,Marco Tarzia,Angelica Vecchiarelli*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文揭示Bethe格上Anderson模型局域相中有限尺寸效应高度反常：逆参与比（IPR）以幂律 $N^{-\kappa(W)}$ 趋近热力学极限，指数 $0<\kappa<1$ 由无序强度决定，且同一指数支配有限虚调节子的修正，并给出 $\kappa=2\beta-1$ 的解析推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域相中有限尺寸效应通常被认为较弱，但Bethe格上Anderson模型的IPR数据外推至热力学极限缺乏合适的标度变量，需澄清其有限尺寸标度的本质与来源。

Method: 在随机正则图（random regular graphs）上数值研究IPR的有限尺寸标度；构造共享Anderson模型临界性的可解模型（solvable model）解析导出 $\kappa=2\beta-1$；并对有限图的环路（loops）做自洽单圈（one-loop）处理，独立重现有限尺寸修正。

Result: 局域相内 $I_2(N)-I_2^\infty\sim N^{-\kappa(W)}$ 且 $I_2(\eta)-I_2^\infty\sim\eta^{\kappa(W)}$，$0<\kappa<1$ 随无序变化；解析关系 $\kappa=2\beta-1$（$\beta$ 为局域态密度分布（local density of states）幂律尾部指数）与数值定量吻合，单圈处理亦独立复现该修正。

Conclusion: 有限尺寸与有限虚调节子修正均由稀有Mott型共振（rare Mott-like resonances）杂化远距离局域本征态所支配：有限图缺失间距超过其尺度的共振对。据此确定了将IPR数据外推至热力学极限所需的标度变量。

Abstract: We show that finite-size effects in the localized phase of the Anderson model on the Bethe lattice are highly anomalous. On random regular graphs of $N$ sites the inverse participation ratio (IPR) approaches its thermodynamic-limit value as $I_2(N)-I_2^\infty\sim N^{-κ(W)}$, with a disorder-dependent exponent $0<κ<1$ throughout the localized phase, and the same exponent governs the correction due to a finite imaginary regulator, $I_2(η)-I_2^\infty\simη^{κ(W)}$. We derive $κ= 2β-1$, with $β$ the power-law tail exponent of the local density of states distribution, in a solvable model sharing the criticality of the Anderson model on the Bethe lattice. We then show that the same mechanism operates in the Anderson model itself, and find quantitative agreement with the numerics. A self-consistent one-loop treatment of the loops of finite graphs independently reproduces the finite-size corrections. Physically, a spectral identity and an exact sum rule tie both corrections to rare Mott-like resonances hybridizing distant localized eigenstates: a finite graph lacks the resonant pairs whose centers would lie farther apart than its size allows. Our results identify the scaling variable needed to extrapolate IPR data to the thermodynamic limit.

</details>


### [300] [Anomalous corrections to the inverse participation ratio in the localized phase on the Bethe lattice: insights from a solvable "toy" non-linear $σ$-model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28709)
*Tommaso Rizzo,Marco Tarzia,Angelica Vecchiarelli*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文在Bethe晶格Anderson模型局域相中解析推导了逆参与比（inverse participation ratio, IPR）随 regulateur η→0 的异常幂律修正 I₂(η)−I₂^∞∝η^{2β−1}，并借助一个"玩具"非线性σ模型、谱恒等式与Mott共振计数，将同一指数β与两本征态关联函数的衰减指数μ=2(1−β)精确联系起来，最后给出以(2β−1)ln N为标度变量的有限尺寸标度形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bethe晶格Anderson转变具有奇异的临界性，其局域相中IPR趋于渐近值的收敛方式为异常幂律而非普通解析行为，其机制与指数此前缺乏解析解释。本文旨在解析刻画该幂律修正的来源，并说明有限尺寸下观测到的"分数维"是否为真正的临界特征。

Method: 在双曲超平面上构造一个与Bethe晶格Anderson转变共享奇异临界性的"玩具"非线性σ模型（non-linear σ-model），将其腔方程（cavity equation）的体解与由对称破缺场H设定的边界层匹配；结合谱恒等式（spectral identity）与精确求和规则，并通过精确对角化（exact diagonalization）、无穷晶格上的布居动力学（population dynamics）以及玩具模型的解析计算进行验证。

Result: 玩具模型中，边界层匹配给出的修正∝H^{2β−1}，由线性化方程的次主导根1−β控制，临界点处变为∝1/|ln H|。该修正对应于η=0腔递推的零模，数值上确认其亦适用于Anderson模型及有限尺寸修正。同一指数精确确定能量间隔为ω的两本征态关联函数的衰减ω^{−μ}，μ=2(1−β)，包括其幅度。有限图上有限尺寸修正反映双峰本征态的缺失。

Conclusion: Mott共振（Mott resonances）计数为上述机制提供了物理图像。IPR的有限尺寸标度以(2β−1)ln N为变量，可统一描述有限η与有限N数据，并给出跑动分数维（running fractal dimension）的Kosterlitz型重整化群流；因此，在可及尺寸上观测到的局域态有限分数维属于对数型有限尺寸效应，而非本征临界性质。

Abstract: In the localized phase of the Anderson model on the Bethe lattice, the inverse participation ratio (IPR) approaches its asymptotic value as the imaginary regulator $η\to 0$ with an anomalous power law, $I_2(η) - I_2^\infty \propto η^{2β-1}$, where $β\in (1/2,1)$ is the tail exponent of the local density of states distribution. We derive it analytically in a "toy" non-linear $σ$-model on the hyperbolic superplane, which shares the exotic criticality of the Bethe-lattice Anderson transition. Matching the bulk solution of its cavity equation to a boundary layer set by the symmetry-breaking field $H$ gives a correction $\propto H^{2β-1}$, controlled by the subdominant root $1-β$ of the linearized equation; at criticality it becomes $\propto 1/|\ln H|$. Under a few natural assumptions the same mechanism operates in the Anderson model, where the correction is a zero mode of the $η=0$ cavity recursion, as we confirm numerically, also for finite-size corrections. Through a spectral identity and an exact sum rule, the same exponent fixes exactly the decay $ω^{-μ}$, with $μ= 2(1-β)$, of the correlation function of two eigenstates at energy distance $ω$, including its amplitude, as we confirm by exact diagonalization, population dynamics on the infinite lattice, and analytically in the toy model. A counting of Mott resonances between distant localized eigenstates explains this physically; on finite graphs, finite-size corrections reflect a deficit of doubly-peaked eigenstates. Finally, we derive the finite-size scaling of the IPR in the variable $(2β-1)\ln N$, which describes both finite-$η$ and finite-$N$ data. It yields a Kosterlitz-type renormalization-group flow of the running fractal dimension: finite fractal dimensions of localized states at accessible sizes are a logarithmic finite-size effect.

</details>


### [301] [Statistical mechanics of linear classification on hierarchical tree data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28824)
*Jerome Garnier-Brun,Federica Kulka,Carlo Lucibello*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该工作研究树广播过程（broadcast process on a tree）叶子数据的线性分类学习问题，提出有限深度复本理论（finite-depth replica theory），发现在大深度与弱正则下存在两个尺度分离阶段的双重下降（double descent）现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计力学（statistical mechanics）分析结构化数据时多依赖高斯等价（Gaussian equivalence），当少数潜变量影响所有输入坐标时该假设会失效，需超越该设定。

Method: 构建简单设定：线性分类器从树的 N 个叶节点推断经典广播过程的根标签；对携带标签方向保留精确分布律，将横向涨落近似为高斯，导出有限深度复本理论。

Result: 该理论准确描述实测学习曲线；在大深度与足够弱正则下出现尺度分离的双重下降：重建阈值以下的噪声范围内，第一次下降所需样本量按 N^ω（0<ω<1）缩放，过拟合峰值出现在 N 量级样本处。

Conclusion: 该设定可作为研究更深架构与层次化数据上生成模型（generative models）的可控起点。

Abstract: Deep networks owe much of their success to their ability to exploit the structure in data, from the grammar of language to the multi-scale organization of images. Yet, many statistical mechanics approaches to structured data rely on Gaussian equivalence, whose assumptions can fail when a few latent variables influence all input coordinates. In this work, we take a step beyond this setting with a simple setup, in which a linear classifier is trained to infer the root of the classical broadcast process on a tree from its $N$ leaves. Despite this simplicity, the data already display non-Gaussian statistics along the direction carrying the label, as well as strong finite-size effects. By retaining the exact law along this direction and approximating the transverse fluctuations as Gaussian, we obtain a finite-depth replica theory that accurately describes the measured learning curves. At large depth and sufficiently weak regularization, we uncover a double descent whose two stages are separated in scale. In a range of broadcast noise below the reconstruction threshold, the first descent occurs at a number of samples scaling as $N^ω$, with $0<ω<1$ set by the data statistics, whereas the overfitting peak occurs at order $N$ samples. Beyond linear classifiers, this setup could serve as a controlled starting point to study deeper architectures and generative models on hierarchical data.

</details>
